### [1] [The Cost of Consensus: Isolated Self-Correction Prevails Over Unguided Homogeneous Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00914)
*Blaž Bertalanič,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究发现，在7-8B参数量级的大语言模型中，同质化多智能体辩论不仅未能有效减少幻觉，反而因盲目从众、推理脆弱性和共识崩塌导致准确率下降，且计算成本比独立自纠错高出2-3倍，成本-准确率权衡不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（团队LLM迭代交换理由并投票）被广泛采用，其假设前提是同行评审能够过滤幻觉。然而，同质化辩论的失败动力学机制尚未被充分理解，研究者旨在通过受控实证研究揭示其失效路径。

Method: 研究采用N=10的同质化智能体团队（包括Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Ministral-3-8B），在GSM-Hard和MMLU-Hard两个高难度基准上进行R=3轮辩论。将同行辩论与独立自纠错及随机噪声控制（注入无关问题的理由）进行对比，并通过消融实验分析通信密度K∈{2,4,9}和采样温度T∈{0.4, 0.7}的影响。

Result: 研究将辩论失败分解为三条模型依赖路径：1）盲目从众——智能体不批判地采纳多数答案（模态采纳率高达85.5%）；2）上下文脆弱性——同行理由破坏原本正确的推理（脆弱率高达70.0%）；3）共识崩塌——多元投票丢弃生成池中已存在的正确答案（Oracle差距高达32.3个百分点）。消融实验显示，最小同行暴露（K=2）即可达到高从众水平。在所有配置下，辩论消耗的token量比自纠错多2.1-3.4倍（每问题高达28,631个token），但准确率相等或更低。

Conclusion: 在7-8B参数类模型中，无结构角色的同质化团队无法从非引导性同行交流中获益，独立自纠错始终提供更优的成本-准确率权衡，这对当前多智能体辩论方法的假设前提提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate, where teams of LLMs iteratively exchange rationales and vote on answers, is widely deployed under the assumption that peer review filters hallucinations. Yet the failure dynamics of homogeneous debate remain poorly understood, therefore we report findings from a controlled empirical study of teams of $N{=}10$ homogeneous agents (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Ministral-3-8B) across $R{=}3$ debate rounds on two high-difficulty benchmarks (GSM-Hard and MMLU-Hard). We compare peer debate against isolated self-correction and a stochastic noise control that injects rationales from unrelated problems. We decompose debate failure into three model-dependent pathways: sycophantic conformity, where agents uncritically adopt majority answers (modal adoption up to 85.5%); contextual fragility, where peer rationales destabilize previously correct reasoning (vulnerability rate up to 70.0%); and consensus collapse, where plurality voting discards correct answers already present in the generation pool (oracle gap up to 32.3 percentage points). Ablations over communication density ($K \in \{2,4,9\}$) and sampling temperature ($T \in \{0.4, 0.7\}$) show that conformity reaches high levels at minimal peer exposure ($K{=}2$) and intensifies with greater initial diversity. Across all configurations, debate consumes 2.1-3.4$\times$ more tokens (up to 28,631 tokens per problem) than self-correction for equal or lower accuracy. Our results indicate that, within the 7-8B parameter class, homogeneous teams without structured roles do not benefit from unguided peer exchange, and that isolated self-correction consistently offers a more favorable cost-accuracy tradeoff.

</details>


### [2] [Breaking the Communication-Accuracy Trade-off: A Sparsified Information Diffusion Framework for Multi-Agent Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00946)
*Jirong Zha,Chenyu Zhao,Nan Zhou,Zhenyu Liu,Tao Sun,Bin Zhang,Xiaochun Zhang,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多智能体协同目标跟踪的快速准确事件触发扩散滤波器（EDC-CIF），在降低通信负担的同时实现了更高的跟踪精度和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中协同感知的通信效率问题日益突出，现有的事件触发滤波机制在估计精度与通信成本之间存在不可避免的权衡，难以同时满足实时性与准确性的需求。

Method: 提出EDC-CIF算法：采用误差最小化的事件触发容积信息滤波器（CIF）进行局部估计，结合相关性感知的扩散策略进行全局融合，以实现数据传输减少、跟踪精度提升和收敛加速。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法具有良好可扩展性，在显著提升通信效率的同时，能够同时降低估计误差和计算时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的EDC-CIF算法有效解决了协同状态估计中精度与通信效率的权衡问题，为实时多智能体协同目标跟踪提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing relevance of multi-agent systems has drawn increasing focus on communication-efficient filters for collaborative perception to alleviate the system's communication burden. While the event-triggered (ET) mechanism can improve communication efficiency in collaborative state estimation, an inevitable trade-off exists between estimation accuracy and communication cost in ET filters. This paper proposes a fast and accurate ET diffusion-based filter for real-time multi-agent collaborative target tracking, aiming to reduce the system's data transmission without compromise in tracking performance. The proposed filter achieves improved tracking accuracy, reduced data transmission, and accelerated convergence using an error-minimized ET cubature information filter (CIF) for local estimation, and a correlation-aware diffusion strategy for global fusion. The experimental results confirm the scalability of the proposed EDC-CIF algorithm and demonstrate its efficacy in simultaneously reducing estimation error and computation time while significantly enhancing communication efficiency.

</details>


### [3] [Separation Assurance between Heterogeneous Fleets of Small Unmanned Aerial Systems via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01041)
*Iman Sharifi,Hyeong Tae Kim,Maheed Hatem Ahmed,Mahsa Ghasemi,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究城市密集空域中异构无人机机队的战术冲突消解问题，发现基于注意力增强PPOA2C的多智能体强化学习方法能使不同机队策略收敛至均衡以保持安全间隔，但均衡结果往往偏向配置更强或策略类型占优的一方，揭示了异构无人机运行中需要关注公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未来密集城市空域中，多家公司将运营异构的小型无人机系统（sUAS）机队，每个机队内的飞机具有同构配置（如装备、感知和通信范围），这使得飞机之间的战术冲突消解变得极其复杂。核心问题是：不同公司的异构机队之间的战术冲突消解策略能否收敛或达到均衡以确保无冲突空域？如果能，收敛后的策略是否会对配置较弱的公司的sUAS造成歧视？

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习范式，提出基于注意力增强的近端策略优化优势演员-评论家（PPOA2C）框架来解决机队内部和机队之间的冲突。每个机队独立训练自己的策略，同时保护隐私。在德克萨斯州达拉斯市上空进行包裹投递任务的仿真实验，研究异构机队中同构飞机的协同运行。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 两个具有不同但共享PPOA2C策略的机队可以达到均衡以维持安全间隔；(2) PPOA2C策略在冲突解决方面优于两个强基于规则的基线方法；(3) PPOA2C策略与基于规则策略交互时表现出更安全的行为，显示了其自适应能力；(4) 广泛的策略-配置评估显示，相似策略类型之间的均衡倾向于偏向配置更强的机队；(5) 即使在相似配置但不同策略类型的情况下，均衡也偏向某一种异构策略。

Conclusion: PPOA2C框架能有效实现异构无人机机队的战术冲突消解并达到均衡，确保空域安全。然而，均衡结果存在不公平性，偏向配置更强或策略类型占优的一方，这强调了在异构sUAS运行中需要开发具有公平意识的冲突管理机制。

Abstract: In the envisioned future dense urban airspace, multiple companies will operate heterogeneous fleets of small unmanned aerial systems (sUASs), where each fleet includes several homogeneous aircraft with identical policies and configurations, e.g., equipage, sensing, and communication ranges, making tactical deconfliction highly complex for the aircraft. This paper aims to address two core questions: (1) Can tactical deconfliction policies converge or reach an equilibrium to ensure a conflict-free airspace when companies operate heterogeneous fleets of homogeneous aircraft? (2) If so, will the converged policies discriminate against companies operating sUASs with weaker configurations? We investigate a multi-agent reinforcement learning paradigm in which homogeneous aircraft within heterogeneous fleets operate concurrently to perform package delivery missions over Dallas, Texas, USA. An attention-enhanced Proximal Policy Optimization-based Advantage Actor-Critic (PPOA2C) framework is employed to resolve intra- and inter-fleet conflicts, with each fleet independently training its own policy while preserving privacy. Experimental results show that two fleets with distinct, shared PPOA2C policies can reach an equilibrium to maintain safe separation. While two PPOA2C policies outperform two strong rule-based baselines in terms of conflict resolution, a PPOA2C policy exhibits safer interaction with a rule-based policy, indicating adaptive capabilities of PPOA2C policies. Furthermore, we conducted extensive policy-configuration evaluations, which reveal that equilibria between similar policy types tend to favor fleets with stronger configurations. Even under similar configurations but different policy types, the equilibrium favors one of the heterogeneous policies, underscoring the need for fairness-aware conflict management in heterogeneous sUAS operations.

</details>


### [4] [rAIson: Developing Reliable Decision-Making Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01351)
*Pavlos Moraitis,Nikolaos Spanoudakis,Antonis Kakas*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了rAIson平台，这是一个无需编写代码即可开发自动化、可靠且可解释决策智能体的高级技术环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决复杂现实应用中决策智能体开发的技术门槛问题，提供一种能够同时实现自动化、可靠性和可解释性的解决方案。

Method: 提出了rAIson平台，这是一个高级技术环境，通过无代码（no-code）的方式支持决策智能体的开发，该平台基于成熟的研究成果和技术进步构建。

Result: 平台的研究与技术已达到成熟阶段，能够支持开发复杂的现实生活应用，且整个过程无需编写任何代码。

Conclusion: rAIson平台为开发自动化、可靠且可解释的决策智能体提供了一种成熟、易用的高级技术解决方案，降低了应用开发的技术门槛。

Abstract: This paper presents the rAIson platform, a high-level technological environment for the development of automated, reliable and explainable decision-making agents. The research underlying the platform and its technological progress has now reached a mature stage that allows the platform to be used for the development of complex real-life applications without writing a single line of code.

</details>


### [5] [Talk is Cheap, Communication is Hard: Dynamic Grounding Failures and Repair in Multi-Agent Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01750)
*Yiheng Yao,Chelsea Zou,Robert D. Hawkins*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出多轮谈判博弈框架，发现多智能体LLM在动态共识任务中无法达成最优结果，揭示了四种失败模式，指出动态共识是关键的未被充分研究的能力瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准主要关注静态、单轮次任务，忽视了智能体在多轮交互中修复共识故障的能力。动态共识（通过交互协商意义）是多智能体协调的核心，需要专门研究。

Method: 设计迭代多轮谈判博弈，两个智能体分配共享资源至私有项目，存在可验证的联合最优结果。引入三个基线进行分解分析：Oracle基线（个体推理）、无对话基线（通信必要性）和完全透明干预（信息交换充分性）。

Result: 智能体配对在开源和闭源模型上均持续无法达成帕累托最优分配。识别出四种失败模式：(1)缺乏共享交互历史导致协调退化；(2)顽固锚定使初始提案被视为公理；(3)依赖表面公平而非收益最大化协调；(4)指称绑定失败导致遗忘承诺。

Conclusion: 动态共识是多智能体协调中关键且未被充分研究的维度。协调差距的瓶颈不在于个体推理能力或信息交换，而在于联合计划形成、承诺和执行的交互过程。

Abstract: Grounding is the collaborative process of establishing mutual belief sufficient for the current communicative purpose. While static grounding maps language to a shared, externally observable context, dynamic grounding is a joint activity where meaning is negotiated through interaction. Current multi-agent Large Language Model (LLM) benchmarks focus on static, one-shot tasks, overlooking the ability to repair grounding breakdowns across turns. We introduce an iterated, multi-turn negotiation game in which two agents allocate shared resources toward private projects with verifiable jointly optimal outcomes. While individual agents can identify Pareto-optimal allocations in isolation, agent dyads consistently fail to reach them across open- and closed-source models. Our investigation reveals four failure modes: (1) coordination degrades when shared interaction history is absent; (2) yet accumulated context can itself become a liability through stubborn anchoring, where initial proposals are treated as axiomatic rather than negotiable; (3) a reliance on perfunctory fairness (equal resource splits) over reward-maximizing coordination; and (4) failures in referential binding, where agents lose track of commitments across turns. These results highlight dynamic grounding as a critical and understudied axis of multi-agent coordination. Our framework decomposes the coordination gap into measurable components: the oracle baseline establishes that the gap is not attributable to individual reasoning limitations; the no-talk baseline establishes that communication is necessary; and a full-transparency intervention establishes that information exchange alone is insufficient: the bottleneck lies in the interactive processes of joint plan formation, commitment, and execution that constitute dynamic grounding.

</details>


### [6] [Koopman Representations for Early Outbreak Warning and Minimal Counterfactual Intervention in Multi-Agent Epidemic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01803)
*Florin Leon*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Koopman算子的框架，用于多智能体流行病仿真中的早期暴发检测和干预选择，通过低维潜空间表征实现暴发风险的早期预警，并发现最小化干预措施即可显著降低感染率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究近临界流行病状态下的早期预警与干预问题。在此临界区域，暴露或时机的微小变化会显著改变最终疫情走向，传统方法难以捕捉这种敏感性，因此需要一种能从早期轨迹中提取关键特征并预测暴发风险的方法。

Method: 构建一个多智能体流行病仿真模型，智能体具有移动模式、异质易感性、免疫依赖的病毒载量进展等特性；利用Koopman算子理论将早期轨迹窗口的聚合日观测数据编码到低维潜空间，利用其近似线性演化进行短期预测和暴发风险评估；结合随机森林分类器预测最终感染率是否超过暴发阈值。

Result: 在系统临界点附近的实验表明，该框架具有强大的早期预警性能，Koopman衍生特征有效促进了类别分离；反事实分析显示，极小干预（如让单选智能体居家一天）即可降低感染率，并常使轨迹降至暴发阈值以下。

Conclusion: Koopman潜空间表征能有效捕捉疫情早期非线性动态，为近临界流行病状态提供了可靠的早期预警信号，并支持识别高效的最小化干预策略，证明了小幅干预在临界状态下改变疫情走向的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a Koopman-based framework for early outbreak detection and intervention selection in a multi-agent epidemic simulation. Agents exhibit mobility patterns, heterogeneous susceptibility, immunity-dependent viral load progression, and local transmission through co-location. The goal of the simulation is to study near-critical epidemic regimes in which small changes in exposure or timing can alter the final outcome. Aggregate daily observables from early trajectory windows are encoded into a low-dimensional Koopman latent space whose approximately linear evolution supports short-horizon forecasting and outbreak risk estimation. These representations are combined with a random forest classifier trained to predict whether the final attack rate exceeds a major outbreak threshold. Experiments near the system tipping points show strong early warning performance, with Koopman-derived features contributing to class separation. Counterfactual analysis further shows that minimal interventions, such as keeping a single selected agent at home for one day, can reduce attack rates and, often, shift the trajectory below the outbreak threshold.

</details>


### [7] [MAGIC: Multi-Step Advantage-Gated Causal Influence for Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01805)
*Haohan Yu,Jinmiao Cong,Shengzhi Wang,Lu Wang,Chanjuan Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAGIC框架，通过因果干预量化多智能体间的长期因果影响并转化为内在奖励，在MPE和SMAC等基准测试中性能提升至少10.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习面临的关键挑战是设计能有效促进智能体间协调的学习信号，这需要能够量化智能体间真实、长期的因果影响。

Method: 提出MAGIC框架，利用因果干预和条件互信息量化长时域智能体影响，并引入基于优势的门控机制，将多步因果影响选择性转化为内在奖励，引导探索朝向有益且目标一致的行为。

Result: 在MPE、SMAC/SMACv2等多个标准MARL基准测试和任务族上的实验表明，MAGIC显著优于最先进方法，主要评估指标至少提升10.1%。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过量化长期因果影响并选择性转化为内在奖励，有效解决了MARL中的协调信号设计问题，显著提升了智能体协调性能。

Abstract: A key challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) lies in designing learning signals that effectively promote coordination among agents. Designing such signals necessitates the ability to quantify the true, long-term causal influence between agents. To address this, we introduce Multi-step Advantage-Gated Interventional Causal MARL (MAGIC), a framework that extracts multi-step causal influences between agents and selectively converts them into intrinsic rewards. MAGIC uses causal intervention with conditional mutual information to quantify long-horizon agent influence, and introduces an advantage-based gating mechanism to ensure exploration is directed toward beneficial, goal-aligned behaviors. Experiments across multiple standard MARL benchmarks and task families, including MPE and SMAC/SMACv2, demonstrate that MAGIC outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a significant margin, achieving an improvement of at least 10.1% in the main evaluation metric.

</details>


### [8] [Quality-Aware Exploration Budget Allocation for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01865)
*Dahyun Oh,Minhyuk Yoon,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应调整内在动机强度的MARL探索框架，通过全局强度控制与基于信号质量的个体预算分配，解决因探索强度不当导致的协作崩溃或策略发现不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作多智能体强化学习中，有效协调配置极其稀少。现有的内在动机方法依赖探索强度参数β，但其设置存在两难：过大会淹没任务奖励导致协作崩溃，过小则无法发现稀有策略。此外，不同智能体的内在奖励信号可靠性存在差异，需差异化分配探索预算。

Method: 提出结合全局与个体层面的自适应框架：（1）全局层面采用返回条件化Sigmoid调度（RCB）动态调整探索强度β；（2）个体层面引入奖励信号质量（RSQ）度量，根据信噪比统计自动识别信号可靠性，将探索预算集中于信号可靠的智能体。框架结合后继距离（SD）这一准度量内在奖励，其天然产生可区分的个体信号质量，并保证收敛性与排序保持性。

Result: 在七个协作基准测试（MPE、SMAX、MABrax）上，该方法在所有环境中均达到顶尖性能回报。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了内在动机探索中的强度自适应与预算分配两大挑战，证明了让接收噪声内在奖励的智能体降低探索强度的有效性，为协作MARL中的高效探索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) requires agents to discover joint strategies in a combinatorially large state-action space, yet effective coordination configurations are exceedingly rare. Intrinsic motivation, which augments task rewards with novelty bonuses, is a popular approach for driving exploration, but its effectiveness hinges on the exploration intensity $β$, where too large a value overwhelms the task signal and causes coordination collapse, while too small a value prevents discovery of rare strategies. We address two complementary challenges: adapting $β$ globally over training, and allocating the exploration budget across agents whose intrinsic reward signals vary in reliability. Our framework combines a return-conditioned sigmoid schedule (RCB) for global intensity control with a per-agent Reward Signal Quality (RSQ) metric that concentrates the exploration budget on agents with reliable signals. The core insight is that agents receiving noisy intrinsic rewards should explore less aggressively, and this allocation can be determined automatically from signal-to-noise statistics. Successor Distance (SD), a quasimetric intrinsic reward, naturally produces distinguishable per-agent signal quality, completing the framework with convergence and ordering preservation guarantees. On seven cooperative benchmarks (MPE, SMAX, MABrax), our method achieves top-tier returns across all environments.

</details>


### [9] [Coopetition-Gym v1: A Formally Grounded Platform for Mixed-Motive Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Strategic Coopetition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02063)
*Vik Pant,Eric Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 发布了Coopetition-Gym v1，这是首个结合连续动作混合动机环境、参数化奖励互惠性、校准相互依存系数、博弈论预言机基准及验证案例研究的平台，包含20个环境、126个参考算法及大规模实验语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合动机多智能体强化学习平台缺乏连续动作环境、奖励可配置性、博弈论基准和现实案例验证。为推动合作竞争（coopetition）策略研究，需要一个整合这四大要素、具备封闭形式收益和可验证历史案例的统一基准平台。

Method: 构建包含20个环境的基准平台，分为四大机制类别（相互依存与互补、信任与声誉、集体行动与忠诚、序贯互动与互惠）。每个环境具有封闭形式收益结构和校准的相互依存矩阵。核心方法是将收益与奖励分离，提供三种可配置奖励模式（私有、集成、合作）以进行奖励消融实验。同时提供Gymnasium和PettingZoo接口，并集成126个参考算法（含16种学习算法、7个博弈论预言机和基线）。对历史验证案例（如三星-索尼LCD、雷诺-日产联盟等）进行校准以验证环境有效性。

Result: 四个环境与历史记录的合作竞争关系校准良好，在验证标准上的复现率分别为98.3%、81.7%、86.7%和87.3%。参考实验对16种学习算法在所有环境和奖励配置下进行七种随机种子训练，生成25,708次运行训练语料库和1,116次行为审计语料库，均以CC-BY-4.0协议发布。

Conclusion: Coopetition-Gym v1是首个集连续动作混合动机环境、参数化奖励互惠性、校准相互依存系数、博弈论预言机基准和验证案例于一体的平台，为研究合作竞争动态中的策略学习提供了完整、可复现的研究基础设施。

Abstract: We present Coopetition-Gym v1, a benchmark platform for mixed-motive multi-agent reinforcement learning under strategic coopetition. The platform comprises twenty environments organized into four mechanism classes that correspond to four foundational technical reports: interdependence and complementarity (arXiv:2510.18802), trust and reputation dynamics (arXiv:2510.24909), collective action and loyalty (arXiv:2601.16237), and sequential interaction and reciprocity (arXiv:2604.01240). Each environment carries a closed-form payoff structure and a calibrated interdependence matrix derived from the corresponding report.
  Every environment exposes a parameterized reward layer configurable across three structurally distinct modes (private, integrated, cooperative). This separation of payoff from reward enables reward-type ablation, the platform's principal methodological apparatus. Four of the twenty environments are calibrated against historically documented coopetitive relationships and reproduce their outcomes at 98.3, 81.7, 86.7, and 87.3 percent on the validation rubric (Samsung-Sony LCD, Renault-Nissan Alliance, Apache HTTP Server, Apple iOS App Store).
  The platform exposes Gymnasium, PettingZoo Parallel, and PettingZoo AEC interfaces and ships 126 reference algorithms: 16 learning algorithms, 7 game-theoretic oracles, 2 heuristic baselines, and 101 constant-action policies. A reference experimental study trained the 16 learning algorithms on every environment under every reward configuration with seven random seeds, producing a 25,708-run training corpus and a 1,116-run behavioral audit corpus, both released under CC-BY-4.0 with Croissant 1.0 metadata. Coopetition-Gym v1 is the first platform to combine continuous-action mixed-motive environments, parameterized reward mutuality, calibrated interdependence coefficients, game-theoretic oracle baselines, and validated case studies.

</details>


### [10] [SOTOPIA-TOM: Evaluating Information Management in Multi-Agent Interaction with Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02307)
*Yashwanth YS,Ruichen Wang,Shihua Zeng,Xuhui Zhou,Koichi Onoue,Vasudha Varadarajan,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了SOTOPIA-TOM基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）智能体在多方信息不对称场景下的交互能力，发现现有模型在信息管理和隐私保护方面存在显著缺陷，但心智理论（ToM）干预能有效提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的智能体越来越多地应用于多方交互场景，它们需要妥善处理信息不对称问题，即知晓何时、向谁披露信息是恰当的。然而，现有的基准测试无法在真实的多方交互环境中衡量这一能力，因此需要一个新的评估框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了一个支持公开（广播）和私密（私信）通信的交互环境，并设计了160个人工审核的场景，涵盖8个行业领域，每个场景涉及3-5个智能体，具有分割的私有知识和基于频道的共享策略。建立了一个多维评估框架来衡量智能体在共享有用信息、寻求缺失细节、高效协调以及保护隐私方面的表现，并整合成综合指标INFOMGMT。

Result: 在6种LLM骨干网络和多种提示策略的测试中，表现最好的高推理模型（GPT-5）仅达到62%的INFOMGMT分数，显示出在信息寻求和隐私感知决策方面的持续不足。基于心智理论（ToM）的干预措施能更一致地改善协调与隐私之间的平衡，例如ToM-Coach将GPT-4o的关键隐私违规从9.9%降低至2.2%，同时将综合InfoMgmt分数从15%提升至40%以上（提升超2.5倍）。

Conclusion: SOTOPIA-TOM揭示了当前LLM智能体在复杂、信息不对称协调任务中的持续局限性，为开发更具隐私意识、具备心智理论能力的多智能体系统提供了一个可扩展的测试平台。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are increasingly interacting in multi-party settings, they need to properly handle information asymmetry, i.e., knowing when and to whom to disclose information is appropriate. Yet, existing benchmarks fail to measure this ability in realistic multi-party settings. Thus, we introduce SOTOPIA-TOM, a multi-dimensional benchmarking framework to evaluate LLM agents' ability to successfully navigate information asymmetric and privacy sensitive multi-party interactions. We create an interaction environment which enables both public (broadcast) and private (direct message) communication, and craft 160 human-reviewed scenarios across eight industry sectors, each involving 3 to 5 agents with partitioned private knowledge and channel-dependent sharing policies. To measure interaction abilities, we create a multi-dimensional evaluation framework to assess how well agents share useful information, seek missing details, coordinate efficiently, and protect privacy, which we also combine into a composite INFOMGMT metric. Results show that, across 6 LLM backbones and prompting strategies (vanilla, CoT-privacy, and ToM-based interventions), even the largest high-reasoning model (GPT-5) reaches only a 62% INFOMGMT score, which indicates persistent deficiencies in information seeking and privacy-aware decision-making. Additionally, ToM-based interventions more consistently improve the overall coordination-privacy balance (for example, relative to the vanilla baseline, ToM-Coach reduces critical privacy violations on GPT-4o from 9.9% to 2.2% while increasing the composite InfoMgmt score more than 2.5x from 15% to 40%). Overall, SOTOPIA-TOM exposes persistent limitations of current LLM agents in complex, information-asymmetric coordination and provides an extensible testbed for developing more privacy-aware, theory-of-mind capable multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [11] [LLM-enabled Social Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02335)
*Önder Gürcan,Moharram Challenger*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文主张LLM智能体应基于角色定义（通过人物描述实现）进行奠基，以将语言能力转化为社会行为，并提出了表示、混合控制和评估的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）使智能体能够通过自然语言进行交流，但流利的语言使用本身并不能产生社会可理解的行为。现有系统在角色、规范、意图和情境约束方面的奠基较弱，限制了其在社会环境中进行有意义互动的能力。

Method: 提出概念基线，论证LLM赋能的社会智能体应奠基于通过人物描述（persona descriptions）可操作化的角色定义，并在此基础上概述了表示、混合控制和评估三个方面的研究方向。

Result: 确立了基于人物的角色定义是连接语言能力与社会行为的概念基础，为构建具有社会智能的LLM智能体提供了理论框架和研究路径。

Conclusion: 基于人物的角色定义是将语言能力转化为社会行为的必要基础，是实现LLM智能体在社会环境中进行有意义参与的关键。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed agent-agent and human-agent interaction by enabling software, physical, and simulation agents to communicate and deliberate through natural language. Yet fluent language use does not by itself yield socially intelligible behaviour. Most current systems remain weakly grounded in roles, norms, intentions, and contextual constraints, limiting their capacity for meaningful participation in social environments. This paper develops a conceptual baseline for LLM-enabled social agents by arguing that they should be grounded in role definitions operationalized through persona descriptions. On this basis, we outline research directions for representation, hybrid control, and evaluation. The paper concludes that persona-based role definitions are a necessary foundation for turning language competence into social behaviour.

</details>


### [12] [When Stress Becomes Signal: Detecting Antifragility-Compatible Regimes in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02463)
*Jose Manuel de la Chica,Juan Manuel Vera,Jairo Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAFE框架，用于检测多智能体LLM系统中是否存在支持未来反脆弱学习的结构化变化，发现所有架构在语义压力下虽性能下降，但均展现出统计学上可检测的反脆弱兼容压力几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多智能体LLM系统的鲁棒性评估仅关注性能在扰动下是否保持，但忽略了语义压力是否可能暴露出可支持反脆弱学习的结构化变化这一关键问题。

Method: 提出CAFE统计框架：首先建模语义压力的受控期望分布，利用多维评判信号重构架构特定的观测有效压力分布，然后通过凸压力势下的分布Jensen Gap比较两个分布，以检测反脆弱兼容区域。

Result: 在银行风险分析基准上评估五种多智能体架构（扁平、层级、辩论、元自适应、集成），结果显示语义压力使平均评判质量下降约三分之一，但所有架构均呈现正向分布Jensen Gap且bootstrap置信区间高于零。

Conclusion: 即时质量下降可与统计学上可检测的反脆弱兼容压力几何共存；CAFE本身不是反脆弱学习器，而是测量层，用于识别何时何地应用反脆弱学习可能具有价值。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly used to solve complex tasks through decomposition, debate, specialization, and ensemble reasoning. However, these systems are usually evaluated in terms of robustness: whether performance is preserved under perturbation. This paper studies a different question: whether semantic stress exposes structured variation that could support future antifragile learning. We introduce CAFE, a statistical framework for detecting antifragility-compatible regimes in multi-agent architectures. CAFE models a controlled expected distribution of semantic stressors, reconstructs an architecture-specific observed effective stress distribution from multi-dimensional judge signals, and compares both distributions using a distributional Jensen Gap under a convex stress potential. A positive gap does not imply immediate performance improvement; instead, it indicates a convex-expansive deformation of the observed stress distribution, suggesting that the architecture exposes learnable stress structure. We evaluate CAFE on a banking-risk analysis benchmark with five multi-agent architectures: flat, hierarchical, debate, meta-adaptive, and ensemble. Across all architectures, semantic stress reduces average judged quality by roughly one third. Yet all architectures exhibit positive distributional Jensen Gaps with bootstrap confidence intervals above zero. These results show that immediate quality degradation can coexist with statistically detectable antifragility-compatible stress geometry. CAFE is therefore not an antifragile learner itself, but a measurement layer for identifying when and where antifragility learning may be worth applying.

</details>

### [13] [Adaptive Alarm Threshold Prediction in 4G Mobile Networks: A Percentile-Guided Deep Learning Framework with Interpretable Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00838)
*Ayon Roy,Sadman Sharif,Shiva Prasad Sarkar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个机器学习框架PCTN，用于从现网行为中自动预测四个告警阈值，在真实4G网络数据集上优于iTransformer等模型，参数量减少83%，实现了阈值的自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动通信网络中，告警阈值的设置依赖人工经验且固定不变，无法适应不同时段、流量和网络状态的变化。这导致高峰时段严重故障被漏检，而低峰时段轻微问题引发不必要的工程人员派出，影响运维效率和服务质量。

Method: 提出了一种百分位引导的标签推导策略解决无真实标签问题，构建了PCTN（混合输出头和动态alpha机制）模型。在来自三个厂商、九个区域的10,648个小区的真实4G网络匿名数据集上，与梯度提升树基线、CNN-BiLSTM注意力模型和iTransformer进行对比评估。框架采用每日重训练机制适应网络变化。

Result: PCTN在四个预测目标中的三个上表现最佳，相比最先进的iTransformer参数量减少83%（p<0.001统计显著性）。其混合输出头和动态alpha机制生成的阈值既准确又可解释，允许运维人员在不重新训练的情况下检查和调整学习策略。

Conclusion: 该框架通过每日重训练实现了阈值的持续自适应调整，解决了传统固定阈值的局限性，为移动通信网络运维提供了一种高效、可解释的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: In mobile telecommunications, alarms act as early warning signals. They are triggered when a cell, the basic unit of radio coverage, shuts down or behaves abnormally. This signals a degradation in service quality, which directly affects the customer experience. To fix the issue, operators rely on preset thresholds to decide when an engineer should be sent out. In practice, these thresholds are set manually and remain fixed regardless of the time of day, traffic levels, or overall network conditions. This often leads to serious faults slipping through during busy hours, while minor issues can cause unnecessary callouts when the network is quiet. This paper presents a machine learning framework that automatically predicts four alarm thresholds, audit window duration, inactive time limit, total fluctuation count, and per hour fluctuation limit, from live network behavior. Since no ground truth labels exist for thresholds, we introduce a percentile guided label derivation strategy and evaluate four models on an anonymized dataset of 10,648 cells across three vendors and nine regions from a real 4G network, comprising a Gradient Boosted Trees baseline, a CNN-BiLSTM with attention, the proposed PCTN, and an iTransformer. PCTN performs the best overall with respect to three of the four targets, outperforming a state-of-the-art iTransformer while using 83 percent fewer parameters. Its mixed output heads and dynamic alpha mechanism produce thresholds that are both accurate and interpretable, allowing operators to inspect and adjust the learned policy without retraining. All comparisons are statistically significant at p < 0.001. The framework undergoes daily retraining using new data, which enables the thresholds to constantly adjust to changes in the network.

</details>


### [14] [AIIM: Adaptive Inter-cell Interference Mitigation for Heterogeneous Multi-vendor 5G O-RAN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01112)
*Samuel Reinders,Alireza Ebrahimi Dorcheh,Ryan Barker,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AIIM，一种用于O-RAN架构的自适应小区间干扰缓解xApp。在全栈O-RAN混合实验平台上验证，AIIM通过协调多基站PRB分配，显著提升了QoS满意度并减少了干扰导致的PRB损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集5G部署中的小区间干扰是一个长期存在的问题，特别是在异构O-RAN环境中，基站间协调受限。现有研究主要依赖仿真或纯硬件测试平台，缺乏可扩展性和成本效益。

Method: AIIM部署在O-RAN近实时RAN智能控制器(near-RT RIC)上，显式建模邻小区间重叠PRB区域，学习协调分配策略以适应用户QoS需求和路径损耗变化。系统基于srsRAN、Open5GS和ORAN-SC构建，采用SDR与虚拟gNB/UE混合部署。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比比例公平调度基准，AIIM提升了QoS满意度，减少了干扰引起的PRB损失，同时保持了可比的聚合网络吞吐量。

Conclusion: AIIM证明了可扩展、学习驱动的O-RAN控制在异构多gNB 5G网络实际干扰管理中的潜力，为O-RAN架构下的干扰缓解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Inter-cell interference is a persistent issue in dense 5G deployments, especially in heterogeneous Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) environments where coordination between base stations is limited. This paper presents AIIM, an adaptive inter-cell interference mitigation xApp for the O-RAN near-real-time RAN Intelligent Controller (near-RT RIC) that performs coordinated physical resource block (PRB) allocation across multiple base stations under diverse traffic demands and channel conditions.
  Unlike prior studies that rely primarily on simulation or fully hardware-centric testbeds, AIIM is developed and evaluated in a full-stack O-RAN system built on srsRAN, Open5GS, and O-RAN Software Community (ORAN-SC), and deployed on a hybrid experimental platform that simultaneously combines software defined radio (SDR)-based and virtual gNodeBs (gNBs) and user equipment (UEs). This design preserves realistic PHY-layer interactions while substantially improving scalability, reproducibility, and cost-effectiveness for multi-cell interference experiments. AIIM explicitly models overlapping PRB regions across neighboring cells and learns coordinated allocation policies that adapt to per-user QoS demand and pathloss variation across the network. Experimental results show that AIIM improves QoS satisfaction and reduces interference-induced PRB loss relative to proportional-fair scheduling baselines while maintaining comparable aggregate network throughput. These results demonstrate the promise of scalable, learning-driven O-RAN control for practical interference management in heterogeneous multi-gNB 5G networks.\footnote{A video demonstration of the running system can be found at https://github.com/sireinders/AIIM-Multi-gNB-Interference.git.}

</details>


### [15] [MORPH: Multi-Environment Orchestrated Reinforcement Learning for PRB Handling in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01128)
*Alireza Ebrahimi Dorcheh,Tolunay Seyfi,Ryan Barker,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出MORPH，一种基于测量的多环境强化学习管道，通过融合真实测量和仿真吞吐量数据，解决O-RAN动态频谱接入中训练数据保真度与可扩展性难以兼得的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在O-RAN动态频谱接入中，RL训练依赖吞吐量信号的保真度。分析型或仅物理层仿真器虽然扩展性好，但缺失协议栈效应（如信令开销、重传）；而在符合标准的5G协议栈上进行详尽吞吐量分析速度慢且不稳定。现有方法难以兼顾仿真速度与模型准确性。

Method: 构建MORPH管道，基于OpenAirInterface (OAI) 5G-NR RF仿真模式，融合三种互补的吞吐量来源：(i) 在OAI协议栈上通过iPerf测量的应用层吞吐量（受控AWGN路径损耗设置）；(ii) 基于路径损耗条件的经验MCS选择分布，构建反映标准兼容链路自适应的分布感知理论吞吐量估计器；(iii) 来自3GPP参数化的物理层保真OFDM仿真器的可扩展吞吐量估计。训练并比较仅吞吐量反馈来源不同的智能体。

Result: 在OAI执行框架上的异构切片场景评估表明，与单一数据源训练相比，MORPH融合真实与合成吞吐量信号进行策略优化，能够产生更稳健的切片级性能，并改善SLA合规性，为单小区协议栈内的PRB级频谱共享与切片隔离提供了实用基础。

Conclusion: MORPH成功弥合了仿真保真度与可扩展性之间的鸿沟，为单小区架构下的频谱共享与切片隔离提供了有效解决方案，并为未来多小区频谱协调和干扰管理研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning (RL) solutions for dynamic spectrum access and radio resource management in Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) depend critically on the fidelity of the throughput signal used for training. Analytical or physical-layer (PHY)-only simulators scale well but often miss protocol-stack effects such as signaling overhead and retransmissions, whereas exhaustive throughput profiling on a standards-compliant 5G stack is slow and can be unstable under software execution constraints. This paper presents MORPH, a measurement-grounded multi-environment RL pipeline {for slice-aware PRB-level spectrum allocation (spectrum sharing and slice isolation within a single gNB)} built on OpenAirInterface (OAI) 5G-NR RF-simulator mode. MORPH leverages three complementary throughput sources: (i) application-layer throughput measured via \texttt{iPerf} on the OAI stack under controlled AWGN pathloss settings, (ii) empirical MCS-selection distributions conditioned on path loss, enabling a distribution-aware theoretical throughput estimator that reflects standards-compliant link adaptation, and (iii) scalable throughput estimates from a 3GPP-parameterized PHY-fidelity OFDM simulator. Using these components, we train and compare agents that differ only in the origin of their throughput feedback: an OAI-grounded practical agent, a simulator-driven agent, and MORPH, which fuses real and synthetic throughput signals for policy optimization. Evaluation on the OAI execution harness across heterogeneous slicing scenarios shows that MORPH yields more robust slice-wise performance and improved SLA compliance than single-source training, providing a practical foundation for PRB-level spectrum sharing and slice isolation within a single-cell stack and a stepping stone toward multi-cell spectrum coordination and interference management.

</details>


### [16] [Dynamic Task and Resource Scheduling Towards Green Space-Air-Ground-Sea Integrated Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01414)
*Yufei Ye,Shijian Gao,Xinhu Zheng,Liuqing Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G空天地海一体化网络，提出了一种动态任务与资源调度方法，通过分层卸载算法和联合优化策略，实现了绿色高效的海洋计算支持，平均任务延迟降低至少23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G泛在连接背景下，空天地海一体化网络（SAGSIN）成为为资源受限的海洋环境提供关键服务的新范式，但该网络面临多层任务调度复杂、系统动态变化大、资源分配不均等挑战。

Method: 提出了一种面向绿色SAGSIN的动态任务与资源调度方法：1）开发分层任务卸载算法，适应实时多维系统动态，并集成预测性切换策略以自适应控制卸载至卫星的数据量，防止切换后拥塞；2）联合优化无人机与基站的带宽分配、无人机轨迹及计算资源分配，以增强低空设备间的连接性并实现按需资源分配。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能更好地适应动态系统资源，与基准方案相比，平均任务延迟至少降低了23%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了SAGSIN中的多层任务调度难题，在保证绿色网络发展的同时显著降低了任务执行延迟，为海洋环境下的6G泛在连接提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In the context of 6G ubiquitous connectivity, the space-air-ground-sea integrated network (SAGSIN) emerges as a new paradigm to provide critical services for resource-limited ocean environments. To realize this paradigm efficiently, we propose an innovative dynamic task and resource scheduling approach for green SAGSIN that delivers computing support for vessels while minimizing overall task execution delay. To address the challenge of multi-layer task scheduling, a layer-wise task offloading algorithm is developed specifically for SAGSIN. It adapts to real-time, multi-dimensional system dynamics and integrates an anticipatory handover strategy that adaptively controls the amount of data offloaded to the satellite, thereby preventing post-handover congestion while improving satellite resource utilization. Furthermore, the bandwidth allocation of uncrewed aerial vehicles and base station, UAV trajectories, and computing resource allocation are jointly optimized to enhance connectivity among low-altitude devices and facilitate demand-driven resource allocation for green network development. Simulation results verify that the proposed method better adapts to dynamic system resources and achieves at least a 23% reduction in average task delay compared with benchmarks.

</details>


### [17] [An Intelligent eUPF for Time-Sensitive Path Selection in B5G Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01475)
*Rodrigo Moreira,Larissa Ferreira Rodrigues Moreira,Tereza Cristina Carvalho,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的用户面功能(eUPF)设计，利用深度Q网络(DQN)智能体实现MEC与云节点间的实时路径选择，并结合eBPF技术实现了低成本延迟测量，实验证明该AI驱动方案显著优于随机基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beyond 5G网络中新兴应用对速度和可靠性提出了严苛要求，迫切需要智能、灵活的流量管理机制来满足这些服务质量需求。

Method: 将路径选择问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，设计深度Q网络(DQN)智能体进行实时路径决策；提出一种新型被动延迟测量方法，利用eBPF程序关联GTP-U流量中基于TEID的时间戳，实现无需主动测试的低成本延迟估计。

Result: 实验表明，DQN智能体在平均延迟、奖励稳定性以及可靠低延迟路径选择方面均显著优于随机基准方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了AI驱动控制在B5G核心网络中的有效性，展示了强化学习在现代网络管理领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: In Beyond 5G (B5G) networks, intelligent, flexible traffic management is essential to meet the stringent speed and reliability requirements of new applications. This paper presents an improved User Plane Function (eUPF) design that uses a Deep Q-Network (DQN) agent for real-time path selection between Multi-access Edge Computing (MEC) and cloud endpoints. The path selection problem is formulated as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). We propose a novel passive delay measurement method that uses eBPF programs to link TEID-based timestamps in GTP-U traffic, allowing for low-cost delay estimation without active testing. Experiments show that the DQN agent substantially outperforms a random baseline, with lower average latency, more stable rewards, and more reliable low-delay path choices. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of AI-driven control in B5G core networks and the promise of reinforcement learning for modern network management.

</details>


### [18] [6G Needs Agents: Toward Agentic AI-Native Networks for Autonomous Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01546)
*Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Abderrahmane Lakas,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic AI-Native 6G架构，通过分层部署异构LLM代理实现网络自主推理，实验表明需在设备-边缘-核心 continuum 上异构部署以平衡时延、吞吐与精度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6G网络方案以优化为中心，依赖闭环控制且推理能力有限，难以满足AI原生网络对自主决策的需求，亟需向具备语义理解和分层推理能力的代理式架构范式转变。

Method: 提出四层架构：确定性网络基础设施层、意图与上下文语义抽象层、分层推理层、跨设备-边缘-核心的分布式多代理网络层；开发概念验证的代理推理与编排框架，并在领域特定6G基准与真实部署约束下进行大规模实证研究。

Result: 发现推理能力与系统效率存在根本性权衡，单模型无法同时满足时延、吞吐与精度要求；在continuum上异构部署LLM代理可有效平衡约束；量化对模型影响不均，需系统级优化而非仅模型压缩。

Conclusion: 代理智能是6G可行架构方向，但实现可扩展、可信、自推理网络面临分层推理、异构部署与系统级优化等核心挑战，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) networks are increasingly envisioned as AI-native infrastructures integrating communication, sensing, and computing into a unified fabric. However, existing approaches remain largely optimization-centric, relying on closed-loop control with limited reasoning capability. In this paper, we argue for a paradigm shift toward Agentic AI-Native 6G, in which Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents operate as bounded, policy-governed reasoning entities within a semantic control plane layered above deterministic 3GPP infrastructure. We propose a four-layer architecture that integrates deterministic network infrastructure, semantic abstraction of intent and context, hierarchical reasoning, and a distributed multi-agent fabric spanning device, edge, and core domains. To assess feasibility, we develop a proof-of-concept agentic reasoning and orchestration framework and conduct an extensive empirical study using a domain-specific 6G benchmark under realistic deployment constraints. Our results reveal a fundamental tradeoff between reasoning capability and system efficiency, showing that no single model simultaneously satisfies latency, throughput, and accuracy requirements. Instead, heterogeneous deployment of LLM agents across the device--edge--core continuum is necessary to balance these constraints. We further demonstrate that quantization introduces non-uniform effects across models, reinforcing the need for system-level optimization rather than model-level compression alone. These findings establish agentic intelligence as a viable architectural direction for 6G and highlight key challenges in achieving scalable, trustworthy, and self-reasoning networks. All experimental results and evaluation scripts are publicly available to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [19] [ShieldShare: Building a VPN-backed Android Hotspot for Secure Internet Sharing with Per-User Traffic Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01569)
*Carlos Semeho Edorh,Jialu Bi,Hanchen Ye,Dawood Sajjadi,Maryam Tanha*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ShieldShare，一个基于代理的Android应用，可在无需root权限的情况下实现基于VPN的热点共享，并提供用户流量统计功能，解决了主流VPN设备限制和Android原生热点不支持VPN流量路由的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VPN在共享环境中存在显著限制：主流VPN提供商施加设备数量限制，Android原生热点功能不支持将共享流量路由通过VPN连接，而现有解决方案要么需要root权限，要么缺乏全面的监控能力。

Method: 采用模块化架构设计，包含VPN检测模块、热点管理模块、支持HTTP、HTTPS和SOCKS5的基于代理的流量转发模块，以及具备配额管理的全面流量计量系统。

Result: 评估结果表明，ShieldShare能够可靠地将客户端流量通过VPN隧道路由，同时保持准确的每客户端带宽分配和统计，验证了系统的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: ShieldShare为审查严格或高监控环境提供了经济实惠、社区控制的安全访问解决方案，惠及活动人士、调查记者和共享家庭网络用户，已作为开源软件发布以支持进一步研究和实际部署。

Abstract: Virtual Private Networks (VPNs) have become essential privacy tools for mobile users, yet current implementations face significant limitations in shared environments. Mainstream VPN providers impose device limits, while Android's native hotspot functionality lacks support for routing shared traffic through VPN connections. Existing solutions either require root access or lack comprehensive monitoring capabilities. This paper presents ShieldShare, a proxy-based Android application that enables secure VPN-backed hotspot sharing with per-user traffic accounting without requiring root access. Our system employs a modular architecture comprising VPN detection, hotspot management, proxy-based traffic forwarding supporting HTTP, HTTPS, and SOCKS5, and comprehensive traffic metering with quota management. Our evaluation shows that ShieldShare reliably routes client traffic through VPN tunnels while maintaining accurate per-client bandwidth allocation and accounting. This enables affordable, community-controlled secure access in censored or high-surveillance environments, benefiting activists, investigative journalists, and shared household networks. We release ShieldShare as open-source software to support further research and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [20] [Toward Resilient 5G Networks: Comparative Analysis of Federated and Centralized Learning for RF Jamming Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01705)
*Samhita Kuili,Mohammadreza Amini,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的干扰检测框架，利用SSB的IQ样本在保护用户隐私的前提下实现了97%的高检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对5G及未来网络中日益增多的干扰攻击威胁，现有的机器学习和深度学习方法通常需要集中收集数据，导致用户设备隐私泄露。因此，迫切需要一种既能有效检测干扰又能保护数据隐私的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于联邦学习（FL）的干扰检测框架，该框架利用从射频域同步信号块（SSB）中提取的空中IQ样本进行工作。采用联邦平均（FedAvg）算法训练一维卷积神经网络（1DCNN），实现在不共享原始射频信号数据的情况下跨多个用户设备（UE）进行协作模型训练。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的FL框架达到了97%的准确率和97%的F1分数，在保护所有参与UE数据隐私的同时，性能优于包括MLP、1DCNN、SVM和逻辑回归在内的集中式基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了联邦学习在5G网络安全领域的有效性，证明了在不牺牲用户隐私的前提下，可以通过分布式协作训练实现对干扰攻击的高精度检测，优于传统集中式方法。

Abstract: Jamming attacks are proliferating and pose a significant threat to the security of 5G and beyond networks. These attacks target 5G radio frequency (RF) domain and can disrupt the communication in wireless networks. While conventional machine learning and deep learning approaches demonstrate its potential for jamming detection, they typically require centralized data collection, compromising the privacy of user equipment (UEs). This work proposes a federated learning (FL)-based jamming detection framework that operates on over-the-air In-phase and Quadrature (IQ) samples extracted from Synchronization Signal Blocks (SSBs) in the RF domain. The framework enables collaborative model training across multiple UEs without sharing raw RF signal data. We adopt Federated Averaging (FedAvg) algorithm to train a 1D convolutional neural network (1DCNN) for effective detection of attacks. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed FL framework achieves 97% accuracy and 97% F1-score, outperforming centralized baselines including MLP, 1DCNN, SVM, and logistic regression, while preserving the data privacy of all participating UEs

</details>


### [21] [GATE: GPU-Accelerated Traffic Engineering for the WAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01748)
*Rahul Bothra,Alexander Krentsel,Saptarshi Mandal,Brighten Godfrey,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Rob Shakir,R. Srikant*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了GATE，一种基于GPU加速的流量工程系统，通过高度可并行化的分解方法，在保持快速运行时的同时收敛到可证明的最优解，比现有最优方法快5-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流量工程解决方案面临一个核心权衡困境：最优解算法随着网络规模增长运行时间变得不可接受，而近似求解器则导致显著的效率损失。网络规模的持续扩大使得这一问题日益严峻。

Method: 提出了GPU加速流量工程(GATE)方法，采用高度可并行化的GPU兼容分解技术，通过迭代方式收敛到可证明的最优解。该方法随网络规模增长并行性增强，支持广泛的公平性目标，并提供理论上保证的收敛性和有界时间内的近优收敛。

Result: 在两个大型云WAN的生产迹上评估表明，GATE实现近优解的速度比最先进方法快5-10倍，同时保持了最优解质量。验证了该方法在保持解质量的同时显著提升运行效率。

Conclusion: GATE成功打破了流量工程中速度与最优性的权衡困境，为大规模网络流量工程提供了一个既快速又最优的解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Traffic engineering (TE) has become a crucial tool for enforcing routing policy and maintaining operational efficiency in large networks. Existing TE solutions pick an objective function to optimize, aiming to balance (i) allocating traffic optimally with (ii) reacting quickly to demand changes and disruption events. However, as the scale of networks grows, the runtime of the existing optimal solution becomes infeasibly large. The alternative - approximate solvers - result in costly inefficiencies.
  We present GPU-Accelerated Traffic Engineering (GATE), which achieves the best of both worlds: enabling fast TE runtimes through a highly-parallelizable GPU-compatible decomposition, while iteratively converging to the provably optimal solution. GATE unlocks a unique set of desirable properties: it becomes increasingly parallelizable with network size, supports a wide spectrum of fairness objectives, and offers theoretically guaranteed convergence to the optimal solution and near-optimal convergence within a bounded time. We evaluate GATE on production traces from two large cloud WANs, and show that GATE achieves near-optimal solutions 5-10x faster than state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [22] [A Protocol-Independent Transport Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02210)
*Kimiya Mohammadtaheri,David Gao,Samuel Zhang,Matthew Chen,Eric Su,Pengyu Ji,Saad Syed,Chris Neely,Mario Baldi,Nachiket Kapre,Mina Tahmasbi Arashloo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了PITA，一种协议无关的传输层架构，通过统一抽象实现了数据平面的完全可编程性，同时保持了线速性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络传输层 increasingly implemented in NIC hardware以满足性能需求，但导致难以演进或部署新协议。现有方案要么固定数据平面协议逻辑，要么在架构中嵌入特定协议假设，限制了单一硬件平台支持的协议范围。

Method: PITA通过围绕事件、状态和指令的统一抽象来构建数据平面，消除了协议特定假设。重新设计了调度、包生成和数据重组等核心组件以在此抽象上运行。在Alveo U250卡上进行评估，运行频率250MHz。

Result: PITA成功支持多样化协议语义，在同一条数据路径上实现了TCP和RoCE，并保持了它们各自的端到端行为。针对性微基准测试显示PITA重新设计的组件在苛刻条件下维持高性能，具有适度的硬件开销。

Conclusion: PITA证明了协议无关的数据平面设计可行性，成功平衡了可编程性和高性能需求，为未来传输协议的创新和部署提供了灵活的硬件平台。

Abstract: The network transport layer is increasingly implemented in the NIC hardware to meet the performance demands of modern workloads, but this has made it difficult to evolve or deploy new transport protocols. Existing approaches either fix protocol logic in the data-path or build protocol-specific assumptions into the architecture that limit the range of protocols that can be supported on a single hardware substrate.
  We present PITA, a protocol-independent transport architecture that enables full data-path programmability while sustaining line-rate performance. PITA eliminates protocol-specific assumptions by structuring the data-path around a uniform abstraction over events, state, and instructions, and rethinks core components, including scheduling, packet generation, and data reassembly, to operate on this abstraction. We evaluate PITA along key dimensions reflecting the goals of its protocol-agnostic datapath design. Specifically, we show that PITA supports diverse protocol semantics by showing it can implement TCP and \roce on the same data path and preserve their distinct end-to-end behavior. Through targeted microbenchmarks and synthesis on Alveo U250 cards, we show that PITA's redesigned components sustain high performance under demanding conditions, with modest hardware overhead and meeting timing at 250MHz.

</details>


### [23] [Throughput Analysis and On-Board Buffer Sizing for Hybrid RF and Optical LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02001)
*Cao-Vien Phung,Thomas Röthig,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合RF/激光卫星网络的吞吐量性能与缓冲区设计问题，提出通过选择合适的传输调度优先级而非增加发射功率，可在最大化吞吐量的同时最小化缓冲区需求并降低丢包率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络日益采用激光（自由空间光通信，FSO）链路以提供大容量通信。尽管激光星间链路具有高吞吐量和低延迟优势，但在恶劣大气条件导致光路中断期间，仍需保留射频（RF）上下行链路以维持连接。在此混合链路场景下，卫星缓冲区设计成为关键挑战，特别是在预期光传输速率达100Gb/s及以上时，严格的星载能量和重量限制使得缓冲区容量规划至关重要。

Method: 本文针对具有有限缓冲区容量、干扰感知调度以及天气相关的激光链路中断概率的混合RF/激光卫星网络，建立了分析模型，对其吞吐量性能和缓冲区尺寸进行了理论分析与数值仿真研究。

Result: 数值结果表明，激光通信能带来显著的性能增益。研究发现，与传统增加卫星发射功率以最大化吞吐量的方法不同，通过选择合适的传输调度优先级，即可在现实的运行条件和约束下实现吞吐量最大化，同时最小化缓冲区需求并降低丢包概率。

Conclusion: 在混合RF/激光卫星网络中，优化的传输调度策略比单纯增加发射功率更为高效，能够在满足严格星载资源约束的前提下，有效平衡吞吐量、缓冲区尺寸与丢包率，为高性能卫星网络设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks are increasingly adopting laser (Free Space Optics, FSO) links to provide high-capacity communications. Although laser inter-satellite links offer high throughput and low latency, RF up- and downlinks remain necessary to maintain connectivity during optical outages caused by adverse atmospheric conditions. In such hybrid link scenarios, satellite buffer design remains a key challenge, since up- and downlink traffic must be buffered and forwarded among satellite nodes. The hybrid RF/FSO scenario requires careful transmission scheduling, especially at envisioned optical transmission rates of 100Gb/s and beyond, making buffer sizing critical under strict onboard energy and weight constraints. Thus, this paper analyzes throughput performance and buffer sizing in hybrid RF/laser satellite networks with finite buffer capacity, interference-aware scheduling, and weather-dependent laser link outage probabilities. Numerical results indicate that laser communications bring significant performance gains. Instead of increasing the transmission power of the satellite to maximize the throughput, we can select a suitable transmission scheduling priority to achieve a maximum throughput, while minimizing the buffer requirement, and lowering packet loss probability under realistic operational conditions and constraints.

</details>


### [24] [Toward the Internet of Space Things: Performance Analysis of LEO Satellite Relay Networks using mmWave and sub-THz links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02061)
*Sergi Aliaga,Ahmad Masihi,Vitaly Petrov,Marc Sanchez Net,Josep M. Jornet*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用毫米波和亚太赫兹星间链路构建卫星中继骨干网，以解决现有地面站对空间用户支持不足的问题，仅需10颗LEO中继卫星即可实现全天候连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着商业航天经济的扩张，现有地面基础设施在支持下一代“空间用户”（如立方星、空间数据中心等）的数据密集型连续连接需求方面面临严重瓶颈，即使使用现有的Ku波段地面中继网络，LEO立方星的每日接触时间也往往仅限于几分钟。

Method: 建立了一个综合数学框架，联合考虑复杂的时变轨道动力学和毫米波/亚太赫兹链路特性，并推导了接触概率、信道容量和能效等关键性能指标。

Result: 数值结果（经计算机仿真交叉验证）表明，该系统相比现有地面站网络可实现数个数量级的性能提升；确定了下载容量的基本界限，并证明仅需10颗LEO中继卫星即可实现24/7全天候连接。

Conclusion: 毫米波和亚太赫兹卫星中继网络是一种有前景、可扩展且节能的解决方案，能够显著改善未来各类空间飞行器的连接性。

Abstract: As the commercial space economy expands, existing ground-based infrastructure faces severe bottlenecks in supporting the data-intensive continuous connectivity needs of next-generation "space users," including CubeSats, space data centers, and more. Even when utilizing existing Ku-band ground relay networks, the contact time with a CubeSat at low-Earth orbit (LEO) is often still limited to minutes per day only. This paper analyzes an alternative system design that leverages emerging high-rate millimeter-wave (mmWave) and sub-terahertz (sub-THz) inter-satellite links to build a high-throughput and high-availability satellite-based relay backbone for space vehicles. To evaluate this concept, we develop a comprehensive mathematical framework that jointly incorporates complex time-variant orbital dynamics and mmWave/sub-THz link characteristics. We then derive the key performance indicators, including contact probability, channel capacity, and energy efficiency. The numerical results, cross-verified by computer simulations, demonstrate that such systems can provide improvements of up to several orders of magnitude compared to existing networks of ground stations. Notably, we identify a fundamental bound on download capacity and show that continuous 24/7 connectivity becomes achievable with only ten LEO relay satellites. These findings establish mmWave and sub-THz satellite relay networks as a promising, scalable, and energy-efficient solution, thus unlocking improved connectivity with various space vehicles of tomorrow.

</details>


### [25] [Graph Transformers and Stabilized Reinforcement Learning for Large-Scale Dynamic Routing Modulation and Spectrum Allocation in Elastic Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02075)
*Michael Doherty,Alejandra Beghelli,Laura Toni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 首次成功将Transformer模型应用于光网络动态RMSA问题，通过结合旋转位置编码、无效动作掩码和有效质量正则化等技术实现稳定训练，在最大143节点、362链路的规模上，相比最优基准算法提升最多13%的可支持流量负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已广泛应用于光网络动态路由、调制和频谱分配(RMSA)，但此前尚无工作将Transformer用于该任务。主要障碍在于Transformer的高数据和计算需求，以及与强化学习结合时潜在的训练不稳定性。

Method: 结合机器学习领域最新进展：针对图结构数据的旋转位置编码、离策略无效动作掩码和有效质量正则化，配合GPU加速仿真，首次实现Transformer在动态RMSA任务上的稳定强化学习训练。

Result: 在TopologyBench数据库的真实网络拓扑（最大143节点、362链路、每链路320个12.5GHz频隙单元、100Gbps流量请求）上进行系统基准测试，这是迄今RL应用的最大规模动态RMSA问题。在低阻塞概率(<0.1%)条件下，相比最优基准算法可支持最多4%的额外流量负载，相比之前RL方法和启发式算法最多提升13%的可支持流量负载。

Conclusion: 这是首个在所有基准测试中均超越传统方法的强化学习RMSA算法，证明了Transformer在大规模光网络资源优化问题上的可行性和优越性。所有代码已开源供复现和未来基准测试使用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been widely applied to dynamic routing, modulation and spectrum assignment (RMSA) in optical networks, yet no prior work has trained a transformer model for this task. We attribute this to the high data and compute requirements of transformers and potential training instabilities with RL. We address this gap by combining recent advances from the machine learning literature (rotary positional encodings for graph-structured data, off-policy invalid action masking, and valid mass regularization) with GPU-accelerated simulation to achieve, for the first time, stable RL training of a transformer for dynamic RMSA. We demonstrate, through systematic benchmarking against previous RL methods and heuristic algorithms, that ours is the first RL method to exceed all benchmarks, increasing the supportable traffic load by up to 13\%. To demonstrate the scalability of our approach, we train on real network topologies from the TopologyBench database up to 143 nodes and 362 links, with 320 x 12.5\,GHz frequency slot units per link, and 100\,Gbps traffic requests. To our knowledge, these are the largest dynamic RMSA problems to which RL has been applied. We find up to 4\% increased traffic load can be supported at low blocking probability (<0.1\%) with our method compared to the best available benchmark algorithm. We present an ablation study of the components of our training algorithm, the dynamics of the loss function during training, and analyze the allocation decisions of the trained models. We make all code used to produce this paper openly available for reproduction and future benchmarking: https://github.com/micdoh/XLRON.

</details>


### [26] [Hierarchical Cooperative MARL for Joint Downlink PRB and Power Allocation in a 5G System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02149)
*Alireza Ebrahimi Dorcheh,Tolunay Seyfi,Ryan Barker,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分层协作多智能体强化学习框架，用于5G下行链路的PRB和功率联合分配，通过三阶段课程训练在物理级仿真环境中实现了比比例公平调度更高的小区吞吐量，同时保持了较好的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G下行无线资源管理需要在时变无线条件下联合优化用户调度和发射功率分配。在OFDMA系统中，由于PRB分配是组合问题、功率分配是连续问题，且性能依赖于信道演化、链路自适应和长期公平性，这使得联合优化极具挑战性。

Method: 提出了分层协作多智能体强化学习框架，采用分阶段课程训练。系统级仿真基于Sionna实现，使用Sionna RT进行无线场景构建和移动感知光线追踪信道生成。控制任务分解为两个顺序阶段：PRB智能体学习用户级资源份额，通过确定性的信道感知配额解析器转换为精确PRB分配；功率智能体在用户及其分配的PRB符号资源间分配基站功率预算。框架在跨层循环中运行，包含自适应调制编码、HARQ反馈、外环链路自适应和基于平滑吞吐量与Jain公平性指数的公平感知奖励。训练通过PRB分配、功率控制和联合微调三阶段课程提高稳定性。

Result: 在匹配信道实现条件下，与等功率传输的比例公平(PF)调度器以及两个消融实验（分别隔离学习的PRB和功率控制组件）进行对比。结果表明，两个学习组件相对于PF调度器都能改善吞吐量分布，而完整的PRB和功率控制器实现了最大的小区吞吐量增益，同时Jain公平性指数仅有轻微下降。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层协作多智能体强化学习框架能够有效解决5G下行链路PRB和功率联合分配问题，在物理级仿真环境中实现了吞吐量和公平性之间的良好平衡，证明了深度强化学习在复杂无线资源管理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient downlink radio resource management in 5G requires jointly optimizing user scheduling and transmit-power allocation under time-varying wireless conditions. This is challenging in OFDMA systems because PRB assignment is combinatorial, power allocation is continuous, and performance depends on channel evolution, link adaptation, and long-term fairness. We propose a hierarchical cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning framework with staged curriculum training for joint downlink PRB and power allocation in a physically grounded 5G environment. System-level simulation is implemented in Sionna, while Sionna RT supports wireless scene construction and mobility-aware ray-traced channel generation. The control task is decomposed into two sequential stages: a PRB agent learns user-level resource shares, which are converted to exact PRB assignments by a deterministic channel-aware quota resolver, and a power agent distributes the base-station power budget across users and their assigned PRB-symbol resources. The framework operates in a cross-layer loop with adaptive modulation and coding, HARQ feedback, outer-loop link adaptation, and a fairness-aware reward based on smoothed throughput and Jain's fairness index. Training stability is improved through a three-phase curriculum for PRB allocation, power control, and joint fine-tuning. Under matched channel realizations, we compare against a PF scheduler with equal-power transmission and two ablations isolating the learned PRB and power-control components. Results show that both learned components improve throughput distribution relative to PF, while the full PRB and power controller achieves the largest cell-throughput gain with only a modest reduction in Jain's fairness index.

</details>


### [27] [Risk-Budgeted Online Scheduling for Continuous Edge Inference over Evolving Time Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02179)
*Houyi Qi,Minghui Liwang,Sai Zou,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出AEGIS框架，一种面向连续边缘推理的预测驱动风险预算在线调度方法，通过动态风险预算和LSTM预测实现长期时效性保障，显著提升及时推理比率并降低违规风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要优化瞬时延迟或单时隙效用，而忽视了连续服务中跨时间截止期限违规动态的调控，难以在时变信道、波动负载和耦合资源约束下维持长期时效性保证。

Method: 将截止期限违规倾向建模为可更新的跨时间控制状态（动态用户级风险预算）；利用LSTM进行短期状态预测以构建平滑的违规风险代理；将时序资源竞争建模为耦合可行性约束下的势对齐博弈；提出具有有限步收敛的异步在线算法。

Result: 实验表明，AEGIS相较于代表性基线方法，提高了及时推理比率，降低了平均违规风险和违规突发长度，并在延迟-风险-收敛性之间实现了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: AEGIS通过跨时间风险预算机制与预测驱动决策，有效解决了连续边缘推理中的长期时效性保障问题，为边缘智能系统提供了一种稳定高效的在线调度方案。

Abstract: Continuous edge inference necessitates not merely low per-timeslot latency, but sustained timeliness guarantees in the presence of time-varying channels, fluctuating edge workloads, and coupled bandwidth-computing resource constraints. Existing studies predominantly optimize instantaneous delay or per-timeslot utility, while largely overlooking the regulation of cross-time deadline violation dynamics in continuous services. To address this, we propose AEGIS, a prediction-empowered risk-budgeted online scheduling framework for continuous edge inference. AEGIS models deadline-violation tendency as an updatable cross-time control state through dynamic user-level risk budgets, so that online scheduling accounts for both instantaneous efficiency and long-term service stability. To support proactive decision making, AEGIS leverages LSTM-based short-term state prediction to construct a smooth deadline-violation risk surrogate, and formulates the resulting time-wise resource competition as a potential-aligned game under coupled feasibility constraints. An asynchronous online algorithm is then developed with finite-step convergence. Experiments demonstrate that AEGIS improves the timely inference ratio, reduces average violation risk and violation burst length, and achieves a favorable delay--risk--convergence trade-off over representative baselines.

</details>


### [28] [Forecasting-Driven Stable Successor Matching for UAV-Assisted Continuous Edge Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02180)
*Houyi Qi,Minghui Liwang,Yuhan Su,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fresco框架，通过LSTM预测中断风险并提前预留备用UAV，实现主动连续服务调度，显著降低了服务中断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人机辅助边缘网络（UEN）中，由于节点移动、能量耗尽或信道动态变化，现有的即时资源优化或被动切换机制往往导致服务发生中断。为了解决这一服务持续性问题，避免故障后的被动恢复，需要一种能够提前准备继任无人机的主动式服务保障方法。

Method: 提出了一个名为Fresco的预测驱动主动预留连续服务调度框架。该框架首先利用LSTM模块根据历史网络观测数据预测正在执行任务的短期中断风险；随后，提出在线风险感知的继任者匹配方案，在满足时延、资源和能量约束下为高风险任务选择合适的备用UAV，并进行最小的通信/计算资源预留和轻量级服务上下文同步，以实现高效的接管准备。

Result: 实验结果表明，相较于被动式和非预测性基准方案，Fresco显著减少了服务中断次数，提高了任务连续性，且仅需引入适度的资源预留开销。

Conclusion: Fresco框架验证了在动态UEN环境中，通过结合深度学习风险预测与主动资源预留策略，能够有效保障计算密集型和时延敏感型应用的服务连续性，为未来无人机边缘网络的服务保障提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Continuous and reliable service support is crucial for emerging latency-sensitive and computation-intensive applications in UAV-assisted edge networks (UENs) due to operational dynamics and environmental uncertainty. Although conventional designs can improve coverage and computing efficiency, they often rely on instantaneous resource optimization or reactive handover, rendering ongoing services vulnerable to non-negligible interruptions when the serving UAV degrades due to mobility, energy depletion, or channel dynamics. To avoid such post-failure recovery, a promising approach is to prepare a successor UAV in advance, i.e., a standby UAV that reserves minimal resources and synchronizes service context for possible takeover. Thus, we consider a dynamic UEN architecture where each mobile user carries an ongoing computing mission requiring persistent service support, while UAVs provide wireless access and computing services under time-varying network dynamics and stringent onboard energy constraints. To facilitate proactive and continuous service provisioning, we propose a forecasting-driven proactive reservation-based continuous service scheduling framework, termed Fresco. In Fresco, an LSTM-based module is first used to predict short-term disruption risks of ongoing missions from historical network observations. Guided by these predictions, an online risk-aware successor matching scheme selects suitable standby UAVs for high-risk missions under delay, resource, and energy constraints, while incorporating minimal communication/computation reservation and lightweight service-context synchronization for efficient takeover preparation. Experiments show that Fresco significantly reduces service interruptions and improves mission continuity over reactive and non-predictive baselines, with only modest reservation overhead.

</details>


### [29] [Zero-Trust Bilateral Edge Service Trading with Deposit-Refund Regulation for Runtime Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02182)
*Houyi Qi,Minghui Liwang,Zhipeng Cheng,Xiaoyu Xia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZEBRIS，一个针对隐私敏感服务的零信任双边边缘服务交易框架，采用押金-退款监管机制，在零信任安全框架下实现了资源调度优化与合规性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私敏感的边缘服务需要在零信任安全框架下优化多类型资源调度以支持可信服务供给，但现有研究很少建模运行时合规性如何联合影响动态边缘市场中的双边清算、事后结算和未来卖方资格，这导致了监管不足的问题。

Method: 提出ZEBRIS框架：1) 将边缘供给建模为零信任合规服务包的交易形式，买方有效估值涵盖服务价值、延迟惩罚和隐私风险，卖方有效要价包含资源和合规成本；2) 设计资源感知的正边际双边清算机制；3) 提出基于可测运行时合规性的上限押金-退款结算规则；4) 根据实际合规结果更新每个卖方未来的安全态势。

Result: ZEBRIS满足双边个体理性和无补贴弱预算平衡。实验表明，相比代表性基准方法，ZEBRIS提高了社会福利和合规鲁棒性，同时降低了服务延迟和隐私风险加权成本。

Conclusion: ZEBRIS框架成功解决了隐私敏感边缘服务中的信任与合规问题，通过押金-退款机制有效约束了事后道德风险，为零信任环境下的边缘服务交易提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-sensitive edge services necessitate optimizing diverse-type resource scheduling to support trustworthy provisioning within a zero-trust security framework. However, existing studies rarely model how runtime compliance jointly affects bilateral clearing, ex-post settlement, and future seller eligibility in dynamic edge markets. To address this issue, we propose ZEBRIS, a zero-trust bilateral edge service trading framework with deposit-refund regulation for privacy-sensitive services. Specifically, edge provisioning is modeled as a trading form of zero-trust-compliant service packages, where the buyer-side effective valuation captures service value, delay penalty, and privacy risk, while the seller-side effective ask incorporates resource and compliance costs. This yields a resource-aware positive-margin bilateral clearing mechanism under shared resource and security constraints. To discipline post-clearing moral hazard, we further design a capped deposit-refund settlement rule based on measurable runtime compliance and update each seller's future security posture according to realized compliance outcomes. ZEBRIS satisfies bilateral individual rationality and no-subsidy weak budget balance. Experiments demonstrate that ZEBRIS improves social welfare and compliance robustness while reducing service delay and privacy-risk-weighted cost over representative baselines.

</details>


### [30] [Rethinking Traffic Matrix Completion: Estimate the Process, Not the Entries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02225)
*Xiyuan Liu,Zihao Wang,Guanzuo Liu,Xiucheng Tian,Wenting Wei*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Utimac，一种面向数据中心网络的不确定性感知流量矩阵补全方法，通过将对数域流量分解为主统计成分和稀疏偏差成分，将补全问题建模为参数推断问题，在稀疏观测条件下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心网络中获取完整流量矩阵面临巨大挑战：全局细粒度测量的成本过高，而网络故障导致部分观测缺失。现有矩阵补全方法虽然在特定场景下表现尚可，但依赖于限制性假设或黑盒映射，缺乏可解释性且无法表征不确定性。

Method: 作者发现在局部平稳窗口内，对数域流量可分解为主统计成分和稀疏偏差成分。基于此，将流量矩阵补全形式化为参数推断问题：利用窗口内多个部分观测帧推断共享参数并恢复缺失条目。为解决原始积分形式边际似然的难处理性和边界退化问题，构建了正则化代理目标函数，并采用块坐标下降法求解联合优化问题。

Result: Utimac在数据中心网络数据集上持续优于所有基线方法，在整体场景和突发场景中均表现出色，且随着观测数据变得更稀疏，其优势更加明显。

Conclusion: Utimac成功实现了不确定性感知的流量矩阵补全，通过创新的参数推断框架和优化方法，在保持可解释性的同时显著提升了稀疏观测条件下的补全性能，为数据中心网络流量测量提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic matrix measurement is fundamental for datacenter operations, but obtaining complete traffic matrices at scale remains challenging due to the prohibitive cost of global fine-grained measurement and partial observations resulting from network faults. Although existing matrix completion methods (reduce cost) achieve satisfactory performance in specific scenarios, their reliance on restrictive assumptions or black-box mappings results in a lack of interpretability and an inability to characterize uncertainty. In this paper, we propose Utimac, an uncertainty-aware traffic matrix completion for data center networks. Our analysis shows that, within a locally stationary window, log-domain traffic can be decomposed into a principal statistical component and a sparse deviation component. Based on this insight, we formulate traffic matrix completion as a parameter inference problem: multiple partially observed frames within a window are used to infer shared parameters and recover missing entries. To avoid the intractability and boundary degeneracy of the original integral-form marginal likelihood, we construct a regularized surrogate objective and solve the resulting joint optimization problem with block coordinate descent. Utimac consistently outperforms all baselines on data center networks datasets in both overall and burst scenarios, with its advantage becoming more pronounced as observations grow sparser. All code is publicly available in an anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Utimac-0551/

</details>


### [31] [Spatial-Temporal Learning-Based Distributed Routing for Dynamic LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02413)
*Po-Heng Chou,Chiapin Wang,Shou-Yu Chen,Hsiang-Ming Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向动态低地球轨道(LEO)卫星网络的时空学习分布式路由框架。该框架融合图注意力网络(GAT)与长短期记忆网络(LSTM)，在深度Q网络(DQN)架构下实现基于局部观测的自适应路由决策。实验表明该方法在吞吐量、丢包率、队列长度和端到端时延上显著优于现有方案，实现了主动拥塞避免，且计算开销低，符合绿色AI理念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络具有动态拓扑和时变流量的特性，传统路由方案难以适应，且卫星节点只能基于局部观测进行决策，面临部分可观测性挑战。因此需要一种能够处理动态变化、支持分布式决策且高效的路由机制。

Method: 将路由问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)；提出基于深度Q网络(DQN)的架构，集成图注意力网络(GAT)以捕获空间特征，集成长短期记忆网络(LSTM)以捕获时间特征，使卫星能够仅基于局部观测做出分布式、自适应的路由决策。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在吞吐量、丢包率、队列长度和端到端时延方面均显著优于传统路由和现有学习型路由方案；实现了主动拥塞避免，队列长度最高减少23.26%；同时保持较低的计算开销和可忽略的碳排放，体现了绿色AI的高效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的时空学习分布式路由框架有效解决了LEO卫星网络中的动态拓扑和部分可观测性问题，在显著提升网络性能的同时保持了计算效率与环境友好性，为未来卫星网络路由设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a spatial-temporal learning-based distributed routing framework for dynamic Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks, where graph attention networks (GAT) and long short-term memory (LSTM) are integrated within a deep Q-network (DQN)-based architecture to enable distributed and adaptive routing decisions based on local observations. The routing problem is formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) to address partial observability under dynamic topology and time-varying traffic. Simulation results show that the proposed method significantly outperforms conventional and learning-based routing schemes in terms of throughput, packet loss, queue length, and end-to-end delay, while achieving proactive congestion avoidance with up to 23.26% queue reduction. In addition, the proposed approach maintains low computational overhead with negligible carbon emissions, demonstrating its efficiency from a Green AI perspective.

</details>


### [32] [Early-Stage IoT Device Identification Using Passive Network Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02449)
*Alex Ciechonski,Fabio Palmese,Alessandro E. C. Redondi,Anna Maria Mandalari*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的物联网设备识别方法，仅基于流量元数据，在设备接入网络的最初几秒内即可实现高达99%的识别准确率，证明了早期被动识别的可行性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网设备的快速普及，由于可见性有限和设备级保障薄弱，带来了显著的安全挑战。现有识别方法通常依赖长期流量观察、载荷检测或依赖基础设施的特征，难以满足准确、及时识别设备的需求，因此急需一种快速且保护隐私的早期识别方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于流级特征的轻量级被动流量分析方法。该方法仅从流量元数据中提取特征，避免了载荷检测和主动探测，并在多个观察窗口下进行了系统性评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，设备特定的签名在通信的最初几秒内即出现，对37种物联网设备的识别准确率高达99%。有趣的是，延长观察窗口并不总能提升性能，甚至可能略微降低准确率，说明最具区分性的行为发生在设备启动初期。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在网络边缘进行快速、隐私保护的物联网设备识别是可行的，该方法支持实时策略执行、设备清查和异常检测，具有重要的实际部署价值。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) devices introduces significant security challenges due to limited visibility and weak device-level guarantees. Accurate and timely identification of devices is essential for enforcing network policies and detecting unauthorised hardware, yet existing approaches often rely on long-term traffic observation, payload inspection, or infrastructure-dependent features. In this paper, we investigate whether IoT devices can be reliably identified during the early stages of network attachment using only passive traffic analysis. We propose a lightweight approach based on flow-level features extracted from metadata, avoiding payload inspection and active probing. Through systematic evaluation across multiple observation windows, we show that device-specific signatures emerge within the first few seconds of communication, enabling high-accuracy identification (up to 99%) across 37 IoT devices. Notably, extending the observation window does not consistently improve performance and may slightly degrade accuracy, indicating that the most discriminative behaviour occurs during initial device startup. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of fast, privacy-preserving IoT device identification at the network edge, supporting real-time enforcement, device inventory, and anomaly detection in practical deployments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云虚拟现实和8K直播等实时宽带通信（RTBC）场景对网络性能提出了极高要求（视频码率>30 Mbps、尾延迟<50 ms、多用户公平性）。经测试分析，现有端到端和网络辅助的速率控制算法均无法同时满足这些性能指标，其核心原因在于缺乏对5G无线接入网（RAN）原生动态延迟和物理层资源分配策略的适配，导致决策性能下降。

Method: 提出Choir方案，一种主要部署于5G基站的协作式解决方案。该方案深度整合5G无线特性与视频流流量模式，利用基站侧的信息指导发送端进行高效的速率控制，从而实现跨层协同优化。

Result: 通过大量仿真和实验床评估，结果表明Choir在不同5G网络场景下均能实现高平均码率、低尾延迟以及良好的流间公平性，显著优于现有方案。

Conclusion: Choir成功验证了将5G无线特性与视频流量模式深度整合的有效性，证明了基站侧协作对于满足RTBC场景严苛性能要求的重要性，为未来5G媒体传输优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Real-time broadband communication (RTBC) scenarios, such as cloud virtual reality and 8K live streaming, further raise the criteria of the performance triangle, requiring video bitrates exceeding 30 Mbps, tail delay below 50 ms, and fairness guarantees for multi-user concurrent access. Based on our testing and analysis, existing RTBC-oriented rate control solutions, including end-to-end algorithms and network-assisted algorithms, fail to simultaneously satisfy all performance metrics. The native dynamic delay and physical-layer resource allocation strategy inherent to the 5G radio access network (RAN) are the key reasons. These solutions lack adaptation to the 5G architecture, leading to reduced decision performance. This paper proposes Choir, an innovative collaborative solution mainly deployed on 5G base stations that deeply integrates 5G radio characteristics and video streaming traffic patterns to guide efficient sender-side rate control. Extensive simulation and testbed evaluations demonstrate Choir's significant performance in achieving high average bitrate, low tail delay, and inter-flow fairness across different 5G network scenarios.

</details>
