*James Lynch,Ziqian Liu,Snehadeep Gayen,Om Chabra,Hari Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IteRate，一个基于多智能体AI的自主研究系统，用于自动化设计Wi-Fi速率控制算法。相比传统Minstrel算法，在58节点测试平台上实现了网页加载速度提升21%、视频QoE提高7%、峰值吞吐量增加21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Wi-Fi速率自适应算法（如Minstrel-HT）十余年来停滞不前，依赖手工调优的启发式方法，无法适应现代无线环境的复杂性，缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 1) 开发新型内核模块，向eBPF程序暴露每帧硬件遥测数据（MCS、重试计数等）；2) 构建结构化多智能体AI架构，包含算法设计、实验执行和数据分析专用智能体，通过假设驱动的研究协议协调工作；3) 建立闭环流水线，自动化完成内核逻辑的交叉编译、部署和评估。系统在全科学周期内自主完成假设提出、eBPF程序编写、无线部署、遥测收集和迭代优化。

Result: 在58节点测试平台和五种工作负载上，相比Minstrel算法：网页加载速度提升21%、视频体验质量(QoE)提高7%、峰值吞吐量增加21%。验证了配备适当内核级钩子和规范科学工作流程的AI智能体能够有效自动化Wi-Fi速率控制器的研究设计。

Conclusion: 证明了AI智能体在配备适当的内核级钩子和规范的科学研究工作流程后，能够有效自动化Wi-Fi速率控制器的研究设计过程，为无线网络算法的自动化开发开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Wi-Fi rate adaptation remains a persistent challenge in wireless networking. Deployed algorithms like Minstrel-HT have remained largely stagnant for over a decade, relying on hand-tuned heuristics that fail to generalize to the complexity of modern wireless environments. We present \name, an autonomous research system that closes the loop on rate control development. IteRate uses a multi-agent AI architecture to conduct the full scientific cycle: formulating hypotheses, writing eBPF programs that run inside the Linux kernel, deploying them over-the-air to Wi-Fi devices, collecting fine-grained telemetry for analysis, and iterating based on experimental evidence, all without human intervention. IteRate makes three contributions. (1) a novel kernel module that exposes per-frame hardware telemetry including modulation and coding schemes (MCS) and retry counts to eBPF programs, (2) a structured agentic AI architecture employing specialized agents for algorithm design, experiment execution, and data analysis, coordinated via a hypothesis-driven research protocol with persistent knowledge, and (3) a closed-loop pipeline that automates the cross-compilation, deployment, and evaluation of in-kernel logic onto embedded Wi-Fi targets.
  On a 58-node testbed running five workloads. relative to the well-known Minstrel algorithm, IteRate achieves 21% faster web-page loads, 7% higher video quality of experience (QoE), and 21% higher peak throughput. Our work demonstrates that AI agents, when equipped with appropriate kernel-level hooks and a disciplined scientific workflow, can effectively automate the research required to design Wi-Fi rate controllers.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond State Machines: Executing Network Procedures with Agentic Tool-Calling Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02584)
*Purna Sai Garigipati,Onur Ayan,Kishor Chandra Joshi,Xueli An*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大语言模型(LLM)的网络智能代理如何执行以工具调用序列表示的网络流程。研究发现，依赖迭代代理端推理的方法延迟更高且易出错，而将流程封装在单一工具内的方法通过限制重复推理可降低延迟。压力测试表明，随着流程步骤增加，所有模型的可靠性都会下降，揭示了多步工具工作流的执行限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI将成为未来移动通信系统设计的关键赋能技术，能够提供灵活定制服务、自动化复杂网络操作并驱动网络自主决策。然而，如何利用基于LLM的网络AI代理有效执行以工具调用序列表达的网络流程，尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 提出四种不同的执行方法，区别在于代理获取流程的方式以及在代理和底层工具之间的执行分配方式。以用户设备(UE)IP分配流程为案例研究，评估各方法的延迟和执行正确性。同时进行压力测试，检验LLM代理在失败前能可靠执行的最大顺序流程步骤数。引入流程特定错误分类法，系统分析多步流程执行中的失败。

Result: 依赖迭代代理端推理的方法延迟更高且更易出现执行错误；将流程封装在单一工具内（内部通过调用其他工具协调所需步骤）的方法通过限制重复推理降低了延迟。具有先进工具调用能力的模型在更长流程中保持可靠执行，但随着流程长度增加，所有模型均表现出可靠性退化，揭示了多步工具工作流的明显执行限制。

Conclusion: 多步工具工作流存在明确的执行限制，随着流程步骤增加模型可靠性必然下降。通过合理的执行方式设计（如单一工具封装）可改善性能。提出的流程特定错误分类法为系统分析多步流程执行失败提供了有效框架，为未来移动通信系统中Agentic AI的实际部署提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Agentic AI will be an essential enabling technology for designing future mobile communication systems, which could provide flexible and customized services, automate complex network operations, and drive autonomous decision-making across the network. This work studies how Large Language Model (LLM)-based network AI agents can be utilized to execute network procedures expressed as sequences of tool invocations. We investigate four approaches, which differ in how the agent obtains the procedure and in how execution is distributed between the agent and the underlying tools. We evaluated the latency and execution correctness across these approaches using a User Equipment (UE) IP allocation procedure as a case study. Furthermore, we conduct a stress test to examine how many sequential procedural steps an LLM agent can reliably execute before failure. Our results show that approaches relying on iterative agent-side reasoning incur higher latency and are more prone to execution errors, while approaches where the procedure is encapsulated within a single tool, which internally orchestrates the required steps by invoking other tools, reduce latency by limiting repeated reasoning. The stress-test results further show that the model with advanced tool-calling capability maintains reliable execution over longer procedures than the other evaluated models; however, all models exhibit reliability degradation as procedure length increases, revealing clear execution limits in multi-step tool-based workflows. To systematically analyze failures in procedure execution, we introduce a procedure-specific error taxonomy that categorizes deviations in multi-step procedural execution.

</details>


### [36] [Tool Use as Action: Towards Agentic Control in Mobile Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02811)
*Purna Sai Garigipati,Onur Ayan,Kishor Chandra Joshi,Xueli An*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于代理式AI（Agentic AI）的移动核心网络工具化接口设计与实验原型，利用模型上下文协议（MCP）和Agent2Agent（A2A）协议作为基础，展示了AI代理结合网络工具如何在6G中执行意图式任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能将在6G中发挥关键作用，从根本上重塑网络架构并驱动网络实体、接口和流程的重大变革。通过在下一代网络中采用具有规划、推理和行动能力的代理式AI，可以增强网络智能性和自主性，同时开拓新的商业机会。现有网络功能需要演化为AI代理和工具，以提供连接服务及超越连接的服务。

Method: 提出了一个基于代理式AI的移动核心网络工具化接口设计与实验原型。采用模型上下文协议（MCP）设计代理与网络工具之间的接口，使用Agent2Agent（A2A）协议进行AI代理间的消息交换。在实验环境中，分析了代理、工具和网络功能之间的分组级消息流，并分解了从提示注入到任务完成的端到端操作时延。

Result: 实验原型成功展示了基于AI代理的核心网络结合特定网络工具的运行机制。通过分组级消息流分析和端到端时延分解，验证了该架构能够有效执行意图式任务，为下一代移动系统中AI代理的应用提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 本研究作为迈向6G智能网络架构的初步尝试，证明了将AI代理与网络工具相结合的可行性。工具化接口设计结合MCP和A2A协议为未来移动核心网络的智能化转型提供了新的技术路径，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) will play an essential role in 6G. It will fundamentally reshape the network architecture itself and drive major changes in the design of network entities, interfaces, and procedures. The adoption of agentic AI in next-generation networks is expected to enhance network intelligence and autonomy through agents capable of planning, reasoning, and acting, while also opening up new business opportunities. Under this vision, existing network functions are expected to evolve into AI-enabled agents and tools that deliver both connectivity and beyond-connectivity services. As an initial attempt to move toward this vision, this paper presents a tool-based interface design and an experimental prototype that are based on agentic AI for the mobile core network, with the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and the Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol as foundational protocols. MCP is selected to design the interface between the agent and network tools, and the A2A protocol is used for message exchange between AI agents. In such an experimental setup, we analyze packet-level message flows between the agents, tools, and network functions and break down the latency of end-to-end operations, starting from the prompt injection until the completion of the input task. This work demonstrates how an AI agent-based core network combined with network-specific tools can be utilized in next generation mobile systems to execute intent-based tasks.

</details>

### [37] [Separating Intelligence from Execution: A Workflow Engine for the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00827)
*Abhinav Singh Parmar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出MCP工作流引擎，通过将智能决策与执行解耦，将Agent推理固化为一劳永逸的声明式工作流蓝图，将大规模Kubernetes同步任务的每次执行Token消耗降低超过99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理架构中，Agent必须在每次会话中对每个工具调用进行推理，导致Token消耗与操作次数成正比，即使任务此前已被解决，仍需重复推理，造成巨大成本浪费。

Method: 提出MCP工作流引擎这一MCP原生编排层，引入MCP Mediator架构模式（同时作为下游MCP服务器的客户端的MCP服务器）。Agent仅需推理一次，生成包含参数化模板、循环、并行分支和数据管道的有向MCP工具调用序列的声明式工作流蓝图（JSON文档），后续执行通过单一run_workflow工具调用触发。使用TypeScript基于MCP SDK实现。

Result: 在生产级Kubernetes CMDB同步任务（67个编排步骤、2个MCP服务器、38个命名空间、13个工作节点、22种资源类型）上验证，每次执行Token成本降低超过99%，在45秒内完成包含1200+节点、2800+关系、20种关系类型的完整集群图谱构建，运行时零Agent介入，实现确定性、幂等性执行。

Conclusion: MCP工作流引擎成功实现了智能决策与执行的解耦，通过一次推理生成可复用的声明式工作流蓝图，显著降低了重复任务的Token成本，实现了无需Agent介入的确定性执行，为LLM代理与外部系统的交互提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly interact with external systems through tool-calling protocols such as the Model Context Protocol (MCP). In prevailing architectures, the agent must reason about every tool invocation in every session, consuming tokens proportional to the number of actions performed--even when the task has been solved before. We present the MCP Workflow Engine, a novel MCP-native orchestration layer that decouples intelligence (deciding what to do) from execution (carrying it out). An agent reasons once to produce a declarative workflow blueprint--a JSON document specifying a directed sequence of MCP tool calls with parameterized templates, loops, parallel branches, and data piping. Subsequent executions are triggered by a single run_workflow tool call, consuming one invocation's worth of tokens regardless of the blueprint's internal complexity. We formalize the MCP Mediator architectural pattern--an MCP server that simultaneously acts as a client to downstream MCP servers--and implement it in TypeScript against the MCP SDK. We evaluate the engine on a production-scale Kubernetes CMDB synchronization task spanning 67 orchestrated steps across 2 MCP servers, 38 namespaces, 13 worker nodes, and 22 distinct resource types. The engine reduces per-execution token cost by over 99%, completes the full cluster graph--comprising 1,200+ nodes and 2,800+ relationships across 20 relationship types--in under 45 seconds, and achieves deterministic, idempotent execution with zero agent involvement at run time.

</details>


### [38] [Canonical LST: A Protocol-Native Liquid Staking Solution for Tezos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00828)
*Mathias Bourgoin,Arthur Breitman,Pierrick Couderc,Zaynah Dargaye,Diane Gallois-Wong,Marina Polubelova,Lucas Randazzo,Julien Tesson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Tezos协议原生的Canonical LST (sTEZ)机制，旨在通过提供中立的、由协议直接管理的流动性质押替代方案来缓解流动性质押中介带来的中心化风险，采用累积式设计使代币汇率反映所有奖励和惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解流动性质押中介带来的中心化风险，为tez持有者提供无需依赖第三方运营商即可参与聚合质押的中立公共替代方案，使流动性质押作为基础网络基础设施而非自由裁量的商业产品。

Method: 采用协议原生机制（enshrined mechanism），通过累积式设计（accrual-based design）实现：所有削减事件和奖励均反映在sTEZ与tez的汇率中，保持代币余额的可替代性，同时使持有者暴露于质押的精确经济机制中。该设计包含确定性的生命周期规则、透明的链上数据，以及锚定于修正流程的治理机制。

Result: 构建了一个由Tezos协议直接管理的流动性质押解决方案，具有确定性的生命周期规则、透明的链上数据以及锚定于协议修正流程的治理机制，确保流动性质押作为基础网络基础设施运行。

Conclusion: Canonical LST作为补充而非替代直接质押的方案，成功将流动性质押转变为具有透明度、可预测性和去中心化特征的基础设施，白皮书同时总结了该机制的监管要点、风险态势及未来发展路线图。

Abstract: Canonical LST (sTEZ) is an enshrined, protocol-native mechanism designed to mitigate the centralization risks associated with liquid staking intermediaries. Intended to complement direct staking rather than replace it, Canonical LST provides a neutral, public alternative managed directly by the Tezos protocol. It allows any tez holder to participate in aggregated staking without reliance on third-party operators. sTEZ follows an accrual-based design: all slashing events and rewards are reflected in the token's exchange rate to tez, keeping balances fungible while exposing holders to the precise economics of staking. This approach ensures that liquid staking functions as fundamental network infrastructure--with deterministic lifecycle rules, transparent on-chain data, and governance anchored in the amendment process--rather than as a discretionary commercial product. This white paper summarises the motivation for enshrining liquid staking, the core mechanics, exchange-rate model, regulatory touchpoints, risk posture, and forward-looking roadmap for Canonical LST.

</details>


### [39] [CvxCluster: Solving Large, Complex, Granular Resource Allocation Problems 100-1000x Faster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01614)
*Obi Nnorom,Stephen Boyd,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CvxCluster，一种基于凸优化的集群资源分配方法。通过将离散放置问题转化为连续凸优化问题，在保证解质量（与最优解偏差在3%以内）的前提下，实现了比传统MIP求解器快100至2500倍的求解速度，并能扩展至10万台服务器规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群资源分配是一个多维搜索问题，搜索空间随规模呈指数增长。现有方法主要采用混合整数规划（MIP）或复杂的搜索启发式算法，计算开销大、扩展性受限。凸优化方法拥有极快的求解算法，因此本文旨在探索如何将集群资源分配转化为凸优化问题，以实现高效且高质量的放置决策。

Method: CvxCluster采用两阶段算法：第一阶段对放置问题进行凸松弛求解，得到一组基于原则的机器资源价格；第二阶段利用这些价格驱动一个轻量级贪心过程完成任务放置。核心创新在于将放置问题从离散形式重构为连续问题，从而利用凸优化的高效求解方法。

Result: 基于Azure真实负载轨迹的实验表明：CvxCluster可扩展至100,480台服务器，支持高达基准轨迹50万倍的到达率；运行速度比最先进MIP求解器快100至2500倍，同时目标值与最优解偏差在3%以内；并能支持作业反亲和性、机器类型、GPU服务器等复杂约束。

Conclusion: 将集群资源分配从离散问题重构为连续凸优化问题，可以显著提升求解速度，同时获得与现有启发式方法相当或更优的解质量，为大规模集群资源分配提供了一种高效可行的新途径。

Abstract: Cluster resource allocation is a multidimensional search problem that finds the best allocation of tasks to servers. Because the search space grows exponentially, modern approaches frame it as a mixed integer program (MIP) or a complex set of search heuristics. This paper proposes using a different approach: convex optimization, which has extremely fast solution methods. The research challenge is devising how to transform cluster resource allocation into a convex problem that generates good placements. We describe CvxCluster, which allocates cluster resources with a two-stage algorithm. The first stage solves a convex relaxation of the placement problem to yield a principled set of per-machine resource prices. The second stage uses these prices to drive a lightweight greedy procedure to place tasks. Experimental results with Azure traces find that CvxCluster scales to 100,480 servers under proportional workload growth and sustains arrival rates up to 500,000x the baseline trace. CvxCluster runs 100 to 2,500x faster than a state-of-the-art MIP solver while remaining within 3% of the optimal objective. CvxCluster can support complex constraints such as job anti-affinity, machine types, and GPU servers. The key insight behind CvxCluster is that reformulating placement as a continuous rather than discrete problem enables much faster methods that find solutions just as good or better than prior heuristics.

</details>


### [40] [Efficient Accelerated Graph Edit Distance Computation on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00830)
*Adel Dabah,Andreas Herten*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了FAST-GED，一个基于GPU的快速可扩展图编辑距离计算框架，相比现有方法实现了数量级的加速，同时保持了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图编辑距离（GED）是生物信息学、机器学习和模式识别等领域的关键度量，但现有最优或近似方法计算复杂度高，难以应用于大规模图，因此迫切需要高性能的并行GED计算方案。

Method: 提出了FAST-GED框架，通过GPU友好的算法设计和到GPU硬件的高效映射，最小化主机与设备间的通信，并在多种GPU架构上进行了优化实现。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上验证，FAST-GED相比Python NetworkX库实现了数量级的加速，且在大多数情况下能找到最优解；在精度和可扩展性上均优于现有的近似方法。

Conclusion: FAST-GED通过结合高精度与快速执行，显著提升了GED计算的效率与可扩展性，有助于推动GED在实际应用中的广泛采用。

Abstract: Graph representation is a powerful abstraction of real-world objects and relations. Computing the Graph Edit Distance (GED) between graphs is critical in domains such as bioinformatics, machine learning, and pattern recognition. GED measures the minimum number of edit operations required to transform one graph into another. However, the high computational complexity of optimal and near-optimal methods limits their applicability to large-scale graphs, making high-performance parallel GED computation essential. To address this, we propose FAST-GED, a fast and scalable open-source framework for GED computation on GPUs. FAST-GED overcomes existing limitations by combining high accuracy with fast execution through GPU-friendly algorithmic design and efficient mapping to GPU hardware, minimizing host-device communication. The implementation is optimized and tested across multiple GPU architectures. We validate FAST-GED on real and synthetic datasets with diverse graph sizes and densities. It achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude over the Python NetworkX library while reaching optimal solutions in most cases. Moreover, it outperforms state-of-the-art approximate methods in both accuracy and scalability. We show that FAST-GED enables broader adoption of GED-based solutions in real-world applications.

</details>


### [41] [GhostServe: A Lightweight Checkpointing System in the Shadow for Fault-Tolerant LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00831)
*Shakya Jayakody,Youpeng Zhao,Chinmay Dhanraj Nehate,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了GhostServe，一种面向大规模语言模型（LLM）服务的容错检查点方案，通过在主机内存中利用纠删码技术保护流式KV缓存，实现了高效的故障恢复，显著降低了检查点和恢复延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着百万级Token、基于代理的应用兴起，LLM推理服务面临前所未有的压力。这类任务运行时间长，极易遭受软硬件故障，导致昂贵的作业失败、资源浪费和用户体验下降。核心挑战在于，随着序列长度增长，有状态的键值（KV）缓存成为分布式服务系统中关键且脆弱的组件。

Method: 提出GhostServe，一种新颖的检查点解决方案。其核心机制是在“影子”中保护流式KV缓存，通过应用纠删码生成校验分片并将其存储在主机内存中。当发生设备故障时，GhostServe能够快速重构丢失的KV缓存，使推理过程无缝恢复，避免了昂贵的全量重计算或状态复制。

Result: 评估结果表明，与现有方法相比，GhostServe在系统故障发生时，将单批次检查点延迟降低了最高2.7倍，恢复延迟降低了2.1倍，并将中位响应延迟降低了1.2倍。

Conclusion: GhostServe为实现大规模、高可用且具有成本效益的LLM服务铺平了道路，有效解决了长上下文场景下的容错难题。

Abstract: The rise of million-token, agent-based applications has placed unprecedented demands on large language model (LLM) inference services. The long-running nature of these tasks increases their susceptibility to hardware and software faults, leading to costly job failures, wasted resources, and degraded user experience. The stateful key-value (KV) cache, which grows with the sequence length, presents a central challenge as it is a critical and vulnerable component in distributed serving systems. In this work, we propose GhostServe, a novel checkpointing solution to facilitate fault-tolerant LLM serving. Specifically, GhostServe protects the streaming KV cache in the shadow by applying erasure coding to generate and store the parity shards in host memory. In the event of device failures, GhostServe enables fast reconstruction of the lost KV cache, allowing the inference process to resume seamlessly without costly full recomputation or state replication. Evaluations demonstrate that GhostServe reduces checkpointing latency by up to 2.7x and recovery latency by 2.1x for a single batch, and 1.2x median response latency compared to existing methods, in the presence of system failures, paving the way for high-availability and cost-effective LLM serving at scale.

</details>


### [42] [SCION: Size-aware Policy Orchestration for Nonstationary Object Caches (Long Paper Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01055)
*Qizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了SCION，一个轻量级的缓存策略编排框架，通过离线训练的线性选择器在六种主流缓存策略间进行动态选择，在多数工作负载上优于强基准SIEVE，且保持了关键路径零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象缓存支撑着云和边缘服务，但生产环境工作负载具有异构性、非平稳性和吞吐量受限的特点。近期SIEVE和S3-FIFO等非机器学习策略设立了强基准，因此任何基于学习的方法都必须具备开销感知能力、在概念漂移下保持鲁棒性，并能与强专家策略竞争。

Method: SCION是一个轻量级策略编排框架，在少量可部署的缓存策略中进行选择，使用在关键路径外计算的小型工作负载指纹（包含对象大小、可缓存性、重用率和缓存大小的短前缀统计信息），应用离线训练的线性选择器在GDSF、S3-FIFO、SIEVE、LHD、W-TinyLFU-AV和DynamicAdaptiveClimb六种策略间选择；另有简化变体SCION-P90仅使用p90阈值。

Result: 在30个公开对象缓存trace和HR-Cache模拟器子集的CPU-only trace驱动评估中，AUTO在大多数工作负载上优于SIEVE的对象级缺失率，平均表现接近最佳单一专家策略，支持显式的OMR/BMR权衡选择，在字节缺失率上保持竞争力；在快速策略预算下，AUTO-fast比最佳固定快速策略成本更低。

Conclusion: SCION在保持热路径不变的前提下降低了策略失配风险，证明了轻量级策略编排在异构、非平稳工作负载下的有效性，为实际部署提供了一种低开销、高性能的缓存策略选择方案。

Abstract: Object caches underpin cloud and edge services, but production workloads are heterogeneous, nonstationary, and throughput-constrained. Recent simple non-ML policies such as SIEVE and S3-FIFO set a strong baseline, so any learned method must be overhead-aware, robust under drift, and competitive with strong experts. We present SCION, a lightweight policy-orchestration framework that selects among a small set of deployable cache policies using a tiny workload fingerprint computed off the critical path. Our prototype, AUTO, uses short-prefix statistics of object size, cacheability, reuse, and cache size, then applies an offline-trained linear selector to choose among GDSF, S3-FIFO, SIEVE, LHD, W-TinyLFU-AV, and DynamicAdaptiveClimb; a simpler SCION-P90 variant uses only a p90 threshold. In a CPU-only, trace-driven evaluation on 30 public object-cache traces and a separate HR-Cache simulator subset, AUTO improves cacheable-only object miss ratio over SIEVE on a majority of workloads, stays close to the best single expert on average, enables explicit OMR/BMR tradeoff selection, and remains competitive on byte miss ratio. Under a fast-policy budget, AUTO-fast achieves lower cost than the best fixed fast policy. SCION reduces regime-mismatch risk while keeping the hot path unchanged.

</details>


### [43] [SURGE: SuperBatch Unified Resource-efficient GPU Encoding for Heterogeneous Partitioned Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01060)
*Shashank Kapadia,Deep Narayan Mishra,Sujal Reddy Alugubelli,Ajay Kumar,Swapnil Yadav,Rishi Bhatia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SURGE是一种流式GPU编码系统，在保持与固定批处理相同吞吐量的前提下，将峰值内存占用降低12.6倍（从32.7GB降至2.6GB），并将首输出时间加快68倍，支持崩溃恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的嵌入向量生成流水线面临逻辑数据分区与GPU高效利用之间的矛盾：独立处理每个分区会产生P次进程间通信（IPC）开销，限制了轻量计算模型的吞吐量；而传统的固定大小批处理方法需要O(N)峰值内存且缺乏容错能力。

Method: 提出SURGE流式GPU编码系统，核心包含：(1)准确度在2%以内的吞吐量代价模型（定理1）；(2)内存安全边界（引理3），支持峰值内存为O(B_min + n_max)而非O(N)的流式双阈值策略；(3)φ/CV决策框架判断适用场景。辅以零拷贝Arrow序列化（22-25倍加速）和异步I/O流水线优化。

Result: 在4块NVIDIA L4 GPU上处理1000万文本时，SURGE达到26,413 texts/s吞吐量（与固定批处理相当），内存仅需2.6GB（对比固定批处理的32.7GB），首输出时间快68倍，支持SuperBatch粒度崩溃恢复。代价模型在bge-base编码器上预测误差仅1.3%，在不同对数正态分布下加速比变化在±3%以内。相比分区批处理基线（PB-PBP-LB），保留7%吞吐量优势和2.5倍更快的首输出时间。

Conclusion: SURGE成功解决了大规模嵌入向量生成中的内存-吞吐量权衡问题，以有界的O(B_min + n_max)内存实现与O(N)内存固定批处理相当的吞吐量，同时提供显著更快的时间到首输出和细粒度容错能力，已在生产环境中部署并处理超过8亿文本。

Abstract: We present SURGE, a streaming GPU encoding system deployed in production to generate embeddings for over 800 million texts across 40,000 logical partitions. Production embedding pipelines face a tension between logical data partitioning and efficient GPU utilization: processing each partition independently incurs $P$ inter-process communication (IPC) calls whose overhead limits throughput for compute-light models. Our contributions are analytical: (i) a cost model (Theorem 1) predicting throughput within 2% across three encoders spanning a 15$\times$ parameter range; (ii) a memory-safety bound (Lemma 3) enabling a streaming two-threshold policy with peak memory $O(B_{\min} + n_{\max})$ rather than $O(N)$; and (iii) a $φ$/CV decision framework characterizing when the pattern applies beyond our workload. The naive fix of batching at fixed size requires $O(N)$ peak memory (32.7 GB at 10M texts; infeasible beyond ~60M on 192 GB nodes), produces no output until all encoding completes, and offers no fault tolerance. SURGE achieves the same throughput with $O(B_{\min} + n_{\max})$ bounded memory (2.6 GB), 68$\times$ faster time-to-first-output, and crash recovery at SuperBatch granularity. On 10M texts with 4 NVIDIA L4 GPUs, SURGE delivers 26,413 texts/s -- matching fixed-batch throughput while using 12.6$\times$ less memory. We validate on bge-base (109M, $d$=768, error 1.3%) and across log-normal $σ$ in {1.0, 1.72, 2.5} (speedup invariant within $\pm$3%), and compare against a partition-batched baseline (PB-PBP-LB), against which SURGE retains a 7% throughput edge and 2.5$\times$ faster TTFO. Complementary engineering -- zero-copy Arrow serialization (22-25$\times$ speedup) and async I/O pipelining (up to 93% benefit) -- realizes the design but is not the contribution.

</details>


### [44] [FPTC: A Fast Parallel Transform-based Codec for Efficient Asymmetric Signal Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01086)
*Ben Mechels,Ryan Billmeyer,Alexander Chen,Shiyang Li,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FPTC的高吞吐率非对称信号编解码器，采用轻量级编码器与大规模并行GPU解码器设计，在多个信号域上实现了优异的压缩比与解压吞吐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高性能计算和物联网部署产生大量信号数据，需要在资源受限的采集设备上高效压缩，并在中心服务器上大规模解压。现有有损压缩方法难以同时优化重建质量与解压吞吐率，且缺乏跨信号域的泛化能力。

Method: FPTC采用窗口化离散余弦变换（DCT）利用频域稀疏性，通过混合三区域映射量化频谱系数，并使用新颖打包方案的霍夫曼编码进行熵编码。其设计为面向GPU吞吐率的流水线，包含轻量级顺序编码器与大规模并行GPU解码器。

Result: 在涵盖生物医学诊断、地震反射、电网生产指标和气象记录四个信号域的十个数据集上评估，FPTC在压缩比上超越现有框架，乘法压缩性能分别为：电力3.6倍、气象3.1倍、生物医学1.5倍、地震1.2倍，同时保持具有竞争力的吞吐率。

Conclusion: FPTC成功实现了一种非对称编解码架构，在保证重建质量的同时优化了GPU上的解压吞吐性能，并展示了跨多个信号域的良好泛化能力，为资源受限设备上的信号压缩提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern high-performance computing and Internet-of-Things deployments increasingly generate large volumes of signal data that must be compressed efficiently on resource-constrained acquisition devices and decompressed at scale on centralized servers. Lossy compression is widely adopted to minimize storage and transmission costs on low-power hardware sensors, yet existing methods rarely optimize for both reconstruction quality and decompression throughput simultaneously, nor do they apply methods that generalize across signal domains. In this work, we introduce FPTC, a high-throughput asymmetric signal codec that pairs a lightweight sequential encoder with a massively parallel GPU decoder designed for server-side batch decompression. FPTC applies a windowed discrete cosine transform (DCT) to exploit frequency-domain sparsity, quantizes spectral coefficients with a hybrid three-zone mapping, and entropy codes the result using Huffman coding with a novel packing scheme. The pipeline used in FPTC is designed to be throughput oriented on the GPU, maximizing performance without sacrificing reconstruction quality. We evaluate FPTC on ten datasets spanning four signal domains: biomedical diagnostic, seismic reflections, power-grid production metrics, and meteorological recordings. Our results demonstrate that FPTC outperforms existing frameworks in compression ratio while maintaining competitive throughput, achieving multiplicative compression performance of 3.6x (power), 3.1x (meteorological), 1.5x (biomedical), and 1.2x (seismic) over existing frameworks.

</details>


### [45] [ncsim: A Lightweight Simulator for Networked Edge Computing with Wireless Interference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01094)
*Bhaskar Krishnamachari,Maya Gutierrez,Jared Coleman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ncsim仿真器，首次将DAG工作流调度与IEEE 802.11 CSMA/CA干扰建模结合。实验发现27.8%-50%的场景存在排序反转，干扰无关模型下的最优调度器可能导致makespan恶化2.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线边缘计算DAG任务调度评估工具将计算放置与无线干扰割裂处理，导致排序反转问题：在无干扰模型下看似最优的调度器，在真实无线条件下可能是最差选择。

Method: 设计并实现ncsim——一个轻量级离散事件仿真器，在单一Python包中集成DAG工作流调度与基于物理原理的IEEE 802.11 CSMA/CA干扰建模。通过108次析因实验和100节点随机几何图扩展实验进行验证。

Result: 108次析因实验中27.8%的场景出现排序反转，无干扰最优调度器的makespan比简单轮询基线差2.7倍；扩展至100节点随机几何图时，反转率升至50%。

Conclusion: 排序反转现象表明忽略干扰的评估方法可能选择完全错误的算法，证明了ncsim设计与使用的必要性，为无线边缘计算调度器评估提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Evaluating DAG task schedulers for wireless edge computing requires jointly modeling compute placement and wireless interference, yet existing tools treat them in isolation. This gap leads to rank inversions: the scheduler that appears optimal under an interference-free model can be the worst choice under realistic wireless conditions. We present ncsim, a lightweight discrete-event simulator that bridges this gap by combining DAG workflow scheduling with physically-grounded IEEE 802.11 CSMA/CA interference modeling in a single Python package. A 108-run factorial experiment reveals rank inversions in 27.8% of scenarios, with the interference-free-optimal scheduler producing up to 2.7x worse makespan than a simple round-robin baseline; scaling to a 100-node random geometric graph raises the inversion rate to 50%. These rank inversions show that interference-free evaluation can select the wrong algorithm entirely, justifying the design and use of ncsim.

</details>


### [46] [Position: LLM Serving Needs Mathematical Optimization and Algorithmic Foundations, Not Just Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01280)
*Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文主张LLM推理服务需要从通用启发式方法转向数学优化和算法基础，以应对LLM推理的独特特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统（如vLLM和SGLang）仍沿用传统分布式计算的通用策略，忽视了LLM推理的独特结构特征，这些启发式方法在某些场景下可能失效且缺乏性能保证。

Method: 提出需要开发能够捕捉LLM推理特征的数学模型，包括动态增长的KV缓存内存、预填充-解码阶段不对称性、未知输出长度和连续批处理约束，以设计具有可证明性能保证的算法。

Result: 运筹学与ML系统交叉领域的新兴研究表明，基于原理的方法可以达到或超越启发式方法的性能，同时提供理论保证。

Conclusion: 呼吁学术界将LLM服务的算法设计视为研究前沿，从启发式方法转向具有理论基础的方法。

Abstract: This position paper argues that LLM inference serving has outgrown generic heuristics and now demands mathematical optimization and algorithmic foundations. Despite rapid advances in serving systems such as vLLM and SGLang, their algorithmic cores remain largely unchanged from classical distributed computing: request routing uses join-shortest-queue or round-robin, scheduling defaults to FIFO, and KV cache eviction follows LRU. These general-purpose policies ignore the distinctive structure of LLM inference--dynamically growing KV cache memory, prefill-decode phase asymmetry, unknown output lengths, and continuous batching constraints. We contend that the field must develop mathematical models capturing these characteristics, enabling the design of algorithms with provable performance guarantees across diverse workloads, rather than heuristics that may succeed in some scenarios but fail unpredictably in others. Emerging work at the intersection of operations research and ML systems demonstrates that principled methods can match or exceed heuristic performance while providing theoretical guarantees. We call on the community to recognize algorithmic design for LLM serving as a research frontier.

</details>


### [47] [SplitZip: Ultra Fast Lossless KV Compression for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01708)
*Yipin Guo,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SplitZip，一种面向GPU的高效KV cache无损压缩方法，通过利用浮点指数冗余实现快速编解码，显著缓解LLM服务中预填充-解码分离架构下的KV cache传输瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代LLM服务系统中，预填充-解码分离架构导致KV cache需要在物理分离的工作节点间传输，这成为系统瓶颈，尤其是对于长输入和智能体工作负载。现有无损压缩方案主要针对离线权重压缩，依赖CPU或使用变长编码，压缩速度过慢无法满足在线服务需求。

Method: SplitZip利用KV激活中浮点指数的冗余特性：对最高频的指数值使用定长编码，将稀有指数的(位置, 值)对编码到转义流中。通过离线校准的top-16指数码本实现在线编码，规则的稠密路径与稀疏转义校正相结合，使编解码在GPU上高效执行。

Result: 在真实BF16激活张量上，SplitZip实现613.3 GB/s压缩吞吐量和2181.8 GB/s解压吞吐量，大幅优于现有无损压缩器。端到端实验显示：KV cache传输加速1.32倍，TTFT提升1.30倍，请求吞吐量增加1.23倍。

Conclusion: SplitZip成功解决了LLM服务中KV cache传输的关键瓶颈问题，证明了面向GPU优化的无损压缩在延迟敏感的在线服务场景中的有效性，为大规模LLM服务提供了实用的性能优化方案。

Abstract: Contemporary systems serving large language models (LLMs) have adopted prefill-decode disaggregation to better load-balance between the compute-bound prefill phase and the memory-bound decode phase. Under this design, prefill workers generate a KV cache that must be transferred to decode workers before token generation can begin. With these workers residing on different physical systems, this transfer becomes a significant bottleneck to serving LLMs at scale. This bottleneck gets exacerbated for long-input and agentic workloads, which typically require long inputs. Existing lossless codecs are not well suited to this setting as they primarily target offline weight compression, rely on CPU-side, or use variable-length coding that decompresses fast but compresses too slowly for online use. SplitZip is a GPU-friendly lossless compressor for KV-cache transfer. It exploits redundancy in floating-point exponents of KV activations, encoding the most frequent exponent values with fixed-length codes, and encoding (position, value) pairs and value of rare exponents in an escape stream. An offline calibrated top-16 exponent codebook enables online encoding, while the regular dense path and sparse escape correction make both encoding and decoding efficient on GPUs. On real BF16 activation tensors, SplitZip achieves 613.3 GB/s compression throughput and 2181.8 GB/s decompression throughput, substantially outperforming prior lossless compressors on the latency-critical codec path. End-to-end transfer experiments show up to 1.32$\times$ speedup for BF16 KV-cache transfer, 1.30$\times$ speedup for TTFT and 1.23$\times$ increase on Request Throughput.

</details>


### [48] [Joint Temporal-Structural Representation Learning for Distributed Fault Discrimination in Microservice Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01776)
*Yihan Xue,Yuxiao Wang,Ao Zhu,Xiaoxuan Sun,Chong Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时序图神经网络的分布式故障判别模型，通过联合建模微服务的时序演化和依赖结构，有效提升了微服务分布式系统的故障判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对微服务分布式系统中故障形态多样、依赖关系复杂以及运行状态时变等挑战，需要设计能够同时捕捉系统动态演化特征和结构交互关系的故障判别方法。

Method: 将微服务运行过程建模为动态图序列演化；对服务级多源观测信号进行对齐和表征以构建节点特征序列；引入时序编码模块提取服务状态的动态演化表示；利用基于注意力的结构化消息传递刻画依赖交互和传播关联；采用双重读出机制聚合节点和时间维度，获取系统级全局表示并输出故障类别分布；使用监督学习目标优化模型参数。

Result: 对比实验结果表明，该方法在多个评价指标上均取得了优越的性能，验证了联合建模时序演化和依赖结构在提升微服务分布式故障判别能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型通过在统一框架内联合学习时序建模和结构交互，能够在复杂交互和多源噪声条件下学习稳定的判别依据，有效提高了微服务分布式系统的故障判别能力。

Abstract: Addressing the diverse fault morphologies, complex dependencies, and time-varying operational states in microservice distributed systems, this paper proposes a distributed fault discrimination model based on temporal graph neural networks. This model characterizes the microservice operation process as a dynamic graph sequence evolving, and performs joint representation learning of temporal modeling and structural interactions within a unified framework. First, service-level multi-source observation signals are aligned and characterized to construct node feature sequences and their corresponding time-dependent dependencies. Then, a temporal coding module is introduced to extract the dynamic evolution representation of service states, and at each time step, attention-based structured message passing is used to characterize dependency interactions and propagation associations, forming a structure-enhanced temporal node representation. Furthermore, a dual readout mechanism is employed to aggregate the node and temporal dimensions, obtaining a system-level global representation and outputting the fault category distribution. Finally, supervised learning objectives are used to optimize model parameters, enabling the model to learn stable discrimination evidence under complex interactions and multi-source noise conditions. Comparative experimental results show that the proposed method achieves superior performance on multiple evaluation metrics, validating the effectiveness of jointly modeling temporal evolution and dependency structures in improving the distributed fault discrimination capability of microservices.

</details>


### [49] [AAFLOW: Scalable Patterns for Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02162)
*Arup Kumar Sarker,Mills Staylor,Aymen Alsaadi,Gregor von Laszewski,Shantenu Jha,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AAFLOW是一个统一的分布式运行时框架，通过将智能体工作流建模为算子抽象，利用零拷贝数据平面和资源确定性调度，在保持LLM生成吞吐量的同时，实现了最高4.64倍的流水线加速和2.8倍的嵌入与更新性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体工作流框架在可扩展性和可复现性方面存在局限，主要源于碎片化的数据编排、序列化开销以及非确定性执行；尽管提高了灵活性，但缺乏遵循高性能计算原则的形式化执行模型。

Method: 提出AAFLOW框架，将智能体工作流建模为算子抽象以创建通信高效的执行计划；利用Apache Arrow和Cylon构建零拷贝数据平面，实现预处理、嵌入和向量检索间的直接互操作，消除序列化开销；采用资源确定性调度和异步批处理来降低协调成本。

Result: 实验结果表明，在保持相当LLM生成吞吐量的前提下，AAFLOW实现了最高4.64倍的流水线加速，以及在嵌入和更新阶段2.8倍的性能提升；这些优势主要源于增强的数据流、批处理和通信效率，而非LLM推理加速。

Conclusion: AAFLOW证明了通过优化数据流编排、消除序列化开销和改进调度策略，可以显著提升智能体工作流的执行效率，为构建高性能、可扩展的LLM系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic workflows in large language model systems integrate retrieval, reasoning, and memory, but existing frameworks suffer from scalability and reproducibility limitations due to fragmented data orchestration, serialization overhead, and non-deterministic execution. Although these frameworks increase flexibility, they don't have a formal execution model that adheres to the principles of high-performance computing. We introduce AAFLOW, a unified distributed runtime that creates communication-efficient execution plans by modeling agentic workflows as an operator abstraction. Using Apache Arrow and Cylon, AAFLOW creates a zero-copy data plane that allows direct interoperability between preprocessing, embedding, and vector retrieval without the need for serialization overhead. To lower coordination costs, it uses resource-deterministic scheduling and asynchronous batching. While retaining comparable LLM generation throughput, experimental results demonstrate up to 4.64 times pipeline speedup and 2.8 times gains in embedding and upsert phases. Rather than LLM inference acceleration, these advantages result from enhanced data flow, batching, and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [50] [nvPAX: Constrained Optimization for Dynamic Power Allocation in Hierarchical and Multi-Tenant Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01837)
*Hadar Sivan,Gil Shabat,Yoel Shkolnisky*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了nvPAX，一种针对层级化数据中心结构的约束优化功率分配策略。该策略通过三阶段QP/LP混合程序，在满足租户级合同约束的前提下动态分配功率。仿真实验显示，其平均满意度达98.92%，优于静态均分分配，且在处理非均匀层级瓶颈时比贪婪比例分配更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心功率密度的增长，功率超额认购已成为运营核心。现有功率分配方法通常假设扁平的功率域，忽略了实际中功率分配的层级结构以及必须遵守的租户级合同约束，这限制了在动态需求下的资源利用率。

Method: 提出nvPAX策略，采用三阶段混合QP/LP（二次规划/线性规划）程序求解可行功率分配：第一阶段根据任务优先级分配功率，最小化与设备请求功率的偏差；第二阶段将剩余功率公平分配给活跃设备；第三阶段将剩余功率分配给空闲设备。该设计旨在通过层级化约束优化最大化数据中心利用率。

Result: 基于生产环境GPU功率遥测数据的驱动仿真显示，nvPAX在每个分配间隔的平均运行时间为264.69毫秒，平均满意度比率达到98.92%。该策略性能优于静态均分分配，且在存在非均匀层级瓶颈的情况下，比贪婪比例分配具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: nvPAX成功解决了层级化数据中心功率超额认购中的动态分配难题，在满足合同约束的同时实现了高满意度和低延迟，证明了其在提升数据中心利用率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Power oversubscription is increasingly central to datacenter operation as power density grows, making it necessary to dynamically allocate limited power budgets across devices based on real-time demand. Existing approaches typically assume flat power domains, whereas in practice power distribution is hierarchical and allocation decisions must additionally respect tenant-level contractual constraints. We present nvPAX, a constrained-optimization policy that computes feasible power allocations at every control step via a three-phase hybrid QP/LP procedure. Phase I allocates power with minimum deviation from each device's power request, while respecting job priorities. Phase II fairly distributes excess power among active devices. Phase III fairly distributes any remaining power to idle devices. The rationale behind the three phases is to allow power oversubscription while maximizing datacenter utilization. On a trace-driven large-scale simulation using GPU power telemetry from a production datacenter, nvPAX runs with a mean wall-clock time of 264.69 ms per allocation interval and achieves a mean satisfaction ratio of 98.92%, outperforming static equal-share allocation and providing robustness beyond greedy proportional allocation in the presence of non-uniform hierarchical bottlenecks.

</details>


### [51] [Decentralized Stratified Sampling for Low-Latency Approximate Geospatial Data Stream Processing in Edge-Cloud Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01922)
*Isam Mashhour Al Jawarneh,Lorenzo Felletti,Luca Foschini,Paolo Bellavista*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出EdgeApproxGeo边缘-云架构，通过在边缘设备进行去中心化空间分层采样，在保持误差可控（MAPE<10%）的同时显著提升实时地理空间数据分析速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备产生的地理空间数据流呈指数级增长，传统基于云的分析架构因数据完全由云端集中管理，导致网络带宽浪费和高延迟问题。

Method: 提出EdgeApproxGeo边缘-云架构，引入EdgeSOS去中心化地理哈希分层采样算法，在资源受限的边缘节点独立运行无需跨节点同步，结合Apache Kafka中的空间感知数据分布和主题路由优化下游数据流处理。

Result: 在真实移动性和空气质量数据集上评估，相比纯云基线显著加速；在80%采样率下MAPE误差率<10%；较粗粒度Geohash-5比较细粒度Geohash-6可减少30%误差，实现可调的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 基于Apache Kafka和Spark构建的原型验证了边缘部署近似查询处理在实时大地理空间数据分析中的实用性。

Abstract: The exponential growth of geospatial data streams flowing from IoT devices challenges conventional cloud-based analytics, which typically suffer from network bandwidth waste and latency, basically attributed to the data being managed completely by Cloud, such as centralized sampling. To address this gap, we propose EdgeApproxGeo, a novel edge-cloud architecture that performs spatial-stratified online sampling at network edge devices near data sources. Our system introduces a novel sampling method called EdgeSOS, which is a unique decentralized, geohash-based stratified sampling algorithm designed to operate independently at resource-constrained edge nodes without cross-node synchronization, coupled with spatial-aware data distribution and topic routing in Apache Kafka data stream ingestion, aiming at optimizing downstream data stream processing analytics. We evaluated our system on two real-world geo-referenced datasets, mobility and air quality, and EdgeApproxGeo achieves a significant speedup over cloud-only baselines while maintaining errors in check (e.g., MAPE < 10% error rate at 80% sampling rate). We further demonstrate that coarser geohash granularity (e.g., Geohash-5) can reduce error figures by 30% as compared to finer counterparts (i.e., Geohash-6), thus revealing a tunable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Our standard-compliant prototype, built atop Apache Kafka and Apache Spark, further validates the utility of edge-deployed approximate query processing for real-time big geospatial data analytics.

</details>


### [52] [Cross-Layer Energy Analysis of Multimodal Training on Grace Hopper Superchips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01938)
*Mahmoud Ahmed,Sameh Abdulah,Olatunji Ruwase,Sam Ade Jacobs,Mathis Bode,Mohamed Elhoseiny,David E. Keyes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在NVIDIA GH200超级芯片上对多模态深度学习训练进行了跨层分析，发现数据移动和重叠而非原始计算利用率主导能效，且运行时最优配置未必能效最优，据此提出了一套平衡卸载策略、序列并行和硬件感知调度的实用指南，以实现高效的多模态训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态深度学习模型的快速扩展带来了显著的内存和通信瓶颈，现有框架（如DeepSpeed）虽通过CPU卸载、激活检查点和通信优化缓解挑战，但引入额外系统活动可能影响能效；同时，NVIDIA GH200等紧耦合异构架构提供高带宽CPU-GPU互连和统一内存，亟需分析其在多模态训练中的性能与能效权衡。

Method: 在NVIDIA GH200系统上对多模态训练进行跨层分析，明确刻画应用层、运行时层和硬件层之间的交互，利用高带宽CPU-GPU互连，系统地研究卸载策略、序列并行和硬件感知调度对性能和能效的影响。

Result: 研究表明能效主要由数据移动和重叠而非原始计算利用率决定；针对运行时优化的配置未必是能效最优配置；通过合理利用高带宽CPU-GPU互连，能在能效、运行时性能和计算吞吐量之间实现良好平衡。

Conclusion: 提出了一套可操作的实践指南，指导从业者平衡卸载策略、序列并行和硬件感知调度以实现能效高效训练；高带宽CPU-GPU互连为现代异构系统上的高效多模态训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Multimodal deep learning models enable joint learning across heterogeneous data sources, including text, images, and video, but their rapid scaling introduces significant memory and communication bottlenecks. As model sizes and sequence lengths increase, training performance becomes increasingly impacted by data movement rather than computation. Frameworks such as DeepSpeed mitigate these challenges through CPU offloading, activation checkpointing, and communication optimizations. However, these techniques introduce additional system activity, which may affect energy efficiency. Meanwhile, tightly integrated heterogeneous architectures, such as the NVIDIA Grace Hopper (GH200) superchip, provide high-bandwidth CPU-GPU interconnects and unified memory, thereby reducing data transfer overhead. In this work, we present a cross-layer analysis of energy and performance trade-offs in multimodal training on GH200 systems, explicitly characterizing the interactions between application, runtime, and hardware layers. Leveraging high-bandwidth CPU-GPU interconnects, our results show that energy efficiency is primarily governed by data movement and overlap rather than raw compute utilization, and that configurations optimized for runtime are not necessarily optimal for energy. Based on these findings, we distill a set of actionable guidelines for practitioners that demonstrate how to balance offloading strategies, sequence parallelism, and hardware-aware scheduling to achieve energy-efficient training. Our results demonstrate that leveraging high-bandwidth CPU-GPU interconnects enables offloading strategies and sequence parallelism, achieving a strong balance among energy efficiency, runtime performance, and computational throughput, providing practical guidelines for efficient multimodal training on modern heterogeneous systems.

</details>


### [53] [FedQueue: Queue-Aware Federated Learning for Cross-Facility HPC Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02125)
*Yijiang Li,Emon Dey,Zilinghan Li,Krishnan Raghavan,Ravi Madduri,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FedQueue协议，通过在线预测排队延迟、截止准入控制和陈旧感知聚合机制，有效解决了跨HPC设施联邦学习中由批处理调度器引起的随机准入延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨多个HPC设施的联邦学习面临批处理调度器带来的随机准入延迟，该延迟主导了墙上时钟时间。同步联邦学习受严重的落后者影响，而异步联邦学习在队列峰值时会积累陈旧的更新，现有方法无法有效平衡这两者。

Method: FedQueue协议包含三个核心组件：（i）在线预测各设施的排队延迟以预算本地工作量；（ii）应用基于截止时间的准入控制，缓冲迟到到达以限制陈旧度；（iii）执行陈旧感知聚合以稳定异构本地工作负载。

Result: 理论证明在非凸目标函数下以O(1/√R)速率收敛，且在排队预测误差下以高概率保证陈旧度有界。真实跨设施部署显示相比基线算法提升20.5%；受控队列模拟中，在高队列方差和非IID分区下达到目标精度的时间减少约34%。

Conclusion: FedQueue成功将调度器延迟直接纳入训练和聚合过程，在保证理论收敛性的同时，显著提升了跨HPC设施联邦学习的实际性能，为大规模分布式机器学习系统提供了有效的调度感知解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) across multiple HPC facilities faces stochastic admission delays from batch schedulers that dominate wall-clock time. Synchronous FL suffers from severe stragglers, while asynchronous FL accumulates stale updates when queues spike. We propose FedQueue, a queue-aware FL protocol that incorporates scheduler delays directly into training and aggregation, which (i) predicts per-facility queue delays online to budget local work, (ii) applies cutoff-based admission that buffers late arrivals to bound staleness, and (iii) performs staleness-aware aggregation to stabilize heterogeneous local workloads. We prove the convergence for non-convex objectives at rate $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{R})$ under bounded staleness, and show that the admission controls yield bounded staleness with high probability under queue-prediction error. Real-world cross-facility deployment of FedQueue shows 20.5% improvement over baseline algorithms. Controlled queue simulations demonstrate robust improvement over the baselines; in particular, about 34% reduction in time to reach a target accuracy level under high queue variance and non-IID partitions.

</details>


### [54] [PipeMax: Enhancing Offline LLM Inference on Commodity GPU Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02189)
*Hongbin Zhang,Taosheng Wei,Jiazhi Jiang,Hui Yan,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PipeMax系统，通过协同流水线并行与卸载技术，在固定预算的GPU服务器上实现高吞吐量LLM推理，性能显著优于现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线LLM推理工作通常孤立地考虑卸载和并行策略，导致在商品级GPU服务器上的性能欠佳，无法充分利用资源。

Method: 提出PipeMax系统，将流水线并行与卸载技术集成。利用流水线并行通信开销低且每时刻仅一个批次在GPU上活跃的特性，将非活跃批次的KV缓存卸载至CPU/内存，通过协调计算与数据移动来扩展GPU内存容量并维持大批量执行。

Result: 在8-GPU节点上，PipeMax的吞吐量相比vLLM提升高达2.51倍，相比最先进的高吞吐量LLM系统分别提升高达1.42倍和1.38倍。

Conclusion: PipeMax成功整合了流水线并行与卸载机制，有效突破了GPU服务器的互连和内存限制，显著提升了离线LLM推理的吞吐量。

Abstract: Offline LLM inference seeks to maximize request processing under fixed budgets, making commodity GPU servers a promising choice. However, prior work typically considers offloading and parallelism in isolation, resulting in suboptimal performance. In this paper, we propose PipeMax, a high-throughput LLM inference system that integrates pipeline parallelism with offloading to overcome interconnect and memory constraints on GPU servers. Particularly, pipeline parallelism naturally incurs low communication overhead and keeps only one batch active on each GPU at a time, which enables offloading the KV cache of inactive batches. By coordinating computation with offloading data movement, PipeMax effectively expands GPU memory capacity and sustains large-batch execution. Experiments show that PipeMax achieves up to 2.51x higher throughput than vLLM, and up to 1.42x and 1.38x higher throughput than state-of-the-art high-throughput LLM systems, respectively, on an 8-GPU node.

</details>


### [55] [A Treasure Trove of Performance: Analyzing the IO500 Submission Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02194)
*Julian Kunkel,Aasish Kumar Sharma,Anila Ghazanfar,Sepehr Mahmoodianhamedani,Sascha Safenreider*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对61个IO500基准测试提交包进行了统计分析，揭示了评分跨越四个数量级，发现了阶段内强相关性，并通过日志分析揭示了隐藏在聚合分数之外的文件系统特定模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IO500基准已成为评估HPC存储系统性能的社区标准，但其提交包中包含的详细数据除总排名外，很大程度上未被探索。为了挖掘这些数据的潜在价值并深入理解存储系统行为，作者决定对其进行全面的统计特征分析。

Method: 收集ISC21至SC22四届竞赛列表中的61个IO500提交数据，进行评分分布分析；采用Spearman相关分析研究阶段间相关性；深入分析伴随提交的详细日志文件，以揭示IOR关闭时间开销、stonewall磨损阶段的掉队者行为及并行查找负载不平衡等模式。

Result: 分析显示IO500评分跨越四个数量级。Spearman相关分析表明带宽和元数据阶段均存在强域内聚类（rs = 0.78至0.98），复合子评分在节点级表现出rs = 0.92。日志级分析揭示了聚合分数中不可见的文件系统特定模式，包括IOR关闭时间开销、stonewall磨损阶段的掉队者行为以及并行查找负载不平衡。

Conclusion: IO500提交包构成了理解存储系统行为的宝贵研究资源。完整的提交数据集和分析脚本已公开发布，便于社区进一步研究和利用。

Abstract: The IO500 benchmark has become the community standard for evaluating HPC storage system performance, yet the detailed data contained in its submission packages remains largely unexplored beyond aggregate leaderboard rankings. We present a statistical characterization of 61 IO500 submissions from four competition lists (ISC21 through SC22), examining score distributions, inter-phase correlations, and insights derived from detailed log files that accompany each submission. Our analysis reveals that IO500 scores span four orders of magnitude. Spearman correlation analysis shows strong within-domain clustering for both bandwidth (rs = 0.78 to 0.96) and metadata (rs = 0.89 to 0.98) phases, with the composite sub-scores exhibiting rs = 0.92 at per-node level (Pearson r = 0.53). Log-level analysis uncovers file-system-specific patterns in IOR close-time overhead, straggler behavior during the stonewall wear-down phase, and parallel-find load imbalance that are invisible in aggregate scores. These findings demonstrate that IO500 submission packages constitute a valuable research resource for understanding storage system behavior. The full submission dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/IO500/submission-data, and analysis scripts at https://gitlab-ce.gwdg.de/hpc-team/io500-analysis.

</details>


### [56] [Leveraging Teaching on Demand: Approaching HPC to Undergrads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02217)
*S. Catalán,R. Carratalá-Sáez,S. Iserte*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了利用树莓派设备构建集群的非强制性课程设计，旨在提升工程专业学生的高性能计算（HPC）技能与知识，课程内容涵盖硬件、网络、软件工具、性能评估和集群管理等，并通过“按需教学”激发学生兴趣，作者提供了完整的课程复制细节并验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现在其所在大学的工程学位（包括计算机科学）中缺乏与高性能计算（HPC）相关的内容，而HPC在企业与机构中需求旺盛，因此有必要为学生提供HPC技能培训以弥补这一缺口。

Method: 设计并开设一门名为“使用树莓派构建自己的集群”的非强制性课程，利用树莓派设备的灵活性与多功能性提供实践操作体验，课程涵盖超级计算基础（硬件、网络、软件工具、性能评估、集群管理等），并引入“按需教学”组件让学生根据兴趣选择探索领域。

Result: 通过该课程，学生掌握了HPC的基本技能与知识，提升了学习积极性，并获得了实践操作经验；学生表示有能力在中期职业生涯中应用所学HPC知识。作者提供了完整的课程复制细节，验证了教学方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 利用树莓派构建集群的教学方法能够有效提升学生的HPC技能与知识，增强其在未来职业生涯中的应用能力。该课程设计具有可复制性与推广价值，可为其他教育机构提供参考。

Abstract: High Performance Computing (HPC) is a highly demanded discipline in companies and institutions. However, as students and also afterwards as professors, we observed a lack of HPC related content in the engineering degrees at our university, including Computer Science. Thus, we designed and offered the engineering students a non-mandatory course entitled ``Build you own Raspberry Pi cluster employing Raspberry Pi'' to provide the students with HPC skills. With this course, we covered the basics of supercomputing (hardware, networking, software tools, performance evaluation, cluster management, etc.). This was possible thanks to leveraging the flexibility and versatility of Raspberry Pi devices, and the students' motivation that arose from the hands-on experience. Moreover, the course included a ``Teaching on demand'' component to let the attendees choose a field to explore, based on their own interests. In this paper, we offer all the details to let anyone fully reproduce the course. Besides, we analyze and evaluate the methodology that let us fulfill our objectives: increase the students' HPC skills and knowledge in such a way that they feel capable of utilizing it in their mid-term professional career.

</details>


### [57] [Taming Request Imbalance: SLO-Aware Scheduling for Disaggregated LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02329)
*Qipeng Wang,Zhendong Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Kairos系统，通过紧迫性优先调度和松弛度引导自适应批处理，解决LLM服务中预填充和解码阶段的负载不均衡问题，显著提升SLO达成率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中LLM服务面临严格的SLO要求和高度变化的请求模式，请求长度的长尾分布导致分离式服务架构中预填充阶段的队头阻塞和解码阶段的滞后者问题。现有系统采用FCFS调度和连续批处理无法适应这种不平衡，导致SLO达成率和吞吐量下降。

Method: 提出Kairos系统，包含两个互补机制：1）预填充阶段采用基于紧迫性的优先级调度，预测预填充完成时间并动态选择请求以最大化TTFT SLO达成；2）解码阶段引入松弛度引导的自适应批处理，利用每步解码时间与TPOT SLO之间的间隙贪婪打包短请求，在严格遵守SLO要求的同时最大化吞吐量。

Result: 在在线服务数据集和先进LLM上的实验表明，与最先进基线相比，Kairos将TTFT SLO达成率提升高达23.9%，TPOT SLO达成率提升高达27.1%，端到端SLO达成率提升高达33.8%，解码吞吐量提升高达19.3%。

Conclusion: Kairos系统有效解决了LLM服务中预填充和解码阶段的调度问题，在保证SLO要求的同时显著提升了系统性能，为生产环境中的LLM服务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In production environments, large language model (LLM) serving is required to meet stringent service-level objectives (SLOs) amid highly variable request patterns. In practice, request lengths follow a long-tail distribution, which gives rise to head-of-line blocking on the prefill side and underutilization caused by stragglers on the decode side in disaggregated serving architectures. Current systems, which adopt first-come-first-served (FCFS) scheduling for prefill and continuous batching for decode, lack the ability to adapt to this imbalance, resulting in compromised SLO attainment and reduced throughput.
  To address these challenges, we propose Kairos, an SLO-aware scheduling system equipped with two complementary mechanisms. On the prefill side, Kairos employs urgency-based priority scheduling: it predicts prefill completion times and dynamically selects requests to maximize the attainment of time-to-first-token (TTFT) SLOs. On the decode side, Kairos introduces slack-guided adaptive batching, which leverages the gap between per-step decode time and the time-per-output-token (TPOT) SLO to greedily pack short requests. This approach maximizes throughput while strictly adhering to SLO requirements. We implement Kairos and conduct evaluations using an online serving dataset and a state-of-the-art LLM. Experimental results demonstrate that, compared with state-of-the-art baselines, Kairos improves TTFT SLO attainment by up to 23.9\%, TPOT SLO attainment by up to 27.1\%, end-to-end SLO attainment by up to 33.8\%, and decode throughput by up to 19.3\%.

</details>


### [58] [FedPLT: Scalable, Resource-Efficient, and Heterogeneity-Aware Federated Learning via Partial Layer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02337)
*Ahmad Dabaja,Rachid El-Azouzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FedPLT方法，通过部分层训练在减少71%-82%可训练参数的同时达到全模型训练性能，有效解决联邦学习中的通信、计算开销和设备异构性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然解决了统计数据异构性问题，但仍面临高通信/计算开销和严重设备异构性挑战。现有子模型训练和部分参数训练方法存在客户端参数分布不一致、全局损失估计不准确、偏差和方差增加等问题。

Method: 提出FedPLT（Federated Learning with Partial Layer Training），这是一种结构化的部分参数训练方法。该方法根据客户端的通信和计算能力分配特定的模型部分进行训练，展现出与全模型训练相似的训练行为。同时结合最优客户端采样策略以减少采样方差。

Result: FedPLT在可训练参数减少71%-82%的情况下，达到甚至超越全模型训练（FedAvg）的性能；在高异构环境中优于现有方法；有效适应客户端资源约束；减少落后客户端数量；结合最优客户端采样在同一通信预算下减少采样方差。

Conclusion: FedPLT成功实现了在显著降低计算和通信开销的同时保持模型性能，为解决联邦学习中的设备异构性和资源约束问题提供了有效解决方案，证明了部分层训练在联邦学习中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has gained significant attention in distributed machine learning by enabling collaborative model training across decentralized system while preserving data privacy. Although extensive research has addressed statistical data heterogeneity, FL still faces several challenges, including high communication and computation overheads and severe device heterogeneity, which require further investigation. Prior work has addressed these issues through sub-model training and partial parameter training. However, such methods often suffer from inconsistent parameter distributions across clients, inaccurate global loss estimation, and increased bias and variance. Guided by our empirical analysis, we propose FedPLT (Federated Learning with Partial Layer Training), an innovative and structured partial parameter training approach that exhibits training behavior similar to full model training while assigning client-specific portions of the model according to their communication and computational capabilities. In addition, we evaluate the performance of FedPLT when combined with optimal client sampling under communication constraints. We show that this integration improves FL performance by reducing sampling variance under the same communication budget. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that FedPLT achieves performance comparable to, or even surpassing, that of full-model training (i.e., FedAvg), while requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters per client. Moreover, FedPLT outperforms existing methods in highly heterogeneous environments, effectively adapts to client resource constraints, and reduces the number of straggling clients. In particular, FedPLT reduces the number of trainable parameters by 71%-82% while achieving performance on par with full-model training.

</details>


### [59] [Caliper-in-the-Loop: Black-Box Optimization for Hyperledger Fabric Performance Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02690)
*Yash Madhwal,Arseny Bolotnikov,Mark Prikhno,Irina Lebedeva,Ivan Laishevskiy,Vladimir Gorgadze,Artem Barger,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究利用贝叶斯优化（BO）结合降维技术（DR）自动化调优Hyperledger Fabric配置参数以提升吞吐量，在317维搜索空间中，最优方法DYCORS-PCA相比初始配置实现了12%的TPS提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyperledger Fabric的性能受众多相互影响的配置参数制约，导致人工调优极为困难，因此亟需一种自动化的高效调优方法。

Method: 将基准测试视为带噪声的黑盒优化问题，构建端到端的Caliper-in-the-loop流水线，自动部署候选配置、执行基准测试并利用观测到的吞吐量更新优化器。研究采用了贝叶斯优化（BO）结合降维（DR）技术处理317维的搜索空间，并在云测试环境中评估了16种BO+DR变体及随机搜索基线。

Result: 实验结果表明，最佳方法DYCORS-PCA实现了12%的TPS提升（相对首个评估配置），MPI-REMBO方法实现了9%的提升。研究同时指出了测量噪声在解释性能增益中的作用。

Conclusion: 结合降维技术的贝叶斯优化是解决高维Hyperledger Fabric调优问题的实用有效途径，但需注意测量噪声对结果解读的影响。

Abstract: Hyperledger Fabric performance depends on many interacting configuration parameters, making manual tuning difficult. We study automated throughput tuning by treating benchmarking as a noisy black-box optimization problem and applying Bayesian optimization (BO) with dimensionality reduction (DR). We implement an end-to-end Caliper-in-the-loop pipeline that deploys candidate configurations, benchmarks them, and updates the optimizer from observed throughput. The search space, derived from Fabric configuration files, has 317 dimensions. In a cloud testbed, we evaluate 16 BO+DR variants and a random-search baseline. The best method, DYCORS-PCA, achieves a 12% TPS improvement relative to the first evaluated configuration, while MPI-REMBO achieves 9%. These results suggest that BO with DR is a practical approach for high-dimensional Hyperledger Fabric tuning, while also highlighting the role of measurement noise in interpreting gains.

</details>


### [60] [Assessing Performance and Porting Strategies for Gravitational $N$-Body Simulations on the RISC-V-Based Tenstorrent Wormhole\textsuperscript{\texttrademark}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02744)
*Jenny Lynn Almerol,Elisabetta Boella,Mario Spera,Daniele Gregori*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于RISC-V架构的Tenstorrent Wormhole加速器，提出了三种N体代码的跨加速器扩展策略，并通过实验确定了性能与能效平衡的最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于RISC-V的加速器最初是为人工智能应用设计的，但其在高性能科学计算领域的潜力正逐渐受到关注。为了探索和验证该架构在科学计算中的适用性，作者选择N体模拟这一典型科学计算负载进行跨加速器扩展研究。

Method: 提出了三种在多个Tenstorrent Wormhole RISC-V加速器上扩展N体模拟代码的策略，通过测量完成代表性模拟所需的执行时间和能耗来评估这些策略的性能。

Result: 通过对比不同策略的执行时间与能耗数据，确定了在效率与性能之间取得最佳平衡的配置方案。

Conclusion: 研究证明了RISC-V加速器在高性能科学计算应用中的可行性与潜力，并为类似架构上的应用扩展与优化提供了参考依据。

Abstract: While RISC-V-based accelerators were initially designed with artificial intelligence applications in mind, they are increasingly being recognized as promising platforms for high performance scientific computing. In this work, we present three strategies for scaling an $N$-body code across multiple Tenstorrent Wormhole accelerators based on the RISC-V architecture. We assess the performance of these approaches by measuring both the execution time and the energy consumption required to complete a representative simulation, ultimately identifying the configuration that offers the most favorable balance between efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [61] [(POSTER) From Sensors to Insight: Rapid, Edge-to-Core Application Development for Sensor-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02844)
*Komal Thareja,Anirban Mandal,Ewa Deelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模式、AI辅助的方法，用于快速开发传感器驱动应用，将工作流开发时间压缩至每个1-1.5天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家日益依赖传感器数据，但由于协调数据和计算流需要广泛的专业知识，将原始数据流转化为跨边缘到云连续体的洞察仍然困难。

Method: 提出基于模式、AI辅助的快速开发方法论，使用Pegasus工作流在FABRIC测试床上执行，采用5步开发循环，将工作流构建和部署从代码优先转变为意图优先设计；以现有Orcasound水听器工作流作为可重用模板，生成并优化空气质量、地震和土壤湿度监测工作流。

Result: 从新手Pegasus用户角度评估，AI辅助的模式重用将多阶段工作流开发压缩至每个工作流1-1.5天，同时保持基于工作流执行的严谨性和可移植性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功实现了传感器驱动应用的快速开发，通过配置和放置而非工作流重设计即可扩展到边缘资源（包括BlueField-3 DPU和树莓派），显著降低了开发门槛和时间成本。

Abstract: Scientists increasingly rely on sensor-based data; however transforming raw streams into insights across the edge-to-cloud continuum remains difficult due to the breadth of expertise required to coordinate the necessary data and computation flow. This paper introduces a pattern-based, AI-assisted methodology for rapid development of sensor-driven applications. Using Pegasus workflows executing on the FABRIC testbed, we demonstrate a 5-step development loop that shifts workflow construction and deployment from code-first to intent-first design. Starting from an existing Orcasound hydrophone workflow as a reusable template, we generate and refine workflows for air quality, earthquake, and soil moisture monitoring applications. We further show how these workflows extend to edge resources-including BlueField-3 DPUs and Raspberry Pis-through configuration and placement rather than workflow redesign. Our evaluation, from the perspective of a novice Pegasus user, shows that AI-assisted pattern reuse compresses multi-stage workflow development to 1-1.5 days per workflow while preserving the rigor and portability of workflow-based execution.

</details>


### [62] [From Sensors to Insight: Rapid, Edge-to-Core Application Development for Sensor-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02859)
*Komal Thareja,Anirban Mandal,Ewa Deelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模式化工程与AI辅助的体验驱动方法，旨在降低传感器驱动应用的开发门槛，使非专家也能在异构基础设施上快速构建和迭代应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着传感器数据的广泛应用，如何在边缘到云的连续统中有效地将原始数据流转化为洞察变得极具挑战性。配置异构基础设施以及在数据处理单元（DPU）等新兴平台上管理执行通常需要跨领域专业知识，这为快速原型设计造成了显著障碍。

Method: 提出了一种基于模式的体验驱动方法论。该方法结合了基于模式的工作流工程与AI辅助开发（通过FABRIC测试平台上的Pegasus实现），利用现有的Orcasound水听器工作流作为可重用模板，生成并优化了空气质量、地震和土壤湿度监测的工作流，并通过模块化配置和部署将这些抽象结构扩展到边缘资源。

Result: 通过空气质量管理、地震监测和土壤湿度监测三个案例研究，验证了该方法能够有效生成和优化工作流，并将抽象结构扩展至边缘资源。评估重点在于用户生产力和实践经验，而非峰值性能。

Conclusion: AI辅助和基于模式的开发方法能够有效降低非专家用户的入门门槛，使其能够在分布式基础设施上对传感器驱动应用进行迭代探索，从而提高了开发效率和用户生产力。

Abstract: Scientists increasingly rely on sensor-based data, yet transforming raw streams into insights across the edge-to-cloud continuum remains difficult. Provisioning heterogeneous infrastructure and managing execution on emerging platforms like Data Processing Units typically requires cross-domain expertise, creating significant barriers to rapid prototyping.
  This paper introduces an experience-driven methodology for the rapid development of sensor-driven applications. By combining pattern-based workflow engineering with AI-assisted development-implemented via Pegasus on the FABRIC testbed - we utilize an existing Orcasound hydrophone workflow as a reusable template. We introduce a pattern-based engineering methodology to generate and refine workflows for air quality, earthquake, and soil moisture monitoring. Furthermore, we show how these abstract structures are extended to edge resources through modular configuration and placement. Our evaluation focuses on user productivity and practical lessons rather than peak performance. Through these case studies, we illustrate how AI-assisted, pattern-based development lowers the entry barrier for non-experts and enables iterative exploration of sensor-driven applications across distributed infrastructures.

</details>

### [63] [Agentopic: A Generative AI Agent Workflow for Explainable Topic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00833)
*Brice Valentin Kok-Shun,Johnny Chan,Gabrielle Peko,David Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentopic，一个基于智能体（agent）的可解释主题建模工作流，利用LLM的推理能力实现透明的主题识别、验证、层次分组与自然语言解释，在BBC数据集上F1值达0.95，兼顾了解释性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主题建模方法（如LDA和BERTopic）在主题分配或分组过程上缺乏透明度，难以追溯推理依据。为了解决黑箱模型的可解释性问题，作者希望构建一个既能保持较高准确性又能提供清晰推理过程的主题建模方案。

Method: Agentopic采用多智能体协作架构，包含主题识别、验证、层次分组和自然语言解释等模块。通过多个智能体分工协作，将可解释性嵌入整个工作流，使每个环节的决策可追溯。实验以BBC数据集为基础，分别进行有种子主题和无种子主题的主题建模。

Result: 在BBC数据集上，有种子时Agentopic的F1分数为0.95，与GPT-4.1持平，优于LDA（0.93），接近BERTopic（0.98）；无种子时生成了2045个语义连贯的主题，组织为六个层次级别，极大丰富了原始五类结构，并成功用生成的解释增强了数据集。

Conclusion: Agentopic通过全流程嵌入可解释性，提供了一种可替代黑箱模型的方案，在金融、医疗等关键领域具有重要应用价值，能够同时满足高准确性与强可解释性的需求。

Abstract: Agentopic is a novel agent-based workflow for explainable topic modeling that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing topic modeling approaches such as Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) and BERTopic often lack transparency on how topics are assigned or grouped. Agentopic addresses this by using multiple agents that collaboratively perform topic identification, validation, hierarchical grouping, and natural language explanation. This design enables users to trace the reasoning behind topic assignments, enhancing interpretability without sacrificing accuracy. When seeded with topics from the British Broadcasting Corporation (BBC) dataset, Agentopic achieves an F1-score of 0.95, matching GPT-4.1, improving on LDA (0.93), and close to BERTopic (0.98). We used Agentopic to augment the BBC dataset with generated explanations to improve the dataset's richness and context. The unseeded Agentopic generated 2045 semantically coherent topics organized across six hierarchical levels, vastly enriching the original five-category structure. By embedding explainability throughout the workflow, Agentopic offers an interpretable alternative to black-box models, making it particularly valuable for crucial applications like finance and healthcare.

</details>


### [64] [Polynomial-Time Optimal Group Selection via the Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00834)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多项式时间算法，通过求解双重交换子矩阵的广义特征值问题，解决了代数多样性框架中的群选择问题，复杂度为O(d²M² + d³)，且可提供可验证的最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数多样性框架中，群选择是一个核心开放问题：给定M维观测数据，寻找有限群使得其谱分解最佳匹配未知的协方差结构。现有方法需要枚举对称群S_M的所有子群，时间复杂度随M呈指数增长，计算代价极高，因此亟需高效的算法。

Method: 证明该组合问题可归约为由协方差矩阵双重交换子导出的广义特征值问题。算法复杂度为O(d²M² + d³)，其中d为生成元基的维度。双重交换子矩阵的最小特征向量可直接解析构造最优群生成元，无需迭代优化。该归约是精确的：当且仅当最优生成元位于基张成空间时，最小特征值为零；否则其幅值提供可验证的最优性间隙。

Result: 所提算法具有三个关键性质：（1）多项式时间复杂度；（2）闭式解析解；（3）可验证的最优性保证。证明了双重交换子方法是唯一同时具备这三点的方法。算法成功将群论、矩阵分析和统计估计联系起来，形成一类新问题。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个新的计算复杂度问题类别，连接了群论、矩阵分析与统计估计。该方法与独立成分分析（JADE）、结构矩阵近似问题和联合矩阵对角化等领域建立了联系，为代数多样性框架提供了理论基础和高效算法，具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。

Abstract: The algebraic diversity framework replaces temporal averaging over multiple observations with algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. The central open problem in this framework is $\textit{group selection}$: given an $M$-dimensional observation with unknown covariance structure, find the finite group whose spectral decomposition best matches the covariance. Naive enumeration of all subgroups of the symmetric group $S_M$ requires exponential time in $M$. We prove that this combinatorial problem reduces to a generalized eigenvalue problem derived from the double commutator of the covariance matrix, yielding a polynomial-time algorithm with complexity $O(d^2M^2 + d^3)$, where $d$ is the dimension of a generator basis. The minimum eigenvector of the double-commutator matrix directly constructs the optimal group generator in closed form, with no iterative optimization. The reduction is exact: the double-commutator minimum eigenvalue is zero if and only if the optimal generator lies in the span of the basis, and its magnitude provides a certifiable optimality gap when it does not. This problem does not appear in the standard catalogs of computational complexity (Garey and Johnson, 1979) and represents a new class linking group theory, matrix analysis, and statistical estimation. We establish connections to independent component analysis (JADE), structured matrix nearness problems, and simultaneous matrix diagonalization, and we show that the double-commutator formulation is the unique approach that is simultaneously polynomial-time, closed-form, and certifiable.

</details>


### [65] [Sparse Regression under Correlation and Weak Signals: A Reproducible Benchmark of Classical and Bayesian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00835)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对经典稀疏回归方法（Lasso、Ridge等）与贝叶斯方法（Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab）进行了全面基准测试，发现贝叶斯方法在预测误差上显著更优，Horseshoe先验提供了接近名义水平的覆盖率，而Lasso在变量选择任务中是实用的默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典惩罚估计器（如Lasso）计算速度快但无法提供不确定性估计，而贝叶斯方法（如Horseshoe和Spike-and-Slab先验）能产生完整的后验分布但计算耗时长。目前缺乏在实际困难条件（相关特征、弱信号、维度增长）下对这两类方法进行头对头比较的研究。

Method: 在合成数据和Diabetes数据集上进行了超过2,600次实验，比较了六种方法（OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab）。实验设计包括三种协方差结构（相关系数最高达0.9）、四种信噪比水平、以及p ∈ {20, 50, 100}的维度设置。

Result: 贝叶斯方法在预测误差上显著更优：MSE为72，而经典方法为108-267。Horseshoe先验实现了接近名义水平的95%覆盖率（94.8%）。Spike-and-Slab虽然置信区间更窄，但覆盖率不足（91.9%），可能与其连续松弛机制有关。在变量选择任务中，Lasso与Spike-and-Slab的F1分数均约为0.47，当不需要后验分布时，Lasso是实用的默认选择。

Conclusion: 在预测任务中贝叶斯稀疏回归方法具有明显优势，Horseshoe先验在不确定性量化方面表现最佳；当需要变量选择且不要求不确定性估计时，Lasso是更高效的选择。研究为实践者在不同场景下选择合适的稀疏回归方法提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Choosing between classical and Bayesian sparse regression methods involves a real trade-off: penalized estimators like Lasso run in milliseconds but give no uncertainty estimates,while Horseshoe and Spike-and-Slab priors produce full posteriors but need MCMC chains that take minutes per fit.Surprisingly few studies compare these two families head-to-head under the conditions that actually make sparse regression hard -- correlated features, weak signals, and growing dimensionality. We benchmark six methods (OLS, Ridge,Lasso, Elastic Net, Horseshoe, Spike-and-Slab) on synthetic data with three covariance structures (rho up to 0.9), four SNR levels, and p in {20, 50, 100}, plus the Diabetes dataset,totalling over 2,600 experiments. The results are clear on some points and nuanced on others. Bayesian methods win on prediction error (MSE 72 vs. 108-267), and the Horseshoe delivers near-nominal 95% coverage (94.8%). But Spike-and-Slab,despite narrower intervals, under-covers at 91.9% -- its continuous relaxation likely plays a role. For variable selection, Lasso and Spike-and-Slab tie at F1 ~ 0.47, making Lasso the practical default when posteriors are not needed. Code and data are available at https://github.com/xiao98/sparse-bayesian-regression-bench.

</details>


### [66] [From Euler to Dormand-Prince: ODE Solvers for Flow Matching Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00836)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究从泰勒展开出发推导并实现了四种经典ODE求解器，发现高阶求解器（如RK4）能以更少的计算成本获得与低阶求解器相当的样本质量，并揭示了求解器选择对欠训练模型更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching生成模型的采样过程依赖于求解常微分方程（ODE），其计算成本主要由神经网络前向传播主导。为了优化采样效率，作者旨在系统性地研究不同经典ODE求解器在Flow Matching任务中的性能表现。

Method: 作者从泰勒展开的基本原理出发，推导了四种经典ODE求解器（欧拉法、显式中点法、经典四阶龙格-库塔法RK4以及Dormand-Prince 5(4)自适应方法），并在PyTorch中从头实现。通过在从2D玩具分布到MNIST数字等多种条件Flow Matching任务上进行系统性基准测试，利用切片Wasserstein距离构建NFE-质量帕累托前沿进行定量评估。

Result: 实验发现RK4求解器仅需80次函数评估即可达到欧拉法200次评估相当的样本质量。研究还揭示了两个经验现象：（1）学习到的速度场雅可比特征值谱在t=1附近急剧变硬，解释了自适应Dormand-Prince求解器为何自动在轨迹末端集中步数；（2）对于欠训练和较小的模型，低阶与高阶求解器之间的质量差距会扩大，表明模型不完美时求解器选择更为重要。

Conclusion: 高阶ODE求解器能显著提升Flow Matching模型的采样效率，且求解器的选择在模型训练不足或模型容量较小时影响更大。这为Flow Matching模型的实际部署提供了重要的算法选择指导。

Abstract: Sampling from Flow Matching generative models requires solving an ordinary differential equation (ODE) whose computational cost is dominated by neural network forward passes. We derive four classical ODE solvers -- Euler, Explicit Midpoint, Classical Runge-Kutta (RK4), and Dormand-Prince 5(4) -- from first principles via Taylor expansion, implement them from scratch in PyTorch, and systematically benchmark their efficiency on Conditional Flow Matching tasks ranging from 2D toy distributions to MNIST digits. On the quantitative side, we use sliced Wasserstein distance to construct NFE-quality Pareto frontiers,finding that RK4 at 80 function evaluations achieves sample quality comparable to Euler at 200. Beyond reproducing known convergence rates, we report two empirical observations: (1) the Jacobian eigenvalue spectrum of the learned velocity field stiffens sharply near t=1, explaining why the adaptive Dormand-Prince solver automatically concentrates its step budget at the end of the trajectory; (2) the quality gap between low-order and high-order solvers widens for undertrained and smaller models, indicating that solver choice matters most when the model is imperfect. Code and all experiment scripts are publicly available.

</details>


### [67] [Fast Log-Domain Sinkhorn Optimal Transport with Warp-Level GPU Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00837)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FastSinkhorn，一种轻量级的原生CUDA实现的log-domain Sinkhorn算法，在保证数值稳定性的同时实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的熵正则化最优传输（OT）实现存在两个主要问题：当正则化参数较小时会出现数值不稳定，或者在使用深度学习框架时会产生显著的性能开销。

Method: 开发了一个轻量级的原生CUDA实现的log-domain Sinkhorn算法，该算法结合了warp级shuffle归约和共享内存平铺技术，在不牺牲数值稳定性的前提下实现了高GPU利用率。求解器完全在log-domain中运行，使其能够处理极小的正则化参数（epsilon = 10^{-4}）。

Result: 在n = m = 8192的密集OT问题上，相比广泛使用的POT库实现了12倍加速，相比GPU加速的PyTorch基线实现了5.9倍加速，同时仅消耗256 MB的GPU内存。在极小的正则化参数（epsilon = 10^{-4}）下仍能保持稳定计算。

Conclusion: 通过精心数值处理的原生CUDA内核为大规模最优传输计算提供了实用且高效的基础，在图像颜色迁移、3D点云匹配和收敛分析等应用中验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Entropic regularized optimal transport (OT) via the Sinkhorn algorithm has become a fundamental tool in machine learning, yet existing implementations either suffer from numerical instability for small regularization parameters or incur significant overhead from deep learning frameworks. We present FastSinkhorn, a lightweight, native CUDA implementation of the log-domain Sinkhorn algorithm that combines warp-level shuffle reductions with shared-memory tiling to achieve high GPU utilization without sacrificing numerical stability. Our solver operates entirely in the log-domain, enabling robust computation for regularization parameters as small as epsilon = 10^{-4} where standard-domain methods fail. On dense OT problems with n = m = 8192, our implementation achieves 12x speedup over the widely-used POT library and 5.9x speedup over GPU-accelerated PyTorch baselines, while consuming only 256 MB of GPU memory. We validate our solver on image color transfer, 3D point cloud matching, and convergence analysis, demonstrating that native CUDA kernels with careful numerical treatment provide a practical and efficient foundation for large-scale optimal transport computation.

</details>


### [68] [GAZE: Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation with Viewer-Level Tools and Literature Retrieval on Rare Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00876)
*Duaa Alim,Mogtaba Alim,Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAZE框架，让医学视觉语言模型通过调用图像查看工具和文献检索工具进行迭代式诊断，在NOVA基准（906例脑MRI，281种罕见神经疾病）上显著提升病灶定位和诊断准确性，尤其对罕见病例效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在单次前向传播中完成图像到文本的生成，而放射科医生通常需要反复查看图像、调整参数并查阅文献后才能撰写报告。这种差异促使研究者探索如何让VLMs采用类似放射科医生的迭代式工作流程。

Method: 提出GAZE（Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation）框架，使医学VLM能够：(1)调用查看器级工具（缩放、窗宽窗位调整、对比度、边缘检测）；(2)使用两种检索工具（PubMed医学文献、Open-i放射影像）；(3)通过结构化提示和模式验证确保输出规范化；(4)记录完整工具调用轨迹以实现可审计性。在无需任务特定微调的情况下进行联合评估。

Result: 在NOVA基准（906例脑MRI，281种罕见神经疾病）上：病灶定位达到58.2 mAP@IoU=0.3；Top-1诊断准确率34.9%。结构化提示和模式验证使mAP@0.3从20.2提升至29.4（相比Gemini 2.0 Flash基线）。工具使用对罕见病理帮助更大：样本≤3例的病例IoU>0.3比例从17%升至58%，而常见病理（≥10例）从25%升至68%。不同模型工具参与度差异显著（Gemini 3 Flash平均11.8次工具调用/例，Cohen's d=0.79；Gemini 2.0 Flash仅8.2%病例使用工具）。检索消融实验揭示诊断提升可能伴随定位下降的权衡关系。

Conclusion: GAZE框架证明了让医学VLM采用迭代式、工具增强的工作流程的有效性，特别是在罕见疾病诊断中效果显著。研究结果强调了在医学VLM评估中联合考察诊断、定位和描述三个任务的必要性，同时也揭示了框架设计本身（如结构化提示）就是重要的实验变量，不同模型与工具的适配程度存在差异。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) read an image and produce text in a single forward pass, whereas radiologists typically inspect an image several times and consult the literature before writing a report. We introduce GAZE (Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation), a framework that lets a medical VLM work in this iterative way by calling viewer-level tools (zoom, windowing, contrast, edge detection) and two retrieval tools backed by the U.S. National Library of Medicine (PubMed for medical literature, Open-i for radiological images), with structured outputs validated against a schema and full tool-call traces recorded for auditability. On NOVA, a benchmark of 906 brain MRI cases covering 281 rare neurological conditions, GAZE reaches 58.2 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union (IoU) 0.3 for lesion localisation and 34.9% Top-1 diagnostic accuracy under a joint protocol that scores captioning, diagnosis, and localisation from the image alone, without task-specific fine-tuning. Before any tool is used, structured prompting and schema-validated outputs already improve over the published Gemini 2.0 Flash baseline (20.2 to 29.4 mAP@0.3), so framework design is itself an experimental variable. Tool use helps rare pathologies disproportionately: the fraction of cases with IoU > 0.3 rises from 17% to 58% for diagnoses with three or fewer examples versus 25% to 68% for common conditions ($\geq$10 cases), with gains tracking engagement (Gemini 3 Flash: Cohen's d = 0.79, 11.8 tool calls per case; Gemini 2.0 Flash: tools used in 8.2% of cases, no significant benefit). Retrieval ablations additionally reveal a model-dependent trade-off in which gains in diagnosis can coincide with losses in localisation, reinforcing the case for joint evaluation of diagnosis, localisation, and captioning in medical VLMs.

</details>


### [69] [StyleShield: Exposing the Fragility of AIGC Detectors through Continuous Controllable Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00924)
*Guantian Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了StyleShield，首个基于流匹配的文本风格迁移框架，用于规避AI内容检测器；同时提出RateAudit算法，证明基于分数的检测评估可靠性存疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成内容（AIGC）检测器在高风险场景（如学术诚信审查）中被广泛部署，但其可靠性存在根本悖论：随着语言模型基于人类语料库训练不断改进，AI与人类写作之间的统计边界将不可避免地消失。此外，商业激励进一步扭曲了这一格局——检测服务与"去AI化"工具常在同一供应链中运作，用内容来源判断替代内容质量评估。

Method: 提出StyleShield，首个用于条件文本风格转移的流匹配框架。该方法通过带有零初始化交叉注意力适配器的DiT骨干网络，在连续token嵌入空间中直接操作，条件化于冻结的Qwen-7B表示。在推理时，将图像合成领域的SDEdit范式适配到文本嵌入，通过单一参数gamma实现对规避-保留权衡的平滑连续控制。此外，还引入RateAudit文档级调度算法。

Result: 在多领域中文基准测试中，StyleShield对训练检测器实现94.6%的规避率，对三个未见检测器实现≥99%的规避率，同时保持0.928的语义相似度。RateAudit算法证明了检测率判定可以被设置为任意值。

Conclusion: 研究结果直接质疑了基于分数的评估方法的可靠性，揭示了当前AI内容检测系统在高风险场景中应用的脆弱性和不可靠性，对检测服务的实际效用提出根本性质疑。

Abstract: AI-generated content (AIGC) detectors are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as academic integrity screening, yet their reliability rests on a fundamental paradox: as language models are trained on human-written corpora, the statistical boundary between AI and human writing will inevitably dissolve as models improve. Commercial incentives have further distorted this landscape -- detection services and "de-AIification" tools often operate within the same supply chain, replacing evaluation of content quality with judgment of content origin. We present StyleShield, the first flow matching framework for conditional text style transfer, operating directly in continuous token embedding space via a DiT backbone with zero-initialized cross-attention adapters conditioned on frozen Qwen-7B representations. At inference, we adapt the SDEdit paradigm from image synthesis to text embeddings, with a single parameter gamma providing smooth continuous control over the evasion-preservation trade-off. On a multi-domain Chinese benchmark, StyleShield achieves 94.6% evasion against the training detector and >=99% against three unseen detectors, maintaining 0.928 semantic similarity. We further introduce RateAudit, a document-level scheduling algorithm that demonstrates detection-rate verdicts can be set to arbitrary values, directly questioning the reliability of score-based evaluation.

</details>
