### [70] [Linking spatial biology and clinical histology via Haiku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00925)
*Yan Cui,Jacob S. Leiby,Wenhui Lei,Dokyoon Kim,Yanxiang Deng,Aaron T. Mayer,Zhenqin Wu,Alexandro E. Trevino,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Haiku——一种三模态对比学习模型，通过联合建模空间蛋白质组学、组织病理学和临床数据，实现跨模态检索、下游任务性能提升和零样本生物标志物推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整合分子、形态学和临床数据对生物医学研究至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的多模态联合建模框架，限制了多源数据的有效利用。

Method: 构建三模态对比学习模型Haiku，基于多重免疫荧光(mIF)数据训练，包含2670万个空间蛋白质组学切片，涵盖11种器官类型的1606名患者，将H&E组织学和临床元数据对齐到共享嵌入空间；并引入反事实预测框架，通过仅修改临床元数据来揭示分子变化。

Result: Haiku在跨模态检索任务上达到Recall@50为0.611（基线接近零）；生存预测C-index为0.737，相对提升7.91%；零样本生物标志物推断在52个生物标志物上平均Pearson相关系数为0.718；反事实分析成功识别了与乳腺癌进展和肺癌生存相关的分子模式变化。

Conclusion: 三模态对齐方法Haiku实现了空间生物学的整合分析，有效桥接了分子测量与临床背景，为生物学探索提供了新的分析范式，展示了多模态学习在转化医学研究中的潜力。

Abstract: Integrating molecular, morphological, and clinical data is essential for basic and translational biomedical research, yet systematic frameworks for jointly modeling these modalities remain limited. Here we present Haiku, a tri-modal contrastive learning model trained on multiplexed immunofluorescence (mIF). It comprises 26.7 million spatial proteomics patches from 3,218 tissue sections across 1,606 patients spanning 11 organ types, with matched hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) histology and clinical metadata aligned in a shared embedding space. Haiku enables three-way cross-modal retrieval, improves downstream classification and clinical prediction tasks over unimodal baselines, and supports zero-shot biomarker inference through fusion retrieval conditioned on clinical metadata-only text descriptions. Across tasks, Haiku outperforms competing approaches, achieving cross-modal retrieval (Recall@50 up to 0.611 versus near-zero baseline), survival prediction (C-index 0.737, +7.91% relative improvement), and zero-shot biomarker inference (mean Pearson correlation 0.718 across 52 biomarkers). Furthermore, we introduce a counterfactual prediction framework in which modifying only clinical metadata while fixing tissue morphology surfaces niche-specific molecular shifts associated with breast cancer stage progression and lung cancer survival outcomes. In a lung adenocarcinoma case study, the counterfactual analysis recovers niche-specific shifts characterized by increased CD8 and granzyme B, reduced PD-L1, and decreased Ki67, broadly consistent with patterns reported for favorable outcomes. We present these counterfactual results as exploratory, hypothesis-generating signals rather than mechanistic claims. These capabilities demonstrate that tri-modal alignment via Haiku enables integrative analysis of spatial biology, bridging molecular measurements with clinical context for biological exploration.

</details>


### [71] [A Review of the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve and a Proof About the Area Beneath It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00926)
*Steven Redolfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了ROC曲线下面积（AUC）的概率解释，证明了在假设不满足时的误差界限，并综述了ROC曲线相关文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分类器的ROC曲线被广泛用于性能评估，其曲线下面积常被认为具有概率解释，即分类器将随机正例排在随机负例之上的概率，但该说法缺乏严格的形式化证明和误差分析。

Method: 对AUC的概率解释进行严格的形式化证明，推导当假设条件不满足时该解释偏离真实值的界限，并回顾ROC曲线相关研究文献。

Result: 成功形式化了AUC的概率解释，给出了假设不满足时的误差界限，提供了ROC曲线研究的小型文献综述。

Conclusion: ROC曲线下面积的概率解释可以得到严格的形式化支持，即使在假设不完全满足的情况下，也可以量化其误差范围，这为AUC作为分类器性能指标提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve of a binary classifier has often been utilized to measure the performance of the classifier. The area beneath this curve is used in particular because of its quoted probabilistic interpretation as being equal to the probability that the classifier will rank a random positive observation above a random negative observation. This paper formalizes this claim, produces a bound on how far away from the truth it is if a hypothesis is not met, and gives a small literature review of the ROC curve.

</details>


### [72] [PhaseNet++: Phase-Aware Frequency-Domain Anomaly Detection for Industrial Control Systems via Phase Coherence Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00929)
*Raviteja Bommireddy,Varshith Bandaru,Lohith Pakala,Pradeep Kumar B*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhaseNet++，首次系统性地利用相位信息进行工控系统时序异常检测，在SWaT数据集上取得了具有竞争力的性能，证明了相位作为一种互补检测模态的有效性和轻量性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工控系统多变量时序异常检测方法（如图神经网络、Transformer）主要基于时域原始幅值进行建模，但忽略了时频变换产生的相位谱。作者认为相位信息构成了ICS异常检测的一种互补且被忽视的检测模态，值得探索其在异常检测中的价值。

Method: 提出PhaseNet++频率域自编码器：1) 对滑动传感器窗口进行短时傅里叶变换(STFT)，同时保留幅度和相位谱；2) 受神经科学中相位锁定值启发，设计相位一致性指数(PCI)将跨频率仓的成对相位一致性总结为连续邻接矩阵；3) 使用PCI矩阵引导图注意力网络，在相位同步的传感器间优先传播信息；4) 采用传感器token Transformer编码器捕获系统级结构；5) 双头解码器通过循环和一致性感知目标联合重建幅度和相位。

Result: 在Secure Water Treatment (SWaT)基准数据集上，PhaseNet++实现了90.98%的F1分数、95.66%的ROC-AUC和91.51%的平均精度。消融实验表明相位感知前端和PCI图模块仅增加264,816个参数，证明了相位归纳偏置的轻量性。虽然在不同的评估协议下，绝对F1分数在所有近期的原始值方法中排名第二，但这是首个系统性的ICS相位域异常检测研究。

Conclusion: PhaseNet++成功证明了相位信息作为工控系统异常检测互补模态的有效性。该工作填补了相位域异常检测的研究空白，为工控系统安全防护提供了新的技术路径，同时证明了相位归纳偏置具有轻量化的特点，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection in ICS has attracted growing attention due to the increasing threat of cyber-physical attacks on critical infrastructure. State-of-the-art methods model inter-sensor relationships from raw time-domain amplitude values, using graph neural networks, Transformers. However, these methods discard the phase spectrum produced by time frequency transformations, We argue that phase information constitutes a complementary and previously overlooked detection modality for ICS anomaly detection. We present PhaseNet++, a frequency-domain autoencoder that operates on the Short-Time Fourier Transform (STFT) of sliding sensor windows, retaining both magnitude and phase spectra. A Phase Coherence Index (PCI), inspired by the Phase Locking Value from neuroscience, summarizes pairwise phase consistency across frequency bins into a continuous adjacency matrix. This matrix guides a graph attention network that propagates information preferentially among phase-synchronized sensors. A sensor-token Transformer encoder captures system-wide structure, and a dual-head decoder reconstructs magnitude and phase jointly via circular and coherence-aware objectives. Evaluated on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, PhaseNet++ achieves an F1-score of 90.98%, ROC-AUC of 95.66%, and average precision of 91.51%. Ablation studies show that the phase-aware front-end and PCI graph module together add only 264,816 parameters, demonstrating that the phase inductive bias is lightweight. While the absolute F1-score is second best than that of all recent raw-value methods evaluated under different protocols, we position this work as the first systematic study of phase-domain anomaly detection for ICS.

</details>


### [73] [Hierarchical Federated Learning for Networked AI: From Communication Saving to Architecture-Aware Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00931)
*Seyed Mohammad Azimi-Abarghouyi,Mehdi Bennis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将分层联邦学习（HFL）从单纯的通信节省协议重新定义为一种架构感知的网络化AI设计框架，围绕架构参数、分层优化分解和分层通信实现三个耦合设计轴展开，强调收敛性依赖于架构选择，并以大规模无线边缘智能为例提供了设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层联邦学习研究主要将其视为一种节省通信资源的可扩展性机制，忽视了其在多层级真实网络环境中作为分布式优化组织方式的潜力。作者旨在推动HFL超越通信协议的局限，建立一种面向网络化AI系统的架构感知设计方法论，以更好地适应异构通信环境和多层级网络结构。

Method: 提出围绕三个耦合设计轴的HFL框架：（1）架构参数轴——通过层级深度、层间不对称性和分层连接性确定学习的协调几何结构；（2）分层优化分解轴——将全局联邦学习目标跨层分解，探索模块化多层优化方法；（3）分层通信实现轴——在异构通信机制下物理实现分布式优化，从干扰受限的低层到可靠的高层。以大规模无线边缘智能为典型案例进行框架验证，并对扁平FL、两层HFL和深层HFL进行比较分析，构建面向不同场景的设计图谱。

Result: 建立了HFL作为架构感知设计框架的理论体系，揭示了收敛性具有架构依赖性：收敛行为直接由所选层级结构、分配的优化角色及连接它们的通信机制共同塑造。提供了面向无线边缘智能场景的对比分析和设计图谱，展示了不同架构参数和优化策略的适用范围。

Conclusion: 分层联邦学习应被视为一种面向网络化AI系统的实用设计方法论，而非仅是通信节省协议。通过架构参数、优化分解和通信实现的协同设计，HFL能够为未来异构多层级网络中的分布式学习系统提供系统性指导，成为设计下一代网络化AI的重要工具。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is fundamentally a distributed optimization problem executed by communicating agents with local data, local computation, and partial system visibility. Once FL is viewed through that lens, hierarchy is not merely a scalability mechanism. It becomes the natural place to rethink how distributed optimization should be organized over real multi-tier networks. This article argues that hierarchical federated learning (HFL) should move beyond its common framing as a communication-saving protocol and instead be viewed as an architecture-aware design framework for networked AI. The framework is organized around three coupled design axes: architectural parameters, layer-wise optimization decomposition, and layer-wise communication realization. The first axis determines the coordination geometry of learning through hierarchy depth, layer asymmetry, and layered connectivity. The second determines how the global FL objective is decomposed across layers and highlights modular multi-layer optimization as a major opportunity beyond one dominant method everywhere. The third determines how the distributed optimization is physically realized under heterogeneous communication regimes, from interference-limited lower tiers to reliable upper tiers. A central message is that, in HFL, convergence becomes architecture-dependent: it is directly shaped by the chosen hierarchy, the assigned optimization roles, and the communication mechanisms that connect them. We develop this viewpoint using large-scale wireless edge intelligence as a flagship networked AI setting, then provide a comparative perspective on flat FL, two-tier HFL, and deep HFL together with a regime-oriented design map. The resulting perspective positions HFL as a practical methodology for designing future networked AI systems.

</details>


### [74] [CGM-JEPA: Learning Consistent Continuous Glucose Monitor Representations via Predictive Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00933)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Zechen Li,Flora Salim,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGM-JEPA自监督预训练框架，通过预测掩码潜在表示而非原始值，学习跨模态迁移的抽象表示，解决CGM在人群规模部署中的视图不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续血糖监测（CGM）可检测早期代谢亚表型，但在人群规模部署中面临两大耦合问题：同一生理状态通过多视图呈现（CGM时间序列、静脉OGTT、Glucodensity摘要），单视图表示在模态或设置转换时无法迁移；基线方法在这些转换中表现不一致。

Method: 提出CGM-JEPA自监督预训练框架，预测掩码潜在表示而非原始值，产生跨模态迁移的抽象表示；扩展版X-CGM-JEPA增加掩码Glucodensity跨视图目标以补充分布信息。在228名受试者约389k未标记CGM读数上预训练。

Result: 在两个临床队列（N=27和N=17）的三种场景（队列泛化、静脉到CGM迁移、家庭CGM）评估中，X-CGM-JEPA在两个终点的AUROC排名均为第一或第二，无基线能达到此效果。队列泛化超越最强基线+6.5pp，静脉到CGM迁移提升+3.6pp（p<0.001）。模态转换下保持平均AUROC同时缩小弱势亚组差距（种族AUROC差距缩小25-54%）；稀疏域内静脉数据上分布视图提升标签感知聚类（ARI+39%，NMI+40%）。

Conclusion: CGM-JEPA通过学习抽象的潜在表示成功解决了CGM部署中的跨模态迁移问题，在多种场景下表现稳健，特别是在模态转换和弱势亚组中表现优异，为CGM人群规模部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM) can detect early metabolic subphenotypes (insulin resistance, IR; $β$-cell dysfunction), but population-scale deployment faces two coupled problems. First, the same physiological state appears through multiple views (CGM time series, venous OGTT, Glucodensity summaries), so single-view representations fail to transfer when deployment shifts the modality or setting. Second, baselines perform inconsistently across these shifts. Both problems point to one remedy: representations that abstract away from any single view to capture higher-level temporal and distributional structure. We propose CGM-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework which predicts masked latent representations rather than raw values, yielding abstraction that transfers across modalities. X-CGM-JEPA adds a masked Glucodensity cross-view objective for complementary distributional information. We pretrain on $\sim$389k unlabeled CGM readings from 228 subjects and evaluate on two clinical cohorts ($N=27$ and $N=17$ public-release subsets) across three regimes (cohort generalization, venous-to-CGM transfer, home CGM) under 20-iteration $\times$ 2-fold cross-validation. X-CGM-JEPA ranks first or second on AUROC for both endpoints across all three regimes while no baseline does, exceeding the strongest baseline by up to $+6.5$ pp in cohort generalization and $+3.6$ pp in venous-to-CGM transfer (paired Wilcoxon, $p<0.001$). Under modality shift, it matches mean AUROC while redistributing toward weaker subgroups (ethnicity AUROC gap shrinks 25-54%); on sparse in-domain venous data, the distributional view lifts label-aware clustering (ARI $+39\%$, NMI $+40\%$). Code and weights: https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/CGM-JEPA

</details>


### [75] [Structured Analytic Coherent Point Drift for Non-Rigid Point Set Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00934)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Analytic-CPD方法，用结构化解析映射替代核位移场，实现更高效准确的非刚性点集配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CPD方法使用基于点的核位移场，参数数量随移动点数增加而增长，导致计算效率低下且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 保留CPD的后验对应层，将后验概率通过重心恒等式转换为加权软目标点，用截断多元泰勒映射表示变形，引入阶次延续策略逐步激活高阶模式。

Result: 在二维解析变形和三维平滑非解析变形实验中，Analytic-CPD相比标准CPD实现了更低的最终误差和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CPD风格概率对应与结构化解析映射相结合，为基于核的非刚性配准提供了紧凑且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Analytic-CPD, a structured analytic variant of coherent point drift for non-rigid point set registration. The method retains the CPD posterior correspondence layer, but replaces the point-indexed Gaussian-kernel displacement-field M-step with a finite-dimensional structured analytic mapping estimator. Posterior probabilities from the Gaussian mixture model are condensed through a barycentric identity into weighted soft target points, converting the CPD pairwise soft-correspondence objective into a weighted analytic fitting problem. The deformation is represented by a truncated multivariate Taylor mapping of a vector-valued function, so the number of deformation parameters is controlled by the ambient dimension and the analytic order rather than by an M-by-M kernel system over the moving points. A degree-continuation strategy is further introduced to stabilize large-deformation registration by progressively activating higher-order analytic modes. Experiments on two-dimensional analytic deformations and three-dimensional smooth non-analytic deformations show that Analytic-CPD achieves lower final errors and faster convergence than standard CPD in representative large-deformation settings. The results suggest that CPD-style probabilistic correspondences and structured analytic mappings provide a compact and interpretable alternative to kernel-based non-rigid registration. Code is available at https://github.com/monge-ampere/Analytic-CPD.

</details>


### [76] [Watch Your Step: Information Injection in Diffusion Models via Shadow Timestep Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00935)
*An Huang,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE)”机制，发现扩散模型中的时间步嵌入可作为隐蔽信息注入的侧信道，既能用于攻击也能用于防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的研究主要集中在提升生成效率与输出质量，而作为关键组件的时间步嵌入在安全风险与可信溯源方面的探索严重不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，研究时间步嵌入空间未被充分利用的潜力。

Method: 作者提出Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE)机制，通过理论分析将时间步嵌入视为位置编码映射，并推导出互相关性评估指标来解释不重叠时间步区间的可分离性。研究发现不同时间步具有不同的表征能力，可通过调度器接口编码侧信道信息，实现信息的注入与提取。

Result: 实验与分析表明，扩散模型的时间步是一个强大的侧信道，能够携带特定信息。不重叠的时间步区间具有良好的可分离性，使得恶意信息注入与溯源成为可能，验证了STE机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 时间步嵌入空间是扩散模型中被忽视的安全维度。本研究揭示了其作为侧信道的潜力，为对抗性生成建模领域提供了新的研究方向，强调了在时间维度上理解与防御扩散模型安全威胁的重要性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the foundation of modern generative systems, with most research focusing primarily on improving generation efficiency and output quality. The timestep embedding component is a crucial part of the diffusion pipeline, which provides a temporal conditioning signal to the denoising network, enabling it to adapt its predictions across different noise levels throughout the process. Despite their potential to contain substantial information, timestep embeddings remain underexplored in current research, especially for security risks and reliable provenance. To fill this gap, we introduce Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE), a novel mechanism that investigates the underutilized temporal space for malicious information injection into diffusion models. In particular, when zooming in on the timestep embedding space, we find that different timesteps exhibit distinct representational capabilities that can encode side-channel information. Moreover, such encoded information can be utilized for attack and defense purposes through the scheduler interface. We present a theoretical analysis of timestep embeddings as position-encoding mappings and derive a mutual coherence evaluation that explains the separability of disjoint timestep intervals. Our findings reveal the diffusion model's timestep as a powerful side channel for carrying dedicated information, motivating new directions for adversarial generative modeling by understanding the temporal dimension.

</details>


### [77] [EventADL: Open-Box Anomaly Detection and Localization Framework for Events in Cloud-Based Service Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00936)
*Luan Pham,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds,Hui Guan,Daniel Kroening*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EventADL，首个基于事件数据的云系统异常检测与定位框架。通过对520个真实事件的分析，该框架学习事件语义模式和频率模式来识别异常，并利用干预图自动定位根因。实验表明其异常检测F1分数至少为90%，根因定位Top-3准确率达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异常检测与定位研究主要关注指标数据和日志数据，而事件数据尚未被探索。为了填补这一空白，作者对520个真实事件进行了系统分析，揭示了异常及其根因如何通过事件数据表现出来，从而设计出首个基于事件数据的ADL框架。

Method: EventADL框架包含三个阶段：离线训练、在线异常检测和根因定位。在训练阶段，首先学习事件语义模式(ESPs)来捕获系统实体间的正常交互，然后学习事件频率模式(EFPs)来捕获已知ESPs的正常频率。在在线检测阶段，任何偏离这些模式的事件流数据被识别为异常。在定位阶段，构建干预图来建模系统交互与检测到的异常之间的关系，实现自动根因定位。

Result: 在三个真实云服务系统和两个真实事件上的评估显示，EventADL优于现有方法。异常检测的F1分数至少达到90%，根因定位的Top-3准确率达到100%。该框架能够高效处理无标签数据，并产生可解释的异常及其对应的根因。

Conclusion: EventADL成功填补了事件数据异常检测与定位的研究空白，通过学习事件语义模式和频率模式实现了高效的异常检测，并通过干预图实现了准确的根因定位。该方法在真实云系统上表现出色，为云系统可靠性维护提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection and localization (ADL) is critical for maintaining reliability and availability in cloud systems. Recent ADL developments focus on metric and log data, leaving event data unexplored. To address this gap, we propose EventADL, the first open-box event-based ADL framework for cloud-based service systems. To motivate the design of our framework, we conduct a systematic analysis on 520 real-world incidents, and provide insights into how anomalies and their root causes manifest through event data. EventADL has three phases: offline training, online anomaly detection, and root cause localization. During the training phase, EventADL first learns Event Semantic Patterns (ESPs), which capture normal interactions between system entities using historical event data, and then learns Event Frequency Patterns (EFPs), which capture the normal frequency of known ESPs. In the online anomaly detection phase, any data in the event stream that deviates significantly from either pattern is identified as anomalous. For localization, EventADL constructs an Intervention Graph that models the relationships between recent system interactions and the detected anomalies for automatic root cause localization. The framework is designed to operate efficiently with unlabeled data and to produce interpretable anomalies with their corresponding root causes. Our evaluation on three real cloud service systems and two real-world incidents demonstrates that EventADL outperforms existing methods, achieving F1-scores of at least 90% for anomaly detection and 100% top-3 accuracy in root cause localization.

</details>


### [78] [Fusing Urban Structure and Semantics: A Conditional Diffusion Model for Cross-City OD Matrix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00938)
*Bin Chen,Zhuoya Meng,Fang Yang,Runkang Guo,Jingtao Ding,Yin Zhang,Chuan Ai,Zhengqiu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEDAN模型，通过融合城市语义属性与空间结构约束，实现跨城市的通勤OD矩阵生成，RMSE相比最优基线提升7.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通勤流建模对城市治理至关重要，但个体意图、地理约束与社会动态的耦合作用导致通勤模式存在显著异质性，使得现有生成模型难以在不同城市间泛化。

Method: 将城市建模为属性图，区域作为具有人口统计与兴趣点特征的节点，通勤流作为加权边。设计融合机制，利用图Transformer建模区域语义交互以捕捉潜在出行需求，同时将邻接矩阵与距离矩阵作为空间约束注入扩散生成过程，邻接矩阵引导注意力权重强化邻域交互，距离矩阵作为扩散条件捕捉空间邻近与出行阻抗。

Result: 在美国城市真实OD数据集上的实验表明，SEDAN在RMSE指标上相比最先进基线WEDAN提升7.38%，并在异构城市场景与多样化结构模式下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SEDAN通过融合城市语义与空间约束，能够生成行为合理且地理连贯的OD矩阵，为跨城市通勤OD矩阵生成提供了有效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of commuting flows is important for urban governance, traffic planning, and resource allocation. However, the combined influence of individual intentions, geographic constraints, and social dynamics leads to considerable heterogeneity in commuting patterns, making it difficult to develop generation models that generalize across cities. To address this issue, we propose SEDAN, a Structure-Enhanced Diffusion model conditioned on Attributed Nodes for generalizable OD matrix generation. SEDAN models a city as an attributed graph. Each region is treated as a node with demographic and point-of-interest features, and commuting flows are modeled as weighted edges. Adjacency and distance matrices are incorporated to characterize spatial structure. Based on this representation, we design a fusion mechanism within SEDAN to jointly model semantic information and spatial information. Regional semantic attributes are used to model latent travel demand through graph-transformer-based node interactions, while spatial structure is injected into the generation process as explicit constraints. The adjacency matrix guides attention weights to strengthen interactions between neighboring regions. Meanwhile, the distance matrix serves as a diffusion condition to capture spatial proximity and travel impedance. The fusion of urban semantics and spatial constraints enables SEDAN to generate OD matrices that are both behaviorally plausible and geographically coherent. Experiments on real-world OD datasets from U.S. cities show that SEDAN achieves a 7.38\% improvement in RMSE over the state-of-the-art baseline, WEDAN. It also remains robust across heterogeneous urban scenarios and varying structural patterns. Our work provides an effective and generalizable solution for commuting OD matrix generation. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SEDAN.

</details>


### [79] [From Flat Facts to Sharp Hallucinations: Detecting Stubborn Errors via Gradient Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00939)
*Yee Zhing Liew,Andrew Huey Ping Tan,Anwar P. P Abdul Majeed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EPGS方法，通过向输入嵌入添加高斯噪声并测量梯度幅度变化来检测大语言模型中的“顽固幻觉”（高置信度错误），实验表明其显著优于基于熵和表示的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的幻觉检测方法在处理“顽固幻觉”时失效，这类错误表现为模型对错误信息具有高度自信，现有方法难以识别。需要开发新的检测机制来区分稳定的知识表征和不稳定的记忆表征。

Method: 提出嵌入扰动梯度敏感性（EPGS）方法。基于假设：鲁棒事实位于平坦极小值，而顽固幻觉位于尖锐极小值。通过对输入嵌入添加高斯噪声，测量梯度幅度的变化作为Hessian谱的高效代理，从而检测尖锐度并区分稳定知识与不稳定记忆。

Result: 实验结果表明，EPGS在检测高置信度事实错误方面显著优于基于熵和基于表示的基线方法，能够提供鲁棒的检测信号。

Conclusion: EPGS为检测大语言模型中的顽固幻觉提供了一种有效的几何方法，通过梯度敏感性分析成功区分了稳定的知识表征和不稳定的记忆表征，为提高LLM可靠性提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: Traditional hallucination detection fails on "Stubborn Hallucinations" -- errors where LLMs are confidently wrong. We propose a geometric solution: Embedding-Perturbed Gradient Sensitivity (EPGS). We hypothesize that while robust facts reside in flat minima, stubborn hallucinations sit in sharp minima, supported by brittle memorization. EPGS detects this sharpness by perturbing input embeddings with Gaussian noise and measuring the resulting spike in gradient magnitude. This acts as an efficient proxy for the Hessian spectrum, differentiating stable knowledge from unstable memorization. Our experiments show that EPGS significantly outperforms entropy-based and representation-based baselines, providing a robust signal for detecting high-confidence factual errors.

</details>


### [80] [Interpretable experiential learning based on state history and global feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00940)
*Anton Kolonin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于状态历史和全局反馈的新型可解释经验学习模型，在Atari Breakout基准测试中达到了与部分神经网络方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决资源受限环境下的强化学习问题，并提高模型的可解释性，研究者希望开发一种不依赖复杂神经网络的学习方法。

Method: 提出了一种新的可解释经验学习模型，该模型通过状态集合之间的转换图来表示行为模型，转换过程包含效用和证据计数属性，并利用状态历史和全局反馈进行学习。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym Atari Breakout基准测试上进行了全面评估，结果显示该模型的性能与一些已知的基于神经网络的解决方案相当。

Conclusion: 该模型适用于资源受限环境下的强化学习问题，在保持可解释性的同时能够达到具有竞争力的性能水平。

Abstract: A new interpretable experiential learning model based on state history and global feedback is presented. It is capable of learning a behavioral model represented by a transition graph between sets of states, with transitions attributed with utility and evidence count. This model is expected to be suitable for solving reinforcement learning problem in resource-constrained environments. The model was thoroughly evaluated on the OpenAI Gym Atari Breakout benchmark, demonstrating performance comparable to some known neural network-based solutions.

</details>


### [81] [Divergence is Uncertainty: A Closed-Form Posterior Covariance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00941)
*Jiarui Xing,Song Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“散度-不确定性恒等式”，无需重训练或架构修改即可通过速度场的散度/雅可比精确计算流匹配模型的后验协方差，计算成本比集成方法低约4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配已成为生成建模的主流框架，但其样本的不确定性量化仍是未解难题。现有方法需重新训练辅助方差头、维护昂贵的集成模型，或在多步积分中传播近似协方差，在训练成本、推理成本或精度之间做权衡。作者旨在打破这些权衡，找到一种无需额外训练即可精确计算不确定性的方法。

Method: 作者从理论上证明，对于任意预训练的流匹配速度场，给定当前状态的干净数据后验协方差的迹可由速度场的散度以闭式精确计算（仅含已知的时间相关前置因子和可加常数）。该性质被称为“散度-不确定性恒等式”。在矩阵层面，该恒等式同样为闭式，仅依赖于速度雅可比。由于该恒等式是精确且后验的，可直接应用于任何预训练模型，无需重新训练或修改架构。对于MeanFlow等单步生成器，该恒等式可在单次前向传播中给出端到端的生成不确定性，避免了所有先前方法所需的多步方差传播。

Result: 在MNIST上的实验表明，所得逐像素不确定性图具有语义意义：不确定性集中在样本间变化最大的数字边界处；标量不确定性分数能够追踪实际预测误差。该方法在总计算量上比集成或蒙特卡洛dropout低约10,000倍。

Conclusion: 流匹配模型的不确定性可由速度场的散度/雅可比精确、闭式计算，无需任何额外训练或架构改动，极大降低了计算成本，同时保持理论精确性，为流匹配生成模型的不确定性量化提供了高效通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Flow matching has become a leading framework for generative modeling, but quantifying the uncertainty of its samples remains an open problem. Existing approaches retrain the model with auxiliary variance heads, maintain costly ensembles, or propagate approximate covariance through many integration steps, trading off training cost, inference cost, or accuracy. We show that none of these trade-offs is necessary. We prove that, for any pre-trained flow matching velocity field, the trace of the posterior covariance over the clean data given the current state equals, in closed form, the divergence of the velocity field, up to a known time-dependent prefactor and an additive constant. We call this the \emph{divergence-uncertainty identity} for flow matching. The matrix-level form of the identity is similarly closed-form, depending solely on the velocity Jacobian. Because the identity is exact and post-hoc, it is computable on any pre-trained flow matching model, with no retraining and no architectural modification. For one-step generators such as MeanFlow, the same identity yields the exact end-to-end generation uncertainty in a single forward pass, eliminating the multi-step variance propagation required by all prior methods. Experiments on MNIST confirm that the resulting per-pixel uncertainty maps are semantically meaningful, concentrating on digit boundaries where inter-sample variation is highest, and that the scalar uncertainty score tracks actual prediction error, all at roughly 10,000$\times$ less total compute than ensembling or Monte Carlo dropout.

</details>


### [82] [Graph Rewiring in GNNs to Mitigate Over-Squashing and Over-Smoothing: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00951)
*Hugo Attali,Nathalie Pernelle,Davide Buscaldi,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统回顾了图重连技术，旨在通过修改图拓扑结构来解决图神经网络中的过压缩和过平滑问题，从而改善信息传播并提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在处理图结构数据时面临两个关键挑战：过压缩导致远距离节点信息被过度压缩；过平滑使得节点表示在重复传播中变得难以区分。这两种现象均源于消息传递与输入拓扑之间的相互作用，最终导致信息流退化并限制了GNN的性能。

Method: 本综述全面回顾了最先进的图重连方法，深入分析了这些方法的理论基础、实际实现细节以及性能权衡，探讨了如何通过修改图拓扑结构来增强GNN中的信息传播。

Result: 提供了对现有图重连技术的系统性梳理，阐明了各类方法如何针对过压缩和过平滑问题设计拓扑修改策略，并分析了不同方法的性能取舍。

Conclusion: 图重连技术作为解决GNN信息传播瓶颈的有效途径，具有重要的研究价值和应用前景，本综述为理解和发展该领域方法提供了系统参考。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks are powerful models for learning from graph-structured data, yet their effectiveness is often limited by two critical challenges: over-squashing, where information from distant nodes is excessively compressed, and over-smoothing, where repeated propagation makes node representations indistinguishable. Both phenomena stem from the interaction between message passing and the input topology, ultimately degrading information flow and limiting the performance of GNNs. In this survey, we examine graph rewiring techniques, a class of methods designed to modify the graph topology to enhance information propagation in GNNs. We provide a comprehensive review of state-of-the-art rewiring approaches, delving into their theoretical underpinnings, practical implementations, and performance trade-offs.

</details>


### [83] [Robust volatility updates for Hierarchical Gaussian Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00966)
*Christoph Mathys,Nicolas Legrand,Peter Thestrup Waade,Nace Mikus,Lilian Aline Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入改进的二次近似方法，解决了HGF网络中波动性耦合节点更新时可能出现负后验精度的问题，使算法在整个参数空间内保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层高斯滤波(HGF)网络中，波动性耦合节点的原始更新方程在某些参数空间区域会导致负后验精度，这在逻辑上是不可能的，会导致算法错误终止。

Method: 提出了一种改进的二次近似方法来近似波动耦合节点的变分能量，核心思想是在变分能量的两个二次展开之间进行插值：一个在先验预测处，另一个在通过Lambert W函数闭式获得的第二模态处。

Result: 所得的更新方程在整个参数空间内具有鲁棒性，能够避免负后验精度的问题，并且即使在预测误差较大的情况下也能准确地跟踪变分后验。

Conclusion: 该修改确保了HGF网络的更新过程在整个参数空间内的数值稳定性和逻辑一致性，扩展了算法的适用范围和可靠性。

Abstract: Hierarchical Gaussian Filtering (HGF) networks allow for efficient updating of posterior distributions (beliefs) about hidden states of an agent's environment. HGF parent nodes can target the mean or variance of their children. New information entering at input nodes leads to a cascade of belief updates across the network according to one-step update equations for each node's mean and precision (inverse variance). However, the original form of the update equations for variance-targeting parents(volatility coupling) can in some regions of parameter space lead to negative posterior precision, a logical impossibility which causes the updating algorithm to terminate with an error. In this report, we introduce a modified quadratic approximation to the variational energy of volatility-coupled nodes that avoids negative posterior precision. The key idea is to interpolate between two quadratic expansions of the variational energy: one at the prior prediction and one at a second mode whose location is obtained in closed form via the Lambert W function. The resulting update equations are robust across the entire parameter space and faithfully track the variational posterior even for large prediction errors.

</details>


### [84] [Physiology-Aware Masked Cross-Modal Reconstruction for Biosignal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00973)
*Hao Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Cyrus Tanade,Keum San Chun,Juhyeon Lee,Migyeong Gwak,Megha Thukral,Justin Sung,Eugene Hwang,Mehrab Bin Morshed,Li Zhu,Viswam Nathan,Md Mahbubur Rahman,Subramaniam Venkatraman,Sharanya Arcot Desai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出xMAE框架，利用具有时序关系的生物信号（如ECG与PPG）进行跨模态掩码重建预训练，在19个下游任务中的15个上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法将不同体表位置的生物信号视为可互换的视图，忽略了连接它们的定向时间动态。典型例子是ECG（捕获心脏电激活）与PPG（记录经血管动力学延迟后的外周脉搏）之间的因果关系。

Method: 引入xMAE生物信号预训练框架，利用时序有序生物信号间的掩码跨模态重建作为训练时约束，促使学习到的表示中编码具有生理意义的时间结构。

Result: 在19个下游任务（包括心血管结果预测、异常实验室检测、睡眠分期、人口统计推断）中的15个上优于单模态和多模态基线，并能跨设备、身体位置和采集设置泛化。进一步分析表明ECG-PPG时序结构反映在学习到的PPG表示中。

Conclusion: 当信号观测共享底层过程的不同阶段时，将时间结构纳入多模态预训练是有效的，xMAE为此提供了成功范例。

Abstract: Biosignals acquired from different locations on the body often provide temporally ordered views of the same underlying physiological process. However, most existing self supervised learning methods treat these signals as interchangeable views, overlooking the directional temporal dynamics that link them. A canonical example is the relationship between electrocardiography (ECG), which captures the electrical activation initiating each heartbeat, and photoplethysmography (PPG), which records the resulting peripheral pulse delayed by vascular dynamics. To capture this structured relationship, we introduce xMAE, a biosignal pretraining framework that leverages masked cross modal reconstruction across temporally ordered biosignals as a training time constraint to encourage physiologically meaningful timing structure in the learned representations. We show that pretraining with xMAE yields representations that outperform both unimodal and multimodal baselines on 15 of 19 downstream tasks, including cardiovascular outcome prediction, abnormal laboratory test detection, sleep staging, and demographic inference, while generalizing across devices, body locations, and acquisition settings. Further analysis suggests that the ECG PPG timing structure is reflected in the learned PPG representations. More broadly, xMAE demonstrates the effectiveness of incorporating temporal structure into multimodal pretraining when signals observe different stages of a shared underlying process. Code is available at https://github.com/hzhou3/xMAE.

</details>


### [85] [Continual Learning of Feedback-based Molecular Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01020)
*Siddhant Setia,Junichi Suzuki,Tadashi Nakano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于持续学习（CL）的性能估计方法，用于反馈型分子通信协议的序贯仿真实验，通过在损失函数中定制正则化和回放策略，使标准神经网络能够增量学习一系列未曾经历的估计任务且不遗忘过去知识，从而提高了估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分子通信协议的仿真研究中，随着实验设置不断变化，传统的估计方法在处理连续到达的仿真数据时存在性能局限。利用持续学习算法使模型能够连续适应新的实验条件、提升估计精度成为一个重要的研究方向。

Method: 提出基于持续学习的性能估计器，以标准神经网络为基础架构，通过在损失函数中引入正则化和回放策略的定制化设计，实现序贯仿真实验场景下的增量学习。模型在处理新的估计任务时，不会遗忘以往学习到的知识。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的估计器能够有效学习持续的仿真数据流，在多种计算成本下显著提升了估计精度，优于基准神经网络方法。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了持续学习在分子通信领域的应用潜力，为分子通信协议的性能估计提供了新的思路和方法，并验证了其有效性与实用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes and evaluates a new performance estimation method that leverages continual learning (CL) algorithms to carry out sequential simulation experiments for a feedback-based molecular communication protocol. As the protocol is sequentially examined in various experimental settings, the proposed CL-based performance estimators incrementally learn a series of unexperienced estimation tasks without compromising those that have been learned in the past. They are designed to work on a standard neural network architecture by customizing regularization and replay strategies in the loss function. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed estimators can effectively learn on a continuous stream of simulation results and enhance the baseline neural network by improving estimation accuracy at a variety of computational costs. This paper's contribution is to establish the implications of CL in the field of molecular communication.

</details>


### [86] [Finite-Sample Analysis of Elimination in Active Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01039)
*Ziyuan Lin,Hoang Ngoc Nguyen,Jie Xu,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强消除机制的Track-and-Stop算法，通过逐步剪枝假设集合并重新分配感知资源，在固定置信度主动假设测试中减少了有限样本下的期望停止时间，同时引入激进参数在快速消除与置信度保证之间进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定置信度有限样本主动假设测试问题出现在许多安全关键应用中，现有方法在有限样本下的停止时间仍有优化空间，需要研究假设消除对停止时间的影响以提升效率。

Method: 提出消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法，为每个候选假设维护特定活跃对手集合并逐步剪枝，将感知努力重新分配给存活的备选假设；引入激进参数调节快速消除与较弱置信度保证之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出期望停止时间的非渐近上界；消除带来的有限样本增益体现在非主导项的尺度上，源于缩减假设集上更紧的跟踪和集中常数；合成高斯实例上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 假设消除策略能有效减少有限样本下的停止时间，增益来自非主导项的改进；激进参数的引入为算法提供了灵活的速度-置信度权衡机制，适用于安全关键场景。

Abstract: A fixed-confidence, finite-sample problem of active hypothesis testing arises in many safety-critical applications. Situated in the context of sequential hypothesis testing, this paper studies the effect of hypothesis elimination on the stopping time. We introduce an elimination-augmented Track-and-Stop algorithm, in which champion-specific active-opponent sets are progressively pruned, and sensing effort is reallocated toward the surviving alternatives. Our analysis derives a non-asymptotic upper bound on the expected stopping time. The gain in finite-sample from elimination appears on the scale of the non-leading term, resulting from tighter tracking and concentration constants on the reduced hypothesis set. Furthermore, we introduce an aggressiveness parameter to modulate the trade-off between faster elimination and weaker confidence guarantee. An experimental study on synthetic Gaussian instances confirms the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [87] [Learning in the Fisher Subspace: A Guided Initialization for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01046)
*Zhi-Quan Feng,Ying-Jia Lin,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Fisher信息的LoRA初始化方法，利用下游数据分布指导低秩子空间选择，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化策略仅依赖预训练权重的内在属性（如权重大小），忽视了模型与下游数据分布的交互，导致可能将容量分配给与任务无关的方向，从而严重影响下游性能。

Method: 将LoRA初始化形式化为在目标数据分布下识别参数空间中方向影响程度的问题；提出了Fisher引导框架，利用下游数据诱导的曲率信息来表征参数扰动如何影响模型预测，从而建立任务依赖的子空间选择准则。

Result: 在多种任务和模态上的实验表明，数据感知的初始化方法持续且显著地优于现有方法，提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 数据感知的敏感度（而非仅基于权重幅度的准则）应主导适配子空间的选择；所提方法为LoRA初始化提供了原则性的、任务依赖的解决方案。

Abstract: LoRA adapts large language models (LLMs) by restricting updates to low-rank subspaces of pre-trained weights. While this substantially reduces training cost, the effectiveness of adaptation critically depends on which subspace is chosen at initialization: a poor initialization that allocates capacity to task-irrelevant directions can severely hinder downstream performance. Existing initialization strategies primarily rely on the intrinsic properties of pre-trained weights, implicitly assuming that weight geometry alone reflects task relevance. However, such criteria overlook how the model interacts with the downstream data distribution. In this work, we formulate LoRA initialization as identifying the degree of impact of directions in parameter space under the target data distribution. We argue that data-aware sensitivity, rather than weight-only magnitude, should govern the choice of adaptation subspaces. Building on this perspective, we propose a Fisher-guided framework that leverages curvature information induced by downstream data to characterize how parameter perturbations influence model predictions. This perspective yields a principled, task-dependent criterion for selecting LoRA directions that better align adaptation with the target objective. Empirical results across diverse tasks and modalities demonstrate that data-aware initialization consistently and significantly improves downstream performance over existing approaches.

</details>


### [88] [LEAP: Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01058)
*Shashank Kapadia,Deep Naryan Mishra,Sujal Reddy Alugubelli,Haoan Wang,Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Rishi Bhatia,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了层级对齐蒸馏与基于收敛的早退机制之间存在系统性不兼容，并提出LEAP方法成功解决了这一问题，实现了显著的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种提升Transformer推理效率的主流范式——层级对齐蒸馏和基于收敛的早退机制——在标准部署条件下存在系统性不兼容：对齐中间层表示的蒸馏目标会抑制早退机制所依赖的表示收敛特性，导致早退机制在蒸馏模型上失效。

Method: 提出LEAP（Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining）辅助训练目标，无需架构修改，在标准蒸馏基础上增加单一约束，确保中间层能够近似最终层的表示，从而协调蒸馏与早退机制的兼容性。

Result: LEAP-MiniLM在θ=0.95时实现1.61倍实测墙钟加速（batch=1，NVIDIA L4），91.9%样本在第7层退出，理论层数减少1.80倍，而标准蒸馏模型无法获得有效加速；在句子相似性任务（STS-B: 0.760±0.006）和检索基准（BEIR）上验证有效性。

Conclusion: LEAP成功解决了蒸馏与早退机制的不兼容问题，在不修改架构的前提下实现了显著的推理加速，为实际部署提供了包括延迟测量、决策阈值和部署准则在内的操作性指导。

Abstract: Layer-aligned distillation and convergence-based early exit represent two predominant computational efficiency paradigms for transformer inference; yet we establish that they exhibit systematic incompatibility under standard deployment conditions for convergence-based early exit. Distillation objectives that align intermediate student layers to teacher representations suppress the representational convergence that early-exit mechanisms exploit, rendering such mechanisms ineffective on distilled models. We introduce LEAP (Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining), an auxiliary training objective that reconciles this incompatibility. LEAP requires no architectural modifications; it augments standard distillation with a single constraint ensuring intermediate layers approximate final-layer representations. LEAP-MiniLM achieves 1.61$\times$ measured wall-clock speedup (batch=1, NVIDIA L4) at $θ$=0.95, with 91.9% of samples exiting by layer 7 and 1.80$\times$ theoretical layer reduction, where standard distilled models achieve zero effective speedup. We validate across sentence similarity (STS-B: 0.760 $\pm$ 0.006) and retrieval benchmarks (BEIR), providing operational guidance including latency measurements, decision thresholds, and deployment criteria.

</details>


### [89] [DBLP: Phase-Aware Bounded-Loss Transport for Burst-Resilient Distributed ML Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01989)
*Zechen Ma,Zixi Qu,Jinyan Yi,David Lin,Yashar Ganjali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DBLP传输协议，通过将模型级容差特性融入梯度通信，动态调整不同训练阶段的梯度丢失容忍度，有效解决了分布式ML训练中瞬态网络拥塞导致的尾延迟问题，平均减少24.4%的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式机器学习训练在应用层优化效率，但忽视了网络层的瞬态拥塞事件（微突发），导致严重的尾延迟和训练时间波动，降低服务质量。现有网络优化对所有梯度同等对待，未能将模型训练洞察充分整合到通信协议设计中。

Method: 提出动态有界丢失协议(DBLP)，这是一种具有突发恢复能力、训练阶段感知和硬件无关特性的传输协议。通过动态调整不同训练阶段的梯度丢失容忍度，将模型级容差特性融入梯度通信中，从而在高丢失事件（微突发）期间缓解尾延迟崩溃。

Result: 与最先进的基线方案相比，DBLP在保持相当测试精度的同时能容忍显著更高的丢失率，平均减少24.4%的端到端训练时间（最高减少33.9%）。在微突发事件中，DBLP实现高达5.88倍的单轮通信延迟加速，有效防止突发引起的尾延迟尖峰并维持稳定的训练性能。

Conclusion: DBLP成功将模型训练洞察整合到网络传输协议设计中，证明了在分布式ML训练中考虑训练阶段特性和梯度重要性的价值，为解决网络拥塞对训练性能的影响提供了有效方案，同时保持模型质量并显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning (ML) training has become a necessity with the prevalence of billion to trillion-parameter-scale models. While prior work has improved training efficiency from the ML perspective at the application layer, it often fails to address transient congestion events at the network layer that introduce severe tail latency and training-time variability, thereby undermining the quality of service (QoS) of distributed ML training systems. Existing network optimizations treat all gradients equally and thus fail to integrate sufficient model-training insights into communication protocol design.
  In this paper, we present Dynamic Bounded-Loss Protocol (DBLP), a burst-resilient, training-phase-aware, and hardware-agnostic transport protocol that incorporates model-level tolerance properties into gradient communication. By dynamically adjusting gradient loss tolerance across training phases, DBLP reduces overall training time and mitigates tail-latency collapse during transient high-loss events (i.e., microbursts).
  Compared to the current state-of-the-art solution (baseline), DBLP tolerates significantly higher loss while achieving comparable test accuracy, and reduces end-to-end training time by an average of 24.4% and a maximum of 33.9%. At microburst events, DBLP achieves up to 5.88x single-round communication latency speedups over the baseline, preventing burst-induced tail-latency spikes and maintaining stable training performance.

</details>


### [90] [GEODE: Angle-Adaptive OOD Detection with Universal Scorer Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01063)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了离群值暴露（OE）的本质是边界校准而非OOD覆盖，据此提出GEODE方法，通过角度自适应范数损失在合成数据上复现这一校准效果，在所有七种标准评分器上均表现优异，避免了现有方法在特定评分器上的灾难性失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Outlier Exposure (OE)方法虽然在标准基准测试中表现优异，但存在评分器依赖的权衡问题（如MSP强但KNN弱），且需要精心策划的辅助数据。作者旨在理解OE为何有效，并设计一种不依赖辅助数据、在所有评分器上都能稳健工作的OOD检测方法。

Method: 作者首先通过几何分析揭示OE的有效性源于其特征位于与真实近域OOD数据相同的几何轨迹上，且边界相邻的四分位数驱动了OE几乎所有的增益，本质是边界校准而非OOD覆盖。基于神经坍塌理论的四个定理，作者提出GEODE (GEOmetry-preserving DEtection)，采用角度自适应范数损失，使目标值随样本与最近类均值的余弦相似度进行缩放，从而在边界结构重要的地方保持特征几何结构。

Result: GEODE在CIFAR-10上的近域OOD检测AUROC达到89.0-92.3，远域OOD达到93.05，在所有七种标准评分器上均无灾难性失效。与OE结合时，在CIFAR-10上达到95.0 MSP / 94.8 KNN，在CIFAR-100上每个评分器都超越OE。在WRN-28-10上的增益为+4.5 Energy（3个随机种子）。相比之下，将OOD推入分类器零空间的方法（如PFS，KNN AUROC仅14.38，比随机更差）表现不佳。

Conclusion: GEODE成功复制了OE的边界校准效果，同时克服了其评分器依赖性和辅助数据需求。由于部署时OOD检测机制未知，GEODE在所有评分器上的稳健性能使其更具实用价值。该方法通过保持距离评分器依赖的几何结构，避免了现有方法的缺陷，为OOD检测提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Outlier Exposure (OE) is among the strongest training-based OOD detectors on standard benchmarks but exhibits scorer-dependent tradeoffs (e.g., strong on MSP, weak on KNN) and requires curated auxiliary data. We show why OE works: its features sit at the same geometric locus as real near-OOD data, with the boundary-adjacent quartile driving nearly all of OE's gain. OE is boundary calibration, not OOD coverage. GEODE (GEOmetry-preserving DEtection) replicates this calibration synthetically through an angle-adaptive norm loss in which targets scale per-sample with cosine similarity to the nearest class mean, preserving feature geometry where boundary structure matters. Four theorems grounded in neural collapse justify the design. GEODE works across all seven standard scorers on CIFAR-10 (near-OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3, far-OOD reaching 93.05; no catastrophic failure on any scorer). Since the OOD regime is unknown at deployment, this is the test that matters. GEODE outperforms vanilla CE at matched epoch counts. Combined with OE, GEODE reaches 95.0 MSP / 94.8 KNN on CIFAR-10 and beats OE on every scorer on CIFAR-100. The gains hold on WRN-28-10 (+4.5 Energy, 3 seeds). Unlike methods that push OOD into the classifier null space (e.g., PFS, 14.38 KNN AUROC, worse than random), GEODE's adaptive target preserves the geometry that distance-based scorers depend on.

</details>


### [91] [Federated Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Mobile Crowdsensing under Incomplete Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02705)
*Sumedh J. Dongare,Patrick Weber,Andrea Ortiz,Walid Saad,Oliver Hinz,Anja Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FDRL-PPO的全去中心化联邦深度强化学习算法，用于解决移动群智感知中移动单元在信息不完备情况下的任务参与策略优化问题。该方法通过联邦学习实现移动单元间的协作学习，在保护隐私的同时有效应对能量收集导致的动态可用性问题。实验表明FDRL-PPO在任务完成率、公平性、能耗和冲突等方面均优于基准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动群智感知系统具有动态性特征，任务需求、移动单元可用性及其资源随时间变化。移动单元需要在不完备信息条件下制定高效的任务参与策略以最大化收益，而平台则追求最大化任务完成数量。由于现实中无法获得完美的非因果系统信息，传统最优策略难以适用，因此需要在信息不完备场景下找到有效的任务参与策略。

Method: 提出了一种全去中心化联邦深度强化学习算法FDRL-PPO。该方法使每个移动单元能够基于自身经验、可用资源和偏好学习任务参与策略，无需依赖完美的非因果系统信息。采用联邦学习框架，移动单元通过交换学习到的模型而非原始私有数据进行协作，有效应对能量收集导致的可用性变化和学习经验碎片化问题，在保护隐私的同时实现集体优化。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上进行了全面评估，FDRL-PPO在任务完成率、任务完成公平性、能量消耗和冲突提案数量等指标上均持续优于基准算法。该方法展现了良好的可扩展性、鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: FDRL-PPO成功解决了移动群智感知中信息不完备条件下的任务参与策略优化问题，通过联邦深度强化学习实现了去中心化的高效决策，为动态环境下的群智感知系统提供了有效的解决方案，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Mobile crowdsensing (MCS) is a distributed sensing architecture that utilizes existing sensors on mobile units (MUs) to perform sensing tasks. A mobile crowdsensing platform (MCSP) publishes the sensing tasks and the MUs decide whether to participate in exchange for money. The MCS system is dynamic: the task requirements, the MUs' availability, and their available resources change over time. The MUs aim to find an efficient task participation strategy to maximize their income while the MCSP focuses on maximizing the number of completed tasks. As optimal strategies require perfect non-causal information about the MCS system, which is unavailable in realistic scenarios, the main challenge is to find an efficient task participation strategy for the MUs under incomplete information. To this end, a novel fully decentralized federated deep reinforcement learning algorithm, FDRL-PPO, is proposed. FDRL-PPO enables every MU to learn its own task participation strategy based on its experiences, available resources, and preferences, without relying on perfect non-causal information about the MCS system. To replenish their batteries, the MUs rely on energy harvesting. As a result, their available energy varies over time, leading to varying availability and fragmented learning experiences. To mitigate these challenges, the proposed approach leverages federated learning, enabling MUs to collaboratively improve their models without sharing private raw data like their own experiences. By exchanging only learned models, MUs collectively compensate for individual limitations, and find more scalable, robust, and efficient task participation strategies. Comprehensive evaluations on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that FDRL-PPO consistently outperforms benchmark algorithms in terms of task completion ratio, fairness in task completion, energy consumption, and number of conflicting proposals.

</details>


### [92] [A dimensional R2 regression metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01066)
*Jaesung Yoo,Stefan Lemke,Jian Zhong Guo,Kanaka Rajan,Adam Hantman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对传统R²分数在多维输入、信息隐藏和噪声敏感性方面的局限，提出了Dim-R2（Dimensional R² score），一种可处理任意维度数据、提供多维精度视角并降低噪声敏感性的扩展指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准R²分数作为回归任务评估指标存在三个关键局限：仅限于二维及以下输入；将评分压缩为单一标量，掩盖了丰富的预测精度模式；对低方差噪声通道敏感，可能产生难以解释的大幅负值。

Method: 提出Dimensional R² score（Dim-R2），作为R²的简单扩展，能够接受任意维度的输入数据，提供多维度的精度视角，并降低对噪声的敏感性。在合成正弦数据和三个多维回归数据集上验证了其优势。

Result: Dim-R2在合成正弦数据和三个多维回归数据集上展示了其优越性，能够提供可解释且灵活的度量指标，突出回归精度的模式，指导回归建模。

Conclusion: Dim-R2作为一种可解释且灵活的度量指标，克服了传统R²的主要局限，能够有效揭示回归精度中的模式，为回归建模提供指导。

Abstract: R2 score is the standard metric for evaluating regression tasks, offering a normalized magnitude-agnostic measure of accuracy that captures variance. However, R2 has three key limitations: it is limited to at most two dimensional inputs, it reduces the score to a single scalar that hides rich patterns of prediction accuracy, and it is sensitive to low-variance noise channels which can yield large, uninterpretable negative values. We introduce the Dimensional R2 score (Dim-R2), a simple extension of R2 that accepts data of arbitrary dimensionality, provides a multidimensional view of accuracy, and reduces sensitivity to noise. We demonstrate its advantages on both synthetic sinusoidal data and three multidimensional regression datasets. Dim-R2 offers an interpretable and flexible metric that highlights patterns in regression accuracy, guiding regression modeling.

</details>


### [93] [Deep Variational Inference Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01067)
*James Butterworth,Gevik Grigorian,Alejandro DiazDelaO*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DVISR，一种深度符号回归(DSR)的变分贝叶斯扩展方法，通过优化证据下界(ELBO)并扩展网络架构来输出常数分布，实现了对表达式树及其相关常数的后验推断，从而在符号回归中量化模型的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归能够发现显式、可解释的方程，贝叶斯方法可以通过候选表达式的概率分布来量化噪声和有限数据下的不确定性。然而，现有的深度符号回归(DSR)仅设计用于识别单一最佳拟合表达式，而非推断模型的后验分布，无法提供不确定性量化。

Method: 提出了深度变分推断符号回归(DVISR)方法，这是DSR的变分贝叶斯扩展。主要改进包括：(1)用证据下界(ELBO)的被积函数替换原始奖励函数；(2)扩展网络架构以输出表达式中常数的分布，从而实现对表达式树及其相关常数的后验推断。

Result: DVISR能够在简单设置下恢复真实的后验分布（包括有无常数标记的情况），并且研究展示了随着表达式空间大小增加时其性能的变化规律。

Conclusion: DVISR向着具有全符号模型不确定性的可扩展贝叶斯符号回归迈出了一步，为在符号回归中进行有效的不确定性量化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Symbolic regression discovers explicit, interpretable equations without assuming a functional form in advance. A Bayesian approach strengthens this through probability distributions over candidate expressions, thus quantifying uncertainty in the presence of noisy and limited data. Deep Symbolic Regression (DSR) uses a neural network to generate symbolic expressions, but it is designed to identify a single best-fitting expression rather than infer a posterior distribution over models. We introduce Deep Variational Inference Symbolic Regression (DVISR), a variational Bayesian extension of DSR. DVISR replaces the original reward with the integrand of the evidence lower bound. It also extends the network architecture to output distributions over constants within expressions, enabling posterior inference over both expression trees and their associated constants. We show that DVISR can recover the true posterior in simple settings, both with and without constant tokens, and we examine how its performance changes as the size of the expression space increases. These results position DVISR as a step toward scalable Bayesian symbolic regression with uncertainty over full symbolic models.

</details>


### [94] [Stochastic Sparse Attention for Memory-Bound Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01910)
*Kyle Lee,Corentin Delacour,Kevin Callahan-Coray,Kyle Jiang,Can Yaras,Samet Oymak,Tathagata Srimani,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SANTA方法，通过随机采样稀疏化KV缓存访问，将值阶段的乘累加替换为收集-加法操作，在保持精度的同时实现1.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码在长上下文中存在带宽瓶颈，因为每生成一个token都需要从KV缓存中读取所有$n_k$个键值向量，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 提出SANTA（随机加性无乘注意力）方法：1）从后softmax分布中采样$S \ll n_k$个索引，仅聚合这些值行；2）引入分层采样设计方差减少、GPU友好的变体；3）提出伯努利$qK^T$采样稀疏化分数阶段。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada上，相比FlashInfer和FlashDecoding实现1.5倍解码步注意力核加速，在32k token上下文中匹配基线精度。

Conclusion: 该方法与三元量化、低秩投影、KV缓存压缩等上游技术正交，为实现稀疏、无乘法器、能效高的推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding becomes bandwidth-limited at long contexts, as generating each token requires reading all $n_k$ key and value vectors from KV cache. We present Stochastic Additive No-mulT Attention (SANTA), a method that sparsifies value-cache access by sampling $S \ll n_k$ indices from the post-softmax distribution and aggregates only those value rows. This yields an unbiased estimator of the post-softmax value aggregation while replacing value-stage multiply-accumulates with gather-and-add. We introduce stratified sampling to design variance-reduced, GPU-friendly variants, demonstrating $1.5\times$ decode-step attention kernel speedup over FlashInfer and FlashDecoding on an NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada while matching baseline accuracy at 32k-token contexts. Finally, we propose Bernoulli $qK^\mathsf{T}$ sampling as a complementary technique to sparsify the score stage, reducing key-feature access through stochastic ternary queries. Both methods are orthogonal to upstream techniques such as ternary quantization, low-rank projections, and KV-cache compression. Together, they point toward sparse, multiplier-free, and energy-efficient inference. We open-source our kernels at: https://github.com/OPUSLab/SANTA.git

</details>


### [95] [Networked Information Aggregation for Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01082)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向无环图（DAG）上的网络化二分类问题。证明了在每$M$个连续智能体共同观测所有特征的条件下，深度为$D$的路径上的过剩损失上界为$O(M/\sqrt{D})$；同时给出了至少$\Omega(k/D)$的下界，揭示了网络深度是网络化逻辑回归信息聚合的根本瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Kearns等人，SODA 2026）已针对线性回归和平方损失证明了顺序分布式训练的信息聚合能力。然而，将这些保证扩展到分类任务面临非平凡挑战，因为原分析依赖于二次结构，无法直接迁移到二元交叉熵（BCE）和逻辑链接函数。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究网络化逻辑回归场景下顺序分布式训练是否同样能实现信息聚合。

Method: 研究有向无环图（DAG）上的顺序分布式二分类协议。每个智能体仅观测共享数据集的特征子集，按DAG顺序行动：接收父节点的预测列，将其与本地特征结合，通过最小化二元交叉熵（BCE）拟合逻辑预测器，然后将预测列转发给子节点。论文分析了这种顺序逻辑传递协议。

Result: 证明了两个主要结果：（i）在每$M$个连续智能体共同观测所有特征的条件下，深度为$D$的路径上的过剩损失上界为$O(M/\sqrt{D})$；（ii）构造了实例证明过剩损失至少为$\Omega(k/D)$，其中$k$为特征空间维度。上界和下界紧密匹配。

Conclusion: 网络深度是网络化逻辑回归中信息聚合的根本瓶颈。研究结果为理解分布式分类学习中的信息传播限制提供了理论基础，表明即使智能体按顺序传递预测信息，网络深度仍限制了系统相对于集中式训练的性能。

Abstract: We study networked binary classification on a directed acyclic graph (DAG) where each agent observes only a subset of the feature columns of a shared dataset. Agents act sequentially along the DAG: each receives prediction columns from its parents (if any), augments its local features with these columns, fits a logistic predictor by minimizing binary cross-entropy (BCE), and forwards its prediction column to its outgoing neighbors. We ask whether this sequential distributed training procedure achieves information aggregation, meaning that some agent attains small excess loss compared to the best logistic predictor trained with access to all feature columns.
  This question was studied for linear regression under squared loss by Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA 2026). Extending their guarantees to classification is nontrivial because their analysis relies on quadratic structure that does not directly transfer to BCE with a logistic link. We analyze the resulting sequential logit-passing protocol and prove: (i) an excess loss upper bound of $O(M/\sqrt{D})$ on depth-$D$ paths under the condition that every $M$ contiguous subsequence of $M$ agents collectively observe all features, and (ii) a close lower bound showing instances with excess loss of at least $Ω(k/D)$ where $k$ is the dimension of the feature space. Together, these results identify network depth as a fundamental bottleneck for information aggregation in networked logistic regression.

</details>


### [96] [SpecKV: Adaptive Speculative Decoding with Compression-Aware Gamma Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02888)
*Shikhar Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpecKV，一种轻量级自适应控制器，通过利用草稿模型的置信度和熵等信号动态选择推测长度γ，在LLM推理中相比固定γ=4基线实现56.0%的加速提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码系统普遍采用固定的推测长度γ（通常为4），但实证表明最优γ值因任务类型而异，且依赖于目标模型的压缩级别。现有方法缺乏对不同场景下最优γ的自适应调整机制。

Method: 提出SpecKV轻量级自适应控制器，通过从草稿模型中提取的信号（包括草稿模型置信度和熵）来选择每步的推测长度γ。使用小型MLP模型基于这些信号训练，以最大化每步预期接受令牌数。构建了包含4个任务类别、4个推测长度和3种压缩级别（FP16、INT8、 NF4）的5,112条步骤级记录数据集进行训练和验证。

Result: 发现最优γ值随压缩级别变化，草稿模型置信度和熵是接受率的强预测因子（相关系数约0.56）。SpecKV相比固定γ=4基线实现56.0%的性能提升，每次决策仅需0.34ms开销（<0.5%步骤时间），统计显著性p<0.001。

Conclusion: 自适应推测长度选择对推测解码性能至关重要，特别是在不同模型压缩级别下。SpecKV提供了一种低成本、高效的自适应解决方案，为推测解码系统的优化提供了新思路。研究开源了所有性能分析数据、训练模型和代码。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a small draft model to propose candidate tokens that a larger target model verifies. A critical hyperparameter in this process is the speculation length~$γ$, which determines how many tokens the draft model proposes per step. Nearly all existing systems use a fixed~$γ$ (typically~4), yet empirical evidence suggests that the optimal value varies across task types and, crucially, depends on the compression level applied to the target model. In this paper, we present \textbf{SpecKV}, a lightweight adaptive controller that selects~$γ$ per speculation step using signals extracted from the draft model itself. We profile speculative decoding across 4~task categories, 4~speculation lengths, and 3~compression levels (FP16, INT8, NF4), collecting 5,112 step-level records with per-step acceptance rates, draft entropy, and draft confidence. We demonstrate that the optimal~$γ$ shifts across compression regimes and that draft model confidence and entropy are strong predictors of acceptance rate (correlation~$\approx 0.56$). SpecKV uses a small MLP trained on these signals to maximize expected tokens per speculation step, achieving a 56.0\% improvement over the fixed-$γ$=4 baseline with only 0.34\,ms overhead per decision ($<$0.5\% of step time). The improvement is statistically significant ($p < 0.001$, paired bootstrap test). We release all profiling data, trained models, and notebooks as open-source artifacts.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Discriminators for Resampling in the Ensemble Gaussian Mixture Filter through a Normalizing Flow Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01089)
*Zain Jabbar,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种判别器引导的重采样方法（discriminator-informed resampling），通过使用正则化流学习得到的判别器来筛选候选粒子，有效解决了集成高斯混合滤波器（EnGMF）中重采样可能产生物理上不合理样本的问题，在低集成数场景下显著降低了滤波误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成高斯混合滤波器（EnGMF）在执行重采样步骤时，可能会生成物理上不真实的后验样本，这会导致后续产生毫无物理意义的预报结果。为了解决这一物理不合理性问题，提高滤波器在非线性滤波中的可靠性，需要改进其重采样过程。

Method: 引入了一种判别器引导的重采样程序。该方法在传统的后验重采样步骤中增加了一个判别器，该判别器基于物理合理性来接受或拒绝候选粒子。判别器通过正则化流方法进行学习和训练。研究在Ikeda映射和Lorenz '63系统上进行了数值实验验证。

Result: 数值实验表明，与标准EnGMF相比，判别器引导的重采样程序在低集成数情形下能够持续降低误差，表现出更优的滤波性能和稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于物理合理性的判别机制，成功改进了EnGMF的重采样过程，解决了物理不合理样本的问题，证明了该方法在中高维非线性滤波问题中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: The ensemble Gaussian mixture filter (EnGMF) is a powerful, convergent particle filter capable of medium-to-high dimensional non-linear filtering. The EnGMF relies on a resampling step that can generate physically unrealistic posterior samples, that would subsequently produce physically meaningless forecasts. This work introduces the discriminator-informed resampling procedure, that augments the posterior resampling step with a discriminator that accepts or rejects candidate particles based on their physical plausibility. In this work these discriminators are learned through a normalizing flow approach. Numerical experiments on both the Ikeda map and the Lorenz '63 system show that discriminator informed resampling procedure consistently reduces error relative to the standard EnGMF in low-ensemble regimes.

</details>


### [98] [Learning to Race in Minutes: Infoprop Dyna on the Mini Wheelbot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01096)
*Devdutt Subhasish,Henrik Hose,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用不确定性感知的模型-based强化学习框架（Infoprop Dyna），让欠驱动独轮车机器人在11分钟的真实世界交互中学会赛道竞速，无需依赖物理仿真器或域随机化即可实现直接从现实交互中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在机器人控制领域虽然潜力巨大，但最新进展通常依赖精心设计的物理仿真器和域随机化技术，才能在合理的墙钟时间内实现从仿真到现实的迁移。这增加了系统复杂性和开发成本，且限制了机器人直接从真实环境中学习的可能性。因此，本文旨在探索是否可以绕过仿真器需求，直接在真实世界中实现高效学习。

Method: 采用Infoprop Dyna框架，这是一种先进的不确定性感知模型-based强化学习（MBRL）方法。该框架能够直接从真实世界的交互中学习，无需依赖物理仿真器或域随机化技术。通过建模环境动力学的不确定性，框架能够在保证安全性的同时实现高效探索和学习。实验对象为Mini Wheelbot，这是一个欠驱动的独轮车机器人。

Result: Mini Wheelbot机器人在仅11分钟的真实世界交互时间内，成功学会了在赛道上进行竞速任务。这一结果证明了Infoprop Dyna框架能够在无需仿真器的情况下，实现快速且有效的真实世界强化学习，达到了接近传统sim-to-real方法的性能水平。

Conclusion: 本研究成功证明了不确定性感知的模型-based强化学习方法可以绕过传统的仿真器和域随机化需求，直接在真实世界中实现高效的机器人学习。11分钟的学习时间表明该方法在实际应用中具有可行性，为机器人强化学习开辟了一条新的技术路径，有望降低复杂动力学机器人学习系统的开发门槛。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to enable robots with fast, nonlinear, and unstable dynamics to reach the limits of their performance. However, most recent advances rely on carefully designed physics-based simulators and domain randomization to achieve successful sim-to-real transfer within reasonable wall-clock time. In this work, we bypass the need for such simulators and demonstrate that Infoprop Dyna, a state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) framework, can enable robots to learn directly from real-world interactions. Using Infoprop Dyna, the Mini Wheelbot, an underactuated unicycle robot, learns to race around a track within 11 minutes of real-world experience.

</details>


### [99] [Almost for Free: Crafting Adversarial Examples with Convolutional Image Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01098)
*Alexander Warnecke,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗性图像滤波器的简单攻击方法，利用经典边缘检测算法优化生成对抗样本，仅需3x3滤波器即可实现30%-80%的成功率，参数量比生成模型方法减少了五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗样本生成方法通常依赖于梯度信息，需要直接访问模型或通过查询近似梯度，过程较为复杂。作者受到可解释机器学习启发，希望设计一种更简单、高效的对抗样本生成方法。

Method: 设计基于经典边缘检测算法的对抗性图像滤波器，通过优化过程使其能够欺骗学习模型。该方法是一种非定向攻击，仅需对输入进行单次传递即可生成对抗样本，使用小型3x3滤波器实现。

Result: 实验表明：1）3x3滤波器在不同神经网络上实现了30%至80%的攻击成功率；2）相比使用生成模型的相关方法，参数量减少了五个数量级；3）学习到的滤波器具有高可迁移性，并展现出与经典图像滤波器相似的结构特性。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了神经网络对恶意噪声的脆弱性，证明了即使是极简的滤波器结构也能有效生成可迁移的对抗样本，为理解神经网络的脆弱性提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Adversarial examples in machine learning are typically generated using gradients, obtained either directly through access to the model or approximated via queries to it. In this paper, we propose a much simpler approach to craft adversarial examples, drawing inspiration from insights of explainable machine learning. In particular, we design \emph{adversarial image filters} that are based on classic edge detection algorithms but optimized to deceive learning models. The resulting untargeted attacks are transferable and require only a single pass over the input. Empirically, we find that 3x3 filters already enable success rates between 30% and 80% on different neural networks. Compared to related approaches using generative models for crafting adversarial examples, we reduce the number of parameters by five orders of magnitude, resulting in a very efficient attack. When investigating the parameters of the learned filters, we observe interesting properties such as a high transferability between models and structures common to classic image filters. Our results provide further insights into the vulnerability of neural networks and their fragility to malicious noise.

</details>


### [100] [Diffusion Operator Geometry of Feedforward Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01107)
*Kanishka Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于算子理论的平滑框架，用于分析前馈神经网络表示几何，克服了传统离散曲率方法的不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用邻域图上的离散曲率来分析神经网络表示几何，揭示了类似Ricci流的行为，但这类方法存在不稳定性。作者希望开发一种更稳定、平滑的算子理论替代方案来研究表示几何特性。

Method: 作者为每层特征快照构建基于高斯核扩散的马尔可夫算子，利用Bakry-Emery Γ-微积分从该算子导出传输、谱、标签边界和局部尺度等可观测量。在具有共享协方差的平衡高斯类条件快照模型下进行理论分析。

Result: 在理论层面，作者推导出了人口算子的闭式类别亲和力、泄漏和粗糙谱，这些性质由成对正则化Mahalanobis分离度控制。证明了算子可观测量在特征扰动下平滑变化，而硬邻域图诊断可能发生不连续变化。在合成实验和MNIST实验中验证了该方法能有效跟踪训练过程、网络宽度和扰动稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个稳定的算子-几何框架，为分析前馈表示几何提供了新的理论基础和实用工具，相比离散方法具有更好的平滑性和稳定性。

Abstract: Neural networks transform data through learned representations whose geometry affects separation, contraction, and generalization. Recent work studies this geometry using discrete curvature on neighborhood graphs, suggesting Ricci-flow-like behavior across layers. We develop a smooth operator-theoretic alternative for feedforward representation snapshots. Each feature cloud induces a Gaussian-kernel diffusion Markov operator, and transport, spectral, label-boundary, and local-scale observables are derived from this single object via Bakry-Emery $Γ$-calculus. In a balanced Gaussian class-conditional snapshot model with shared covariance, the population operator has closed-form class affinities, leakage, and coarse spectra, all controlled by pairwise regularized Mahalanobis separations $c_\varepsilon^{(a,b)}$. We also prove that the resulting operator observables vary smoothly under feature perturbations, while hard neighborhood-graph diagnostics can change discontinuously. Synthetic experiments validate the closed-form Gaussian bridge, while learned MNIST experiments show that the same operator observables track training, width, and perturbation stability. Together, these results give a stable operator-geometric framework for analyzing feedforward representation geometry.

</details>


### [101] [Topological Neural Tangent Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01110)
*Sanjukta Krishnagopal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了拓扑神经正切核（TopoNTK），这是一种针对单纯复形上的边特征进行单纯形消息传递的无限宽度核，能够捕获图模型无法感知的高阶拓扑结构，并提供了可解释的谱学习几何特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经正切核虽然为图神经网络提供了原理性的无限宽度理论，但继承了图模型的基本局限性：它们只能看到成对结构。许多关系系统包含高阶相互作用，这些相互作用更自然地由单纯复形表示。因此，需要一种能够捕获这些高阶拓扑结构的理论框架。

Method: 引入拓扑神经正切核（TopoNTK），这是一种针对单纯复形上边特征的无限宽度核。TopoNTK结合了下Hodge相互作用（通过共享顶点捕获类似图的耦合）和上Hodge相互作用（通过填充单纯形捕获耦合）。该方法利用Hodge结构将边信号分解为梯度类、谐波和局部环流分量，并通过TopoNTK的谱决定每个分量的学习速度。

Result: TopoNTK对图核不可见的拓扑结构敏感，允许具有相同图但不同填充单纯形的复形诱导不同的核。该方法表现出拓扑形式的谱偏置：与大特征值模式对齐的分量学习得快，而全局谐波模式学习得慢。在合成单纯形任务和DBLP高阶链接预测上的实验验证了表达性、Hodge对齐、谱学习和稳定性特性。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构不仅仅是额外的结构，它可以提供坐标，使关系学习更加忠实、可解释和有效。拓扑神经正切核成功地将高阶拓扑信息纳入无限宽度理论框架，为处理高阶相互作用的关系系统提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Graph neural tangent kernels give a principled infinite-width theory for graph neural networks, but inherit a basic limitation of graph models: they see only pairwise structure. Many relational systems contain higher-order interactions that are more naturally represented by simplicial complexes. We introduce the Topological Neural Tangent Kernel (TopoNTK), an infinite-width kernel for simplicial message passing on edge features. TopoNTK combines lower Hodge interactions, capturing graph-like coupling through shared vertices, with upper Hodge interactions, capturing coupling through filled simplices. This makes the kernel sensitive to topology invisible to graph kernels, allowing complexes with the same graph but different filled simplices to induce different kernels. Beyond expressivity, the Hodge structure gives the kernel an interpretable learning geometry. Edge signals decompose into gradient-like, harmonic, and local circulation components, and the spectrum of the TopoNTK determines how quickly each component is learned. This yields a topological form of spectral bias: components aligned with large-eigenvalue modes are learned quickly, while global harmonic modes, retained through the residual channel, often lie at smaller eigenvalues and are learned more slowly. We prove expressivity, Hodge-alignment, spectral learning, and stability properties, and validate them on synthetic simplicial tasks and DBLP higher-order link prediction. The results show that topology is not merely extra structure; it can provide coordinates that make relational learning more faithful, interpretable, and effective.

</details>


### [102] [When Less is Enough: Efficient Inference via Collaborative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01111)
*Yilei Chen,Sharut Gupta,Yannis Paschalidis,Ayush Sekhari,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DUET框架，通过能力模型生成推理信号、轻量模型生成最终答案的两阶段协作推理方式，在保持推理性能的同时节省高达60%的大模型输出token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖单一大型模型进行端到端推理和预测会产生巨大的推理成本，这促使研究者寻求一种既能保持任务性能又能降低推理成本的高效方案。

Method: 提出DUET（双模型高效两阶段推理）框架，将推理过程分解为两阶段：能力模型产生推理信号，轻量模型根据该信号生成最终答案。同时提出长度惩罚联合训练目标，促使能力模型仅传递轻量模型解决任务所需的最小信息量。

Result: 在AIME和GPQA等具有挑战性的推理基准测试上，DUET在保持强推理性能的同时，相比单独使用大型模型进行端到端推理，节省了高达60%的大型模型输出token。

Conclusion: DUET成功实现了推理密集型计算由能力模型处理、非推理密集型组件委托给轻量模型的分工，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下显著降低了推理成本。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DUET (Dual-model Efficient Two-stage inference), a collaborative inference framework in which a capable model and a lightweight model work together to solve a task. Relying on a single large model to perform end-to-end reasoning and prediction often incurs substantial inference cost. In contrast, DUET decomposes inference into two stages: the capable model produces a reasoning signal, and the lightweight model interprets this signal to generate the final answer, allowing reasoning-intensive computation to be handled by the capable model while non-reasoning-intensive components are delegated to the lightweight model without sacrificing task performance. To achieve this objective, we propose a length-penalized joint training objective that encourages the capable model to transmit only the information that is sufficient for the lightweight model to solve the task. As a result, DUET maintains strong reasoning performance with substantially lower inference cost than end-to-end inference using a large model alone, saving up to 60% of the large model's output tokens on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including AIME and GPQA.

</details>


### [103] [Machine Learning-Augmented Acceleration of Iterative Ptychographic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01122)
*Bowen Zheng,Katayun Kamdin,David Shapiro,Alexander Ditter,Dayne Sasaki,Emma Bernard,Roopali Kukreja,Petrus H. Zwart,Slavomír Nemšák,Apurva Mehta,Nicholas Schwarz,Alexander Hexemer,Tanny Chavez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器学习增强的叠层衍射成像重建方法，通过引入学习型快进算子加速迭代收敛，在保持物理一致性的同时将计算时间减少两倍以上，并已在同步辐射束线上实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的迭代叠层衍射成像重建算法在现实实验条件下收敛缓慢，限制了实时实验操作的效率，亟需一种既能加速收敛又不损失物理准确性的方法。

Method: 提出一种机器学习增强方法：在标准迭代进行初始预热后，引入一个学习得到的快进算子（fast-forward operator）将重建状态推进到更接近收敛的状态，随后恢复常规迭代更新。模型在多样化数据集上训练，并集成至现有重建流程中。

Result: 该方法在相隔一年采集的实验数据上表现出良好的时间泛化性与鲁棒性；与传统迭代求解器相比，在重建质量相当的情况下，以泊松负对数似然度量收敛更快，实际运行时间减少超过两倍。

Conclusion: 机器学习增强的叠层重建方法在保持物理一致性与求解器灵活性的前提下显著加速收敛，具备良好的鲁棒性与泛化能力，已在同步辐射束线生产环境中部署，证明了其在实时实验中的实用价值。

Abstract: Iterative ptychographic reconstruction algorithms are widely used for coherent diffractive imaging but can exhibit slow convergence under realistic experimental conditions. We propose a machine learning-augmented approach that accelerates iterative ptychographic reconstruction by introducing a learned fast-forward operator applied during reconstruction. Following an initial warm-up using standard iterations, the fast-forward operator advances the reconstruction toward a more converged state, after which conventional iterative updates are resumed. This strategy preserves the physical consistency and flexibility of established ptychographic solvers while reducing the number of iterations required for convergence. The model is trained on diverse ptychographic datasets and evaluated on experimental data acquired in a different year, demonstrating robustness and temporal generalization. Compared with conventional iterative solvers, the machine learning-augmented method achieves comparable reconstruction quality while converging faster in terms of Poisson negative log-likelihood, yielding over a two-fold reduction in wall-clock time. The approach has been integrated into an existing reconstruction pipeline and deployed in production at a synchrotron beamline, demonstrating practicality for real-time experimental operation.

</details>


### [104] [Extreme Weather Bench: A framework and benchmark for evaluation of high-impact weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01126)
*Amy McGovern,Taylor Mandelbaum,Daniel Rothenberg,Nicholas Loveday,Corey Potvin,Montgomery Flora,Linus Magnusson,Eric Gilleland,John Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了极端天气基准（Extreme Weather Bench, EWB），这是一个新的社区驱动的开源基准测试套件，旨在通过提供标准化的高影响天气案例、观测数据、影响指标和开源代码，促进人工智能和数值天气预报模型在全球范围内对高影响天气事件的验证与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前AI天气模型的评估主要依赖于全球尺度评估或少量手工挑选的案例，缺乏一个专注于高影响天气事件的广泛使用的开源基准测试套件。为了推动所有尺度天气模型的科学发展，提高AI模型在影响公众的高影响事件上的可信度，需要一个标准化的评估体系。

Method: 引入了极端天气基准（EWB），这是一个社区驱动的基准测试套件。EWB提供了标准化的案例研究集（跨越多个时空尺度和天气谱系的不同部分）、观测数据、基于影响的评估指标以及开源代码，使用户能够对其模型进行验证和确认。

Result: EWB为模型验证提供了一个标准化的平台，使得不同模型之间可以进行真实的比较，并能够针对特定的高影响天气现象进行评估。作为一个免费开源的社区驱动系统，它能够促进天气模型科学的进步，并提高AI模型在应对高影响天气事件时的可信度。

Conclusion: EWB作为一个持续发展的开源社区驱动系统，将继续与世界范围内的天气和预报验证社区合作，纳入更多的现象、测试案例和指标，从而推动天气模型科学的发展，并增强AI模型在高影响天气事件上的验证能力和可信度。

Abstract: Forecasting the wide variety of high-impact weather events experienced globally is a challenge for both Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) models and it is critical that such models be properly verified before deployment. Although AI weather models are rapidly evolving, much of their evaluation is currently done either with a global-scale evaluation or by hand-picking a small number of case studies or a region. A widely-used open-source benchmark suite focusing on high-impact weather will help to drive the science forward for all scales of weather models, as it has for other AI fields. Here we introduce Extreme Weather Bench (EWB), a new community-driven benchmark suite that facilitates model validation and verification on a variety of high-impact hazards that matter to people around the globe. EWB provides a standard set of case studies (spanning across multiple spatial and temporal scales and different parts of the weather spectrum), observational data, impact-based metrics, and open-source code for users to evaluate their models. Verifying that a model works against a standard set of case studies, especially events that are high-impact for the general public, is a key piece of improving the trustworthiness of AI models. EWB will help to drive the science forward for all weather models, enabling true comparisons across models and evaluating models on specific high-impact phenomena through the use of case studies. EWB is a free open-source community-driven system and will continue to evolve to include additional phenomena, test cases and metrics in collaboration with the worldwide weather and forecast verification community.

</details>


### [105] [Forager: a lightweight testbed for continual learning with partial observability in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01131)
*Steven Tang,Xinze Xiong,Anna Hakhverdyan,Andrew Patterson,Jacob Adkins,Jiamin He,Esraa Elelimy,Parham Mohammad Panahi,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Forager，一个轻量级、部分可观察的持续强化学习环境，揭示了现有智能体在部分可观察设定下仍面临可塑性丢失问题，且利用状态构建比现有缓解方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习（CRL）研究主要关注完全可观察环境中的可塑性丢失问题，忽视了部分可观察性及记忆机制的重要性，且现有部分可观察CRL环境计算成本过高。

Method: 引入Forager环境，这是一个具有恒定内存占用、轻量级的部分可观察CRL环境，并提供一系列实验和任务用于评估当前CRL智能体。

Result: 实验表明智能体在Forager环境中仍表现出可塑性丢失，现有缓解方法有所帮助，但最有效的方法是利用状态构建。研究还提出了Forager变体，可生成无尽的新任务流，突显了当前CRL智能体的局限性。

Conclusion: Forager为研究部分可观察持续强化学习提供了实用且具有挑战性的平台，当前CRL智能体在部分可观察和无尽任务设定下仍有明显不足。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning (CRL), good performance requires never-ending learning, acting, and exploration in a big, partially observable world. Most CRL experiments have focused on loss of plasticity -- the inability to keep learning -- in one-off experiments where some unobservable non-stationarity is added to classic fully observable MDPs. Further, these experiments rarely consider the role of partial observability and the importance of CRL agents that use memory or recurrence. One potential reason for this focus on mitigating loss of plasticity without considering partial observability is that many partially-observable CRL environments are prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Forager, a light-weight partially-observable CRL environment with a constant memory footprint. We provide a set of experiments and sample tasks demonstrating that Forager is challenging for current CRL agents and yet also allows for in-depth study of those agents. We demonstrate that agents exhibit loss of plasticity, proposed mitigations can help, but that most useful is to leverage state construction. We conclude with a variant of Forager that generates an unending stream of new tasks to learn that clearly highlights the limitations of current CRL agents.

</details>


### [106] [Spectral Graph Sparsification Preserves Representation Geometry in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01136)
*Sanjukta Krishnagopal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多项式滤波图神经网络，证明了谱稀疏化能在隐藏表示的几何结构（如成对距离、类均值、协方差）上仅引入O(ε)扰动，并保证有限时间训练稳定性；实验验证了理论预测，表明谱稀疏化在大幅降低图复杂度的同时能有效保持嵌入表示的几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱图稀疏化是一种降低图复杂度的经典工具，通常用于加速图神经网络计算并保持预测性能。然而，现有工作主要关注计算效率，忽略了一个重要的表示层面问题：稀疏化是否保持学习到的嵌入表示的几何结构？这个问题对于GNN嵌入在下游任务（如可解释性）中的应用至关重要。

Method: 1. 针对多项式滤波GNN，理论证明任意ε-谱稀疏化算子在多项式图滤波器、多层隐藏表示及其Gram矩阵上仅引入O(ε)扰动；
2. 建立有限时间训练稳定性理论，证明在光滑性和有界性假设下，稠密图和稀疏图上的梯度下降产生的权重轨迹分离度与稀疏化失真度成比例增长；
3. 使用有效电阻稀疏化方法在合成图和真实数据集（FashionMNIST、Cora、Paul15）上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 理论结果：证明了谱稀疏化在嵌入空间中对平方成对距离、类均值和协方差结构的稳定性保证；
2. 实验验证：有效电阻稀疏化在合成图上验证了预测的扰动链理论；
3. 实证发现：即使在大规模稀疏化下，Gram矩阵和训练动力学仍显示出低发散性，与谱稀疏化下的稳定性预测一致；
4. 预测能力：隐藏Gram保持性强预测了邻域保持性和类中心稳定性。

Conclusion: 谱稀疏化不仅保持图算子的性质，还能保持支持GNN嵌入下游应用（特别是可解释性）的表示几何结构。这表明谱稀疏化是一种既高效又表示保持的图简化方法，为GNN的实际应用提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Spectral graph sparsification is a classical tool for reducing graph complexity while preserving Laplacian quadratic forms. In graph neural networks (GNNs), sparsification is often used to accelerate computation while maintaining predictive performance. In this work, we study a complementary representation-level question: does sparsification preserve the geometry of learned embeddings?
  For polynomial-filter GNNs, we prove that any $ε$-spectral sparsifier induces $O(ε)$ perturbations in polynomial graph filters, multilayer hidden representations, and their Gram matrices. These guarantees imply stability of squared pairwise distances, class means, and covariance structure in embedding space. We further establish finite-time training stability: under smoothness and boundedness assumptions, gradient descent on dense and sparsified graphs produces weight trajectories whose separation grows at most proportionally to the sparsification distortion.
  Empirically, effective-resistance sparsification validates the predicted perturbation chain on synthetic graphs and preserves hidden representation geometry on real datasets. In our experiments, the gram matrix and training dynamics show low divergence even under substantial sparsification, consistent with the predicted stability under spectral sparsification. Hidden Gram preservation strongly predicts neighborhood preservation and class-centroid stability across FashionMNIST, Cora, and Paul15. Together, these results show that spectral sparsification preserves not only graph operators, but also the representation geometry that supports downstream use of GNN embeddings for interpretability.

</details>


### [107] [Metric-Normalized Posterior Leakage (mPL): Attacker-Aligned Privacy for Joint Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01137)
*Gaoyi Chen,Minghao Li,Weishi Shi,Yan Huang,Yusheng Wei,Sourabh Yadav,Chenxi Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG
