TL;DR: 本文针对联合观察场景下度量差分隐私(mDP)的隐私泄露问题，提出了度量归一化后验泄露(mPL)作为攻击者对齐的隐私度量，发现满足mDP在联合观察下仍可能存在高泄露，进而形式化概率有界mPL(PBmPL)并提出自适应mPL(AmPL)框架，在词嵌入案例中有效降低了隐私违规频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量差分隐私(mDP)通过根据语义距离缩放噪声来增强本地差分隐私(LDP)，但在联合观察场景下(如机器学习系统)，模型无关的单记录隐私保证可能忽略证据聚合带来的泄露。许多ML系统在联合观察下被使用，攻击者可以聚合跨相关项的证据来推断隐私信息，而现有mDP机制无法有效防范此类攻击。

Method: 1) 提出度量归一化后验泄露(mPL)：攻击者对齐、距离校准的后验概率偏移度量；2) 证明单次或独立发布时mPL一致有界等价于mDP；3) 形式化概率有界mPL(PBmPL)：限制mPL超出目标预算的频率；4) 设计自适应mPL(AmPL)框架：包含扰动、学习攻击者审计、参数自适应(可选贝叶斯重映射)，平衡隐私与效用。

Result: 在词嵌入案例研究中，神经对抗攻击在联合消费场景下成功突破满足每条记录mDP扰动后的隐私保护；而AmPL框架大幅降低了此类隐私违规的发生频率，同时仅造成较低的效用损失，证明PBmPL作为联合消费场景下可实践、可认证的隐私保护方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合观察场景下的隐私保护需要超越单记录差分隐私的保证。本文提出的PBmPL框架通过概率方式限制后验泄露，并通过AmPL框架实现，为联合消费场景提供了实用且可认证的隐私保护方案，弥补了现有mDP机制在应对证据聚合攻击方面的不足。

Abstract: Metric differential privacy (mDP) strengthens local differential privacy (LDP) by scaling noise to semantic distance, but many machine learning (ML) systems are consumed under joint observation, where model-agnostic, per-record guarantees can miss leakage from evidence aggregation. We introduce metric-normalized posterior leakage (mPL), an attacker-aligned, distance-calibrated measure of posterior-odds shift induced by releases, and show that for single or independent releases, uniformly bounding mPL is equivalent to mDP. Under joint observation, however, satisfying mDP may still leave mPL high because learned aggregators compound evidence across correlated items. To make control practical, we formalize probabilistically bounded mPL (PBmPL), which limits how often mPL may exceed a target budget, and we operationalize it via Adaptive mPL (AmPL), a trust-and-verify framework that perturbs, audits with a learned attacker, and adapts parameters (with optional Bayesian remapping) to balance privacy and utility. In a word-embedding case study, neural adversaries violate mPL under joint consumption despite per-record mDP perturbations, whereas AmPL substantially lowers the frequency of such violations with low utility loss, indicating PBmPL as a practical, certifiable protection for joint-consumption settings.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Perspective Transformers in ARC-AGI-2 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01154)
*Caleb Talley,Vedant Tibrewal,Seun Adekunle,Weiwen Dong,Xinyu Wu,Fariha Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用TinyLM结合测试时训练(TTT)和专家乘积(POE)技术解决ARC-AGI-2视觉谜题，在训练集达到96.1%准确率，评估集达到21.7%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC-AGI-2是一个衡量机器从有限样本中泛化、解释符号意义以及在不同情境中灵活应用规则能力的人类直觉视觉谜题基准。作者旨在探索如何让模型在此类需要抽象推理的任务上取得更好表现。

Method: 采用TinyLM作为基础模型，并在测试时引入额外的微调策略，具体包括：测试时训练(Test-Time-Training, TTT)和专家乘积(Products of Experts, POE)方法。

Result: 模型在训练集上达到96.1%的准确率，在评估集上达到21.7%的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过结合TinyLM与测试时微调策略，模型在ARC-AGI-2谜题上展现了一定的推理能力，但训练集与评估集之间仍存在显著的性能差距，表明模型的泛化能力仍有提升空间。

Abstract: ARC-AGI-2 is a benchmark of human-intuitive visual puzzles that measures a machine's ability to generalize from limited examples, interpret symbolic meaning, and flexibly apply rules in varying contexts. In this paper, we discuss our approach to solving the ARC-AGI-2 puzzles with TinyLM, with additional fine-tuning at test time, including Test-Time-Training (TTT) and Products of Experts (POE). Our model achieves 96.1% accuracy on the training set and 21.7% accuracy on the evaluation set.

</details>


### [109] [Minimizing Collateral Damage in Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01167)
*Tam Nguyen,Tu Anh Nguyen,Sina Alemohammad,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对激活引导中的“附带损伤”问题，提出了一种基于约束优化的新框架，通过利用激活的经验二阶矩矩阵加权，实现了更精准且对无关任务影响更小的模型行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导方法（如向量加法）常会导致“附带损伤”，即干预过程不仅改变了目标特征方向，还意外改变了其他非目标特征方向的对齐。这种问题的根源在于标准方法隐含地假设非目标特征具有各向同性，而实际并非如此。

Method: 作者首先对“附带损伤”进行了数学形式化定义，随后将引导过程建模为一个约束优化问题。该方法寻找一个新的激活向量，该向量在满足目标特征约束的同时，最小化由经验二阶矩矩阵加权的预期附带变化平方和。这种加权方式能够编码不同特征方向上的非均匀扰动成本，克服了各向同性方法的局限性。

Result: 所提出的方法在实现精准控制目标行为的同时，有效降低了模型在无关任务上的性能退化，相比标准各向同性干预方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过考虑激活的经验二阶矩统计特性，该框架成功缓解了激活引导中的附带损伤问题，为大语言模型提供了一种更精确、破坏性更小的对齐干预手段。

Abstract: Activation steering is a method for controlling Large Language Model (LLM) behavior by intervening in its internal representations to increase the alignment with a specific target feature direction. However, standard interventions, such as vector addition, often cause ``collateral damage", defined as unintended changes in the alignment of activations along other non-target feature directions. This damage occurs because standard methods implicitly assume the isotropy of non-target features. In this work, we provide a mathematical formalization of collateral damage and introduce a principled framework that models steering as a constrained optimization problem. Our method finds a new activation that minimizes the expected squared collateral change weighted by the empirical second-moment matrix of activations. This weighting encodes the nonuniform cost of the perturbation in different feature directions, in contrast to isotropic approaches that penalize changes uniformly in all feature directions. By accounting for the empirical second-moment of activations, our approach achieves more precise control while reducing the degradation of model performance on unrelated tasks.

</details>


### [110] [A Theory of Generalization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01172)
*Elon Litman,Gabe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了深度学习泛化的非渐近理论，基于经验神经正切核（NTK）对输出空间的划分，解释了良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏置和顿悟（grokking）等现象，并提出了一个可从单次训练中精确估计噪声的目标函数，可作为Adam的SNR预条件器，显著改善多种任务的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习理论中存在诸多尚未统一解释的现象，如良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏置和顿悟（grokking）。现有理论多为渐近分析，难以刻画有限样本和特征学习条件下的泛化行为。本文旨在建立一个非渐近的理论框架，揭示这些现象背后的共同机制，并为实际训练提供可操作的理论工具。

Method: 基于经验神经正切核（NTK）对输出空间进行划分：信号方向对应较大的特征值，误差快速耗散；噪声方向对应极小的特征值，残差被限制在测试不可见的区域。在小批量随机梯度下降（SGD）下，信号通道中群体信号通过快速的线性漂移累积，而对样本特异性记忆被抑制为缓慢的扩散随机游走。该分析覆盖了NTK算子范数发生$\\mathcal{O}(1)$变化的完整特征学习情形。进一步推导出一个精确的总体风险目标函数，其可从单次训练轨迹中估计，无需验证数据。

Result: 在理论上证明了即使在NTK发生显著演化时泛化能力仍可保持；提出的目标函数精确测度信号通道中的噪声，适用于任意架构、损失和优化器。实践上，该方法转化为一个SNR预条件器叠加于Adam之上，仅增加一个状态向量且无额外成本；在顿悟任务上加速训练$5\\times$，在PINNs和隐式神经表示中抑制过拟合记忆，在含噪偏好的DPO微调中提升性能且与参考策略的距离减小$3\\times$。

Conclusion: 该理论为深度学习中的多种现象提供了统一的解释框架，揭示了NTK谱结构在泛化中的关键作用。所提目标函数为模型选择、超参数优化和训练监控提供了无需验证集的工具，有望成为通用深度学习实践的新标准组件。

Abstract: We present a non-asymptotic theory of generalization in deep learning where the empirical neural tangent kernel partitions the output space. In directions corresponding to signal, error dissipates rapidly; in the vast orthogonal dimensions corresponding to noise, the kernel's near-zero eigenvalues trap residual error in a test-invisible reservoir. Within the signal channel, minibatch SGD ensures that coherent population signal accumulates via fast linear drift, while idiosyncratic memorization is suppressed into a slow, diffusive random walk. We prove generalization survives even when the kernel evolves $\mathcal{O}(1)$ in operator norm, the full feature-learning regime. This theory naturally explains disparate phenomena in deep learning theory, such as benign overfitting, double descent, implicit bias, and grokking. Lastly, we derive an exact population-risk objective from a single training run with no validation data, for any architecture, loss, or optimizer, and prove that it measures precisely the noise in the signal channel. This objective reduces in practice to an SNR preconditioner on top of Adam, adding one state vector at no extra cost; it accelerates grokking by $5 \times$, suppresses memorization in PINNs and implicit neural representations, and improves DPO fine-tuning under noisy preferences while staying $3 \times$ closer to the reference policy.

</details>


### [111] [Linear-Readout Floors and Threshold Recovery in Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01192)
*Hector Borobia,Elies Seguí-Mas,Guillermina Tormo-Carbó*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概念上澄清了两种叠加态计算方法的容量差异源于不同的接口不变量，并将Welch界作为兼容性阈值解释了$d^{3/2}$规模的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两种叠加态计算方法达到不同的递归容量：Hänni等人的方法认证$\tilde{O}(d^{3/2})$个特征，而Adler和Shavit的方法达到近二次容量，两个结果看似矛盾，需要澄清其差异根源。

Method: 建立双正交线性读出的秩迹Welch型下界，分析随机支撑阈值恢复与线性读出在Bernoulli稀疏状态下的性能差异，将Welch界与Hänni校正层的容差匹配。

Result: 对于$F \gg d$，单位对角线性读出的最坏情况离对角串扰为$\Omega(d^{-1/2})$；该界对单位范数紧框架平均紧确；$d^{3/2}$规模是Hänni模板的兼容性阈值而非通用上界。

Conclusion: 两种结果并不矛盾，因为它们维护不同的接口不变量；超越Hänni模板的鲁棒非线性重置机制仍有待研究。

Abstract: Two recent approaches to computation in superposition reach different recursive capacity regimes: Hänni et al. certify $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2})$ computable features in width $d$ via an approximate-linear recursive template, while Adler and Shavit reach near-quadratic capacity (up to logarithmic factors) using thresholded Boolean recovery. The main contribution of this paper is conceptual: we argue these results are not contradictory because they maintain different interface invariants, and we formalize the distinction.
  As a tool, we record a rank-trace Welch-type lower bound for biorthogonal linear readouts: for $F \gg d$, the worst-case off-diagonal cross-talk of any unit-diagonal linear readout is $Ω(d^{-1/2})$, and the bound is tight on average for unit-norm tight frames. At quadratic feature load $F=d^2$, random-support threshold recovery succeeds for sparsities $s=O(d/\log d)$, while linear readouts still incur $Ω(s/d)$ average per-coordinate squared error on Bernoulli sparse states. Matching the Welch floor against the published tolerance of the Hänni correction layer explains the $d^{3/2}$ scale as a compatibility threshold for that template, not a universal upper bound. Robust nonlinear reset beyond the Hänni template is left open.

</details>


### [112] [Focus and Dilution: The Multi-stage Learning Process of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01199)
*Zheng-An Chen,Pengxiao Lin,Zhi-Qin John Xu,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过梯度流分析揭示了一层Transformer在马尔可夫数据上存在“聚焦-稀释”循环动态，将每个周期分解为嵌入压缩、高频聚焦、质量重分配稀释及低频不对称性启新周期四个阶段，并在合成数据及真实文本上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer模型在众多领域取得了显著成功，但其训练动态机制的理解仍然有限，本文旨在揭示注意力学习的内在循环规律与阶段性特征。

Method: 针对马尔可夫数据下的一层Transformer，采用梯度流分析，并在临界点附近进行阶段式线性化，将“聚焦-稀释”周期分解为一系列独特阶段进行分析。

Result: 发现嵌入与投影迅速收缩至秩一结构，随后注意力参数增加并诱发对高频token的聚焦；注意力演化导致嵌入产生次阶扰动并引发质量重分配，逐步稀释该聚焦；低频token间微小不对称性解除简并临界点并启动下一周期。

Conclusion: 所预测的阶段性与循环动态在合成马尔可夫数据、WikiText及TinyStories实验中得到验证，为理解Transformer训练动态提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, yet our understanding of their training dynamics remains limited. In this work, we identify a recurrent focus-dilution cycle in attention learning and provide a rigorous explanation in a one-layer Transformer setting for Markovian data via gradient-flow analysis. Using stage-wise linearization around critical points, we show that a single focus-dilution cycle can be decomposed into a sequence of distinct stages. First, embedding and projection rapidly condense to a rank-one structure, while attention parameters remain effectively frozen. Then, the attention parameters begin to increase, inducing a frequency-driven focus toward high-frequency tokens. As attention continues to evolve, it generates next-order perturbations in embeddings, leading to a mass-redistribution mechanism that progressively dilutes this focus. Finally, small asymmetries among low-frequency tokens lift a degenerate critical point, opening new embedding directions and initiating the next cycle. Experiments on synthetic Markovian data as well as WikiText and TinyStories corroborate the predicted stages and cyclical dynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Local Hessian Spectral Filtering for Robust Intrinsic Dimension Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01221)
*Genki Osada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部Hessian谱维度(LHSD)方法，通过谱滤波过滤高维噪声，解决了现有扩散模型局部内禀维度估计在高维空间失效的问题，实现了线性计算复杂度并在大规模模型记忆检测中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的局部内禀维度(LID)估计方法在高维空间中失效，因为来自大量法向的噪声会淹没切向信号，导致无法准确估计数据流形的维度。

Method: 提出局部Hessian谱维度(LHSD)方法：对对数密度Hessian矩阵应用谱滤波，显式截断与法向相关的大特征值，仅统计零曲率的切向方向；利用随机Lanczos求积(SLQ)实现，避免完整Hessian矩阵构造，实现与维度D的线性复杂度。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验验证了LHSD具有更强的鲁棒性，并在大规模扩散模型的记忆检测任务中展现出实用价值。

Conclusion: LHSD成功克服了高维空间中法向噪声的干扰，为高维数据的局部内禀维度估计提供了可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案，在大规模扩散模型分析中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: While diffusion models enable new approaches for estimating Local Intrinsic Dimension (LID), existing methods fail in high-dimensional spaces where noise from vast normal directions overwhelms the tangent signal. We propose Local Hessian Spectral Dimension (LHSD), which resolves this by applying spectral filtering to the log-density Hessian, explicitly cutting off large eigenvalues associated with normal directions to count zero-curvature tangent directions. Implemented using Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature (SLQ), LHSD avoids full Hessian construction, achieving linear scalability with dimension $D$. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm LHSD's superior robustness and its utility in detecting memorization in large-scale diffusion models.

</details>


### [114] [Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows for Spatiotemporal Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01226)
*Keyan Chen,Qiwei Yuan,Zhitong Xu,Bin Shen,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARCH（Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows），一个用于时空事件建模的分层流匹配框架。该方法能够捕捉复杂的事件分布，同时支持灵活的条件推理，在预测和条件推理任务上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空系统中事件无处不在，但其复杂分布的建模仍然具有挑战性。现有点过程模型通常依赖强结构假设，且局限于自回归的逐事件预测，难以支持更广泛的推理任务，如逆向推理、轨迹重建和缺失事件位置恢复。

Method: 提出ARCH框架，采用历史编码器-生成解码器架构，引入混合掩码策略实现对任意观测事件的灵活条件化。该分层流匹配方法能够精确计算条件强度（量化瞬时事件风险），在统一框架内处理预测、逆向推理和部分轨迹恢复任务。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，ARCH在预测和条件推理任务上始终优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ARCH成功提供了一个表达能力强且灵活的时空事件建模框架，能够捕捉复杂事件分布并支持多种推理任务，为时空事件建模领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Events in spatiotemporal systems are ubiquitous, yet modeling their complex distributions remains challenging. Existing point process models often rely on strong structural assumptions and are typically limited to autoregressive, event-by-event prediction. As a result, they struggle to support broader inference tasks such as inverse inference, trajectory reconstruction, and recovery of missing event locations. We introduce Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows (ARCH), a hierarchical flow matching framework for spatiotemporal event modeling. ARCH is expressive enough to capture complex event distributions while enabling tractable and accurate computation of conditional intensities, which quantify instantaneous event risk. Built on a history-encoder-generative-decoder architecture, ARCH introduces a hybrid masking strategy for flexible conditioning on arbitrary observed events. This enables a unified treatment of forecasting, inverse inference, and partial trajectory recovery within a single framework. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that ARCH consistently outperforms existing baselines across both prediction and conditional inference tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Attention Sinks in Massively Multilingual Neural Machine Translation:Discovery, Analysis, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01229)
*Hillary Mutisya,John Mugane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现NLLB-200等NMT模型中存在“注意力汇聚”现象，非内容词元（如序列结束标记、语言标签和标点）占据了83%-91%的跨注意力权重，导致原始分析严重失真。本文提出内容过滤方法纠正该偏差，揭示了被掩盖的语言学信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨注意力模式广泛用于分析多语言模型的句法对齐，但本文发现现有分析中存在系统性伪影（artifact）：非内容词元捕获了绝大部分注意力权重，导致原始指标严重低估内容级相似性（原始值36.7% vs 过滤后70.7%），使得未修正的分析结果不可靠。

Method: 1. 系统性识别并验证NLLB-200模型中存在的“注意力汇聚”现象，将LLM领域的发现扩展至NMT跨注意力；\n2. 提出内容过滤方法，移除非内容词元并重新归一化分布；\n3. 在非洲语言（斯瓦希里语、基库尤语、索马里语、卢奥语）和非非洲语言基准（德语、土耳其语、中文、印地语）的1000个平行句对上进行验证。

Result: 1. 确认该伪影具有普遍性：非内容词元捕获83%-91%的跨注意力权重；\n2. 确定其因果机制源于词汇表设计而非位置偏差；\n3. 成功恢复被掩盖的语言学信号：教师强制与生成模式间存在16.9个百分点的差距、注意力熵呈现清晰的语言家族聚类、发现索马里语中SOV语序与单调对齐相关的隐藏悖论。

Conclusion: 未经修正的跨注意力分析会产生误导性结论。本文提出的内容过滤方法可有效纠正该偏差，为多语言NMT的可解释性研究提供可靠的分析工具，并发布过滤工具包和修正数据集以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Cross-attention patterns in neural machine translation (NMT) are widely used to study how multilingual models align linguistic structure. We report a systematic artifact in cross-attention analysis of NLLB-200 (600M): non-content tokens - primarily end-of-sequence tokens, language tags, and punctuation - capture 83 percent to 91 percent of total cross-attention mass. We term these "attention sinks," extending findings from LLMs [Xiao et al., 2023] to NMT cross-attention and identifying a causal mechanism rooted in vocabulary design rather than position bias. This artifact causes raw metrics to underestimate content-level similarity by nearly half (36.7 percent raw vs. 70.7 percent filtered), rendering uncorrected analyses unreliable. To address this, we validate a content-only filtering methodology that removes non-content tokens and renormalizes the distribution. Applying this to 1,000 parallel sentences across African languages (Swahili, Kikuyu, Somali, Luo) and non-African benchmarks (German, Turkish, Chinese, Hindi), we confirm the artifact is universal and recover masked linguistic signals: a 16.9 percentage-point gap between teacher-forcing and generation modes, clear language-family clustering in attention entropy, and a hidden Somali paradox linking SOV word order to monotonic alignment. We release our filtering toolkit and corrected datasets to support reproducible interpretability research on multilingual NMT.

</details>


### [116] [CombinationTS: A Modular Framework for Understanding Time-Series Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01231)
*Xiaorui Wang,Fanda Fan,Chenxi Wang,Yuxuan Yang,Rui Tang,Kuoyu Gao,Simiao Pang,Yuanfeng Shang,Zhipeng Liu,Wanling Gao,Lei Wang,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CombinationTS框架，通过模块化归因发现：在良好的数据视图设计下，无参数的恒等编码器即可媲美复杂模型，挑战了通过增加架构复杂性提升预测性能的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测领域虽然架构复杂性快速增长，但许多报告的最先进（SOTA）性能提升在统计上具有脆弱性或归因错误。为了实现实质性进展，研究动机从传统的模型选择转向模块化归因，即识别真正驱动性能的组件。

Method: 提出CombinationTS，一个自包含的概率评估框架。该方法将预测模型分解为五个正交模块：输入变换、嵌入、编码器、解码器和输出变换。在共享的评估条件空间下，通过边缘化性能（μ）和稳定性（σ）量化每个组件的贡献，从而实现超越脆弱点估计的鲁棒归因。

Result: 通过大规模配对评估，揭示了“恒等悖论”：一旦数据视图（嵌入）设计得当，无参数的恒等编码器往往能够匹配甚至超越复杂的骨干网络。此外，通过输入变换引入显式结构先验，比增加编码器复杂性更能获得有利的性能-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 研究建立了架构必要性的原则基准，证明了显式结构先验优于单纯的架构复杂性增加，为时间序列预测领域的模型设计提供了新的指导方向，即应优先考虑输入变换和嵌入设计而非盲目增加编码器复杂性。

Abstract: Recent progress in time-series forecasting has led to rapidly increasing architectural complexity, yet many reported State-of-the-Art gains are statistically fragile or misattributed. We argue that progress requires a shift from model selection to modular attribution, identifying which components truly drive performance. We propose CombinationTS, a self-contained probabilistic evaluation framework that decomposes forecasting models into orthogonal modules--Input Transformation, Embedding, Encoder, Decoder, and Output Transformation--and evaluates them under a shared evaluation condition space. By quantifying each component via marginalized performance ($μ$) and stability ($σ$), CombinationTS enables robust attribution beyond fragile point estimates. Through large-scale paired evaluation, we uncover the Identity Paradox: once the data view (Embedding) is well-designed, a parameter-free Identity Encoder often matches or outperforms complex backbones. We further show that explicit structural priors introduced via Input Transformations yield a more favorable performance-stability trade-off than increasing Encoder complexity, establishing a principled baseline for architectural necessity.

</details>


### [117] [Rhamba: Region-Aware Hybrid Attention-Mamba Framework for Self-Supervised Learning in Resting-State fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01240)
*Ruthwik Reddy Doodipala,Pankaj Pandey,Pratheek Eranki,Carolina Torres-Rojas,Manob Jyoti Saikia,Ranganatha Sitaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rhamba框架，结合区域感知掩码和混合Attention-Mamba架构进行fMRI自监督预训练，在精神分裂症和ADHD分类任务中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督预训练在大规模神经影像分析中具有潜力，但区域感知掩码和混合序列建模的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 在ABIDE数据集上预训练，采用区域对齐的patch嵌入和三种掩码策略（Any、Majority、Pure），评估四种架构变体（纯Mamba、交替架构、AM和MA混合编码器-解码器配置），在COBRE和ADHD-200数据集上微调进行下游分类任务，使用Integrated Gradients进行可解释性分析。

Result: 掩码策略显著影响重建行为（重建损失排序：Any > Majority > Pure），但对下游性能影响较小且依赖数据集；MA混合架构在两个数据集上达到最高平均AUROC，Rhamba在比较评估中优于现有最先进方法；区域分析显示峰值性能取决于掩码策略与架构的交互作用。

Conclusion: Rhamba为大规模fMRI表示学习提供了一个灵活框架，能够平衡可解释性、可扩展性和性能，揭示了掩码策略与架构交互对模型性能的重要性。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining is promising for large-scale neuroimaging, yet the impact of region-aware masking and hybrid sequence modeling remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce Rhamba, a region-aware pretraining framework that integrates anatomically guided masking with hybrid Attention-Mamba architectures for resting state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) analysis. Models were pretrained on the ABIDE dataset using region-aligned patch embeddings and three masking strategies (Any, Majority, and Pure) with increasing spatial specificity. We evaluated four architectural variants: a Mamba only model, an Alternate architecture with interleaved Mamba and Attention blocks, and two hybrid encoder-decoder configurations (Attention-Mamba (AM) and Mamba-Attention (MA)). The pretrained models were fine-tuned on downstream classification tasks using the COBRE and ADHD-200 datasets for schizophrenia and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder discrimination. We employed Integrated Gradients, an explainable AI method, to identify the brain regions contributing to model predictions. Masking strategy strongly influenced reconstruction behavior, with reconstruction loss following a consistent ordering (Any > Majority > Pure). However, this trend did not directly translate into downstream performance, where differences were modest and dataset-dependent. The hybrid architecture with the MA configuration achieved the highest average AUROC across both datasets, and Rhamba outperformed state-of-the-art methods in comparative evaluation. Region-wise analysis showed that peak performance depends on the interaction between masking strategy and architecture rather than a single dominant configuration. Overall, Rhamba offers a flexible framework for balancing interpretability, scalability, and performance in large-scale fMRI representation learning.

</details>


### [118] [Breaking the Computational Barrier: Provably Efficient Actor-Critic for Low-Rank MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01242)
*Ruiquan Huang,Donghao Li,Yingbin Liang,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于策略评估预言机的乐观行动者-评论家算法，在低秩MDP设置下实现了更优的样本复杂度，同时避免了传统方法中计算昂贵的规划或优化预言机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习算法虽然在函数近似设置下具有良好的样本复杂度，但往往依赖于计算上难以处理的预言机（如规划或优化预言机），这限制了算法的实际可用性。作者旨在寻找计算效率更高的替代方案，建立不同RL预言机之间的计算复杂度层级关系。

Method: 作者首先利用监督学习作为计算代理，建立了低秩马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）下常见RL预言机的清晰层级，证明策略评估是计算最高效的预言机。基于此发现，提出了一种仅依赖策略评估预言机的新型乐观行动者-评论家算法。该方法避免了传统方法中计算昂贵的规划或优化步骤。理论分析进一步扩展至近似低秩MDP设置。

Result: 证明了所提算法在低秩MDP设置下的样本复杂度优于现有保证，同时完全避免了计算昂贵的规划或优化预言机。理论结果进一步推广至近似低秩MDP，证明了该设置涵盖广泛的现实环境类别。在多个标准Gym环境上的实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 策略评估预言机在计算效率上具有显著优势，基于该预言机设计的乐观行动者-评论家算法能够在保持甚至提升样本复杂度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为低秩及近似低秩MDP下的强化学习提供了理论完备且实际可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a fundamental framework for sequential decision-making, in which an agent learns an optimal policy through interactions with an unknown environment. In settings with function approximation, many existing RL algorithms achieve favorable sample complexity, but often rely on computationally intractable oracles. In this paper, we use supervised learning as a computational proxy to establish a clear hierarchy of commonly adopted RL oracles under low-rank Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). This hierarchy shows that policy evaluation is the most computationally efficient oracle, provided that supervised learning can be efficiently solved. Motivated by this observation, we propose a novel optimistic actor-critic algorithm that relies solely on the policy evaluation oracle. We prove that our algorithm outperforms the existing sample complexity guarantees for low-rank MDPs while avoiding computationally expensive planning or optimization oracles commonly assumed in prior works. We further extend our theoretical results to approximately low-rank MDPs and demonstrate that this setting captures a broad class of real-world environments. Finally, we validate our theoretical results with experiments on several standard Gym environments.

</details>


### [119] [S^3-R1: Learning to Retrieve and Answer Step-by-Step with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01248)
*Harsh Goel,Akhil Udathu,Susmija Jabireddy,Pradnesh Kalkar,Atharva Parulekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S^3-R1框架，通过合成数据管道和密集奖励信号解决强化学习后训练中的稀疏奖励问题，在OOD数据集上获得最高10%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习后训练模型在智能体工具使用方面存在两大局限：(1) 稀疏的基于结果的奖励信号难以进行有效的信用分配；(2) 缺乏涵盖不同难度问题的训练数据，导致模型无法执行深度搜索来收集问答所需的证据。

Method: 提出S^3-R1框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 合成数据生成与筛选管道，通过编程方式从现有文档中生成多样化的多跳问题，并引入基于检索的验证步骤来分离中等难度的问题；(2) 设计新的奖励结构，同时评估中间搜索质量和最终答案的正确性，从而缓解稀疏奖励带来的信用分配问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，S^3-R1通过学习更有效的搜索和合成策略，在分布外(OOD)数据集上实现了最高10%的鲁棒泛化性能提升，超越了现有的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过结合以数据为中心的方法和密集学习信号，S^3-R1框架成功解决了强化学习后训练中的关键挑战，显著提升了模型在复杂搜索任务中的泛化能力和策略学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has enabled newer capabilities in models, such as agentic tool-use for search. However, these models struggle primarily due to limitations with sparse outcome-based rewards and a lack of training data that encapsulates questions of differing hardness, which results in models not performing deeper searches with tools to collect evidence for question-answering. To address these limitations, we introduce S^3-R1 (Synthetic data and stabilized Search R1), a framework that couples a data-centric approach with denser learning signals. We first develop a synthetic generation and curation pipeline that programmatically derives diverse, multi-hop questions from existing documents. This pipeline incorporates a retrieval-based verification step to specifically isolate questions of intermediate difficulty. We then pair this expanded training set with a reward structure that evaluates both intermediate search quality and the correctness of the final answer. This setup directly mitigates the credit assignment problems inherent to sparse rewards. Our evaluations show that S^3-R1 outperforms existing baselines by learning more effective search and synthesis strategies, yielding up to a 10% improvement in robust generalization on out-of-domain datasets.

</details>


### [120] [Activation Compression in LLMs: Theoretical Analysis and Efficient Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01255)
*Wen-Da Wei,Han-Bin Fang,Yang-Di Liu,Jiang-Xin Shi,James Kwok,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种激活-梯度协同压缩方法，理论证明线性算子的激活压缩是安全且不改变收敛率的，并在Qwen和LLaMA模型上验证了其有效性和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大型语言模型（LLM）内存消耗巨大，需存储权重、优化器状态及反向传播的中间激活值。现有内存高效方法主要关注梯度和优化器状态，而激活压缩因缺乏针对LLM的理论与保证，研究相对较少。

Method: 建立了理论框架，证明无偏激活压缩对线性算子是安全的，但对非线性算子存在问题；在L-平滑假设下推导了梯度方差界限并建立了收敛保证，证明激活压缩不改变收敛率。基于此理论，提出了激活-梯度协同压缩方法，复用低秩激活因子来压缩线性层梯度，无需额外计算或引入额外梯度误差。

Result: 在Qwen和LLaMA模型上进行了广泛的实验，包括预训练基准和多个微调基准。实验验证了理论的正确性，并展示了该方法在准确性和压缩效率方面具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究为LLM激活压缩提供了坚实的理论基础，证明了线性算子激活压缩的安全性与收敛性。提出的激活-梯度协同压缩方法在理论与实践上均表现优异，为降低LLM训练内存开销提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) is highly memory-intensive, as training must store not only weights and optimizer states but also intermediate activations for backpropagation. While existing memory-efficient methods largely focus on gradients and optimizer states, activation compression is less well established due to the lack of LLM-tailored theory and guarantees. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework showing that activation compression is safe for linear operators when activation compression is unbiased, but problematic for nonlinear ones. We further derive gradient variance bound and establish convergence guarantees for applying activation compression to all linear operators under the standard $L$-smoothness assumption, showing that it does not change the convergence rate. Guided by the theory, we propose an activation-gradient co-compression method that reuses low-rank activation factors to compress linear-layer gradients without extra computation or additional gradient error. We conduct extensive experiments on Qwen and LLaMA models using a pretraining benchmark and multiple fine-tuning benchmarks to validate our theory and demonstrate competitive performance of our method in both accuracy and compression efficiency. We provide our code in the supplementary material for reproducibility.

</details>


### [121] [Continuous Temporal Representations of Event-Based Signals via Interference-Based Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01270)
*Magnus Bengtsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于干涉波表示的连续时间建模框架，用于处理事件驱动的生物信号（如sEMG），通过复数值潜在波场和相位调制编码时间结构，在无需显式递归的情况下实现了优于实值表示的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动的生物过程（如表面肌电信号sEMG）产生的时空信号具有异步且高度结构化的激活模式，传统的离散或纯实值表示方法难以有效建模这些复杂的信号特征。

Method: 提出基于干涉波的连续时间建模框架：将事件型输入信号映射到复数值潜在波场，通过相位调制和潜在分量间的相互作用编码时间结构；将波场投影到能量域，诱导结构化激活模式，在有限观测窗口内捕获时间定位和关系依赖，无需依赖显式递归或因果状态传播。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法相比纯实值表示提供了更好的表示质量，同时保持适合实际部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该框架特别适用于事件驱动的生物信号处理，能够支持从sEMG数据中学习以用于生物力学系统（如假肢和外骨骼）的下游控制任务，连续表示使得基于梯度的优化高效且特征提取鲁棒。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal signals arising from event-driven biological processes, such as surface electromyography (sEMG), exhibit asynchronous and highly structured activation patterns that are challenging to model using conventional discrete or purely real-valued representations.
  In this work, we propose a continuous temporal modeling framework based on interference-based wave representations. The approach maps event-like input signals into a complex-valued latent wave field, where temporal structure is encoded through phase modulation and interactions between latent components.
  By projecting the resulting wave field onto an energy domain, the model induces structured activation patterns that capture both temporal localization and relational dependencies within finite observation windows, without relying on explicit recurrence or causal state propagation.
  The proposed formulation is particularly suited for event-driven biosignals, where continuous representations enable efficient gradient-based optimization and robust feature extraction. In particular, the method is designed to support learning from sEMG data for downstream control tasks in biomechanical systems, such as prosthetic devices and exoskeletons.
  Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed interference-based wave model provides improved representation quality compared to purely real-valued representations, while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [122] [A Theory of Saddle Escape in Deep Nonlinear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01288)
*Divit Rawal,Michael R. DeWeese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对小初始化深度非线性网络，推导出权重矩阵范数不平衡的精确恒等式，将激活函数分为四个普适类，并发现逃逸时间由瓶颈层数r决定（τ∝ε^-(r-2)），而非总深度L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在小初始化下训练呈现长平台期和特征获取的突变，但现有理论主要局限于浅层非线性网络或深层线性网络，难以扩展到深层非线性网络。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，理解深层非线性网络的训练动力学。

Method: 1. 推导权重矩阵Frobenius范数不平衡的精确恒等式（适用于任意光滑激活函数和可微损失）；
2. 基于该恒等式将激活函数分类为四个普适类；
3. 在置换对称子流形上结合近似平衡律，将完整的矩阵流简化为标量ODE；
4. 分析He-normal初始化下瓶颈层缩放对动力学的影响。

Result: 1. 发现逃逸时间临界定律：τ* = Θ(ε^-(r-2))，其中r为瓶颈尺度的层数而非总深度L；
2. 在He-normal初始化（瓶颈层缩放ε）下，对称流形被流保持但不吸引，且恢复相同的r-2指数；
3. 理论预测与数值模拟高度吻合。

Conclusion: 本文为深层非线性网络的训练动力学提供了理论框架，揭示了逃逸时间由瓶颈层数而非总深度决定的关键规律，并通过激活函数普适类分类为理解不同激活函数的影响提供了新视角。

Abstract: In deep networks with small initialization, training exhibits long plateaus separated by sharp feature-acquisition transitions. Whereas shallow nonlinear networks and deep linear networks are well studied, extending these analyses to deep nonlinear networks remains challenging. We derive an exact identity for the imbalance of Frobenius norms of layer weight matrices that holds for any smooth activation and any differentiable loss and use this to classify activation functions into four universality classes. On the permutation-symmetric submanifold, the identity combines with an approximate balance law to reduce the full matrix flow to a scalar ODE, giving a critical-depth escape time law $τ_\star = Θ(\varepsilon^{-(r-2)})$ governed by the number $r$ of layers at the bottleneck scale rather than the total depth $L$. We find that this same $r-2$ exponent is recovered under He-normal initialization with $r$ bottleneck layers rescaled by $\varepsilon$, where the symmetry manifold is preserved by the flow but not attracting. We find close agreement between our theory and numerical simulations.

</details>


### [123] [Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01291)
*Dewei Bai,Hongxiang Peng,Yunyun Zeng,Ziyu Zhang,Hong Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拥堵感知动态轴突延迟机制，通过将延迟分解为通道级静态基础延迟和全局活动条件偏移，在减少约50%参数量的同时，显著提升了SNN在语音任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脉冲神经网络延迟学习方法主要将静态延迟分配给单个突触，导致延迟参数数量庞大且对输入依赖的活动动态适应性有限。

Method: 提出拥堵感知动态轴突延迟机制，将延迟分解为用于时间结构化的通道级静态基础延迟和全局活动条件偏移，后者根据不同的脉冲强度动态调节状态更新率；使用可微分线性插值学习延迟参数，并在推理时进行离散化。

Result: 在SHD数据集上达到93.75%准确率，在SSC上达到80.49%，在GSC-35上达到95.53%；与现有最先进的基于延迟的方法相比，在相同架构下参数量减少约50%。

Conclusion: 在突触信号传输中引入拥堵感知延迟能够有效提高SNN在时序任务上的准确性，同时显著降低参数量。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are widely regarded as an energy-efficient paradigm for modeling and processing temporal and event-driven information. Incorporating delays in SNNs has been proven to be an effective mechanism for improving spike alignment in event-driven tasks. However, existing delay learning approaches predominantly assign static delays to individual synapses, resulting in a large number of delay parameters and limited adaptability to input-dependent activity dynamics. To this end, we propose a Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay mechanism, decomposing the delay into a channel-wise static base delay for temporal structuring and a global, activity-conditioned shift that dynamically regulates the state update rate under varying spike intensities. The delay parameters are learned using differentiable linear interpolation and discretized at inference time, preserving the benefits of our dynamic delay while incurring only minimal additional cost. Experiments on speech benchmarks, including the Spiking Heidelberg Dataset, Spiking Speech Commands, and Google Speech Commands, demonstrate that introducing congestion-aware delays into synaptic signal transmission effectively improves accuracy on temporal tasks, notably achieving 93.75\% accuracy on SHD, 80.49\% accuracy on SSC, and 95.53\% on GSC-35, while reducing the parameter count by approximately 50\% compared to state-of-the-art delay-based methods with the same architecture.

</details>


### [124] [Autonomous Drift Learning in Data Streams: A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01295)
*Xiaoyu Yang,En Yu,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自主学习系统中的非平稳性问题，提出了一种基于系统运行状态的新型三维分类体系，系统梳理了193项研究，为构建能够自主持续学习的自演化智能系统提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在构建自主学习系统时，传统的平稳性假设（即数据分布和模型行为保持不变）从根本上难以成立。虽然研究界通过“概念漂移”视角关注时序变化已有时日，但随着学习系统日趋自主化和复杂化，仅适应时间维度的非平稳性已显不足，亟需更全面的视角来理解和应对环境变化。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的三维分类体系来系统化该领域：
1. **时间流漂移**：区分随机任意模式与结构性节律动态；
2. **数据流漂移**：解耦特征表示的变化（表示漂移）与底层语义的变化（语义漂移）；
3. **模型流漂移**：从序列可塑性、去中心化异质性和策略不稳定性三个视角刻画学习系统内部的内生分歧。
基于此框架，系统回顾了193项代表性研究。

Result: 通过三维分类体系，成功系统化了原本碎片化的漂移适应、持续学习和时序泛化等范式，识别出了关键的开放性挑战，并整合了现有研究脉络。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建统一的分类框架，跨越了漂移适应、持续学习和时序泛化之间的范式鸿沟，为构建具备持续变化中自主学习能力、能够自我演化的智能系统指明了研究方向和路径。

Abstract: In the pursuit of autonomous learning systems, the foundational assumption of stationarity, the premise that data distributions and model behaviors remain constant, is fundamentally untenable. Historically, the research community has addressed non-stationary environments almost exclusively under the scope of concept drift, focusing primarily on temporal shifts in streams. However, as learning systems become increasingly autonomous and complex, merely adapting to temporal non-stationarity is no longer sufficient. Evolving beyond this traditional perspective, we propose a novel, three-dimensional taxonomy that systematizes the field based on the operational state of the system. First, time stream drift distinguishes between stochastic arbitrary patterns and structural rhythmic dynamics. Second, data stream drift disentangles shifts in feature representations, identified as representation drift, from changes in underlying semantics, recognized as semantic drift. Third, model stream drift characterizes the internal endogenous divergence of learning systems through the lenses of sequential plasticity, decentralized heterogeneity, and policy instability. Based on this framework, we systematically review 193 representative studies and identify key open challenges. By bridging the fragmented paradigms of drift adaptation, continual learning, and temporal generalization, this survey outlines a roadmap for building self-evolving intelligent systems capable of learning autonomously through continuous change.

</details>


### [125] [GA-VisAgent: A Multi-Agent application for code generation and visualization in interactive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01299)
*Wang Jian,Zhou Jianbo,Xiong Yuhao,Liu Zhenxia,Luo Wen,Yuan LinWang,Yu ZhaoYuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GA-VisAgent，一个基于几何代数大语言模型（GAGPT）的多智能体交互式学习应用，用于GA代码生成与可视化。该系统通过任务规划机制和ReAct推理策略，将复杂操作分解为五个标准化子任务，在40个典型共形GA任务中实现了90%的代码生成成功率，比GPT-4o提高了70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何代数（GA）因其高度抽象的数学结构和复杂的运算规则给学习者带来挑战，将代数运算转化为具体的几何解释是一个非直观的过程。现有的GA软件包依赖手写脚本进行代码生成和可视化，学习曲线陡峭；而基于大语言模型的方法在生成特定GA脚本时经常产生逻辑错误，准确率较低。

Method: 提出了GA-VisAgent系统，该系统基于几何代数大语言模型（GAGPT）构建，集成了任务规划机制与ReAct推理策略。系统能够将复杂操作分解为五个标准化子任务（包括几何积、旋转和反射等核心操作），支持自然语言和数学公式作为输入，自动生成可执行代码，并配备交互式可视化功能辅助用户理解。

Result: 在40个典型共形GA任务的实验中，GA-VisAgent实现了90%的代码生成成功率，相比GPT-4o提升了70%。系统成功支持自然语言和数学公式输入，能够自动生成可执行代码并提供交互式可视化。

Conclusion: GA-VisAgent为教授几何代数和开发相关数学概念的可视化工具引入了一个可扩展的新范式，有效降低了GA学习的门槛，提高了代码生成的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Geometric Algebra (GA) presents challenges to learners due to its highly abstract mathematical structure and complex operational rules, as translating algebraic manipulations into concrete geometric interpretations is a non-intuitive process when developing related code. Currently, some existing GA software packages rely on manually written scripts for code generation and visualization, but their high learning curve hinders widespread adoption. Meanwhile, methods based on Large Language Models (LLMs) often produce logical errors when generating specific GA scripts, such as GAALOPScript, resulting in generally low accuracy. To address these issues, this study proposes GA-VisAgent -- a multi-agent interactive learning application for GA code generation and visualization -- building upon a Geometric algebra large language model (GAGPT). Integrating task planning mechanisms with ReAct reasoning strategies, GA-VisAgent can decompose complex operations into five standardized subtasks, including core operations like geometric products, rotations, and reflections. It supports natural language and mathematical formulas as input to automatically generate executable code, accompanied by interactive visualizations to aid user comprehension. Experimental results show that GA-VisAgent achieved a 90% code generation success rate across 40 typical Conformal GA tasks, representing a 70% improvement over GPT-4o. This application introduces an extensible new paradigm for teaching GA and developing visualization tools for related mathematical concepts. The online service for this project will be available at http://gagis.cn/gacrac.

</details>


### [126] [GraphSculptor: Sculpting Pre-training Coreset for Graph Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01310)
*Chuang Liu,Zelin Yao,Xueqi Ma,Luzhi Wang,Mukun Chen,Pinghua Xu,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphSculptor，一种用于图自监督预训练的数据集核心集构建方法，仅使用10%的数据即可保留99.6%的下游性能，并将预训练时间减少近90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图自监督学习通常依赖大规模无标签数据集，导致计算成本高昂。经验证据表明这些数据集存在大量冗余（均匀采样50%的图可保留96%以上的下游性能），这为通过构建核心集来提高数据效率提供了动机。

Method: 提出GraphSculptor方法，通过内在结构和上下文语义两个互补视角构建核心集：1）使用内在图统计量量化结构多样性，为每个图生成结构特征向量；2）利用预训练语言模型编码图转文本生成的描述，捕获语义多样性；3）将两种信号整合到统一度量空间，执行聚类感知选择以保持联合结构-语义多样性。该方法无需额外训练信号或标签，是一种无标签解决方案。

Result: 在10%核心集上实现了完整数据集99.6%的性能，同时将预训练时间减少近90%。理论分析推导了核心集与完整数据预训练之间的损失差距界限。广泛实验证明GraphSculptor能够有效精炼数据集，显著提高图预训练的可扩展性。

Conclusion: GraphSculptor成功解决了图自监督学习中的数据冗余问题，通过联合考虑结构多样性和语义多样性构建高质量核心集，在大幅降低计算成本的同时几乎不损失性能，为数据高效的图预训练提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning typically relies on large-scale unlabeled datasets, heavily inflating computational costs. However, empirical evidence suggests that these datasets contain substantial redundancy-our analysis reveals that uniformly subsampling 50% of graphs retains over 96% of downstream performance. To exploit this redundancy, we introduce GraphSculptor for pre-training coreset construction. Unlike methods dependent on additional training-time signals or limited solely to topological statistics, GraphSculptor provides a label-free solution that constructs coresets via two complementary perspectives: intrinsic structure and contextual semantics. Concretely, structural diversity is quantified using intrinsic graph statistics, yielding a structural feature vector for each graph, while semantic diversity is captured by utilizing a pre-trained language model to encode descriptions generated via graph-to-text. GraphSculptor integrates these signals into a unified metric space and performs cluster-aware selection to preserve joint structural-semantic diversity. We further derive a theoretical bound on the loss gap between coreset and full-data pre-training, offering theoretical motivation for our selection formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GraphSculptor effectively sculpts the dataset: a 10% coreset achieves 99.6% of full-data performance while reducing pre-training time by nearly 90%, offering a scalable solution for data-efficient graph pre-training.

</details>


### [127] [The Partial Testimony of Logs: Evaluation of Language Model Generation under Confounded Model Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01311)
*Jikai Jin,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种三源数据设计（观测日志+小规模随机实验+离线模拟器），利用随机实验和模拟器消除混淆偏差，实现了对语言模型的因果离线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型离线评估存在混淆偏差：用户选择模型的因素与模型输出评价结果相关，导致直接比较日志分数无法反映真实性能差异；而消除偏差的随机实验成本高昂且稀缺。

Method: 提出三源设计框架：大规模混淆观测日志(OBS)提供规模、小规模随机实验(EXP)提供无偏评分、离线模拟器(SIM)在缓存上下文重放候选模型；通过识别定理证明EXP和SIM足以恢复因果模型值，OBS仅用于减少估计误差。

Result: 在受控半合成验证实验和两个真实任务缓存基准（摘要和代码生成）上评估了六种估计器家族；结果表明没有单一估计器在所有情况下占优，性能取决于无偏EXP监督量和目标奖励与OBS结构的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了小规模随机实验与离线模拟器结合可突破观测数据的混淆偏差，为实际场景中低成本、高可靠的语言模型离线因果评估提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Offline evaluation of language models from usage logs is biased when model choice is confounded: the same user-side factors that influence which model is used can also influence how its output is judged, so raw comparisons of logged scores mix self-selected populations rather than estimating a common quantity of interest. A small randomized experiment can break this bias by overriding model choice, but in practice such experiments are scarce and costly. We study a three-source design that combines a large confounded observational log (OBS) for scale, a small randomized experiment (EXP) for unconfounded scoring, and an offline simulator (SIM) that replays candidate models on cached contexts. Our main result is an identification theorem showing that the randomized experiment and the simulator are together enough to recover causal model values; the observational log enters only afterward, to reduce estimation error rather than to make the causal comparison valid. Six estimator families are evaluated in a controlled semi-synthetic validation and in two real-task cached benchmarks for summarization and coding. No family dominates every regime; relative performance depends on the amount of unbiased EXP supervision and on how closely the target reward aligns with OBS-derived structure.

</details>


### [128] [Robust Parameter Learning for Uncertain MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01339)
*Yannik Schnitzer,Alessandro Abate,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于参数化马尔可夫决策过程（pMDP）的学习方法，通过将统计不确定性映射到参数空间并引入多面体外逼近层级，解决了传统不确定MDP忽略转移概率间依赖关系的问题，实现了更紧致的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的未知马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）验证方法通常采用不确定MDP模型，例如使用置信区间来捕捉转移不确定性。然而，这种方法通常独立量化各个转移概率的不确定性，忽略了由于共享潜在量而产生的依赖关系，导致不确定性估计不够精确。

Method: 提出使用参数化MDP（pMDP）来学习模型，其中转移概率是参数集上的表达式。将来自经验转移频率的统计不确定性投影到pMDP的参数空间，生成一个可能近似正确（PAC）的不确定性模型。针对求解困难的问题，提出了诱导置信集的可靠多面体外逼近层级方法。

Result: 实验评估表明，与传统的基于区间的不确定MDP学习技术相比，该方法能够获得显著更紧致的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 通过利用参数化MDP和参数空间投影，该方法能够更准确地捕捉转移概率之间的代数依赖关系，从而提供比传统方法更紧致的不确定性估计，为未知MDP的验证提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to verifying unknown Markov decision processes (MDPs) often employ uncertain MDPs. These models use, for example, confidence intervals to capture transition uncertainty and allow synthesis of policies that are robust to this uncertainty. However, this approach typically quantifies uncertainty independently for individual transition probabilities, ignoring dependencies due to shared latent quantities. We propose to learn such models using parametric MDPs (pMDPs), where transition probabilities are expressions over a set of parameters. We project statistical uncertainty from empirical transition frequencies onto the pMDP's parameter space, yielding a probably approximately correct (PAC) uncertainty model for the underlying MDP that respects the algebraic dependencies between transitions. The resulting models are algorithmically challenging to solve, so we propose a hierarchy of sound polytopic outer approximations of the induced confidence set. We implement and evaluate our approach, demonstrating substantially tighter uncertainty estimates than classical interval-based uncertain MDP learning techniques.

</details>


### [129] [Model-Based Proactive Cost Generation for Learning Safe Policies Offline with Limited Violation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01356)
*Ruiqi Xue,Lei Yuan,Kainuo Cheng,Jing-Wen Yang,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROCO框架，针对缺乏不安全样本的离线安全强化学习场景，利用大语言模型（LLM）的常识知识构建保守代价函数，并通过模型推演合成反事实不安全样本，有效解决了传统方法在稀缺风险数据下的安全决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险安全关键场景中，在线试错不可行，导致离线数据集缺乏或完全不包含不安全样本。现有方法通常将所有数据视为均匀安全，忽视了“安全但不可行”状态（当前满足约束但几步内必然违规的状态），从而导致部署失败。传统依赖从大量不安全样本学习代价函数的方法在此限制下失效。

Method: 提出PROCO（知识数据融合的离线安全强化学习框架）。该方法首先从离线数据学习动力学模型，然后利用大语言模型（LLM）中的自然语言知识来构建保守代价函数，实现在无观测违规情况下的风险估计。基于该代价函数和学习到的模型，PROCO执行基于模型的推演来合成多样化的反事实不安全样本，支持可靠的可行性识别和可行性引导的策略学习。

Result: 在Safety-Gymnasium任务上进行了广泛实验，训练数据仅包含安全或极少量风险样本。PROCO能够与多种离线安全强化学习算法无缝集成，相比原始方法和其他行为克隆基线，在约束违规率和安全性能方面均表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的自然语言知识与离线数据相结合，PROCO成功解决了数据集中缺乏不安全样本时的安全强化学习难题。该方法证明了知识数据集成范式在安全关键决策中的有效性，为无法进行在线风险探索的高风险场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning constraint-satisfying policies from offline data without risky online interaction is crucial for safety-critical decision making. Conventional methods typically learn cost value functions from abundant unsafe samples to define safety boundaries and penalize violations. However, in high-stakes scenarios, risky trial-and-error is infeasible, yielding datasets with few or no unsafe samples. Under this limitation, existing approaches often treat all data as uniformly safe, overlooking safe-but-infeasible states - states that currently satisfy constraints but inevitably violate them within a few steps - leading to deployment failures. Drawing inspiration from the concept of knowledge-data integration, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to incorporate natural language knowledge into the policy to address this challenge. Specifically, we propose PROCO, a model-based offline safe reinforcement learning (RL) framework tailored to datasets largely free of violations. PROCO first learns a dynamics model from offline data and constructs a conservative cost function by grounding natural-language knowledge of unsafe states in LLMs, enabling risk estimation even without observed violations. Using the cost function and learned model, PROCO performs model-based rollouts to synthesize diverse counterfactual unsafe samples, supporting reliable feasibility identification and feasibility-guided policy learning. Across a range of Safety-Gymnasium tasks with exclusively safe or minimally risky training data, PROCO integrates seamlessly with a variety of offline safe RL algorithms and consistently demonstrates reduced constraint violations and improved safety performance compared to both the original methods and other behavior cloning baselines.

</details>


### [130] [PACE: Parameter Change for Unsupervised Environment Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01358)
*Fang Yuan,Quanjun Yin,Siqi Shen,Yuxiang Xie,Junqiang Yang,Long Qin,Junjie Zeng,Qinglun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PACE方法，通过策略参数变化直接评估环境的学习价值，解决了现有UED方法中评估信号偏差大、方差高或计算开销大的问题，在MiniGrid和Craftax上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督环境设计(UED)方法依赖间接代理信号（如regret、基于价值的误差或蒙特卡洛）来评估环境质量，这些方法存在偏差大、方差高或计算开销大等问题，且无法真实反映智能体的实际学习进展。

Method: 提出参数变化环境设计(PACE)方法，通过在特定环境上训练所引起的策略参数变化来评估环境价值。该方法使用策略优化目标的一阶近似，将环境改进量化为对应参数更新的L2范数平方，无需额外rollout即可实现低方差且计算高效的评估。

Result: 在MiniGrid和Craftax基准测试上，PACE方法持续优于现有UED基线方法，在分布外(OOD)评估中实现了更高的IQM和更小的Optimality Gap。具体而言，在MiniGrid上达到96.4%的IQM和17.2%的Optimality Gap。

Conclusion: PACE通过直接将环境选择建立在已实现的学习进展之上，为UED提供了一种可靠、高效的环境评估方法，有效提升了强化学习的泛化能力。

Abstract: Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) offers a promising paradigm for improving reinforcement learning generalization by adaptively shaping training environments, but it requires reliable environment evaluation to remain effective. However, existing UED methods evaluate environments using indirect proxy signals such as regret, value-based errors, or Monte Carlo, which suffer from bias, high variance, or substantial computational overhead and fail to reflect agent realized learning progress. To address these limitations, we propose Parameter Change Environment Design (PACE), which evaluates an environment through the policy parameter change induced by training on that environment, directly grounding environment selection in realized learning progress. Specifically, PACE assigns environment value using a first-order approximation of the policy optimization objective, where the improvement induced by an environment is proportional to the squared L2 norm of the corresponding parameter update, enabling low-variance and computation-efficient evaluation without additional rollouts. Experiments on MiniGrid and Craftax show that PACE consistently outperforms established UED baselines, achieving higher IQM and smaller Optimality Gap on OOD evaluations, including an IQM of 96.4% and an Optimality Gap of 17.2% on MiniGrid.

</details>


### [131] [Decision-Focused Learning via Tangent-Space Projection of Prediction Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01361)
*Junhyeong Lee,Sangjin Jin,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PEAR方法，通过将预测误差投影到活动约束的切空间来计算遗憾梯度，避免了通过求解器微分，实现了更优的决策质量和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习(DFL)方法在计算遗憾梯度时需要通过求解器微分或依赖代理损失，存在计算昂贵或偏离真实目标的问题，亟需一种更直接高效的梯度计算方法。

Method: 基于标准正则性条件和局部稳定活动约束假设，推导出遗憾梯度具有闭式几何表征：等于投影到活动约束切空间的预测误差，并按局部曲率缩放。据此提出PEAR算法，通过活动约束上的简化线性系统计算遗憾梯度，避免了求解器迭代微分或额外优化求解。

Result: 在LP基准测试和真实世界QP任务上的实验表明，PEAR在所有基线方法中实现了最佳决策质量，同时计算效率最高，且在约束偏移情况下优势持续存在。

Conclusion: 遗憾梯度可通过过滤MSE梯度中的决策无关分量获得，该几何洞察为DFL提供了更简单直接的替代方案，PEAR方法在效果和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Decision-Focused Learning (DFL) trains predictors to improve downstream decision quality, but computing regret gradients typically requires differentiating through solvers or relying on surrogate losses, which can be computationally expensive or deviate from the true objective. We show that, under standard regularity with locally stable active constraints, the regret gradient admits a closed-form geometric characterization, equivalent to the prediction error projected onto the tangent space of active constraints, scaled by local curvature. This reveals that regret gradients can be obtained by filtering decision-irrelevant components from the MSE gradient, providing a simpler and more direct alternative to existing approaches. Based on this, we propose PEAR (Projected Error As Regret-gradient), which computes regret gradients via a reduced linear system over active constraints, avoiding differentiation through solver iterations or additional optimization solves. Experiments on LP benchmarks and a real-world QP task show that PEAR achieves the best decision quality among all baselines while being the most computationally efficient, with gains that persist under constraint shifts.

</details>


### [132] [Toward a foundational thermal model for residential buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01364)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Kingsley Nweye,Dev Niyogi,Zoltan Nagy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息驱动的Transformer架构，旨在构建通用的建筑热动力学基础模型。该模型在无需针对特定建筑校准的情况下，实现了跨建筑、气候和控制策略的泛化能力，并在247栋住宅建筑的测试中取得了优异的预测精度和零样本迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗领域长期缺乏一个能够跨不同建筑、气候区和控制策略进行泛化的基础热动力学模型。现有的热模型通常需要针对特定建筑进行校准，缺乏通用性。为了解决这一限制，研究旨在通过设计能够捕捉普适热动力学规律的架构原则，而非记忆特定建筑模式，从而建立一个单一预训练的通用模型。

Method: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的Transformer架构。该方法将领域知识嵌入到Decoder-only框架中，具体包括导数增强和基于欧拉法的数值积分。模型融合了从仿真模型中提取的静态建筑特征，并采用旋转位置嵌入注意力机制来捕捉时间依赖性。

Result: 在涵盖三个气候区247栋住宅建筑的CityLearn数据集上，模型实现了高精度的一步预测（德克萨斯州RMSE为0.30°C，佛蒙特州RMSE为0.29°C），性能优于传统基线模型和经过微调的时间序列基础模型。此外，验证了零样本迁移能力：仅在两栋建筑上训练的模型即可在无需微调的情况下泛化至未见过的建筑和气候区。

Conclusion: 尽管研究局限于仿真环境下的住宅建筑，但实验结果证明了将物理信息驱动的架构原则作为通用建筑热模型基础具有巨大潜力。这为未来开发无需针对特定建筑进行繁琐校准的通用能耗预测模型提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The building energy community lacks a foundational thermal model, i.e., a single pretrained model capable of generalizing across diverse buildings, climates, and control strategies without building-specific calibration. Achieving this vision requires architectural principles that capture universal thermal dynamics rather than memorizing building-specific patterns. We take a step toward this goal by presenting a physics-informed transformer architecture that embeds domain knowledge, e.g., derivative enrichment and Euler-based numerical integration, into a decoder-only framework. We incorporate static building features extracted from simulation models and employ Rotary Position Embedding attention to capture temporal dependencies. Evaluated on the CityLearn dataset spanning 247 residential buildings across three climate zones, our model achieves one-step prediction accuracy (RMSE of 0.30°C in Texas, 0.29°C in Vermont) while outperforming both traditional baselines and fine-tuned Time-Series Foundation Models. We also demonstrate zero-shot transferability: models trained on as few as two buildings generalize to unseen buildings and climate zones without fine-tuning. Despite the limitation of simulated residential buildings, our results establish physics-informed architectural principles as a promising foundation for universal building thermal models.

</details>


### [133] [Sequential Learning and Catastrophic Forgetting in Differentiable Resistor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01383)
*Maniru Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用可微分电阻网络作为物理可解释的持续学习测试平台，揭示了拓扑结构和任务冲突如何控制灾难性遗忘与适应性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究持续学习中的灾难性遗忘现象，作者需要一个简单、可解释的物理系统作为模型。可微分电阻网络提供了一个理想的研究平台，能够将学习过程与物理平衡约束（基尔霍夫定律）联系起来，从而从物理角度理解学习过程中的遗忘机制。

Method: 研究者构建了遵循基尔霍夫定律的可微分电阻网络，通过梯度下降调整边电导来训练网络完成输入-输出映射任务。他们采用序列训练方式，先后在冲突任务上训练网络，测试遗忘程度。同时，引入了统一锚定和归一化梯度加权锚定策略，并在Erdős-Rényi、小世界、无标量和随机几何图等多种网络拓扑上进行了对比实验。

Result: 实验发现：（1）遗忘程度由任务冲突强度和适应新任务的程度共同控制；（2）锚定策略只能通过牺牲新任务性能来减少遗忘，存在明确的遗忘-适应权衡；（3）遗忘与高电流边上的电导变化相关，具有重配置主导传输路径的物理解释；（4）当第二个任务逆转第一个任务施加的输出排序时，遗忘最严重；（5）不同网络拓扑会改变遗忘-适应平衡。

Conclusion: 可微分电阻网络作为一个紧凑、物理可解释的测试平台，能够有效研究可调物质中的持续学习问题，为理解灾难性遗忘的物理机制提供了新视角，揭示了网络拓扑、任务冲突与学习性能之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Differentiable physical networks provide a simple setting in which learning can be studied through the interaction between trainable parameters and physical equilibrium constraints. We investigate sequential learning in differentiable resistor networks governed by Kirchhoff's laws. Although individual input--output mappings can be learned by gradient-based adjustment of edge conductances, sequential training on conflicting tasks produces catastrophic forgetting. We show that forgetting is controlled by task conflict and by the degree of adaptation to the new task. Uniform anchoring and normalised gradient-weighted anchoring reduce forgetting only by increasing the final loss on the new task, giving a clear forgetting--adaptation trade-off. We also show that forgetting is associated with localised conductance changes on high-current edges, giving a physical interpretation as reconfiguration of dominant transport pathways. Broader random-task ensembles show that the strongest forgetting occurs when the second task reverses the output ordering imposed by the first task. Finally, comparisons across Erdős--Rényi, small-world, scale-free, and random-geometric graph ensembles show that topology changes the forgetting--adaptation balance. These results position differentiable resistor networks as compact, physically interpretable testbeds for studying continual learning in tunable matter.

</details>


### [134] [Rethinking Multi-Label Node Classification: Do Tuned Classic GNNs Suffice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01403)
*Yuxuan Xiao,Shengzhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性调优经典全图GNN骨干网络（GCN、SSGConv、GCNII），发现在多标签节点分类任务中，这些优化后的基线模型在五个基准数据集的四个上超越了现有复杂专用方法，并在多个设置下达到SOTA性能。这表明在多标签图学习研究中，经典模型的精心调优是一个被忽视的关键因素，未来需要更严格的强基线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多标签节点分类（MLNC）研究领域涌现出越来越多复杂的标签感知设计，这些方法显式地建模节点-标签交互和标签间依赖关系。然而，目前尚不清楚这些方法的优势是否真正源于其专用设计，还是仅仅因为基线模型未被充分优化。作者希望从强基线的视角重新审视MLNC任务，探究经过精心调优的经典全图GNN是否能够作为该任务的强力解决方案。

Method: 作者系统性地研究了三种代表性的GNN骨干网络：GCN、SSGConv和GCNII。通过采用标准化（normalization）、dropout和残差连接等标准且有效的技术对这些经典模型进行系统性优化和调优，构建了强基线模型。实验在五个代表性基准数据集上进行，与现有的代表性专用方法进行全面对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过精心调优的经典GNN基线模型在五个基准数据集中的四个上超越了代表性的专用方法，并在多个实验设置下达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能表现。这一发现证明了经典骨干网络的仔细调优在MLNC任务中是一个极具影响力但经常被忽视的因素。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调了在多标签图学习研究中进行更严格强基线评估的必要性。结果表明，复杂的专用设计所带来的性能提升可能被高估，而经典模型的系统性调优能够取得竞争力甚至更优的性能。这一发现对领域发展具有重要指导意义，提醒研究者在提出新方法时应确保基线模型得到充分优化，以得出更可靠的结论。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification (MLNC) has recently been addressed by increasingly complex label-aware designs that explicitly model node-label interactions and inter-label dependencies.However, it remains unclear whether the advantages of these methods truly stem from their specialized designs, or simply from insufficiently optimized baselines. In this paper, we revisit MLNC from a strong-baseline perspective and investigate whether carefully tuned classic full-graph GNNs can already serve as strong solutions to this task. We systematically study several representative backbones, including GCN, SSGConv, and GCNII, and optimize them using standard yet effective techniques such as normalization, dropout, and residual connections. Experiments on five representative benchmark datasets show that our tuned baselines outperform representative specialized methods on four datasets and achieve state-of-the-art performance in multiple settings. These results indicate that careful tuning of classic backbones is a highly influential but often overlooked factor in MLNC, and highlight the need for more rigorous strong-baseline evaluation in future research on multi-label graph learning.

</details>


### [135] [Quantifying Multimodal Capabilities: Formal Generalization Guarantees in Pairwise Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01424)
*Richeng Zhou,Xuelin Zhang,Liyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态度量学习，通过理论分析推导出泛化误差的新上下界，证明了利用细粒度模态特征能降低假设空间复杂度，从而提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习在现实场景中常面临模态数据不完整或冗余的问题，且学术界对于模态选择与算法性能之间的关系缺乏深入的理论理解。

Method: 建立了不同模态子集对应函数类之间的层次关系，量化了学习映射与真实映射间的差异，并通过分析多模态学习框架内的成对复杂性，推导出新的泛化误差界。

Result: 推导出的泛化误差上下界揭示了模态数量和粒度对模型性能的联合影响；理论分析表明，引入细粒度模态特征能够通过增强模态互补性来降低假设空间的复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究为多模态学习系统提高收敛速度和准确率提供了理论依据和实践启示，填补了多模态学习泛化性质分析的理论空白。

Abstract: Multimodal learning leverages the integration of diverse data modalities to enhance performance in complex tasks. Yet, it frequently encounters incomplete or redundant modality data in real-world scenarios. This paper presents a fine-grained theoretical analysis of the generalization properties of multimodal metric learning models, addressing critical gaps in understanding the relationship between modality selection and algorithmic performance. We establish hierarchical relationships between function classes corresponding to different modality subsets and quantify the discrepancy between learned mappings and ground truth. Through rigorous analysis of pairwise complexity within the multimodal learning framework, we derive novel generalization error bounds that reveal the joint impact of modality quantity and granularity on model performance. Our theoretical findings on both upper and lower bounds demonstrate that incorporating fine-grained modality features reduces the complexity of the hypothesis space by enhancing modality complementarity. This work offers both theoretical foundations and practical implications for improving convergence rates and accuracy in multimodal learning systems.

</details>


### [136] [Barriers to Counterfactual Credit Attribution for Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01425)
*Aloni Cohen,Chenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究具有反事实信用归因（CCA）的生成式模型，发现自回归模型中CCA不可组合，且在弱最优性要求下，对黑盒模型进行CCA改造需要输出长度指数级的查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI打破了传统的信用归属实践。理想的生成模型应能对其输出显著依赖的前作给予信用。Livni等人（2024）提出的反事实信用归因（CCA）形式化了这一目标，这是差分隐私的一种松弛形式，但目前尚无对CCA生成模型的研究。

Method: 研究自回归模型如何对部署时数据集（如RAG数据库）进行信用归因。分析了两种自然方法：(1) 对底层下一token预测器施加CCA约束；(2) "改造"方法——为不具信用归因能力的模型添加信用归因功能。

Result: 发现两个主要障碍：(1) 对底层下一token预测器施加CCA约束不能保证整个模型满足CCA——与差分隐私不同，CCA在自回归方式下不可组合；(2) 在弱最优性要求下，改造黑盒模型需要输出长度指数级的查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 自回归生成模型实现CCA存在根本性困难，包括CCA的自回归不可组合性以及改造方法的指数级查询复杂度下界，这些发现为构建具有信用归因能力的生成模型提供了理论边界。

Abstract: Generative AI disrupts the practice of giving credit to work that came before. Ideally, a generative model would give credit to any work on which its output depends in a significant way. \emph{Counterfactual credit attribution} (CCA) is a technical condition formalizing this goal--a relaxation of differential privacy--recently introduced by Livni, Moran, Nissim, and Pabbaraju [2024] who studied it in the PAC learning setting.
  We initiate the study of CCA generative models. Specifically, we consider autoregressive models giving credit to a deployment-time dataset (e.g., a RAG database). We uncover barriers to two natural approaches to CCA autoregressive models. First, we show that imposing CCA on the underlying next-token predictor does not guarantee that the model is CCA: CCA does not compose autoregressively (unlike DP). Second, we consider a different approach to building CCA models which we call \emph{retrofitting}. Retrofitting takes a model that does not attribute credit, and adds credit onto it. We prove a lower bound for CCA retrofitting under a weak optimality requirement. Given black-box access to the starting model, retrofitting requires query complexity exponential in the length of the model's outputs.

</details>


### [137] [Evaluating LLMs on Large-Scale Graph Property Estimation via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01484)
*Sunil Kumar Maurya,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对大规模不可完全访问图的推理基准数据集EstGraph，设计了四个图属性估计任务，并提出了基于随机游走采样的提示构建方法，使LLM能在有限上下文长度内处理百万级节点的大图推理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图推理基准仅包含小规模图，无法评估LLM在真实世界大规模、不可完全访问图上的推理能力。随着LLM推理能力的提升，亟需评估其在受限访问条件下处理大规模图结构数据的能力。

Method: 构建大规模图基准数据集EstGraph，设计四个图属性估计任务；提出基于随机游走采样的任务特定提示构建方法，在上下文长度限制内有效向LLM传递大规模图（可达百万节点）的充分信息。

Result: 在多种图数据集上评估了LLM的推理能力，验证了所提出的随机游走采样提示方法能够有效使LLM在上下文限制下处理大规模图，并完成了对大图属性估计任务的系统性评测。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了大规模图推理基准的空白，为评估LLM在真实世界大规模图数据上的推理能力提供了有效方法和基准，证明了通过合理的采样和提示构建可以使LLM处理超出其上下文长度限制的大规模图结构问题。

Abstract: With the rapidly improving reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is also a rising demand to use them in a wide variety of domains. This brings about the need to carefully evaluate the limits of the capabilities of these models with various tests and benchmarks. Graph structures are ubiquitous in real-world data, and are often used to represent and analyze relationship patterns within data. Many benchmarks have already been proposed in the graph literature to test the reasoning ability of LLMs to follow and execute graph algorithms. However, due to the limited context length of LLMs, these benchmarks consist of very small graphs. In real-world data, the size of graphs can be significantly larger, and in many cases, not fully accessible. In this paper, we examine a class of problems that arises with very large graphs having limited accessibility. We propose a large graph benchmark dataset, EstGraph, and introduce four distinct tasks designed to estimate large graph properties. We evaluate the reasoning abilities of LLMs on these tasks using a wide variety of graph datasets. In addition, we provide task-specific prompt constructions based on random walk sampling of large graphs (up to millions of nodes) that effectively convey sufficient information to LLMs within the limits of context length.

</details>


### [138] [Protein-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Full-Length mRNA Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01513)
*Zixi Shao,Tao Wang,Yibei Xiao,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProMORNA，一个多目标生成框架，能够直接从目标蛋白序列从头设计完整的mRNA转录本，并在计算评估中提升了预测半衰期和翻译效率的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗性信使RNA（mRNA）的设计需要在全长转录本中精细平衡稳定性、翻译效率和免疫安全性，这是一个具有挑战性的多目标优化问题，现有方法难以同时满足这些相互制约的生物目标。

Method: 首先在超过600万个天然蛋白质-mRNA配对数据上训练BART风格的编码器-解码器模型，然后引入多目标群体相对策略优化（MO-GRPO）算法，在统一框架下同时优化多种生物学目标。研究以萤火虫荧光素酶为目标进行案例评估，该目标被排除在监督训练数据和提示池之外。

Result: ProMORNA相对于标准监督基线改善了预测半衰期和翻译效率的计算机帕累托前沿；在相同评估流程下，其预测功能得分高于最先进基线。

Conclusion: 计算研究结果证明了使用多目标强化学习对未见目标进行全长mRNA设计的可行性，为治疗性mRNA设计提供了新的方法路径。

Abstract: Designing therapeutic messenger RNA (mRNA) requires creating full-length transcripts that carefully balance stability, translation efficiency, and immune safety. To address this challenge, we propose ProMORNA, a multi-objective generation framework that produces complete mRNA transcripts \textit{de novo} directly from a target protein sequence. Our approach begins by training a BART-style encoder-decoder model on over 6 million natural protein-mRNA pairs. We then introduce Multi-Objective Group Relative Policy Optimization (MO-GRPO) to simultaneously optimize for various biological objectives in a unified way. As a case study, we evaluated ProMORNA on the widely used firefly luciferase target, excluding it from both our supervised training data and the prompt pool. The results indicate that ProMORNA improves the \textit{in silico} Pareto frontier for predicted half-life and translation efficiency relative to standard supervised baselines. Additionally, it achieves higher predicted functional scores than a state-of-the-art baseline under the same evaluation pipeline. These computational findings demonstrate the feasibility of using multi-objective reinforcement learning for full-length mRNA design on unseen targets.

</details>


### [139] [Mesh Based Simulations with Spatial and Temporal awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01542)
*Paul Garnier,Vincent Lannelongue,Elie Hachem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对CFD代理模型中现有训练范式的局限性，提出了包含多节点预测、时间校正和几何归纳偏置的统一框架，显著提升了仿真的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CFD机器学习代理模型（如GNN和Transformer）虽然在架构上有所进步，但其训练范式仍受限于节点级监督和显式欧拉时间步进等朴素假设。这些方法忽略了偏微分方程求解（如有限元法）中固有的刚性动力学和局部通量连续性，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了三个关键创新点：(1) 多节点预测：在模板层级预测节点完整局部拓扑的场值，强制空间导数一致性；(2) 时间校正：通过时间交叉注意力机制，用预测-校正器替代不稳定的显式方案；(3) 几何归纳偏置：利用3D旋转位置编码（RoPE）稳健捕获非结构化网格中的旋转对称性。

Result: 在三种架构（MeshGraphNet、Transolver和Transformer）和多种物理数据集上的评估表明，该方法在准确性和稳定性方面均有持续提升，特别是在长时间范围的推演中表现优异。此外，生成的潜在表示能够泛化到未见过的子任务，如壁面剪切应力或压力预测。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了几何深度学习与严格数值分析之间的鸿沟，通过引入更符合物理本质的训练目标和方法，显著提升了机器学习代理模型在物理仿真中的性能，为未来研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Machine Learning surrogates for Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), particularly Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Transformers, have become a new important approach for accelerating physics simulations. However, we identify a critical bottleneck in the field: while architectures have advanced significantly, the common underlying training paradigms remain bound to naive assumptions, such as node-wise supervision and explicit Euler time-stepping. These legacy choices ignore the stiff dynamics and local flux continuity inherent to numerous partial differential equations resolution methods, such as Finite Element, Difference, or Volume (FEM). In this work, we propose a unified framework to bridge the gap between geometric deep learning and rigorous numerical analysis. We introduce three key innovations: (1) Multi Node Prediction, a stencil-level objective that predicts field values for a node's full local topology, enforcing spatial derivative consistency; (2) Temporal Correction, replacing unstable explicit schemes with a predictor-corrector via temporal Cross-Attention; and (3) Geometric Inductive Biases, leveraging 3D Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE) to robustly capture rotational symmetries in unstructured meshes. We evaluate this framework across three architectures (MeshGraphNet, Transolver, and a Transformer) on diverse physics datasets. Our approach yields consistent improvements in accuracy and stability, particularly in long-horizon rollouts, while producing latent representations that generalize to unseen subtasks such as Wall Shear Stress or Pressure prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/DonsetPG/graph-physics.

</details>


### [140] [ECG-biometrics-bench: A Unified Framework for Reproducible Benchmarking of ECG Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01548)
*Milad Parvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ECG-biometrics-bench标准化基准框架，揭示了现有研究中因数据泄漏导致的“随机划分谬误”，发现跨会话评估下性能严重下降，并提出多会话模板融合策略缓解时序老化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG生物识别研究普遍存在数据泄漏问题（如同会话内随机划分），导致结果过度乐观，缺乏跨会话和长期时序分离的真实评估，阻碍了该技术在可穿戴系统中的可靠部署。

Method: 构建模块化、可复现的ECG-biometrics-bench基准框架，涵盖7个公共ECG数据集的标准化预处理、分割和评估流程；支持闭集和开集评估协议；设计跨会话和长期时序分离等渐进式真实评估协议；评估DeepECG、ResNet1D、CNN-LSTM等多种架构。

Result: 揭示了“随机划分谬误”：会话内评估协议人为提升性能，掩盖了时序漂移和未见身份导致的严重性能下降；该问题在不同模型架构中普遍存在；通过基于动态多会话模板融合的重度注册、轻量认证策略可部分缓解时序老化导致的性能下降。

Conclusion: 建立了更现实的ECG生物识别基线，证明了当前监督特征学习范式的固有局限性，强调了跨会话和长期评估的重要性，为可穿戴系统中可靠的ECG认证部署提供了关键见解和解决方案。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) biometrics have emerged as a promising modality for continuous, liveness-aware authentication in wearable systems. However, many prior studies report overly optimistic results due to data leakage (e.g., random splits within the same session). To address this issue, we introduce ECG-biometrics-bench, a modular, reproducible benchmarking framework that standardizes preprocessing, segmentation, and evaluation across seven widely used public ECG datasets spanning clinical, ambulatory, and large-scale cohort settings. The framework supports both closed-set and open-set (i.e., subject-disjoint generalization in this work) evaluation, as well as progressively realistic protocols including cross-session and long-term temporal separation. To facilitate reproducible research in the community, the ECG-biometrics-bench repository will be made publicly accessible on GitHub upon the acceptance of this manuscript. Through a comprehensive multi-dataset analysis, we expose the Random Split Fallacy, demonstrating that intra-session evaluation protocols artificially inflate performance while masking severe degradation caused by temporal drift and unseen identities. Furthermore, by evaluating multiple architectures, including DeepECG, ResNet1D, and CNN-LSTM, we show that these failures are not model-specific but are likely inherent to current supervised feature-learning paradigms. Finally, we demonstrate that performance degradation due to temporal aging can be partially mitigated through a heavy enrollment, lightweight authentication strategy based on dynamic multi-session template fusion. These findings establish a more realistic baseline for ECG biometrics and highlight critical challenges that must be addressed for reliable real-world deployment.

</details>


### [141] [Hybrid Quantum Reinforcement Learning with QAOA for Improved Vehicle Routing Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01574)
*T. Satyanarayana Murthy,B. Swathi Sowmya,Santhosh Voruganti,Sai Varshini Giridi,Chaitanyya Pratap Agarwal,Vanteddu Akshitha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将量子近似优化算法（QAOA）集成到量子强化学习（QRL）策略网络中的混合方法，用于解决车辆路径问题（VRP）。实验表明，该方法相比传统QRL和Grover自适应搜索收敛更快、能处理更大规模实例，并在近期量子硬件模拟器上表现出更优的解质量和内存使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆路径问题（VRP）是运输和物流领域中最复杂的NP难组合优化问题之一，需要动态的解决方案。现有的量子强化学习（QRL）和Grover自适应搜索（GAS）方法在处理大规模VRP实例时存在局限性，无法有效探索解空间。

Method: 提出了一种新的混合方法，将量子近似优化算法（QAOA）层（包括混合哈密顿量和代价哈密顿量层）整合到QRL策略网络中，替代传统的变分层。这种增强使智能体能够利用特定问题的量子相关性来学习策略，从而实现对路由解空间更丰富的探索。

Result: 在标准VRP实例上的实验表明，QAOA增强的QRL框架在训练中收敛更快，能够解决超出GAS和传统QRL方法处理能力的大规模VRP实例。该方法获得了更好的解决方案，需要更少的训练回合收敛，并且在近期量子硬件模拟器上展现出良好的内存使用效率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将QAOA集成到QRL中是一种可行的、可扩展且高质量的量子辅助组合优化方法，为解决复杂的NP难组合优化问题提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP) is one of the most complex NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem in transportation and logistics that requires a dynamic solution approach. In this paper we present a new hybrid approach that combines the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) into the QRL policy network, instead of the usual variational layers, QAOA mixing and cost Hamiltonian layers. This enhancement enables the agent to exploit problem specific particular quantum correlations when learning policies, and so richer exploration of the routing solution space. The QAOA-augmented QRL framework shows quicker convergence in training and can tackle larger VRP instances that are beyond the reach of Grover's Adaptive Search (GAS) and Quantum Reinforcement Learning (QRL) approaches. Experiments on standard VRP instances demonstrate better solutions, fewer episodes to converge and good memory usage on near term quantum hardware simulators. These findings demonstrate QAOA- integrated QRL as a viable approach to scalable, high quality quantum-assisted combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [142] [Model Merging: Foundations and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01580)
*Donato Crisostomi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种替代传统深度学习模型独立训练范式的模型合并方法，通过在权重空间直接组合独立训练的神经网络，无需原始训练数据即可实现模型能力的组合、复用和扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习将模型视为独立工件，分别训练、专门化并在出现改进版本时被替换。本文旨在探索模型合并作为替代范式，直接在权重空间组合独立训练的神经网络，以实现能力的组合与复用。

Method: 针对单任务场景（模型共享目标但初始化不同），提出基于Frank-Wolfe优化的循环一致合并算法C²M³，将多个网络对齐到共享的无参考参数空间；针对多任务场景（从共同预训练初始化微调不同下游任务），首先建立任务向量作为近似梯度的理论框架，解释任务算术的有效性与局限性，进而提出任务奇异向量（TSV）分解实现压缩和干扰减少的TSV-Merge，以及利用TSV几何的输入自适应路由方法MASS，最后提出基于项目反应理论的进化合并框架MERGE³。

Result: 在单任务场景实现了无偏置的权重平均；在多任务场景揭示了任务向量的低秩结构，提出的TSV-Merge有效实现压缩和干扰减少，MASS实现了推理时的任务相关子空间选择，MERGE³在保持解质量的同时将评估成本降低高达50倍。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为模型合并提供了理论和算法基础，支持了一种新的学习范式，使得学习到的能力可以跨模型组合、复用和扩展。

Abstract: Modern deep learning usually treats models as separate artifacts: trained independently, specialized for particular purposes, and replaced when improved versions appear. This thesis studies model merging as an alternative paradigm: combining independently trained neural networks directly in weight space, with little or no optimization and without requiring access to the original training data.
  The thesis considers two main regimes. In the single-task setting, where models share an objective but differ in initialization, we introduce C$^2$M$^3$, a cycle-consistent merging algorithm based on Frank-Wolfe optimization. C$^2$M$^3$ aligns multiple networks into a shared, reference-free parameter space, making weight averaging meaningful without privileging any individual model.
  In the multi-task setting, where models are fine-tuned for different downstream tasks from a common pretrained initialization, we first develop a theoretical account of task vectors as approximate gradients. This explains both the effectiveness and the limitations of task arithmetic. Building on this view, we show that task vectors inherit the low-rank structure of gradients and introduce Task Singular Vectors (TSV), a decomposition that enables compression and interference reduction through TSV-Merge. We then present MASS, an input-adaptive routing method that uses TSV geometry to select task-relevant subspaces at inference time. Finally, we introduce MERGE$^3$, an evolutionary merging framework that uses Item Response Theory to reduce evaluation costs by up to 50$\times$ while preserving solution quality.
  Together, these contributions provide theoretical and algorithmic foundations for model merging, supporting a paradigm in which learned capabilities can be composed, reused, and extended across models.

</details>


### [143] [Concepts Whisper While Syntax Shouts: Spectral Anti-Concentration and the Dual Geometry of Transformer Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01609)
*Pratyush Acharya,Nuraj Rimal,Habish Dhakal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Whitened Causal Alignment方法未能实现跨语言概念迁移，但揭示了Transformer模型存在双重几何结构：激活空间概念方向反集中于低方差区域，而静态反嵌入行对比集中于高方差区域，表明模型可能将语义内容编码在光谱安静区域以减少语法干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在测试Park等人(2024)提出的因果内积是否能够实现跨语言概念迁移，通过在17个模型和4个语言对上进行实验验证Whitened Causal Alignment的有效性。

Method: 采用匹配谱随机化检验方法，在17个模型和4个语言对上测试白化因果对齐方法；使用稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征和线性探针分析激活空间；在Gemma和Llama上进行分割注入因果干预；在8个模型上进行词性标注探针实验。

Result: Whitened Causal Alignment与单独的谱正则化无显著差异(p=0.95)；发现激活空间概念方向在谱尾部呈现反集中现象(p<10^-33)，SAE特征支持该发现(p=4.5×10^-19)；静态反嵌入行对比在高方差方向呈现集中现象(p<10^-4)；分割注入因果干预显示功能基础效应(Cohen's d高达1.80)；词性标注探针显示语法信息优先编码在高方差子空间(8个模型中6个，p<0.013)，Qwen 2.5系列呈现显著反转。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了Transformer模型的双重几何结构：激活空间概念方向在谱尾部反集中，而静态反嵌入行对比在高方差方向集中。这表明Transformer可能在上下文处理过程中将语义内容旋转到谱安静区域进行编码，从而在操作概念时减少语法干扰，为理解模型的内部表征机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We test whether the causal inner product of \citet{park2024linear} -- defined by the unembedding covariance $Σ$ -- enables cross-lingual concept transport. Across 17 models and 4 language pairs, a matched-spectrum randomization test finds that Whitened Causal Alignment is indistinguishable from spectral regularization alone ($p = 0.95$). However, this failure reveals a broader phenomenon: anti-concentration is observed in residual-stream difference-of-means vectors across five architecture families ($p < 10^{-33}$) and supported by SAE features (e.g., $p = 4.5 \times 10^{-19}$) and linear probes on Gemma and Llama. We discover a \emph{dual geometry}: activation-space concept directions anti-concentrate in the spectral tail, while static unembedding-row contrasts \emph{concentrate} in high-variance directions ($p < 10^{-4}$). Split-injection causal interventions support the functional basis on Gemma and Llama (Cohen's $d$ up to $1.80$), and POS-tag probing across 8 models shows syntax preferentially encodes in the high-variance subspace in 6 of 8 architectures ($p < 0.013$), with the Qwen~2.5 family showing a significant reversal consistent with architecture-specific spectral structure. These results suggest transformers may rotate semantic content into spectrally quiet regions during contextualized processing, encoding concepts where they can be manipulated with reduced grammatical disruption.

</details>


### [144] [From Packets to Patterns: Interpreting Encrypted Network Traffic as Longitudinal Behavioral Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01616)
*Rameen Mahmood,Omar El Shahawy,Souptik Barua,Zachary Beattie,Jeffrey Kaye,Xuhai "Orson'' Xu,Chao-Yi Wu,Danny Yuxing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG
