TL;DR: 本研究验证了加密智能手机网络流量可作为一种被动的、大规模的行为感知方式。通过可解释的深度学习方法，研究发现压力、孤独和睡眠障碍在加密流量中呈现出不同的时间动态结构，且学习到的特征能捕捉到传统预定义特征无法发现的个体内变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类行为难以在大规模范围内进行持续观察。虽然智能手机等日常设备的使用会留下可测量的痕迹，但如何利用加密网络流量这一无处不在、始终在线的被动感知模态来捕捉与睡眠、压力和孤独相关的行为模式，仍是一个有待探索的问题。

Method: 研究者使用带有每用户适配器的Transformer主干网络对共享行为结构进行建模，以同时表征典型的个体行为及其偏差。为了增强可解释性，应用稀疏自编码器提取对应不同活动模式的可解释行为特征。随后，利用带有Mundlak分解的广义估计方程（GEE）将这些特征与睡眠障碍、压力和孤独感相关联，从而分离个体间差异与个体内随时间的变化。

Result: 研究结果显示三种结果反映了截然不同的时间结构：压力主要与稳定的个体间差异相关，孤独感主要与个体内变化相关，而睡眠障碍则是两者的结合。值得注意的是，预定义的网络流量特征无法捕捉这些个体内动态，这证明了学习表征在纵向行为感知中的价值。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了加密网络流量作为一种可行的被动感知模态的地位。它能够揭示可解释的行为动态，特别是偏离个体基线的变化，而这些信息在原始流量特征中是不可见的。这为利用加密流量进行大规模、无侵入性的心理健康监测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Human behavior is difficult to observe continuously at scale, yet it leaves measurable traces in everyday device use. We test whether encrypted smartphone network traffic -- a ubiquitous, always-on, passive sensing modality -- can passively capture behavioral patterns related to sleep, stress, and loneliness. We model shared behavioral structure using a transformer backbone with per-user adapters, allowing the model to represent both typical individual behavior and deviations from it. To make these representations interpretable, we apply a sparse autoencoder to extract behavioral features corresponding to distinct patterns of activity. We relate these features to sleep disturbance, stress, and loneliness using generalized estimating equations with Mundlak decomposition, separating between-person differences from within-person changes over time. We find that the three outcomes reflect distinct temporal structures: stress is primarily associated with stable between-person differences, loneliness with within-person variation, and sleep disturbance with a combination of both. Notably, these within-person dynamics are not captured by predefined network-traffic features, demonstrating the value of learned representations for longitudinal behavioral sensing. These results establish encrypted network traffic as a viable passive sensing modality, revealing interpretable behavioral dynamics -- particularly deviations from an individual's baseline -- that are not visible in raw traffic features.

</details>


### [145] [PRIME: Protein Representation via Physics-Informed Multiscale Equivariant Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01625)
*Viet Thanh Duy Nguyen,John K. Johnstone,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRIME框架，通过五个物理基础结构图的嵌套层级（表面、原子、残基、二级结构、蛋白质水平）统一建模蛋白质，在折叠分类和反应类预测任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质是本质上的多尺度物理系统，其功能属性源于多个空间分辨率上协调的结构组织。然而现有的蛋白质表示学习方法通常在单一结构层面操作，或将不同结构信息视为并行模态，未显式建模其层次关系。

Method: 引入PRIME（Protein Representation via Physics-Informed Multiscale Equivariant Hierarchies）统一框架，将蛋白质建模为五个物理基础结构图的嵌套族，涵盖表面、原子、残基、二级结构和蛋白质层面。相邻层级通过确定性的物理信息赋值算子连接，实现通过自底向上聚合和自顶向下上下文细化的双向信息交换。

Result: 在标准蛋白质表示学习基准测试中表现强劲且具竞争力。在Fold Classification基准的困难Superfamily和Fold分割上分别超越最强几何GNN基线13.80和18.30个百分点；在Reaction Class预测上达到84.10%的最先进准确率，超越包括ESM在内的所有基线方法。消融研究证实每个结构层级贡献互补且非冗余的信息。

Conclusion: PRIME成功实现了跨多个物理尺度的蛋白质结构层次化建模，自适应跨注意力分析表明模型能够在预测时自主识别最任务相关的结构分辨率，为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的多尺度物理信息融合方法。

Abstract: Proteins are inherently multiscale physical systems whose functional properties emerge from coordinated structural organization across multiple spatial resolutions, ranging from atomic interactions to global fold topology. However, existing protein representation learning methods typically operate at a single structural level or treat different sources of structural information as parallel modalities, without explicitly modeling their hierarchical relationships. We introduce PRIME (Protein Representation via Physics-Informed Multiscale Equivariant Hierarchies), a unified framework that models proteins as a nested family of five physically grounded structural graphs spanning surface, atomic, residue, secondary-structure, and protein levels. Adjacent levels are connected through deterministic, physics-informed assignment operators, enabling bidirectional information exchange via bottom-up aggregation and top-down contextual refinement. Experiments on standard protein representation learning benchmarks demonstrate strong and competitive performance across diverse tasks, with particularly notable gains on the Fold Classification benchmark, where PRIME outperforms the strongest geometric GNN baseline by margins of 13.80 and 18.30 points on the harder Superfamily and Fold splits, and achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy of 84.10% on Reaction Class prediction, surpassing all baseline methods, including ESM. Ablation studies confirm that each structural level contributes complementary and non-redundant information, and adaptive cross-attention analysis reveals that PRIME autonomously identifies the most task-relevant structural resolutions at prediction time. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/HySonLab/PRIME

</details>


### [146] [Importance-Guided Basis Selection for Low-Rank Decomposition of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01627)
*Daniel Agyei Asante,Ernie Chang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BSI框架，通过基于二阶泰勒展开的导数重要性分数来选择保留的奇异向量基，实现了更优的大语言模型低秩压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩分解方法（如Basel）通过调整奇异值系数并剪枝小幅值基底，该启发式方法忽略了损失函数的局部几何性质，可能导致剪枝决策与任务性能不匹配。

Method: 提出BSI框架，从任务损失关于奇异值的二阶泰勒展开推导导数重要性分数，结合一阶敏感性和二阶曲率量化剪枝影响；设计了基于Hutchinson随机探测方法的Hessian对角估计器，使用对称参数扰动实现高效计算。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试上，BSI持续优于最先进的低秩分解基线方法，在深度压缩场景下优势尤为显著。

Conclusion: BSI提供了理论基础扎实且实用的高效低秩压缩方案，证明了直接估计基底移除带来的损失增量是更优的剪枝准则。

Abstract: Low-rank decomposition is a compelling approach for compressing large language models, but its effectiveness hinges on selecting which singular-vector bases to retain for a target task. Existing methods such as Basel adapt singular-value coefficients on downstream data and prune bases with small re-learned magnitudes, a heuristic that can be misaligned with task performance because it ignores the local geometry of the loss landscape. We present Basis Selection with Importance (BSI), a principled low-rank compression framework that ranks and prunes bases by directly estimating the expected loss increase incurred when each basis is removed. BSI derives a derivative-based importance score from a second-order Taylor expansion of the task loss with respect to singular values, combining first-order sensitivity and second-order curvature to quantify pruning impact. To make this criterion practical for LLMs, we develop an efficient Hessian-diagonal estimator by adapting the Hutchinson randomized-probing method to loss curvature with symmetric parameter perturbations. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis, including loss-increase bounds under basis pruning, explicit propagation of Hessian-diagonal estimation error into these bounds, variance characterization tied to the Hessian spectrum, high-probability sample-complexity guarantees for achieving a target estimation accuracy, and guidance on perturbation intensity. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that BSI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art low-rank decomposition baselines, with especially strong improvements under deep compression.

</details>


### [147] [Perturb and Correct: Post-Hoc Ensembles using Affine Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01632)
*Eleanor Quint*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Perturb-and-Correct (P&C)方法，利用单个预训练网络通过随机隐层扰动和最小二乘校正构建认知多样的预测器，实现了优异的分布内/分布外权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，在分布内数据上表现不可区分的模型在分布偏移下可能表现出截然不同的行为，这启发研究者探索如何从单一预训练网络构建具有认知多样性的预测器集合，以提升模型在分布外场景的鲁棒性。

Method: P&C是一种事后处理方法：首先对预训练网络的隐层施加随机扰动，然后在后续的仿射层中使用最小二乘法进行校正。这种机制使预测器在校准数据上保持一致，同时允许其在远离校准分布时产生分歧。研究者通过后校正残差及其一阶敏感性分析了该机制的理论基础。

Result: 在MuJoCo动力学预测和CIFAR-10分布外检测任务上的实验表明，P&C实现了强分布内/分布外权衡，性能匹配或优于标准的事后处理基线方法，且仅需单个预训练模型。残差在校准分布附近由杠杆项控制，而校正后的敏感性随着输入偏离校准几何结构而增长。

Conclusion: 研究发现揭示了深度学习模型的过参数化特性具有被进一步利用的潜力，通过P&C方法可以有效地将单一模型转化为具有认知多样性的预测器集合，从而在保持分布内性能的同时提升分布外检测能力。

Abstract: Models that are indistinguishable on in-distribution data can behave very differently under distribution shift. We introduce Perturb-and-Correct (P&C), a post-hoc method for constructing epistemically diverse predictors from a single pretrained network. P&C applies random hidden layer perturbations with a least-squares correction in the subsequent affine layer, producing predictors that agree on calibration data while remaining free to disagree away from it. We analyze this mechanism through the post-correction residual and its first-order sensitivity: the residual is controlled near the calibration distribution by a leverage term, while corrected sensitivity grows as inputs deviate from the calibration geometry. Empirically, P&C achieves a strong ID/OOD tradeoff across MuJoCo dynamics prediction and CIFAR-10 OOD detection, matching or outperforming standard post-hoc baselines while requiring only a single pretrained model. Our findings highlight the potential in further exploiting overparameterization as a strength of deep learning models.

</details>


### [148] [Chebyshev-Augmented One-Shot Transfer Learning for PINNs on Nonlinear Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01634)
*Yiqi Rao,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合切比雪夫多项式代理与一次性迁移学习的方法，实现了针对非线性微分方程的PINN快速求解，无需针对新实例重新训练网络主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准物理信息神经网络（PINNs）存在实例特异性的限制：对于每一个新的强迫项、边界/初始条件或参数设置，通常都需要重新训练。现有的一次性迁移学习（OTL）虽然能解决线性算子的迁移问题，但由于损失函数在输出层的非凸性，无法直接推广到非线性问题。

Method: 提出将一般平滑的弱非线性项通过截断切比雪夫展开式近似为多项式非线性，再通过摄动分解将其转化为若干线性子问题。训练一个多头PINN来学习与主导线性算子相关的可复用潜在空间；在测试阶段，新实例的求解仅需在输出层进行一系列闭式线性求解，无需重新训练网络主体。

Result: 该方法在包括非多项式和奇异非线性ODEs，以及具有饱和动力学的反应-扩散PDE等多个非线性基准测试中，展示了良好的求解精度和快速的在线适应能力。

Conclusion: 本文显著拓宽了适用于一次性PINN迁移的非线性问题类别，证明了该方法在多次查询场景下的实用性，为非线性微分方程的高效求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a flexible paradigm for solving differential equations by embedding governing laws into the training objective. A persistent limitation is instance specificity: standard PINNs typically require retraining for each new forcing term, boundary/initial condition, or parameter setting. One-shot transfer learning (OTL) addresses this bottleneck for linear operators by freezing a pretrained latent representation and computing optimal output weights in closed form, but for nonlinear problems closed-form adaptation is generally unavailable because the loss is nonconvex in the output layer.
  In this paper we substantially broaden the class of nonlinearities amenable to one-shot PINN transfer by combining OTL with Chebyshev polynomial surrogates. We approximate general smooth weakly nonlinear terms by truncated Chebyshev expansions over a prescribed solution range, yielding a polynomial nonlinearity that can be handled by a perturbative decomposition into linear subproblems. A multi-head PINN learns a reusable latent space associated with the dominant linear operator; at test time, solutions to new instances are obtained via a sequence of closed-form linear solves in the output layer, without retraining the network body.
  We provide a unified derivation of the framework for ODEs and PDEs and demonstrate accuracy and fast online adaptation on nonlinear benchmarks, including non-polynomial and singular ODE nonlinearities as well as a reaction-diffusion PDE with saturating kinetics, demonstrating the method's utility in many-query regimes.

</details>


### [149] [The Banach-Butterfly Invariant: Influence-Adaptive Walsh Geometry for Ternary Polynomial Threshold Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01637)
*Gorgi Pavlov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Banach-Butterfly不变量(BBT)，这是一种基于坐标影响力的自适应Banach几何结构，用于刻画布尔函数的稀疏性，并验证了其在WHT激活能量代理中的实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Walsh-Hadamard变换在布尔函数分析和神经网络量化中有重要应用，但缺乏一个能自适应地刻画函数稀疏性的几何不变量。现有的总影响力指标无法区分具有相同总影响力的不同函数，需要一个新的不变量来捕捉更细致的结构信息。

Method: 提出Banach-Butterfly不变量(BBT)：对布尔函数$f$，根据坐标影响力$\mathrm{Inf}_\ell(f)$为butterfly层的第$\ell$层分配指数$p_\ell = 1+\mathrm{Inf}_\ell(f)$，构建收缩不变量$μ(f)=\prod_\ell 2^{-\mathrm{Inf}_\ell/(1+\mathrm{Inf}_\ell)}$。证明了$μ$的Jensen下界和严格Schur凸性，并在$n\leq 4$的有限测试集上使用MILP计算最小支撑证书。

Result: 理论结果表明：(1)$μ$是严格Schur凸的，能区分相同总影响力的函数(n=3时有122对)；(2)不同函数类的$μ$量级不同(奇偶性$2^{-n/2}$，多数函数$2^{-Θ(\sqrt{n})}$，独裁函数$2^{-1/2}$)；(3)在n=4时所有65536个布尔函数的平均最小支撑为6.42。实验发现条件Spearman相关系数在n=4和n=5之间符号反转，表明$μ$是有效的Schur凸集中不变量，但非最小支撑的通用单调预测器。伴随应用论文显示WHT激活能量代理在五个预训练LLM上使wikitext-2困惑度降低15-58%。

Conclusion: BBT成功建立了布尔函数影响力与Walsh-Hadamard稀疏性之间的理论桥梁，作为Schur凸不变量能有效刻画函数结构。虽然与最小支撑无通用单调关系，但启发的实值代理在LLM量化中展现出显著效果，为从布尔理论到实值应用提供了定性指导。

Abstract: We introduce the Banach-Butterfly Invariant (BBT), an influence-adaptive Banach geometry on the Walsh-Hadamard butterfly factorization. For a Boolean function $f:\{-1,+1\}^n\to\{-1,+1\}$ with coordinate influences $\mathrm{Inf}_\ell(f)$, BBT assigns exponent $p_\ell = 1+\mathrm{Inf}_\ell(f)$ to butterfly layer $\ell$, yielding the contraction invariant $μ(f)=\prod_\ell 2^{-\mathrm{Inf}_\ell/(1+\mathrm{Inf}_\ell)}$. We prove a Jensen lower bound $\log_2μ(f) \ge -I(f)/(1+I(f)/n)$ and that $μ$ is strictly Schur-convex in the influence vector (modulo permutation), giving scaling classes $μ\sim 2^{-n/2}$ (parity), $2^{-Θ(\sqrt{n})}$ (majority), $2^{-1/2}$ (dictators). $\log_2μ$ is rational but not polynomial in the Fourier coefficients while $μ$ is algebraic, and $μ$ separates functions with identical total influence (122 pairs at $n=3$).
  Using the certified $n \le 4$ ternary Walsh-threshold universe from a companion synthesis manuscript as a finite testbed, we compute exact MILP minimum-support certificates for all 65,536 Boolean functions at $n=4$ (mean 6.42, max 9, all-odd by a parity argument) and on 10,000 of the 616,126 NPN-canonical representatives we enumerate at $n=5$ (matching OEIS A000370). Conditional Spearman $ρ(μ,|\mathrm{supp}|)$ at fixed total influence is $+0.571$ in the largest stratum at $n=4$ but reverses to $-0.38$ at $n=5$ under both function-uniform and NPN-canonical sampling: $μ$ is a valid Schur-convex concentration invariant, not a universal monotone predictor of minimum support across $n$.
  A companion application paper validates a real-valued WHT activation-energy proxy inspired by this theory on five pretrained LLMs at W2A16, cutting wikitext-2 perplexity by 15-58% versus vanilla auto-round; the transfer from Boolean theory to the real-valued proxy is qualitative, not formal.

</details>


### [150] [Prescriptive Scaling Laws for Data Constrained Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01640)
*Justin Lovelace,Christian Belardi,Srivatsa Kundurthy,Shriya Sudhakar,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种适用于数据受限场景的新型扩展定律，通过单参数加性过拟合惩罚项准确刻画了重复数据对模型性能的影响，并发现强权重衰减可将过拟合系数降低约70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练算力增长速度远超高质量数据的供给，研究重点需从最优算力分配转向如何在有限数据中提取最大价值；传统Chinchilla扩展定律假设每个训练token唯一，无法有效指导数据受限场景下的预训练决策。

Method: 提出一个简单的加性过拟合惩罚项来建模数据重复带来的额外损失，构建了单参数形式的扩展定律；该形式将过拟合隔离在单一系数中，便于跨训练配置的直接比较；通过案例研究验证强权重衰减（λ=1.0）对该系数的影响。

Result: 所提扩展定律准确描述了模型在数据重复下的行为；导出了定性上全新的计算最优分配建议：超过一定阈值后，继续重复数据适得其反，算力更应投入模型容量；遵循该定律推荐的配置在数据受限场景下提升了性能；强权重衰减（λ=1.0）将过拟合系数降低约70%。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将扩展定律从数据充足场景扩展到数据受限场景，为数据受限预训练提供了理论指导和配置优化依据；同时从扩展定律角度解释了近期关于数据受限场景下最优权重衰减应比标准实践大约一个数量级的发现。

Abstract: Training compute is increasingly outpacing the availability of high-quality data. This shifts the central challenge from optimal compute allocation to extracting maximum value from limited data. The widely adopted Chinchilla scaling law assumes every training token is unique. This limits its ability to guide pretraining decisions in data-constrained regimes. We model the excess loss under repetition with a simple additive overfitting penalty and find that it accurately describes model behavior. Our scaling law yields qualitatively new compute-optimal allocation advice. Beyond a point, further repetition is counterproductive and compute is better spent on model capacity. We show that following our law's recommended configuration improves performance in data-constrained regimes. Finally, because our one-parameter form isolates overfitting in a single coefficient, it enables direct comparison across training configurations. As a case study, we show that strong weight decay ($λ=1.0$) reduces this coefficient by approximately 70%, providing a scaling-law explanation for recent findings that optimal weight decay in data-constrained regimes is an order of magnitude larger than standard practice.

</details>


### [151] [Adaptive Pluralistic Alignment: A pipeline for dynamic artificial democracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01642)
*Rachel Freedman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应多元对齐（APA）框架，通过低秩奖励基分解、基于社会选择论的投票机制和动态权重更新三个阶段，使AI系统能够跟踪社会价值观演变并避免价值锁定，无需重复昂贵的预训练或大规模数据收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐方法针对固定的偏好集，面临"价值锁定"风险——当社会规范随时间演变时，AI系统无法适应新的价值观。重新训练代价高昂，因此需要一种高效、可扩展的方法来实现价值观的动态更新。

Method: APA采用三阶段模块化流程：（1）通过低秩奖励基分解学习紧凑的个性化奖励模型；（2）将这些模型组成评审团，利用社会选择理论中的投票机制在候选输出中集体决策；（3）当价值观变化时，仅需在固定的奖励基上拟合新的标注者权重，高效地更新评审团。

Result: 在PRISM多用户对齐数据集和模拟历史标注者上实现了概念验证系统。初步分析表明：评审团组成和投票规则的选择会显著影响输出结果，尤其在评审团偏好异质性较强时影响更为明显。

Conclusion: APA框架实现了高效、可解释、可控且模块化的价值观更新机制，为解决AI系统价值锁定问题提供了可行方案，代码和偏好数据集已开源。

Abstract: Prevailing alignment methods target a fixed set of preferences and therefore risk forcing value lock-in as societal norms evolve over time. We introduce Adaptive Pluralistic Alignment (APA), a modular pipeline for updating pluralistically aligned AI systems to track evolving values and avoid value lock-in without repeating costly pretraining or large-scale data collection. APA has three stages: (1) learning compact personalized reward models via low-rank reward basis decomposition, (2) using these models as a jury that collectively selects among candidate outputs through social-choice-theoretic voting, and (3) efficiently adapting the jury over time by fitting new annotator weights over the fixed reward bases as values shift. The resulting system is efficient, explainable, steerable, and modular. We implement a proof-of-concept instantiation using the PRISM multi-user alignment dataset and simulated historical annotators, and provide preliminary analysis showing that jury composition and the choice of voting rule can substantially affect outcomes, particularly when jury preferences are heterogeneous. We provide full code and resulting preference datasets at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/apa.

</details>


### [152] [AI Alignment via Incentives and Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01643)
*Rohit Agarwal,Joshua Lin,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从法经济学威慑模型视角研究AI对齐问题，提出求解器-审计器博弈框架，将奖励设计建模为双层优化问题，并通过实验验证自适应奖励分配能有效维持监督压力并减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法通常将错误行为视为外部失效，忽略了智能体可能对激励做出策略性响应。本文指出，在智能体AI流程中，求解器可能从不合规行为中获益（如生成看似合理但错误的答案），而审计器需权衡监控成本与收益，形成类似法经济学中的威慑博弈。对齐问题实质上是一个不动点问题：更强的惩罚虽能威慑求解器，但也会削弱审计器的检查激励，因为表面上“更对齐”的群体使监督显得不那么必要。

Method: 1. **博弈建模**：建立求解器-审计器两方博弈模型，求解器权衡违规收益与惩罚期望，审计器权衡检查成本与发现违规的收益。
2. **双层优化**：委托人设计奖励函数，目标是诱导期望的行为均衡，而非直接赋予语义意义。奖励分配基于完整纠错事件（是否出错、是否检查、是否发现错误等）。
3. **外层搜索算法**：采用老虎机算法在奖励配置空间中搜索，利用含噪声的交互反馈，找到能维持有效监督激励的奖励策略。

Result: 在LLM编程任务流程上的实验表明：
1. 自适应奖励配置相比静态人工设计的奖励，能更好地维持有意义的监督压力；
2. 显著降低了产生幻觉式错误尝试的频率；
3. 实现了更优的委托人对齐结果（即同时优化求解器行为和审计器监督效率）。

Conclusion: 将AI对齐问题重构为委托-代理博弈，为后训练阶段的奖励设计提供了新视角。核心洞见是：奖励的价值应以其诱导的行为均衡来评判，而非其表面语义。通过自适应奖励分配，可以在求解器行为与审计器激励之间找到平衡，实现持续有效的监督。这为构建可扩展、鲁棒的AI对齐机制提供了理论基础和实践路径。

Abstract: We study AI alignment through the lens of law-and-economics models of deterrence and enforcement. In these models, misconduct is not treated as an external failure, but as a strategic response to incentives: an actor weighs the gain from violation against the probability of detection and the severity of punishment. We argue that the same logic arises naturally in agentic AI pipelines. A solver may benefit from producing a persuasive but incorrect answer, hiding uncertainty, or exploiting spurious shortcuts, while an auditor or verifier must decide whether costly monitoring is worthwhile. Alignment is therefore a fixed-point problem: stronger penalties may deter solver misbehavior, but they can also reduce the auditor's incentive to inspect, since auditing then mainly incurs cost on a population that appears increasingly aligned.
  This perspective also changes what should count as a post-training signal. Standard feedback often attaches reward to the final answer alone, but a solver-auditor pipeline exposes the full correction event: whether the solver erred, whether the auditor inspected, whether the error was caught, and whether oversight incentives remained active. We formalize this interaction in a two-agent model in which a principal chooses rewards over joint correction outcomes, inducing both solver behavior and auditor monitoring. Reward design is therefore a bilevel optimization problem: rewards are judged not by their immediate semantic meaning, but by the behavioral equilibrium they induce. We propose a bandit-based outer-loop procedure for searching over reward profiles using noisy interaction feedback. Experiments on an LLM coding pipeline show that adaptive reward profiles can maintain useful oversight pressure and improve principal-aligned outcomes relative to static hand-designed rewards, including a substantial reduction in hallucinated incorrect attempts.

</details>


### [153] [Geospatial foundation-model embeddings improve population estimation unevenly across space and scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01650)
*Wenbin Zhang,Eimear Cleary,Francisco Rowe,Somnath Chaudhuri,Maksym Bondarenko,Shengjie Lai,Andrew J. Tatem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了人口动态基础模型（PDFM），通过学习地理空间基础模型的嵌入表示来改进亚国家层面的人口估计，在巴西、尼日利亚和美国的数据稀缺地区平均提升20.1%的预测精度，但发现其性能受空间尺度匹配的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的亚国家人口估计对许多应用至关重要，但在人口普查稀疏、过时或空间粗糙的地区仍然困难。现有的人口制图工作流程依赖手工构建的地理空间协变量（如定居点范围、夜间灯光和环境条件），这些协变量需要跨尺度和地理区域进行组装和协调，成本高昂且难以推广。

Method: 研究将人口动态基础模型（PDFM）的嵌入表示与协调的地理空间协变量进行基准对比，用于巴西、尼日利亚和美国的亚国家人口估计。采用地理结构化验证方法，评估PDFM在预测拟合度和KL散度方面的性能表现，并分析其在不同空间尺度和区域特征下的适用性。

Result: 在地理结构化验证下，PDFM将预测拟合度中位数提高了20.1%（四分位距：10.0-33.2%），减少了未解释方差，并将KL散度降低了23.2%（9.2-26.2%）。但性能提升不均匀：PDFM在地理空间协变量对定居环境特征化较弱的地区（如较大且欠发达的亚国家区域）优势最明显。同时发现PDFM性能与尺度耦合，嵌入表示在空间聚合间的迁移灵活性不如地理空间协变量。

Conclusion: 地理空间基础模型的场所表示可以在数据贫乏环境中改善人口估计，但其优势在空间尺度不匹配时会预测性地下降，揭示了当前地理空间AI的一个基本局限性。这一发现为指导何时以及如何使用基础模型进行人口估计提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Reliable subnational population estimates are essential for applications, yet remain difficult where censuses are sparse, outdated or spatially coarse. Existing population-mapping workflows rely on hand-built geospatial covariates, such as settlement extent, night-time lights, and environmental conditions, which must be assembled and harmonised across scales and geographies. Geospatial foundation models offer an alternative by learning reusable representations of place from more multifaceted and heterogeneous data sources. Here, we benchmark Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM) embeddings against the harmonised geospatial covariates for subnational population estimation in Brazil, Nigeria and the United States. Under geographically structured validation, PDFM increased predictive fit by a median of 20.1% (IQR: 10.0-33.2%, across country-model comparisons) reduction in unexplained variance, and reduced Kullback-Leibler divergence by 23.2% (9.2-26.2%). However, these gains were uneven. PDFM was most advantageous where the geospatial covariates weakly characterised settlement context, such as larger and less-developed subnational areas. Moreover, PDFM performance was scale-coupled with embeddings providing less flexible transfer across spatial aggregations than geospatial covariates. These findings showed that geospatial foundation-model representations of place can improve population estimation in data poor settings, but their benefits break down predictably under spatial scale mismatch, revealing a fundamental limitation of current geospatial AI.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Efficient and Expressive Offline RL via Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01663)
*Sungyoung Lee,Dohyeong Kim,Eshan Balachandar,Zelal Su Mustafaoglu,Keshav Pingali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FAN算法，通过简化流策略和分布式评论家的计算过程，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了离线强化学习的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习方法虽然通过流策略和分布式评论家提升了性能，但计算成本高昂：流策略需要迭代采样生成动作，分布式评论家需要多个样本计算分位数来估计价值。

Method: 提出Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning (FAN)算法，采用行为正则化技术，仅使用单次流策略迭代，且分布式评论家仅需单个高斯噪声样本进行计算。

Result: 在机器人操作和运动任务上的实验表明，FAN达到了最先进的性能水平，同时显著减少了训练和推理的运行时间。理论分析也证明了收敛性和性能界限。

Conclusion: FAN成功解决了离线强化学习中高性能与计算效率之间的权衡问题，证明了通过适当的设计简化可以在提升效率的同时获得更优的任务性能。

Abstract: We propose Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning (FAN), a highly efficient and high-performing offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm. Recent work has shown that expressive flow policies and distributional critics improve offline RL performance, but at a high computational cost. Specifically, flow policies require iterative sampling to produce a single action, and distributional critics require computation over multiple samples (e.g., quantiles) to estimate value. To address these inefficiencies while maintaining high performance, we introduce FAN. Our method employs a behavior regularization technique that utilizes only a single flow policy iteration and requires only a single Gaussian noise sample for distributional critics. Our theoretical analysis of convergence and performance bounds demonstrates that these simplifications not only improve efficiency but also lead to superior task performance. Experiments on robotic manipulation and locomotion tasks demonstrate that FAN achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing both training and inference runtimes. We release our code at https://github.com/brianlsy98/FAN.

</details>


### [155] [Benchmarking Single-Pose Docking, Consensus Rescoring, and Supervised ML on the LIT-PCBA Library: A Critical Evaluation of DiffDock, AutoDock-GPU, GNINA, and DiffDock-NMDN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01681)
*Youssef Abo-Dahab,Xiaoiang Xiang,Xiaoiang Xiang,Xiaoiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在真实实验数据集上的大规模评估表明，传统对接软件AutoDock-GPU结合GNINA重打分是目前最强的单一方法；监督机器学习重排序可显著提升虚拟筛选性能，但整体早期富集能力仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期AI驱动的分子对接工具（如DiffDock和NMDN）在基准测试中表现优异，但其在真实、实验衍生的数据集上的实用性尚不明确。研究旨在评估这些新工具与传统方法在实际虚拟筛选场景中的性能差异。

Method: 在LIT-PCBA库（15个靶点，578,295个配体-靶点对）上进行大规模评估。比较AutoDock-GPU和DiffDock的构象生成能力，随后使用GNINA和NMDN进行重打分。进一步评估基于排序的共识策略和基于对接特征的监督机器学习模型。

Result: AutoDock-GPU构象经GNINA重打分（AutoDock-GNINA）是表现最佳的单一方法，中位EF1%为2.14。基于DiffDock的方法表现逊于AutoDock-GNINA，特别是在OPRK1等挑战性靶点上。精心设计的共识排序提高了鲁棒性但未超越最佳单一打分器。监督机器学习重排序带来了最大提升，中位EF1%达到4.49（比AutoDock-GNINA提高110%）。

Conclusion: 即使是最佳的经典+机器学习混合工作流，在真实基准上仅提供适度的早期富集。没有单一对接方法在所有靶点上占主导地位。经过严格验证、具有成本效益的方法组合结合监督重排序，目前为虚拟筛选提供最具实用价值。

Abstract: Virtual screening performance depends heavily on the chosen docking and scoring methods. Recent AI-based tools such as DiffDock and NMDN have reported strong benchmark results, but their practical utility on realistic, experimentally-derived datasets remains unclear. Here we perform a large-scale evaluation on the LIT-PCBA library (15 targets, 578,295 ligand-target pairs with experimentally confirmed actives and inactives). We compare AutoDock-GPU and DiffDock for pose generation, followed by rescoring with GNINA and NMDN. We further evaluate rank-based consensus strategies and supervised machine learning models trained on docking features.
  GNINA rescoring of AutoDock-GPU poses (AutoDock-GNINA) emerged as the strongest single method with a median EF1% of 2.14. DiffDock-based approaches underperformed relative to AutoDock-GNINA, particularly on challenging targets such as OPRK1. Carefully designed consensus ranking improved robustness but did not surpass the best single scorer. Supervised ML re-ranking delivered the largest gains, achieving a median EF1% of 4.49 (+110% over AutoDock-GNINA).
  Our results highlight that even the best classical+ML hybrid workflows provide only modest early enrichment on realistic benchmarks. We conclude that no single docking method dominates across targets and that rigorously validated, cost-effective combinations with supervised re-ranking currently offer the most practical value for virtual screening.

</details>


### [156] [Complex Diffusion Maps with $ω$-Parameterized Kernels Revealing Inherent Harmonic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01691)
*Tongzhen Dang,Weiyang Ding,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了复扩散映射(CDM)框架，通过复值核函数揭示高维数据的主导复谐波，在噪声鲁棒性、判别能力和计算效率方面优于传统实值核方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实值核的扩散映射方法在处理易混淆样本时判别能力有限，且难以有效捕获高维数据中的非局部时空动态特征。作者受热方程的局部高斯核和薛定谔方程的非局部薛定谔核启发，旨在构建一个统一的框架来权衡局部与非局部连接，从而更好地揭示数据的复谐波结构。

Method: 提出了一个ω参数化的复值核函数族，统一了局部高斯核和非局部薛定谔核；基于算子谱理论建立了扩散算子、扩散距离和复谐波映射的理论基础；开发了基于优化的解释框架，在复扩散空间中保持角度结构而非仅依赖实值幅度。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的广泛评估表明：复值核增强了易混淆样本间的差异，判别能力优于基于实值核的线性和非线性方法；在高噪声环境中保持鲁棒性，产生更清晰的特征间隙以增强谱分离；对静息态fMRI数据能捕获更强的相关性和非局部时空动态；在公共EEG睡眠数据集上无需任务特定调优即达到竞争性能，且相比传统机器学习和深度神经网络方法具有更高的计算效率。

Conclusion: CDM框架成功地将扩散映射扩展到复数域，通过复值核的引入有效增强了数据的判别能力和噪声鲁棒性，在多个实际应用场景中展现出优越的性能和计算效率，证明了该方法的通用性和实用价值，为高维数据的特征提取和分析提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Complex Diffusion Maps (CDM), a novel diffusion mapping framework that aims to reveal the dominant complex harmonics of high-dimensional data. Inspired by the local Gaussian kernel relevant to the heat equation and the nonlocal Schrödinger kernel relevant to the Schrödinger equation, we propose a unified family of $ω$-parameterized complex-valued kernels for the trade-off between local and nonlocal connections. We establish the theoretical foundation based on the operator spectrum theory, where the corresponding diffusion operator, diffusion distance, and complex harmonic maps are well-defined. An optimization-based interpretation of the maps is also developed, aiming to preserve angular structure in the complex diffusion space rather than relying solely on real-valued magnitude. We extensively evaluate CDM on both synthetic and real-world datasets. The complex-valued kernel amplifies differences among easily confusable samples, improving discriminative power over both linear and nonlinear methods based on real-valued kernels. CDM remains robust in high-noise settings, yielding a clearer eigengap that enhances spectral separation. For resting-state fMRI data, CDM captures more strongly correlated and nonlocal spatiotemporal dynamics. Without task-specific tuning, CDM achieves competitive performance on a public EEG sleep dataset, while maintaining high computational efficiency compared with both traditional machine learning and deep neural network approaches, highlighting its generality and practical value.

</details>


### [157] [Probe-Geometry Alignment: Erasing the Cross-Sequence Memorization Signature Below Chance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01699)
*Anamika Paul Rupa,Anietie Andy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型的行为遗忘会在内部表示中留下可恢复的痕迹，并提出了一种名为PGA的外科手术式擦除方法，可以在不影响模型能力的前提下彻底消除这些记忆痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的攻击研究表明，大语言模型的行为遗忘方法并不彻底，攻击者可以通过对抗性探针恢复被遗忘的信息。这暴露了一个关键问题：行为遗忘后的模型内部仍然保留着记忆痕迹，存在安全隐患。因此，需要深入理解这些痕迹在模型中的存在位置和性质，并开发能够真正消除这些痕迹的方法。

Method: 研究方法包括三个关键步骤：(1) 设计留一法跨序列探针，测试记忆签名是否能够在未见序列上泛化，从而定位记忆痕迹在模型表示中的位置；(2) 验证探针方向的因果可分性，证明可以将探针方向投影消除而不影响行为回忆；(3) 提出探针几何对齐(PGA)方法，通过在每个深度上沿探针的实时读出方向对齐激活值，实现外科手术式的记忆擦除，并扩展对抗性PGA来应对重新训练探针的攻击者。

Result: 实验结果展示了四个重要发现：(1) 记忆签名真实存在且跨规模一致：Pythia-70M、GPT-2 medium和Mistral-7B上的记忆特异性差距分别为+0.32、+0.19和+0.30；(2) 探针方向与行为回忆因果可分离：投影消除探针方向后签名从+0.44降至-0.19，而行为回忆几乎不变；(3) PGA方法成功将跨序列探针性能降至随机机会以下（toy模型0.17、Pythia-70M 0.07、Mistral-7B 0.45、GPT-2 medium 0.06），并对六种对抗性探针变体保持鲁棒；(4) 对抗性PGA在击败重新拟合攻击的同时，五个零样本能力基准任务的性能变化在2.8个百分点以内（平均准确率变化+0.2pp）。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了跨序列记忆签名是预训练表示的一个真实、因果可分、特定于机制的属性。通过每个深度仅需一个秩为1的干预，PGA方法能够在无可测量能力成本的情况下将记忆签名消除至随机机会以下。这一发现为大语言模型的安全遗忘提供了理论理解和实践工具，表明真正的遗忘是可以实现的。

Abstract: Recent attacks show that behavioural unlearning of large language models leaves internal traces recoverable by adversarial probes. We characterise where this retention lives and show it can be surgically removed without measurable capability cost. Our central protocol is a leave-one-out cross-sequence probe that tests whether a memorisation signature generalises across held-out sequences. The signature is real and consistent across scale: memorisation-specific gaps of +0.32, +0.19, +0.30 on Pythia-70M, GPT-2 medium, and Mistral-7B; on Pythia-70M, the random-initialisation control collapses to -0.04 at the deepest layer where the pretrained signature peaks. The probe direction is causally separable from recall -- projecting it out collapses the signature locally (+0.44 -> -0.19) while behavioural recall barely changes -- and a probe trained on naturally memorised content does not classify fine-tuning-injected secrets, marking two representationally distinct regimes. We then introduce probe-geometry alignment (PGA), a surgical erasure that aligns activations along the probe's live readout direction at each depth. PGA drives the cross-sequence probe below random chance at all four scales tested (toy depth-4: 0.17; Pythia-70M: 0.07; Mistral-7B: 0.45; GPT-2 medium: 0.06 via MD-PGA k=2) and remains robust to six adversarial probe variants. Against a re-fitting attacker who trains a fresh probe on PGA-treated activations, we extend PGA adversarially, defeating the re-fit probe at every memorisation-relevant depth while preserving five zero-shot capability benchmarks within 2.8 percentage points per task (mean Δacc = +0.2pp). The cross-sequence signature is a real, causally separable, regime-specific property of pretrained representations -- removable below chance with a single rank-one intervention per depth at no measurable capability cost.

</details>


### [158] [Stability and Generalization for Decentralized Markov SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01701)
*Jiahuan Wang,Ziqing Wen,Ping Luo,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对数据由马尔可夫链生成且学习过程去中心化的实际场景，分析了去中心化SGD和SGDA的泛化性能，建立了非渐近泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度方法的泛化理论通常依赖于独立采样假设，但在许多实际应用中，数据是由马尔可夫链生成的，且学习过程以去中心化方式执行，这带来了显著的分析挑战。因此需要研究在马尔可夫链采样下，去中心化算法的稳定性和泛化问题。

Method: 利用基于稳定性（stability-based）的分析框架，刻画马尔可夫依赖性和去中心化通信对泛化行为的联合影响。分析过程中考虑了网络拓扑结构、马尔可夫链混合性质以及原始-对偶动态等因素。

Result: 为去中心化随机梯度下降（SGD）和随机梯度下降上升（SGDA）算法建立了非渐近泛化界。这些结果将现有的马尔可夫随机梯度方法的研究扩展到了去中心化和极小极大（minimax）设置。

Conclusion: 研究成功地将马尔可夫随机梯度方法的泛化理论扩展到了更复杂的去中心化和极小极大优化场景，揭示了网络拓扑、数据依赖结构与算法动态之间的相互作用机制，为大规模分布式学习提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient methods are central to large-scale learning, yet their generalization theory typically relies on independent sampling assumptions. In many practical applications, data are generated by Markov chains and learning is performed in a decentralized manner, which introduces significant analytical challenges. In this work, we investigate the stability and generalization of decentralized stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and stochastic gradient descent ascent (SGDA) under Markov chain sampling. Leveraging a stability-based framework, we characterize how Markovian dependence and decentralized communication jointly influence generalization behavior. Our analysis captures the effects of network topology, Markov chain mixing properties, and primal-dual dynamics. We establish non-asymptotic generalization bounds for both algorithms, extending existing results on Markov stochastic gradient methods to decentralized and minimax settings.

</details>


### [159] [Floating-Point Networks with Automatic Differentiation Can Represent Almost All Floating-Point Functions and Their Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01702)
*Sejun Park,Yeachan Park,Geonho Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 证明了在浮点数运算和自动微分条件下，神经网络仍能同时逼近任意函数值和梯度，填补了理论假设与实际实现之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逼近理论假设神经网络使用实数参数和精确运算，但实际实现仅使用有限的浮点数子集且存在舍入误差。作者旨在验证在浮点数运算环境下，类似的函数值与梯度逼近结论是否依然成立。

Method: 在浮点数运算框架下，证明给定浮点函数φ，存在浮点神经网络f，使得f可表示任意目标函数值，而自动微分算法D^AD(φ∘f)可表示任意梯度；进一步推广至多个函数φ_1,...,φ_n的情形，在温和条件下实现函数值与多组梯度的同时表示。

Result: 证明了浮点神经网络可以同时表示任意函数值和梯度，该结论适用于ReLU、ELU、GeLU、Swish、Sigmoid、tanh等实际常用激活函数。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了浮点数运算环境下神经网络的逼近理论基础，表明自动微分与浮点网络结合能够有效表示函数值和梯度，为理论指导实际深度学习系统提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Theoretical studies show that for any differentiable function on a compact domain, there exists a neural network that approximates both the function values and gradients. However, such a result cannot be used in practice since it assumes real parameters and exact internal operations. In contrast, real implementations only use a finite subset of reals and machine operations with round-off errors. In this work, we investigate whether a similar result holds for neural networks under floating-point arithmetic, when the gradient with respect to the input is computed by the automatic differentiation algorithm $D^\mathtt{AD}$. We first show that given a floating-point function $φ$ (e.g., a loss function), arbitrary function values and gradients can be represented by a floating-point network $f$ and $D^\mathtt{AD}(φ\circ f)$, respectively. We further extend this result: given $φ_1,\dots,φ_n$, $D^\mathtt{AD}(φ_i\circ f)$ can simultaneously represent arbitrary gradients while $f$ represents the target values, under mild conditions. Our results hold for practical activation functions, e.g., $\mathrm{ReLU}$, $\mathrm{ELU}$, $\mathrm{GeLU}$, $\mathrm{Swish}$, $\mathrm{Sigmoid}$, and $\mathrm{tanh}$.

</details>


### [160] [CoAction: Cross-task Correlation-aware Pareto Set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01712)
*Xinyue Chen,Yingxuan Liang,Yiqin Huang,Chikai Shang,Hai-Lin Liu,Fangqing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoAction框架，利用任务感知的Transformer实现了跨任务的帕累托集学习，首次解决了传统方法一次只能处理单个多目标优化任务的问题，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的帕累托集学习（PSL）方法存在局限性：每次只能解决一个多目标优化问题。在多目标多任务优化场景中，这导致需要为每个任务训练单独模型，增加了计算成本，且未能充分利用任务之间的相关性。

Method: 提出跨任务相关性感知的帕累托集学习（CoAction）框架。该框架使用任务感知的Transformer作为骨干网络，通过为每个任务分配特定的嵌入向量来区分不同任务，并利用Transformer的自注意力机制捕捉复杂的任务依赖关系，从而促进任务间的知识共享，实现同时处理多个任务。

Result: 在包含基准问题和实际应用的综合多任务测试套件上进行了评估，所提方法在Hypervolume、Range和Sparsity等指标上均表现出有效性和具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: CoAction框架成功扩展了帕累托集学习的能力，使其能够高效地处理多任务场景，通过挖掘任务间相关性降低了计算成本，并为多目标多任务优化提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pareto set learning (PSL) is an emerging paradigm in multi-objective optimization that trains neural networks to map preference vectors to Pareto optimal solutions. However, existing PSL methods primarily focus on solving a single multi-objective optimization problem at a time. This limitation not only increases computational costs in multi-objective multitask optimization scenarios by requiring a separate model for each task, but also fails to exploit the inter-task correlations across tasks. To address this, we propose a Cross-tAsk correlation-aware Pareto Set Learning (CoAction) framework, which leverages task-aware transformer to handle multiple tasks simultaneously. Specifically, by assigning task-specific embedding vectors to individual tasks, the model effectively distinguishes between tasks while facilitating knowledge sharing among them. We utilize a Transformer encoder as the backbone architecture to leverage its self-attention mechanism for capturing complex task dependencies. The proposed approach is evaluated on comprehensive multitask test suites covering both benchmark problems and real-world applications, demonstrating effectiveness and competitive performance in Hypervolume, Range, and Sparsity.

</details>


### [161] [Stable GFlowNets with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01729)
*Zengxiang Lei,Ananth Shreekumar,Jonathan Rosenthal,Ruoyu Song,Alvaro A. Cardenas,Daniel J. Fremont,Dongyan Xu,Satish Ukkusuri,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成流网络训练不稳定的问题，提出了Stable GFlowNets算法，通过理论分析建立了损失函数与分布总变差距离的界限，实现了更稳定的训练和更高的分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成流网络在理论上具有采样优势，但在实际训练中经常出现不稳定现象，表现为严重的损失尖峰和模式崩塌。作者发现小的总变差距离并不能保证训练损失有界，这种不匹配促使了本研究。

Method: 首先评估GFlowNet目标的敏感性，推导出损失到总变差距离的界限，建立从有界的轨迹平衡损失到全局保真度的反向保证。基于这些理论结果，提出Stable GFlowNets算法来稳定训练过程。

Result: 通过实验验证，所提出的Stable GFlowNets算法在训练稳定性方面表现更好，同时实现了更优的分布保真度。理论分析证明了损失函数与分布距离之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了GFlowNet训练不稳定的核心问题，通过理论指导的算法设计，在保持理论严格性的同时显著提升了实际训练效果和分布学习质量。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) learn to sample states proportional to an unnormalized reward. Despite their theoretical promise, practical training is often unstable, exhibiting severe loss spikes and mode collapse. To tackle this, we first assess the sensitivity of GFlowNet objectives, demonstrating that a small Total Variation (TV) distance between the learned and target distributions does not preclude unbounded training loss. Motivated by this mismatch, we establish converse guarantees by deriving loss-to-TV bounds that certify global fidelity from bounded trajectory balance losses. Lastly, we propose Stable GFlowNets, an algorithm that leverages our theoretical results to stabilize training, and empirically demonstrate improved training behavior and superior distributional fidelity.

</details>


### [162] [Robust Linear Dueling Bandits with Post-serving Context under Unknown Delays and Adversarial Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01752)
*Youngmin Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性决斗老虎机在波动环境下的挑战，提出了一种抗延迟、抗干扰且对延迟分布不敏感的算法，其后悔界具有加性代价结构，避免了以往工作中常见的乘性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性决斗老虎机在同时存在服务后上下文、反馈延迟和对抗性干扰的波动环境下的决策问题。现有工作通常导致乘性退化，本文旨在解决这些挑战并改进性能界限。

Method: 提出\term算法，该算法集成了一个学习近似器，用于从服务前信息预测服务后上下文，并采用自适应加权策略，通过裁剪特征向量来同时缓解被污染和延迟观测的影响。

Result: 在标准正则性条件和参数化服务后映射下，算法对延迟分布不敏感，达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d(\sqrt{T} + \mathcal{C} + \mathcal{D}))$的后悔上界，其中$d$为总特征维度，$\mathcal{D}$为延迟复杂度。关键发现是干扰和延迟之间存在加性代价结构，避免了乘性退化。在无服务后上下文的情况下，针对对抗性延迟建立了几乎匹配上界的下界（仅差$\sqrt{d}$因子）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法成功解决了线性决斗老虎机在波动环境下的挑战，实现了对延迟分布不敏感的后悔界限，并通过加性代价结构显著改进了以往工作的性能界限，为该领域提供了新的理论保证。

Abstract: We study linear dueling bandits in volatile environments characterized by the simultaneous presence of post-serving contexts, delayed feedback, and adversarial corruption. Feedback is subject to unknown stochastic or adversarial delays and a cumulative corruption budget $\mathcal{C}$. To address these challenges, we propose \term, which integrates a learned approximator that predicts post-serving contexts from pre-serving information. It further employs an adaptive weighting strategy that clips feature vectors to mitigate the impact of corrupted and delayed observations simultaneously. Under standard regularity conditions and a parametric post-serving mapping, we rigorously establish that our algorithm is delay-regime-agnostic, achieving a regret upper bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d(\sqrt{T} + \mathcal{C} + \mathcal{D}))$, where $d$ is the total feature dimension and $\mathcal{D}$ encapsulates the delay complexity. Crucially, our analysis reveals an additive cost structure between corruption and delay, avoiding the multiplicative degradation typical of prior works. We further establish lower bounds that nearly match our upper bounds up to a $\sqrt{d}$ factor for adversarial delays in the absence of post-serving contexts.

</details>


### [163] [Mitigating Multimodal LLMs Hallucinations via Relevance Propagation at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01766)
*Itai Allouche,Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LIME，一种无需训练的推理时模态增强框架，通过在解码过程中显式增强感知输入的利用，有效缓解多模态大语言模型中的幻觉问题，提升模型对视觉和音频输入的 grounding 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理复杂视觉和音频语言任务时表现优异，但存在严重的幻觉问题，生成内容常与感知输入不符。其根源在于推理过程中存在模态利用不平衡：文本标记的主导地位削弱了感知输入的作用，导致模型倾向于依赖文本语言先验而非基于证据的推理。

Method: 提出Learning Inference-time Modality Enhancement (LIME)框架：1) 利用Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP)量化标记级别的贡献度；2) 定义基于相关性的目标函数，促进模型增加对感知输入的依赖；3) 通过推理时更新模型的键值表示来执行该目标，整个过程无需修改模型参数或额外训练数据。

Result: 在视觉和音频领域的多个多模态基准测试中验证了LIME的有效性：1) 持续降低幻觉现象；2) 增强模型的grounding能力；3) 保持生成质量；4) 分析表明LIME增加了模态贡献度，产生更加局部化和语义对齐的相关性模式。

Conclusion: LIME作为首个无需训练的推理时模态增强方法，成功解决了MLLMs中的模态利用不平衡问题，为提升多模态模型的grounding能力提供了一种高效、通用的解决方案，且不增加训练成本。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have revolutionized the landscape of AI, demonstrating impressive capabilities in tackling complex vision and audio-language tasks. However, a critical challenge remains: these models often suffer from hallucinations, generating outputs that diverge from the provided perceptual inputs. This tendency stems from an inherent imbalance in modality utilization during inference, where the dominance of textual tokens undermines the potential of perceptual inputs. As a result, the model frequently resorts to textual language priors at the expense of grounded evidence. To tackle this issue, we propose Learning Inference-time Modality Enhancement (LIME), a training-free framework designed to bolster multimodal grounding by explicitly enhancing modality usage during decoding. LIME leverages Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) to quantify token-level contributions and defines a relevance-based objective that promotes increased reliance on perceptual inputs. This objective is enforced through inference-time updates to the model's key-value representations, without modifying model parameters or requiring additional training data. We evaluate LIME across multiple multimodal benchmarks in both vision and audio domains, demonstrating consistent reductions in hallucinations and enhanced grounding while preserving generation quality. Further analysis shows that LIME increases modality contribution and produces more localized and semantically aligned relevance patterns.

</details>


### [164] [Adversarial Imitation Learning with General Function Approximation: Theoretical Analysis and Practical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01778)
*Tian Xu,Zhilong Zhang,Zexuan Chen,Ruishuo Chen,Yihao Sun,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了优化对抗模仿学习框架（OPT-AIL），首次在一般函数近似下实现了具有理论保证的高效模仿学习，并在实验中超越了现有深度AIL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗模仿学习理论分析主要局限于表格和线性函数近似等简化场景，且算法设计复杂，难以实际应用，导致理论与实践之间存在显著差距。

Method: 提出优化对抗模仿学习框架（OPT-AIL），包含两个具体方法：无模型OPT-AIL和有模型OPT-AIL。该框架对奖励学习进行在线优化，对策略学习进行乐观正则化优化，只需近似优化两个目标函数，便于实际实现。

Result: 理论分析表明，两种OPT-AIL变体在学习接近专家策略时都能实现多项式级的专家样本复杂度和交互复杂度，这是首次在一般函数近似下实现可证明高效的AIL方法。实验研究表明，OPT-AIL在多个挑战性任务上优于之前的深度AIL方法。

Conclusion: OPT-AIL框架成功弥合了对抗模仿学习理论与实践之间的差距，在保持理论保证的同时实现了实际可行性，为一般函数近似下的模仿学习提供了首个可证明高效的方法。

Abstract: Adversarial imitation learning (AIL), a prominent approach in imitation learning, has achieved significant practical success powered by neural network approximation. However, existing theoretical analyses of AIL are primarily confined to simplified settings, such as tabular and linear function approximation, and involve complex algorithmic designs that impede practical implementation. This creates a substantial gap between theory and practice. This paper bridges this gap by exploring the theoretical underpinnings of online AIL with general function approximation. We introduce a novel framework called optimization-based AIL (OPT-AIL), which performs online optimization for reward learning coupled with optimism-regularized optimization for policy learning. Within this framework, we develop two concrete methods: model-free OPT-AIL and model-based OPT-AIL. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that both variants achieve polynomial expert sample complexity and interaction complexity for learning near-expert policies. To the best of our knowledge, they represent the first provably efficient AIL methods under general function approximation. From a practical standpoint, OPT-AIL requires only the approximate optimization of two objectives, thereby facilitating practical implementation. Empirical studies demonstrate that OPT-AIL outperforms previous state-of-the-art deep AIL methods across several challenging tasks.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond ECE: Calibrated Size Ratio, Risk Assessment, and Confidence-Weighted Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01796)
*Fernando Martin-Maroto,Nabil Abderrahaman,Gonzalo G. de Polavieja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出广泛使用的期望校准误差(ECE)存在缺陷，提出了新的校准指标CSR和风险评估P_risk，并引入置信度加权指标（如cwA、cwAUC）作为补充，实验验证了新方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的期望校准误差(ECE)作为线性度量，对所有置信度水平的校准偏移等同计数，可能导致在存在任意大的过度自信风险时ECE仍然很小，无法有效评估模型校准质量。

Method: 提出可解释的校准指标Calibrated Size Ratio (CSR)，完美校准时值为1，并由此推导出量化过度自信统计证据的风险概率P_risk。同时引入置信度加权指标（如置信度加权准确率cwA和置信度加权AUC）来衡量置信度是否有效区分正确与错误预测，证明cwAUC能够捕获校准信息。

Result: 在多个合成置信度分布和十五个真实数据集上的实验表明，CSR在所有测试条件下均获得接近完美的灵敏度和特异性，验证了所提指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的CSR、P_risk以及置信度加权指标体系能够更全面、可靠地评估模型校准质量和过度自信风险，克服了ECE的固有局限性。

Abstract: Confidence calibration has been dominated by the Expected Calibration Error (ECE), a linear metric that counts calibration offset equally regardless of the confidence level at which it occurs. We show that ECE can remain small even under arbitrarily large overconfidence risk, so we propose Calibrated Size Ratio (CSR) instead, an interpretable metric that equals 1 under perfect calibration, from which we derive the risk probability $P_{\mathrm{risk}}$ that quantifies the statistical evidence for overconfidence. We further argue that overconfidence risk assessment must be complemented by a measure of discriminative value: whether the assigned confidences actively distinguish correct from incorrect predictions. We show that confidence-weighted accuracy $\mathrm{cwA}$ is the natural such complement, and that confidence-weighting extends to all standard classification metrics. In particular, we prove that the confidence-weighted AUC (cwAUC) captures the information about calibration while the classical AUC cannot. We validate the proposed indicators on several synthetic confidence distributions under multiple controlled calibration profiles and on fifteen real datasets with and without post-hoc calibration. Experiments demonstrate that CSR achieves near-perfect sensitivity and specificity across all tested conditions.

</details>


### [166] [Federated Semi-Supervised Graph Neural Networks with Prototype-Guided Pseudo-Labeling for Privacy-Preserving Gestational Diabetes Mellitus Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01810)
*G. Victor Daniela,A. Mallikarjuna Reddya,Uday Kumar Addankia,Sridhar Reddy Gogua,Sravanth Kumar Ramakuria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对妊娠期糖尿病(GDM)早期风险分层中标签稀缺和数据隐私两大瓶颈,提出了一种隐私保护的联邦半监督框架FedTGNN-SS,在多个糖尿病数据集上显著优于现有联邦学习方法,尤其在标签缺失严重时表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠期糖尿病(GDM)是高发妊娠并发症,需要准确的早期风险分层以降低母婴发病率。然而机器学习在真实临床环境中的部署面临两个耦合约束:(i)标签稀缺,大量电子病历(EHR)缺乏确诊标签;(ii)数据隐私问题,阻止了跨医院共享患者级别数据。

Method: 提出FedTGNN-SS框架:每家医院构建局部k近邻患者相似度图并训练拓扑自适应GNN编码器;通过四种策略利用无标签记录:(1)结合邻域一致性的原型引导伪标签,(2)利用学习到的嵌入周期性更新k-NN图的自适应图优化,(3)仅应用于连续变量的临床感知一致性增强,(4)仅交换类别级质心的隐私安全原型共享。

Result: 在三个糖尿病相关数据集(GDM: N=3,525; Pima: N=768; Early Stage: N=520)上,在每层10%-80%标签缺失条件下,FedTGNN-SS对11个联邦基线方法取得56次显著胜利(p<0.05);在极端标签稀缺情况下达到强AUROC(Pima数据集80%缺失时0.8037,Early Stage数据集80%缺失时0.9634)。

Conclusion: FedTGNN-SS成功解决了临床EHR数据中标签稀缺与隐私保护的双重挑战,通过创新的半监督学习策略和隐私安全机制,实现了在极端标签缺失条件下的优异预测性能,为妊娠期糖尿病等临床应用的机器学习部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Gestational Diabetes Mellitus (GDM) is a high-prevalence pregnancy complication that requires accurate early risk stratification to reduce maternal and fetal morbidity. However, real-world clinical deployment of machine learning is hindered by two coupled constraints: (i) label scarcity, where a large fraction of electronic health records (EHR) lack confirmed diagnostic labels, and (ii) data privacy, which prevents sharing patient-level data across hospitals. This paper proposes FedTGNN-SS, a privacy-preserving federated semi-supervised framework for clinical tabular EHR. Each hospital builds a local k-nearest-neighbor patient similarity graph and trains a topology-adaptive GNN encoder. To robustly exploit unlabeled records, FedTGNN-SS combines (1) prototype-guided pseudo-labeling with neighborhood agreement, (2) adaptive graph refinement that periodically updates the k-NN graph using learned embeddings, (3) clinical-aware consistency augmentation applied only to continuous variables, and (4) privacy-safe prototype sharing that exchanges only class-level centroids. Across three diabetes-related datasets (GDM: N = 3,525; Pima: N = 768; Early Stage: N = 520) under 10\%-80\% missing labels per silo, FedTGNN-SS achieves 56 significant wins ($p < 0.05$) against 11 federated baselines and attains strong AUROC under extreme scarcity (Pima: 0.8037 at 80\% missing, Early Stage: 0.9634 at 80\% missing).

</details>


### [167] [Skipping the Zeros in Diffusion Models for Sparse Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01817)
*Phil Sidney Ostheimer,Mayank Nagda,Andriy Balinskyy,Gabriel Vicente Rodrigues,Jean Radig,Carl Herrmann,Stephan Mandt,Marius Kloft,Sophie Fellenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了稀疏利用扩散模型（SED），专门针对稀疏连续数据优化传统扩散模型，通过仅建模非零值保留稀疏性，在保持或提升生成质量的同时实现计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型在处理稀疏连续数据时存在局限性：无法建模代表信号刻意缺失的精确零值，会破坏稀疏模式，并对大量零条目进行不必要的计算，导致计算资源浪费和性能下降。

Method: 提出稀疏利用扩散模型（SED），核心策略是仅建模非零值而跳过零值。在训练和推理阶段通过跳过零值操作来保留数据的稀疏性模式，从而减少不必要的计算开销。

Result: 在物理学和生物学基准测试中，SED在生成质量上匹配或超越传统扩散模型及领域特定基线方法，同时实现了计算节省。视觉实验直观揭示了密集扩散模型的局限性和SED的优势。

Conclusion: SED成功解决了传统扩散模型在稀疏连续数据上的不足，证明了在保持甚至提升生成质量的前提下，通过利用数据稀疏性可以实现显著的计算效率提升，为稀疏数据生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) excel on dense continuous data, but are not designed for sparse continuous data. They do not model exact zeros that represent the deliberate absence of a signal. As a result, they erase sparsity patterns and perform unnecessary computation on mostly zero entries. With Sparsity-Exploiting Diffusion (SED), we model only non-zero values, preserving sparsity. SED delivers computational savings while maintaining or improving generation quality by skipping zeros during training and inference. Across physics and biology benchmarks, SED matches or surpasses conventional DMs and domain-specific baselines, while vision experiments provide intuitive insights into the limitations of dense DMs and the benefits of SED.

</details>


### [168] [Molecular Representations for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01822)
*Nicholas T. Runcie,Fergus Imrie,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MolJSON，一种面向大语言模型的新型分子表示格式。实验表明，与SMILES和IUPAC等现有格式相比，MolJSON在翻译、最短路径推理和约束生成任务中显著提升了LLM的性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地支持科学发现，化学领域的反应预测和结构解析等任务要求模型能够可靠地推理分子结构。然而，先前研究主要使用SMILES字符串或IUPAC名称作为分子表示，尚未系统评估这些格式对LLM的适用性。为了填补这一空白，本文旨在探究不同分子表示格式对LLM性能的影响，并寻求更优的表示方法。

Method: 研究提出了MolJSON这一新型分子表示格式，并在GPT-5-nano、GPT-5-mini、GPT-5和Claude Haiku 4.5四个模型上，使用涵盖翻译、最短路径推理和约束生成任务的78,045个问题，系统地将MolJSON与五种常见化学表示格式进行了对比评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，不同表示格式下LLM解释和生成分子图的能力存在显著差异，MolJSON表现最佳。在翻译任务中，GPT-5将IUPAC名称转换为MolJSON的准确率为71.0%，转换为SMILES仅为43.7%。在约束生成任务中，GPT-5生成MolJSON的准确率达95.3%，远高于IUPAC的76.3%和SMILES的64.0%。在最短路径推理任务中，使用MolJSON作为输入，GPT-5的回答准确率为98.5%，高于SMILES的92.2%和IUPAC的82.7%，且使用的推理令牌更少。此外，SMILES和IUPAC存在与原子数量及环复杂度相关的系统性错误，而MolJSON对此更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，分子表示格式的选择对大语言模型的性能具有实质性影响。显式的分子图模式（如MolJSON）能够显著提升模型在化学任务中的表现，是构建基于LLM的化学系统的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used to support scientific discovery. In chemistry, tasks such as reaction prediction and structure elucidation require reasoning about the structures of molecules. As such, LLM-based systems for chemistry must interact reliably with molecular structures. Most previous studies of LLMs in chemistry have used SMILES strings or IUPAC names as molecular representations; however, the suitability of these formats has not been systematically assessed. In this work, we introduce MolJSON, a novel molecular representation for LLMs, and systematically compare it with five common chemical formats. We evaluated each representation with GPT-5-nano, GPT-5-mini, GPT-5, and Claude Haiku 4.5 using a set of 78,045 questions spanning translation, shortest path, and constrained generation reasoning tasks. We observed substantial variation across representations in the ability of LLMs to interpret and generate molecular graphs, with MolJSON consistently outperforming existing formats. On translation tasks, GPT-5 achieved 71.0% accuracy when converting IUPAC names to MolJSON, compared with 43.7% when converting the same inputs to SMILES. For constrained generation, GPT-5 reached 95.3% accuracy generating MolJSON, compared with 76.3% for IUPAC and 64.0% for SMILES. As an input format for shortest-path reasoning, GPT-5 successfully answered 98.5% of questions with MolJSON, compared with 92.2% for SMILES and 82.7% for IUPAC, whilst also using fewer reasoning tokens. We observed systematic errors associated with atom count and ring complexity for SMILES strings and IUPAC names, whereas MolJSON was more robust to these failure modes. Our results show that the choice of molecular representation has a material impact on LLM performance, and that explicit molecular graph schemas, such as MolJSON, are a promising direction for LLM-based systems in chemistry.

</details>


### [169] [Selector-Guided Autonomous Curriculum for One-Shot Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01823)
*Rudray Dave,Vedang Dubey,Smit Deoghare,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SGAC方法，通过选择器模型动态筛选题目，发现输出分歧度（熵）比奖励方差更能预测推理增益，在有限数据下将数学推理准确率提升至68.0%，超越SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的1-shot RLVR方法在选择训练实例时采用奖励方差等启发式策略，但本文发现这种度量具有误导性，无法有效衡量实例的可迁移性价值，因此需要一种更智能的数据筛选方法。

Method: 提出Selector-Guided Autonomous Curriculum (SGAC)方法，使用可学习的选择器模型在多维特征空间（成功概率、奖励方差、输出分歧度/熵、语义难度）上进行选择，开发了自主课程算法动态从候选池中筛选题目，并运行微批量的1-shot GRPO训练。

Result: 在Hendrycks MATH基准测试上，使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B模型，本文方法达到68.0%准确率，优于SOTA模型的64.0%以及Wang等人提出的1-shot RLVR checkpoint的66.0%；发现输出分歧度而非奖励方差是后续迭代推理增益的最强预测因子。

Conclusion: 基于熵的智能数据筛选在严格推理提升方面优于静态训练方法，特别是在数据严重受限的条件下，验证了动态自主课程学习的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has been established as a highly effective technique for augmenting the math reasoning skills of Large Language Models (LLMs) based on a single instance. Current state-of-the-art 1-shot RLVR models adopt heuristics for selecting instances, mostly based on historical variance in rewards, which we find to be inherently misleading as a measure of transferability value. In this paper, we propose a Selector-Guided Autonomous Curriculum (SGAC) approach, which employs a learnable selector model on a multi-dimensional feature space consisting of success probability, reward variance, output disagreement (entropy), and semantic difficulty level, instead of the static reward variance heuristic. In our empirical evaluation on pools of candidate problems, we observed that output disagreement, rather than reward variance, is the strongest predictor of reasoning gains in subsequent iterations. Leveraging this finding, we develop an autonomous curriculum algorithm for dynamically siphoning candidate problems from a large pool, ranking them by the learned selector, and running micro-bursts of 1-shot GRPO. Our framework is evaluated using the Hendrycks MATH benchmark, with the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B model serving as the baseline. Our framework obtains an accuracy of 68.0\% on the hold-out dataset, which is better than the accuracy obtained from the state-of-the-art model, 64.0\%, as well as the 1-shot RLVR checkpoint proposed by Wang et al., which achieved an accuracy of 66.0\%. The results confirm that entropy-based intelligent data curation leads to strict reasoning improvement over static training methods, particularly in severely limited data conditions.

</details>


### [170] [Learning Koopman operators for coupled systems via information on governing equations of subsystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01835)
*Tatsuya Naoi,Jun Ohkubo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用子系统微分方程学习耦合系统Koopman算子的方法，解决了纯数据驱动方法EDMD在有限数据下不稳定和不准确的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性耦合系统在科学与工程中普遍存在，其高维度和子系统间复杂的相互作用使得分析与建模极具挑战性。尽管基于Koopman算子的扩展动态模态分解(EDMD)是一种流行的近似方法，但作为一种纯数据驱动方法，它在有限数据可用性下对耦合系统表现出不稳定和不准确的问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用描述每个子系统的微分方程来学习耦合系统Koopman算子的新方法，结合了物理模型知识与算子理论。

Result: 通过耦合振荡器系统的数值实验，证明了该方法的有效性，相比于纯数据驱动方法在有限数据条件下表现更优。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了EDMD在有限数据下的问题，为非线性耦合系统的分析与建模提供了一种更稳定、更准确的方法。

Abstract: Nonlinear coupled systems are ubiquitous in science and engineering. The analysis and modeling of such systems is challenging due to their high dimensionality and complex interactions among subsystems. In recent years, operator-theoretic methods based on the Koopman operator have attracted attention as a powerful tool for analyzing and modeling nonlinear dynamical systems. Extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) is one of the most popular methods to approximate the Koopman operator. However, EDMD is a purely data-driven method, and it could be unstable and inaccurate for coupled systems under limited data availability. In this paper, we propose a method to learn the Koopman operator for coupled systems using the differential equations governing each subsystem. We also demonstrate its effectiveness through numerical experiments on coupled oscillator systems.

</details>


### [171] [QHyer: Q-conditioned Hybrid Attention-mamba Transformer for Offline Goal-conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01862)
*Xing Lei,Jincheng Wang,Xuetao Zhang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QHyer方法，通过结合流参数化的Q估计器和混合注意力-Mamba架构，解决了离线目标条件强化学习在部分可观测和历史依赖数据集上的两大挑战：长短期依赖建模困难和稀疏奖励下行为拼接能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的离线数据集往往具有部分可观测性和历史依赖性，同时包含马尔可夫和非马尔可夫特性，违反了标准强化学习的假设。现有的历史感知序列模型（如Decision Transformer）在同时处理局部马尔可夫结构和长程上下文时效率低下且脆弱；混合架构（如LSDT）的固定窗口提取机制无法自适应地处理时序异构场景中变化的依赖长度。此外，在稀疏奖励下，return-to-go（RTG）在子轨迹间缺乏区分度，难以从多样化演示中拼接目标导向行为。

Method: 提出QHyer方法，包含两大核心创新：(1) 用流参数化的、状态条件化的目标导向Q估计器替代RTG，支持跨演示的行为拼接；(2) 引入门控混合注意力-Mamba骨干网络，实现内容自适应的历史压缩，同时保留局部动态特性，能够根据依赖长度自适应调节有效记忆。

Result: 广泛的实验证明，QHyer在非马尔可夫和马尔可夫数据集上均达到了最先进的性能，验证了其在多样化场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: QHyer成功解决了离线目标条件强化学习在复杂现实数据集上的关键瓶颈，通过自适应历史压缩和改进的目标导向信号，实现了在各种数据特性下的鲁棒性能，为处理部分可观测和历史依赖的离线强化学习问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned RL (GCRL) learns goal-reaching policies from static datasets, but real-world datasets are often partially observable and history-dependent, exhibiting a mix of Markovian and non-Markovian that violate standard RL assumptions. History-aware sequence models such as Decision Transformer (DT) are a natural fit for long-term dependency modeling, yet pure attention is inefficient and brittle when handling local Markovian structure and long-range context simultaneously. Although recent hybrid architectures (e.g., LSDT) introduce local extractors to improve local dependencies modeling, the fixed-window extraction cannot adapt its effective memory to varying dependency lengths in temporally heterogeneous settings, often truncating long-range context rather than compressing its content adaptively. Moreover, sequential offline GCRL faces a key bottleneck: under sparse rewards, return-to-go (RTG) becomes non-discriminative across sub-trajectories, providing little guidance signal for stitching goal-reaching behaviors from diverse demonstrations. To address these, we propose \textbf{QHyer}, which replaces RTG with a flow-parameterized, state-conditioned goal-reaching Q-estimator to support stitching across demonstrations, and introduces a gated Hybrid Attention-Mamba backbone that performs content-adaptive history compression while preserving local dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{QHyer} achieves state-of-the-art performance on both non-Markovian and Markovian datasets, validating its effectiveness for diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [172] [Robust Conditional Conformal Prediction via Branched Normalizing Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01868)
*Rui Xu,Xingyuan Chen,Wenxing Huang,Minxuan Huang,Weiyan Chen,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了分支归一化流（BNF）方法，通过构建双分支架构实现校准分布与测试分布间的双向可逆变换，在分布偏移下有效控制了条件覆盖误差，解决了传统共形预测仅保证边缘覆盖而条件覆盖不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测（CP）在分布偏移条件下仅对齐边缘分布分数，虽能保证边缘覆盖率但无法控制单个测试输入的条件覆盖误差，导致在条件分数分布不匹配的区域预测结果不可靠。

Method: 推导了分布偏移下CP条件无效性的上界（基于校准分布与测试分布间的Wasserstein距离），揭示了可逆传输在缓解条件覆盖退化中的作用；据此提出分支归一化流（BNF）双分支架构，第一分支将测试输入归一化至校准分布，第二分支将归一化输入的预测集变换回测试分布，同时保持条件覆盖保证。

Result: 在九个数据集上的大量实验表明，BNF在广泛的置信水平范围内持续提升条件覆盖鲁棒性，有效验证了理论分析与所提方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上建立了分布偏移下条件覆盖误差的界，并提出了实用的BNF方法，为共形预测在分布偏移场景下的可靠应用提供了新的理论洞察和技术方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) constructs prediction sets with marginal coverage guarantees under the assumption that the calibration and test distributions are identical. However, under distribution shift, existing approaches primarily align marginal conformal score distributions, which is sufficient to preserve marginal coverage but does not control the conditional coverage error at individual test inputs. As a consequence, CP can remain unreliable in regions where the conditional score distributions are mismatched. In this work, we bound the conditional invalidity of CP under distribution shift in terms of the Wasserstein distance between the calibration and test distributions. This result highlights the role of invertible transport in mitigating conditional coverage degradation. Motivated by this insight, we introduce Branched Normalizing Flow (BNF), a two-branch architecture that normalizes a test input to the calibration distribution and transforms the prediction set of the normalized input back to the test distribution while preserving conditional guarantees. Empirically, BNF consistently improves conditional coverage robustness on nine datasets across a wide range of confidence levels.

</details>


### [173] [Leveraging Data Symmetries to Select an Optimal Subset of Training Data under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01874)
*Kumar Shubham,Pavan Karjol,Kiran M K,Prathosh AP*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对标签噪声环境下的数据子集选择问题，理论证明了Cutstats方法的性能依赖于k-NN的精度，并提出利用数据不变性提升k-NN性能，从而在高维数据中更准确地识别高质量训练子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明在含噪声标签的数据中存在一个子集，在其上训练模型可达到接近无噪声数据集的性能。广泛使用的Cutstats方法利用k-NN来识别低噪声样本，但其在高维数据上的性能尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究高维噪声环境下如何更有效地识别优质训练子集。

Method: 首先，从理论上建立了通过Cutstats选择的训练子集性能与k-NN准确度之间的关系；其次，证明在噪声环境中利用数据不变性和底层对称性知识可以显著提升k-NN的性能，使其在高维情况下更接近贝叶斯最优分类器；最后，针对现实场景中不变性信息不完全已知的情况，探索使用学习到的不变性表示来辅助识别近最优子集。

Result: 理论分析表明Cutstats方法的性能受k-NN准确度的影响；实验证明利用数据不变性可以在高维噪声环境中显著提升k-NN的性能，使其接近贝叶斯最优分类器；在实际应用中，即使底层不变性信息不完整，学习到的不变性表示仍能有效帮助识别高质量的训练子集。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实践两个层面证明了在高维噪声数据环境下，利用数据不变性可以提高k-NN性能，进而改善Cutstats方法在子集选择中的表现。这为处理现实世界中的标签噪声问题提供了新的解决思路和方法。

Abstract: The performance of machine learning models often relies on large labeled datasets; however, data collected from diverse sources can contain label noise. Recent work has shown that, in noisy settings, there may exist a subset of the training data on which models can achieve performance comparable to training on a noise-free dataset. A widely used method for identifying such subsets is cutstats, which employs k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) to detect low-noise samples. However, its performance on high-dimensional data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we formally establish that the performance of a classifier trained on a subset of a noisy dataset selected via cutstats is influenced by the accuracy of k-NN. We further demonstrate that, in noisy environments, exploiting data invariance and knowledge of underlying symmetries can significantly enhance the performance of k-NN, bringing it closer to the Bayes optimal classifier even in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we show that for real-world scenarios, where information about the underlying invariance is only partially known, learnt invariant representations can still facilitate the identification of near-optimal subsets.

</details>


### [174] [How Label Imbalance Shapes Geometry: A General Spectral Analysis of Multi-Label Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01897)
*Xiaoxuan Ma,Yixuan Yang,Song Li,Xiangyun Hui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经坍塌理论从多分类扩展到多标签分类，通过提出频谱控制框架解决标签相关性和数据不平衡问题，揭示了中心化标签协方差频谱对终端几何稳定性的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于多标签分类中神经坍塌现象的研究依赖于“标签平均”结构，该结构隐含假设标签平衡和组合对称性，忽略了实际场景中常见的标签相关性和数据不平衡导致的几何畸变，且未能解决Li等人(2024)提出的multiplicity-one不平衡猜想。

Method: 提出严格的频谱控制框架分析一般不平衡条件下多标签学习的终端阶段；引入标签协方差频谱κ_m（源自标签分布二阶矩矩阵），用于控制分布依赖的下界几何；从理论上分析中心化标签协方差频谱如何量化最弱的中心化类间对比方向。

Result: 证明了高重数原型遵循类频率加权合成规则而非均匀平均，解决了multiplicity-one不平衡猜想；发现中心化标签协方差频谱通过量化最弱中心化类间对比方向来控制终端几何的稳定性；证明经典标签平均仅在完美正交条件下作为特例出现；合成分布上的数值实验验证了理论界限。

Conclusion: 本文解决了不平衡猜想中缩放平均方面的问题，建立了统一的理论框架，将神经坍塌理论扩展至复杂、不平衡的多标签场景，为理解多标签分类中的几何特性提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: This work investigates the phenomenon of Neural Collapse (NC) in multi-label classification, extending its conceptual framework from multi-class learning to general correlated and imbalanced multi-label settings. Although recent studies have identified a ''tag-wise averaging'' structure for multi-label features, this view relies on implicit assumptions of label balance and combinatorial symmetry. Consequently, it fails to account for the geometrical distortions caused by intrinsic label correlations and data imbalance, which are common in practice. We resolve the multiplicity-one imbalance conjecture raised by Li et al. (2024), showing that higher-multiplicity prototypes obey a class-frequency-weighted synthesis rule rather than uniform averaging. To address this, we propose a rigorous spectral-control framework to analyze the terminal phase of multi-label learning under general imbalanced conditions. We introduce the label covariance spectrum $κ_m$, a scalar controlling the distribution-dependent lower-bound geometry, derived from the second-order moment matrix of the label distribution. Contrary to the averaging perspective, our analysis reveals that the centered label covariance spectrum controls the stability of terminal geometry by quantifying the weakest centered inter-class contrast directions. We prove that the classical Tag-wise Averaging emerges only as a special case under perfect orthogonality. Numerical experiments on synthetic distributions validate our theoretical bounds. This work resolves the scaled-average aspect of the imbalance conjecture and establishes a unifying theoretical framework that extends Neural Collapse to complex, imbalanced multi-label settings.

</details>


### [175] [RefusalGuard: Geometry-Preserving Fine-Tuning for Safety in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01913)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示标准微调会导致安全相关表示发生系统性漂移和几何结构扭曲，进而引发对齐退化；提出表示级微调框架REFUSALGUARD，通过约束隐藏表示空间的更新来保持安全相关结构的稳定性，在多个模型家族和安全基准上取得了与基础安全对齐模型相当的攻击成功率，同时保持了具有竞争力的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对已进行安全对齐的语言模型进行下游任务微调往往会导致拒绝行为显著退化，使模型容易受到对抗性滥用的攻击。尽管先前工作表明安全相关特征在模型激活空间中以结构化表示形式编码，但在微调过程中这些表示如何变化以及为何对齐会退化，仍然缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出REFUSALGUARD，一个表示级微调框架。该方法通过约束隐藏表示空间中的更新，确保安全介导组件保持稳定，同时允许任务特定学习在互补方向上进行，从而在模型适应过程中保留安全相关的结构。

Result: 在包括LLaMA、Gemma和Qwen在内的多个模型家族上，于AdvBench、DirectHarm4和JailbreakBench等对抗性安全基准以及下游效用任务上进行了评估。该方法实现了与基础安全对齐模型相当的攻击成功率，同时保持了具有竞争力的任务性能，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 标准微调通过引起安全相关表示的系统性漂移、几何结构扭曲以及任务优化与安全特征之间的干扰，共同导致有害合规性增加。通过在表示空间约束更新以保持安全相关结构，可以有效缓解对齐退化问题，实现安全性与效用的更好平衡。

Abstract: Fine-tuning safety-aligned language models for downstream tasks often leads to substantial degradation of refusal behavior, making models vulnerable to adversarial misuse. While prior work has shown that safety-relevant features are encoded in structured representations within the model's activation space, how these representations change during fine-tuning and why alignment degrades remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate the representation-level mechanisms underlying alignment degradation. Our analysis shows that standard fine-tuning induces systematic drift in safety-relevant representations, distorts their geometric structure, and introduces interference between task optimization and safety features. These effects collectively lead to increased harmful compliance. Motivated by these findings, we introduce REFUSALGUARD, a representation-level fine-tuning framework that preserves safety-relevant structure during model adaptation. Our approach constrains updates in hidden representation space, ensuring that safety-mediating components remain stable while allowing task-specific learning in complementary directions. We evaluate REFUSALGUARD across multiple model families, including LLaMA, Gemma, and Qwen, on adversarial safety benchmarks such as AdvBench, DirectHarm4, and JailbreakBench, as well as downstream utility tasks. Our approach achieves attack success rates comparable to base safety-aligned models while maintaining competitive task performance, significantly outperforming baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Deep learning-based pavement performance modeling using multiple distress indicators and road work history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01914)
*Lu Gao,Zhe Han,Yunshen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用卷积神经网络(CNN)和长短期记忆网络(LSTM)等深度神经网络对路面退化过程进行建模，基于德州交通部18年来收集的超过10万个路段的21项柔性路面状况指标数据，结果表明CNN模型在预测路面状况方面优于标准机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路面退化是一个受材料、环境、设计等多种因素影响的复杂动态过程，准确的路面状况预测可以帮助路面管理机构通过更好地协调养护和维修活动，最大化利用现有资源。

Method: 使用德州交通部过去18年收集的路面状况数据和养护修复历史，涵盖超过10万个路面路段的21项柔性路面状况指标（包括裂缝、车辙、松散和平整度），采用卷积神经网络(CNN)和长短期记忆网络(LSTM)等深度神经网络对路面退化过程进行建模。

Result: 获得了有前景的初步结果，案例研究表明所提出的CNN模型在预测路面状况数值方面优于标准机器学习模型。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络（特别是CNN）在路面状况预测任务中表现优异，能够有效建模复杂的路面退化过程，为路面管理机构的资源优化配置提供了可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: The deterioration of pavement is a complex and dynamic process determined by different factors including material, environment, design, and some other unobserved variables. Accurate predictions of pavement condition can help maximize the use of available resources for pavement management agencies through better coordinated preservation and maintenance activities. This paper uses deep neural networks such as the convolutional neural network (CNN) and the long short-term memory (LSTM) to model the pavement deterioration process. In this paper, pavement condition data and maintenance and rehabilitation history collected by the Texas Department of Transportation over the past 18 years were used. Twenty-one flexible pavement condition indicators, including cracking, rutting, raveling, and roughness, collected from more than 100,000 pavement sections were included in the proposed models. Promising preliminary results were obtained. Case study results show that the proposed CNN model outperforms standard machine learning models in predicting pavement condition values.

</details>


### [177] [Training Non-Differentiable Networks via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01928)
*An T. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PolyStep，一种无梯度优化器，通过在压缩子空间中的多面体顶点评估损失并进行重心投影来更新参数，能够有效训练包含不可微组件（如脉冲神经元、量化层等）的神经网络，在多个任务上显著优于现有无梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络中越来越多地集成不可微组件（如脉冲神经元、量化层、离散路由、黑盒模拟器等），导致反向传播无法应用，而替代梯度方法会引入偏差。现有的无梯度方法在这些不可微模型上性能接近随机，亟需一种有效的无梯度优化方法。

Method: PolyStep是一种无梯度优化器，仅使用前向传播更新参数。每一步在压缩子空间中的结构化多面体顶点处评估损失，对生成的成本矩阵计算softmax加权分配，并通过重心投影将粒子向低成本顶点移动。该更新对应于正则化最优传输问题的单侧极限，继承其几何结构而无需Sinkhorn迭代。

Result: 在硬LIF脉冲网络上达到93.4%测试准确率，超过所有无梯度基线60个百分点以上，距离替代梯度Adam上限仅差4.4个百分点。在int8量化、argmax注意力、阶梯激活、硬MoE路由等四种不可微架构上均领先所有无梯度竞争者。在MAX-SAT问题上（100到100万变量），保持92%以上的子句满足率，而进化策略下降8-12个百分点。在RL策略搜索中，在经典控制任务上匹配OpenAI-ES，并在整数和二值量化下保持性能。

Conclusion: 证明了在分段平滑损失上以O(log T/√T)的速率收敛到保守驻点，在主要架构上升级为Clarke驻点，并通过命中时间界扩展到分段常数情形。这些速率匹配已知的前向方法零阶查询复杂度下界。PolyStep为不可微神经网络训练提供了一种高效、理论有保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly embed non-differentiable components (spiking neurons, quantized layers, discrete routing, blackbox simulators, etc.) where backpropagation is inapplicable and surrogate gradients introduce bias. We present PolyStep, a gradient-free optimizer that updates parameters using only forward passes. Each step evaluates the loss at structured polytope vertices in a compressed subspace, computes softmax-weighted assignments over the resulting cost matrix, and displaces particles toward low-cost vertices via barycentric projection. This update corresponds to the one-sided limit of a regularized optimal-transport problem, inheriting its geometric structure without Sinkhorn iterations.
  PolyStep trains genuinely non-differentiable models where existing gradient-free methods collapse to near-random accuracy. On hard-LIF spiking networks we reach 93.4% test accuracy, outperforming all gradient-free baselines by over 60~pp and closing to within 4.4~pp of a surrogate-gradient Adam ceiling. Across four additional non-differentiable architectures (int8 quantization, argmax attention, staircase activations, hard MoE routing) we lead every gradient-free competitor. On MAX-SAT scaling from 100 to 1M variables, we sustain above 92% clause satisfaction while evolution strategies drop 8--12~pp. On RL policy search, we match OpenAI-ES on classical control and retain performance under integer and binary quantization that collapses gradient-based methods. We prove convergence to conservative-stationary points at rate $O(\log T/\sqrt{T})$ on piecewise-smooth losses, upgraded to Clarke-stationary on the headline architectures and extended to the piecewise-constant regime via a hitting-time bound. These rates match the known zeroth-order query-complexity lower bounds that all forward-only methods inherit. Code is available at https://github.com/anindex/polystep.

</details>


### [178] [Pandora's Regret: A Proper Scoring Rule for Evaluating Sequential Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01936)
*Gerardo A. Flores,Yash Deshpande,Jannis R. Brea,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pandora's Regret，一种面向序贯搜索场景的新型评分规则，相比传统的对数损失等指标，它能更准确地反映搜索成本并更好地预测实际诊断开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序贯搜索中，替代项会被逐一测试直至找到真实类别。然而，标准的严格评分规则（如对数损失）是局部的，忽略了竞争者的排名，导致模型评估与搜索效用不一致。

Method: 通过分析不同测试成本下最优搜索的期望成本，作者推导出了Pandora's Regret：一种闭式、成对可加且严格的评分规则。该规则既能引出真实概率，又能惩罚干扰项排名高于真实类别的排名颠倒校准错误。作者还构建了一个单参数Beta族，用于平衡排名交换惩罚与概率幅度。

Result: 证明了log loss、准确率和macro-F1所依赖的隐式决策模型与序贯搜索不一致。在597个MedMNIST模型上的实验表明，基于Pandora的指标在预测临床诊断成本方面优于标准替代指标。

Conclusion: 本研究将决策理论评分规则构造扩展到了多类场景，提供了一种既具理论基础又有实际应用价值的评估指标，能有效评估序贯搜索场景下的模型性能。

Abstract: In sequential search, alternatives are tested until the true class is found. Standard proper scoring rules like log loss are local, ignoring the ranking of competitors and misaligning model evaluation with search utility. We show that sequential search induces a pairwise structure that overcomes this. By analyzing the expected cost of optimal search under varying testing costs, we derive Pandora's Regret: a closed-form, pairwise-additive, and strictly proper scoring rule. Pandora's Regret both elicits true probabilities and penalizes rank-reversing miscalibrations where distractors outrank the true class. Our construction yields a one-parameter Beta family that balances penalties for rank-swapping versus probability magnitude, while retaining a grounded interpretation as expected search cost. We prove that log loss, accuracy, and macro-F1 rely on implicit decision models misaligned with sequential search. Across 597 MedMNIST models, Pandora-based metrics better predict clinical diagnostic costs than standard alternatives, extending decision-theoretic scoring rule construction to the multiclass setting.

</details>


### [179] [PepSpecBench: A Unified Evaluation Benchmark for Peptide Tandem Mass Spectrometry Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01945)
*Zhiwen Yang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Yunhua Zhong,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PepSpecBench，一个用于肽段MS/MS光谱预测的统一基准测试框架，解决了评估中数据预处理不一致、数据分割缺陷和缺乏跨物种基准测试三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的肽段MS/MS光谱预测模型评估存在三大问题：(1)不一致的数据预处理和不兼容的模型输出空间阻碍了公平的模型比较；(2)有缺陷的数据分割策略可能导致序列泄露并夸大报告性能；(3)现有评估缺乏全面的跨物种基准测试和对实验条件的系统性稳健性评估。这些挑战模糊了该领域的真实进展。

Method: 提出PepSpecBench统一基准框架，包括：标准化跨互补公共数据集的数据预处理；执行严格的主干不相交分割策略以消除序列泄露；在共享的碎片离子表示空间中评估不同架构；引入全面的多物种评估套件；设计基于物理的元数据扰动探针来评估模型稳健性和仪器感知能力。

Result: 在六个代表性模型上发现了先前未被识别的性能差异和稳健性局限性，揭示了现有模型在不同物种和实验条件下的表现差异。
