Conclusion: PepSpecBench为肽段MS/MS光谱预测提供了公平、全面的评估框架，其发现为未来的模型设计、评估和实际部署提供了可操作的见解，有助于推动计算蛋白质组学领域的发展。

Abstract: Tandem mass spectrometry provides a high-throughput framework for identifying and quantifying proteins in complex biological samples. In computational proteomics, predicting peptide MS/MS spectra is a critical task, enabling downstream applications such as large-scale peptide identification and quantification. While deep learning architectures have substantially improved prediction accuracy, three evaluation challenges obscure the true progress of the field. First, inconsistent data preprocessing and incompatible model output spaces hinder fair model comparison. Second, flawed data splitting strategies can permit hidden sequence leakage and inflate reported performance. Third, existing evaluations typically lack comprehensive cross-species benchmarking and systematic assessment of model robustness to influential experimental conditions. To address these challenges, we propose PepSpecBench, a unified benchmark for peptide MS/MS spectrum prediction. PepSpecBench standardizes data preprocessing across complementary public datasets, enforces a strict backbone-disjoint splitting strategy to eliminate sequence leakage, and evaluates diverse architectures within a shared fragment-ion representation space. It further introduces a comprehensive multi-species evaluation suite and physically grounded metadata perturbation probes to assess model robustness and instrument awareness. We uncover previously unrecognized performance discrepancies and robustness limitations across six representative models, providing actionable insights for future model design, evaluation and practical deployment.

</details>


### [180] [TRAP: Tail-aware Ranking Attack for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01950)
*Siyuan Duan,Ke Zhang,Xizhao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对世界模型提出了一种新型的后门攻击框架TRAP，利用世界模型中想象轨迹排名的长尾结构脆弱性，通过操纵决策关键轨迹的排序来劫持规划过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后门攻击主要针对局部特征、单步预测或即时策略输出，这对反应式模型有效，但在世界模型中效果有限，因为世界模型的学习动力学先验和规划过程会抵消浅层扰动的影响。作者发现世界模型存在一种独特的后门漏洞，源于想象轨迹排名的长尾排序结构，破坏少数决策关键轨迹的排序即可系统性劫持规划。

Method: 提出TRAP后门攻击框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）尾感知排名损失，将优化重点集中在决策关键轨迹上；（2）双重门控机制，稳定优化过程并调节攻击惩罚的应用时机和位置。在触发条件下，TRAP改变想象轨迹的相对排名以重定向规划结果，同时在干净输入上保持正常排名结构。

Result: 在DreamerV3和TD-MPC2上的实验表明，TRAP在多种任务中持续诱导行为偏差并导致显著的性能下降，证明了该攻击方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 世界模型存在独特的排名操纵脆弱性，传统的后门攻击方法不足以应对。研究强调了针对基于世界模型的智能体进行专门安全性评估的必要性，为未来防御机制的开发提供了重要方向。

Abstract: World models enable long-horizon planning by internally generating and evaluating imagined trajectories, making them a promising foundation for generalist agents. However, this imagination-driven decision process also introduces new security risks. Existing backdoor attacks typically aim to manipulate local features, one-step predictions, or instantaneous policy outputs. While such objectives may suffice for weaker reactive models, they are often ineffective against world models, where the learned dynamics prior and planning process can absorb or wash out the effects of shallow perturbations. More importantly, we find that world models exhibit a distinct backdoor vulnerability rooted in the long-tailed ranking structure of imagined trajectories, where disrupting the ordering of a few decision-critical trajectories can systematically hijack planning.
  To exploit this vulnerability, we propose TRAP, a backdoor attack framework for world models that targets imagined trajectory ranking. TRAP combines a tail-aware ranking loss to focus optimization on decision-critical trajectories with dual gating mechanisms that stabilize optimization and regulate when and where the attack penalty is applied. Under trigger conditions, TRAP alters the relative ranking of imagined trajectories to redirect planning outcomes, while largely maintaining the normal ranking structure on clean inputs. Experiments on DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2 across diverse tasks show that TRAP consistently induces sustained behavioral deviations and significant performance degradation, highlighting the need for dedicated security evaluation of world-model-based agents.

</details>


### [181] [Flexi-LoRA with Input-Adaptive Ranks: Efficient Finetuning for Speech and Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01959)
*Zongqian Li,Yixuan Su,Han Zhou,Zihao Fu,Nigel Collier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Flexi-LoRA框架，通过根据输入复杂度动态调整LoRA秩，实现了比静态LoRA更少的参数量和更优的性能，尤其在需要严格推理链的任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）采用静态参数分配策略，无法适应不同复杂度的输入，导致在处理复杂程度各异的输入时性能欠佳。

Method: 提出Flexi-LoRA框架，在训练和推理阶段根据输入复杂度动态调整LoRA的秩（rank），保持训练与推理动态的一致性，实现输入依赖的参数分配。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和语音任务上的实验表明：1) 动态秩调整在更少参数下实现更高性能；2) 数学推理任务对秩动态的依赖度高于QA任务；3) 在需要严格推理链的任务上增益更明显。该方法实现了专家混合框架的关键优势，同时简化了实现并减少了参数冗余。

Conclusion: 保持训练与推理动态的一致性对有效适应至关重要，Flexi-LoRA通过输入自适应的参数分配策略建立了高效微调的新范式，为未来输入自适应微调方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) have become essential for deploying large language models, yet their static parameter allocation remains suboptimal for inputs of varying complexity. We present Flexi-LoRA, a novel framework that dynamically adjusts LoRA ranks based on input complexity during both training and inference. Through empirical analysis across question answering, mathematical reasoning, and speech tasks, we demonstrate that maintaining consistency between training and inference dynamics is important for effective adaptation, particularly for sequential reasoning tasks. Our findings reveal that input-dependent parameter allocation achieves higher performance with fewer parameters by optimally matching rank configurations to question complexity. Furthermore, task-specific dependency on rank dynamics varies, with mathematical reasoning tasks exhibiting higher dependency than QA tasks. Successful adaptation manifests not only in correctness but also in reasoning quality and instruction adherence. Flexi-LoRA consistently outperforms static LoRA while using fewer parameters, with performance gains more pronounced on tasks requiring strict reasoning chains. Our approach realizes key benefits of mixture-of-experts frameworks through a more streamlined implementation, reducing parameter redundancy while improving model capabilities. We provide comprehensive empirical studies across diverse tasks, establishing a basis for future work in input-adaptive and efficient fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [182] [Multi-User Dueling Bandits: A Fair Approach using Nash Social Welfare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01961)
*Maheed H. Ahmed,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对偏好异构用户场景下的决斗老虎机问题，引入Nash社会福利目标以促进公平性，建立了该问题的首个遗憾下界Ω(T^{2/3}min(K,D)^{1/3})，并提出两个算法实现近乎最优的遗憾上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的从人类偏好数据学习的方法通常在所有评估者的平均偏好上进行训练。当偏好存在较大差异时，这种方法对少数群体可能不公平。为了解决在线偏好学习（决斗老虎机）中的公平性问题，特别是在用户偏好异构的场景下，需要新的框架和算法。

Method: 作者假设每个用户拥有特定的Condorcet获胜者，并以之为参考点评估臂的性能。采用Nash社会福利目标（最大化用户效用的乘积）来促进异构用户间的公平性，该目标天然地惩罚不平等并防止单个用户被边缘化。在此基础上，构建困难实例推导遗憾下界，并提出Fair-Explore-Then-Commit和Fair-ε-Greedy两种算法，均包含Condorcet获胜者识别阶段。

Result: 建立了公平决斗老虎机问题的首个遗憾下界：Ω(T^{2/3}min(K,D)^{1/3})，其中T为时间跨度、K为臂数、D为用户数。提出的两种算法具有与下界在T的依赖关系上（至多对数因子）相匹配的遗憾上界，从而量化了异构偏好场景下公平性的代价。

Conclusion: 本文首次在异构偏好的决斗老虎机设置下，量化了公平性的代价，并通过设计算法达到了近乎最优的遗憾保证，为在线偏好学习中的公平性问题提供了理论基准和实践方案。

Abstract: Learning from human preference data is becoming a useful tool, from fine-tuning large language models to training reinforcement learning agents. However, in most scenarios, the model is trained on the average preference of all human evaluators, which, under large variations of preferences, can be unfair to minority groups. In this work, we consider fairness in dueling bandits, a standard framework for online learning from preference data. We assume that each user has a (potentially distinct) Condorcet winner, which is an arm preferred to every other arm. Using these user-specific Condorcet winners as reference points, we evaluate and score arms according to their performance relative to the corresponding winner. To promote fairness across heterogeneous users, we adopt the well-established Nash Social Welfare objective, which maximizes the product of user utilities, thereby inherently penalizing inequality and preventing the marginalization of any single user. Within this framework, we construct a hard instance to establish a regret lower bound of $Ω(T^{2/3}\min(K,D)^\frac{1}{3})$ for a time horizon $T$, $K$ arms, and $D$ users, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first result quantifying the cost of fairness in dueling bandits with heterogeneous preferences. We then present the Fair-Explore-Then-Commit and Fair-$ε$-Greedy algorithms with a Condorcet winner identification phase. We further derive their regret upper bounds that match the lower-bound dependence on $T$ up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [183] [Retrieval with Multiple Query Vectors through Anomalous Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01965)
*Allassan Tchangmena A Nken,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Miriam Rateike,Celia Cintas,Skyler Speakman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多查询向量的检索方法，利用异常模式检测思想，通过识别查询向量集中的异常维度，在向量数据库中检索具有相似异常模式的向量，实验表明该方法在多查询场景下性能优于传统单查询检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的向量检索问题通常只考虑单个查询向量作为输入，但复杂的推理和检索任务往往需要多个查询向量而非单一向量。现有的单查询向量检索方法无法充分利用多个查询向量中蕴含的信息，因此需要一种能够同时处理多个查询向量的检索方法。

Method: 该方法利用异常模式检测的概念：首先接收一组查询向量Q（|Q|≥1），识别Q中突出（异常）于其他维度的向量维度子集；然后扫描向量数据库，检索在先前识别的维度上同样表现出异常特征的向量集合，将其作为检索结果返回。

Result: 在两个图像数据集、一个文本数据集和一个表格数据集上验证了该方法。实验结果表明：在大多数数据集上，更大的查询集会带来更好的检索性能；当查询集大小从1增加到8时，性能提升最为显著，超过8后增益逐渐减小。

Conclusion: 所提出的多查询向量检索方法能够有效利用多个查询向量的信息，相比传统单查询检索方法具有更好的检索性能。该方法为复杂推理和检索任务提供了一种有效的解决方案，且查询集规模在8个左右时能达到较好的性价比。

Abstract: A classical vector retrieval problem typically considers a \emph{single} query embedding vector as input and retrieves the most similar embedding vectors from a vector database. However, complex reasoning and retrieval tasks frequently require \emph{multiple query vectors}, rather than a single one. In this work, we propose a retrieval method that considers multiple query vectors simultaneously and retrieves the most relevant vectors from the database using concepts from anomalous pattern detection. Specifically, our approach leverages a set of query vectors $Q$ (with $|Q|\geq 1$), and identifies the subset of vector dimensions within $Q$ that standout (anomalous) from the rest of dimensions. Next, we scan the vector database to retrieve the set of vectors that are also anomalous across the previously identified vector dimensions and return them as our retrieved set of vectors. We validate our approach on two image datasets, a text dataset, and a tabular dataset. Overall, we observe that, across most datasets, larger query sets lead to improved retrieval performance. The improvement is most pronounced when increasing the query sets from 1 to 8, while the gains become smaller beyond that.

</details>


### [184] [MER-DG: Modality-Entropy Regularization for Multimodal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01967)
*Yavuz Yarici,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态域泛化中的“融合过拟合”问题，提出了一种通用的正则化方法MER-DG，通过最大化各编码器特征分布的熵来保持特征多样性，在多个基准测试上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模态域泛化任务中，标准架构通过联合优化训练编码器和融合模块。本文发现这种联合优化会导致编码器利用跨模态共现性——即源于源域特定记录条件的统计关系，而非学习域不变特征。作者将这种失效模式定义为“融合过拟合”（Fusion Overfitting）。

Method: 提出模态熵正则化域泛化方法（MER-DG）。该方法通过最大化每个编码器特征分布的熵来保持特征多样性，从而避免编码器过度依赖跨模态共现性。MER-DG与架构无关，可作为一个附加的损失项集成到现有的多模态框架中。

Result: 在EPIC-Kitchens和HAC基准数据集上进行了广泛实验。结果表明，相比标准融合方法，平均性能提升约5%；相比最先进的方法，平均性能提升约2%。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多模态域泛化中的融合过拟合现象，并提出了一种简单有效且通用的解决方案MER-DG。该方法能有效促使编码器学习域不变特征，显著提升了模型在未见过的环境中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Deploying multimodal models in real-world scenarios requires generalization to new environments where recording conditions differ from training, a challenge known as multimodal domain generalization (MMDG). Standard architectures employ separate encoders for each modality and a fusion module, training the system end-to-end by optimizing on the fused features. In this paper, we identify that such joint optimization causes encoders to exploit cross-modal co-occurrences, statistical relationships between modalities that arise from source-specific recording conditions, rather than learning domain-invariant features. We term this failure mode Fusion Overfitting. To address this, we propose Modality-Entropy Regularization for Domain Generalization (MER-DG), which maximizes the entropy of each encoder's feature distribution to preserve feature diversity. MER-DG is architecture-agnostic and integrates into existing multimodal frameworks as an additive loss term. Extensive experiments on EPIC-Kitchens and HAC benchmarks demonstrate average improvements of approximately 5% over standard fusion and approximately 2% over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [185] [AdamO: A Collapse-Suppressed Optimizer for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01968)
*Nan Qiao,Sheng Yue,Shuning Wang,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了优化器动态本身可直接引发或抑制离线强化学习中的TD误差爆炸问题，提出了带正交约束的AdamO优化器，从理论和实验上保证了稳定性与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，自举的时序差分（TD）更新常因误差放大导致Q值崩溃。现有观点多归咎于备份规则或网络架构，本文旨在揭示优化器动态本身也是导致不稳定的关键因素，并从控制论视角寻求解决之道。

Method: 从控制论视角将离线TD学习建模为反馈系统，分析Adam更新动态，推导出稳定性的充要条件（更新算子的谱半径严格小于1）。针对Adam可能扭曲参数几何的问题，提出AdamO优化器，引入由严格任务对齐预算控制的解耦正交校正约束。

Result: 理论分析证明AdamO能保证最坏情况下的任务安全性并保留Adam的连续时间耗散动态。实验表明，AdamO广泛兼容多种离线RL基线，在多个基准测试中均提升了稳定性与回报。

Conclusion: 优化器动态对离线RL稳定性至关重要。AdamO通过正交约束有效抑制了TD误差放大，为离线RL提供了一种理论有据、兼容性强且性能优越的优化方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) can fail spectacularly when bootstrapped temporal-difference (TD) updates amplify their own errors, driving the critic toward extreme and unusable Q-values. A key counterintuitive insight of this work is that collapse is not only a property of the backup rule or network architecture: optimizer dynamics themselves can directly trigger or suppress instability. From a control-theoretic viewpoint, we model offline TD learning as a feedback system and analyze Adam-based critic updates. This yields a necessary and sufficient condition for stability of the induced local update dynamics: within the regime we analyze, these dynamics are stable if and only if the spectral radius of the corresponding update operator is strictly below one. Further analysis suggests that standard Adam updates can inadvertently distort the parameter geometry, motivating explicit orthogonality constraints to prevent TD error amplification. To this end, we propose AdamO, an Adam-based optimizer with a decoupled orthogonality correction regulated by a strict task-alignment budget. We prove that this design theoretically guarantees worst-case task safety and preserves Adam's continuous-time dissipative dynamics. Empirically, AdamO is broadly compatible with diverse offline RL baselines, improving stability and returns across a broad suite of benchmarks.

</details>


### [186] [Misclassification Rate and Privacy-Utility Trade-offs in Graph Convolutional Networks via Subsampling Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01987)
*Yexin Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Qiaosheng Zhang,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过子采样稳定性框架对差分隐私下的图卷积网络（GCN）进行了严格的理论分析，推导出了与子采样概率相关的错误率上界，并确定了隐私-效用权衡的可行参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图卷积网络在处理图结构数据时面临隐私泄露风险，而现有的差分隐私机制在GCN中的理论分析尚不完善。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过子采样稳定性框架建立DP-GCN的严格理论基础。

Method: 采用子采样稳定性（subsampling stability）作为核心理论框架，推导分类错误率关于子采样概率$p_s$的上界表达式，并分析$p_s$参数变化对隐私保障条件和模型精度的双重影响。

Result: 1. 推导出显式依赖于子采样概率$p_s$的错误率上界；2. 揭示了$p_s$的可行范围：$p_s$过大时稳定性条件难以满足导致隐私保证失效，$p_s$过小时模型精度下降；3. 建立了隐私-效用权衡的理论刻画。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解差分隐私下图卷积网络的子采样稳定性提供了首个严格的理论框架，明确了子采样概率的可行区间选择原则，为DP-GCN的实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study differential privacy (DP) in Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) through the framework of \textit{subsampling stability}. We derive upper bounds on the misclassification rate that depend explicitly on the subsampling probability $p_s$. Furthermore, we characterize the \textit{privacy--utility trade-off} by identifying feasible ranges of $p_s$; if $p_s$ is too large, the stability-based privacy condition becomes difficult to satisfy, yielding vacuous guarantees, whereas if it is too small, accuracy deteriorates. Our results provide the first rigorous theoretical framework for understanding subsampling stability in GCNs under DP.

</details>


### [187] [RamanBench: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Machine Learning on Raman Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02003)
*Mario Koddenbrock,Christoph Lange,Robin Legner,Martin Jäger,Martin Kögler,Mariano N. Cruz Bournazou,Peter Neubauer,Felix Biessmann,Erik Rodner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RamanBench，这是首个大规模、完全可复现的拉曼光谱机器学习基准，涵盖74个数据集和超过32万条光谱。基准测试发现表格基础模型表现最优，但所有方法均无法跨数据集泛化，揭示了领域内关键的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱作为一种广泛使用的非侵入式分子分析技术，其机器学习应用进展受到数据集碎片化、评估标准不一致以及模型无法有效捕捉光谱数据结构等问题的限制，缺乏标准化基准严重制约了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建RamanBench基准平台，整合74个数据集（含16个首次发布），涵盖四个领域共325,668条光谱，包含分类和回归任务。制定标准化评估协议，对28个模型进行系统基准测试，包括经典方法（如PLS）、拉曼专用模型（如RamanNet）、表格基础模型（如TabPFN）和时序方法（如ROCKET）。

Result: 表格基础模型（TFM）持续优于领域专用模型和梯度提升基线方法，时序模型也表现良好。然而，没有任何一种方法能够实现跨数据集泛化，揭示了该领域存在根本性的研究空白。

Conclusion: RamanBench填补了拉曼光谱机器学习领域标准化基准的空白，为模型评估提供了统一平台。当前方法的泛化能力不足表明需要开发新的方法论。研究团队邀请社区贡献新方法，以推动医学诊断、生物研究和材料科学等关键应用的发展。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) has transformed many scientific fields, yet key applications still lack standardized benchmarks. Raman spectroscopy, a widely used technique for non-invasive molecular analysis, is one such field where progress is limited by fragmented datasets, inconsistent evaluation, and models that fail to capture the structure of spectral data. We introduce RamanBench, the first large-scale, fully reproducible benchmark for ML on Raman spectroscopy, consisting of streamlined data access, evaluation protocols and code, as well as a live leaderboard. It unifies 74 datasets (including 16 first released with this benchmark) across four domains, comprising 325,668 spectra and spanning classification and regression tasks under diverse experimental conditions. We benchmark 28 models under a standardized protocol, including classical methods (e.g., PLS), Raman-specific (e.g., RamanNet), Tabular Foundation Model (TFM) (e.g., TabPFN), and time-series approaches (e.g., ROCKET). TFM consistently outperform domain-specific and gradient boosting baselines, while time-series models remain competitive. However, no method generalizes across datasets, revealing a fundamental gap. Therefore, we invite the community to contribute new approaches to our living benchmark, with the potential to accelerate advances in critical applications such as medical diagnostics, biological research, and materials science.

</details>


### [188] [How Can One Choose the Best CAM-Based Explainability Method for a CNN Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02007)
*Daniel da Silva Costa,Pedro Nuno de Souza Moura,Adriana C. F. Alvim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多种距离度量评估深度学习可解释性方法（显著性图）与人类感知的相似性，发现曼哈顿距离和相关性度量效果最佳，LayerCAM、Score-CAM和IS-CAM最能反映人类感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在高风险领域广泛应用，需要清晰解释。现有可解释性评估主要依赖IoU指标，但显著性图形状不规则，假设存在更好指标能评估可解释性方法与人类感知的相似性。

Method: 使用ImageNet吉娃娃图像子集，通过多种CAM-based方法生成显著性图。应用不同距离度量测量人类标注边界框与显著性图的对齐程度，生成排名并与众包收集的人类选择排名比较，使用RBO度量评估排名相似性。

Result: 方法可行，能找到最符合人类感知的可解释性方法。曼哈顿距离和相关性度量最能反映人类感知；LayerCAM、Score-CAM和IS-CAM是最佳可解释性方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法可有效评估可解释性方法与人类感知的一致性，为选择合适指标提供依据，替代传统IoU度量。

Abstract: In recent years, several advances have been observed in Deep Learning with surprising results. Models in this area have been increasingly used in numerous applications, including those sensitive to human life, which require clear explanations and justifications. Various explainability methods have been proposed, but not many metrics to evaluate these methods. The most commonly used metric is the Intersection over Union (IoU). However, due to the characteristics of the results of the explainability methods, called saliency maps, which do not have a known shape, we hypothesise that there must be a better metric that allows one to find an explainability method that produces results that best resemble the human perception. We propose using different metrics to assess the similarity between human perception and the explanation saliency maps to find a better metric. An investigation was conducted employing a subset of the Chihuahuas images from ImageNet dataset. Several CAM-based explainability methods were used to generate saliency maps for each chihuahua image. Alignment was measured by applying distance metrics between the bounding box of human annotations and the saliency maps produced by each explainability method. Rankings of the best saliency maps were created using the results of the distance metrics and compared to the ranking obtained using people's choice, collected through crowdsourcing, of the best explanation saliency maps for each selected image. Comparison between rankings was performed using the Rank-Biased Overlap (RBO) metric. The results indicate the feasibility of our method to find the explainability method that best resembles human perception. In our experiments, the two metrics that best resemble human perception corresponded to Manhattan and Correlation. Besides, the best explainability methods regarding human perception were LayerCAM, Score-CAM, and IS-CAM.

</details>


### [189] [Robust and Explainable Divide-and-Conquer Learning for Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02015)
*Yan Zhou,Kevin Hamlen,Michael De Lucia,Murat Kantarcioglu,Latifur Khan,Sharad Mehrotra,Ananthram Swami,Bhavani Thuraisingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种相关感知的分治学习技术，通过将复杂的入侵检测问题分解为多个子问题，使轻量级模型在资源受限设备上实现了更高的准确率、更小的模型尺寸以及更好的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于机器学习的入侵检测模型为了处理高维、噪声大且类别不平衡的原始网络流量，往往需要复杂的模型结构，这使得在处理能力和内存受限的资源受限设备上进行部署变得不切实际。

Method: 提出一种相关感知的分治学习技术，将复杂的学习问题分解为更小、更易于管理的子问题，从而可以在聚焦的子任务上训练如决策树等轻量级模型。

Result: 在真实网络入侵检测数据集上，该方法实现了局部准确率提高高达43.3%，模型尺寸缩减高达257倍，同时增强了对抗鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过任务分解，可以用极轻量级的模型在资源受限环境下实现高效、鲁棒且可解释的网络入侵检测。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection requires complex models to capture patterns in high-dimensional, noisy, and class-imbalanced raw network traffic, yet deploying such models remains impractical on resource-constrained devices with limited processing power and memory. In this paper, we present a correlation-aware divide-and-conquer learning technique that decomposes a complex learning problem into smaller, more manageable subproblems. This enables lightweight models as simple as decision trees to be trained on focused subtasks, yielding up to 43.3% higher local accuracy and up to 257 times reduction in model size on real-world network intrusion detection datasets, while also improving adversarial robustness and explainability.

</details>


### [190] [Towards Systematic Generalization for Power Grid Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02026)
*Zeeshan Memon,Yijiang Li,Hongwei Jin,Kibaek Kim,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个联合学习框架，通过共享的图神经网络骨干网络同时建模ACOPF和SCUC两个电力系统核心优化问题，实现了跨问题的知识共享和更好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACOPF和SCUC是电力系统运行中的基础优化问题，虽然它们共享相同的传输网络和物理定律，但在决策变量和时间耦合方面存在差异。现有的基于学习的方法将它们孤立处理，导致模型和表示相互割裂，无法充分利用它们之间的内在联系。

Method: 提出一个联合学习框架，包含：(1)共享的图骨干网络，捕获电网拓扑和物理交互；(2)针对静态和时序决策的任务特定解码器；(3)结合求解器监督和物理信息目标函数的训练方式，以强制AC可行性和跨时间运行约束。评估包括跨案例迁移测试和UC-ACOPF问题的系统泛化测试。

Result: 在多个电网规模上的实验表明，该模型相比现有的学习基线方法具有更好的性能和可迁移性。模型能够在未见过的电网拓扑上对ACOPF和SCUC进行零样本迁移，无需重新训练，并能有效支持跨异构电力系统优化问题的学习。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合学习框架成功打破了ACOPF和SCUC问题的孤立建模方式，通过共享的图表示和物理约束实现了跨问题的知识迁移，为异构电力系统优化问题提供了统一的学习方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: AC Optimal Power Flow (ACOPF) and Security-Constrained Unit Commitment (SCUC) are fundamental optimization problems in power system operations. ACOPF serves as the physical backbone of grid simulation and real-time operation, enforcing nonlinear power flow feasibility and network limits, while SCUC represents a core market-level decision process that schedules generation under operational and security constraints. Although these problems share the same underlying transmission network and physical laws, they differ in decision variables and temporal coupling, and prior learning-based approaches address them in isolation, resulting in disjoint models and representations.We propose a learning framework that jointly models ACOPF and SCUC through a shared graph-based backbone that captures grid topology and physical interactions, coupled with task-specific decoders for static and temporal decision-making. Training includes solver supervision with physics-informed objectives to enforce AC feasibility and inter-temporal operational constraints. To evaluate generalization, we assess cross-case transfer on unseen grid topologies for ACOPF and SCUC without retraining, and systematic generalization on the UC-ACOPF problem using unsupervised, physics-based objectives and a power-dispatch consensus mechanism. Experiments across multiple grid scales demonstrate improved performance and transferability relative to existing learning-based baselines, indicating that the model can support learning across heterogeneous power system optimization problems.

</details>


### [191] [Large margin classifier with graph-based adaptive regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02027)
*Vítor M. Hanriot,Turíbio T. Salis,Luiz C. B. Torres,Frederico Coelho,Antonio P. Braga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了基于Gabriel图的二分类器中的逐类正则化超参数，实现了更灵活的阈值设定，有效处理了异常值和类别不平衡问题，提升了分类器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于Gabriel图的二分类器使用固定阈值，在处理异常值和类别不平衡问题时存在局限性。作者希望通过引入逐类正则化超参数，使阈值更加灵活，从而扩大解空间并优化分类器性能。

Method: 在基于Gabriel图的二分类器中引入逐类正则化超参数，分析正则化质量指标在边际区域和异常值存在情况下的行为特征，通过超参数调优算法优化灵活阈值，为多数类和少数类分别生成高低不同的阈值。

Result: 灵活阈值方案能够有效消除训练过程中的异常值，通过为多数类设置较高阈值、少数类设置较低阈值来解决类别不平衡问题。Friedman检验结果表明，灵活阈值能够显著提升基于Gabriel图的分类器性能。

Conclusion: 相比于固定阈值的单一解，基于逐类正则化超参数的灵活阈值方法扩展了解空间，在异常值处理和类别不平衡方面表现出优越性，为Gabriel图分类器的改进提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper introduces the use of per-class regularization hyperparameters in Gabriel graph-based binary classifiers. We demonstrate how the quality index used for regularization behaves both in the margin region and in the presence of outliers, and how incorporating this regularization flexibility can lead to solutions that effectively eliminate outliers while training the classifier. We also show how it can address class imbalance by generating higher and lower thresholds for the majority and minority classes, respectively. Thus, rather than having a single solution based on fixed thresholds, flexible thresholds expand the solution space and can be optimized through hyperparameter tuning algorithms. Friedman test shows that flexible thresholds are capable of improving Gabriel graph-based classifiers.

</details>


### [192] [TIJERE: A Novel Threat Intelligence Joint Extraction Model Based on Analyst Expert Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02041)
*Inoussa Mouiche,Sherif Saad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIJERE联合抽取框架，通过多序列标注表示方法解决威胁情报抽取中的特征混淆和语言歧义问题，并构建了首个公开的网络安全联合标注数据集DNRTI-JE，在NER和RE任务上分别取得超过0.93和0.98的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合抽取方法在处理威胁情报报告时面临特征混淆、语言歧义、噪声传播和重叠关系等挑战，导致准确率低、模型性能差，且缺乏公开的网络安全领域联合标注数据集。

Method: 将联合抽取建模为多序列标注表示(MSLR)问题，为每个实体对生成独立序列，融合专家领域特征增强位置、上下文和语义表示；利用SecureBERT+上下文语言模型减少语言歧义并增强领域泛化能力。

Result: 在构建的DNRTI-JE数据集上，TIJERE在命名实体识别(NER)任务达到F1分数超过0.93，在关系抽取(RE)任务达到F1分数超过0.98，显著优于现有方法，达到最先进性能水平。

Conclusion: TIJERE框架和DNRTI-JE数据集成功解决了网络安全威胁情报抽取的关键问题，实现了高性能的知识抽取，具有向医疗保健、金融和生物信息学等领域迁移应用的潜力。

Abstract: The extraction of entities and relationships from threat intelligence reports into structured formats, such as cybersecurity knowledge graphs, is essential for automated threat analysis, detection, and mitigation. However, existing joint extraction methods struggle with feature confusion, language ambiguity, noise propagation, and overlapping relations, resulting in low accuracy and poor model performance. This paper presents TIJERE, an innovative joint entity and relation extraction framework that formulates joint extraction as a multisequence labeling representation (MSLR) problem. Specifically, separate sequences are generated for each entity pair. Unlike prior tagging schemes, MSLR integrates expert domain features to enrich positional, contextual, and semantic representations of entities, thereby enhancing feature distinction and classification accuracy. Additionally, TIJERE reduces language ambiguity and enhances domain-specific generalization by leveraging SecureBERT+, a contextual language model fine-tuned on cybersecurity text. This improves both named entity recognition (NER) and relation extraction (RE). This paper also introduces DNRTI-JE, the first publicly available jointly labeled dataset for cybersecurity entity and RE, filling a crucial gap in cyber threat intelligence automation. Empirical evaluations on the curated DNRTI-JE dataset demonstrate that TIJERE achieves state-of-the-art performance, with F1-scores exceeding 0.93 for NER and 0.98 for RE, outperforming existing methods. Together, TIJERE and the standardized benchmarking DNRTI-JE dataset enable high-performance cybersecurity intelligence extraction, with transferable applications in healthcare, finance, and bioinformatics.

</details>


### [193] [Bringing Order to Asynchronous SGD: Towards Optimality under Data-Dependent Delays with Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02043)
*Tehila Dahan,Roie Reshef,Sharon Goldstein,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动量的异步SGD框架，解决了现有方法因丢弃延迟梯度导致的信息偏差问题，首次在数据相关延迟下实现了最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步随机梯度下降（SGD）缓解策略（如延迟自适应学习率或过滤）通常会衰减或丢弃延迟梯度，导致系统性偏差：简单样本的更新被过度代表，而复杂样本的梯度被延迟或抑制。先前针对数据相关延迟的方法依赖Lipschitz假设导致收敛率次优，或未解决平滑凸情况。

Method: 提出一种基于动量的异步框架，旨在保留延迟梯度的信息同时缓解陈旧性的影响，并在标准假设下推导出鲁棒的学习率调度以简化超参数调整。

Result: 在凸和非凸平滑设置下，首次建立了数据相关延迟的最优收敛率，为标准假设下的异步优化提供了新结果。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅解决了异步SGD中的梯度陈旧性与信息偏差问题，还提供了理论上的最优收敛保证，具有重要的理论价值与实际应用意义。

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (SGD) enables scalable distributed training but suffers from gradient staleness. Existing mitigation strategies, such as delay-adaptive learning rates and staleness-aware filtering, typically attenuate or discard delayed gradients, introducing systematic bias: updates from simpler or faster-to-process samples are overrepresented, while gradients from more complex samples are delayed or suppressed. In contrast, prior approaches to data-dependent delays rely on a Lipschitz assumption that yields suboptimal rates or leave the smooth, convex case unaddressed. We propose a momentum-based asynchronous framework designed to preserve information from delayed gradients while mitigating the effects of staleness. We establish the first optimal convergence rates for data-dependent delays in both convex and non-convex smooth setups, providing a new result for asynchronous optimization under standard assumptions. Additionally, we derive robust learning-rate schedules that simplify hyperparameter tuning in practice.

</details>


### [194] [NeuroViz: Real-time Interactive Visualization of Forward and Backward Passes in Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02044)
*Reza Rawassizadeh,Tanvi Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了NeuroViz，一个用于全连接神经网络训练的交互式可视化工具，用户研究显示其具有较高的可用性评分，并显著提升了用户对神经网络训练过程的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练过程难以解释，尤其对于新手而言，缺乏直观的理解工具。这激发了对能够实时探索和可视化神经网络训练过程的交互式工具的需求。

Method: 开发了NeuroViz交互式可视化工具，允许用户配置网络架构、激活函数、学习率和数据集。工具实时显示激活值、权重更新和损失变化，将权重变化与前向和反向传播中的激活信号直接对应，支持在单个训练周期内区分更新前后的状态，并动态显示每个神经元的计算方程。

Result: 与6种现有可视化工具进行的对比用户研究（31名参与者）显示，NeuroViz获得了最高的可用性评分（SUS 80.97，属于“优秀”级别）；清晰度平均排名2.47，有用性平均排名2.23（数值越低越好）；超过70%的参与者认为可视化显著提高了对神经网络训练透明度的感知。

Conclusion: NeuroViz有效提升了神经网络训练过程的可解释性和透明度，为用户提供了直观的实时可视化体验，具有良好的可用性和教育价值。

Abstract: Training neural networks is difficult to interpret, particularly for newcomers. We introduce NeuroViz, an interactive visualization tool that supports real-time exploration of fully connected neural network training. Users can configure network architecture, activation functions, learning rates, and datasets, then observe activations, weight updates, and loss progression. NeuroViz visualizes weight changes in direct correspondence with activation signals in both forward and backward passes, enabling users to distinguish pre- and post-update states within individual epochs and view dynamically updating per-neuron equations. We conduct a comparative user study with 31 participants against six established visualization tools and we achieved the highest usability score (SUS 80.97, in the 'excellent' range), with mean rankings of 2.47 for clarity and 2.23 for usefulness (lower is better). Over 70% of participants reported that the visualizations substantially increased their perception of neural network training transparency. The implemented instance is accessible at https://neuroviz.org.

</details>


### [195] [DR-SNE: Density-Regularized Stochastic Neighbor Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02060)
*Maksim Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出密度正则化随机邻域嵌入(DR-SNE)，通过直接对齐归一化密度估计来解决现有降维方法忽略数据密度分布的问题，在保持邻域保真度的同时显著提升了密度保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降维方法（如t-SNE）虽然能保持局部邻域结构，但未显式考虑概率质量分布，常导致数据密度失真。现有方法（DensMAP、DenSNE）依赖局部尺度一致性，缺乏简单且尺度不变的密度保持机制。

Method: 将降维重构为两个组件的联合对齐：（i）捕捉局部关系的条件结构，（ii）通过局部密度统计捕捉的相对密度结构。基于此提出DR-SNE，在随机邻域嵌入目标函数中增加源自归一化对数密度估计的密度正则化项，直接对齐归一化密度估计。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，DR-SNE在保持有竞争力的邻域保真度的同时，提升了密度保持能力，并在异常检测等密度敏感任务上获得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 融合密度信息是对降维中以几何为中心的目标函数的有效补充，为密度保持型降维提供了简单且尺度不变的新范式。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction methods such as t-SNE are designed to preserve local neighborhood structure but do not explicitly account for how probability mass is distributed, often leading to distortions of data density. We reformulate dimensionality reduction as the joint alignment of two components: (i) conditional structure, capturing local relationships, and (ii) relative density structure, captured via local density statistics. Based on this perspective, we introduce Density-Regularized SNE (DR-SNE), which augments the stochastic neighbor embedding objective with a density regularization term derived from normalized log-density estimates. Unlike prior approaches such as DensMAP and DenSNE, which rely on local scale consistency, DR-SNE directly aligns normalized density estimates, providing a simple and scale-invariant mechanism for preserving relative density variations. Empirically, DR-SNE improves density preservation while maintaining competitive neighborhood fidelity, and yields gains on density-sensitive tasks such as anomaly detection across multiple datasets. These results suggest that incorporating density information complements geometry-focused objectives in dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [196] [Weight Clipping for Robust Conformal Inference under Unbounded Covariate Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02072)
*James Wang,Surbhi Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对加权共形预测（WCP）在协变量偏移下因密度比估计方差过大导致覆盖率不足的问题，提出了CLISF方法进行低方差密度比估计，并通过膨胀覆盖率目标来校正偏差，提供了首个权重裁剪的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测（CP）依赖于训练和测试数据的可交换性，但在实际应用中常因协变量偏移而失效。现有的加权共形预测（WCP）在密度比无界或需估计时，会因密度比学习的过拟合和非一致性分数阈值估计的高方差，导致严重的覆盖率不足。

Method: 提出了截断最小二乘重要性拟合（CLISF）方法，用于降低密度比估计的方差。将CLISF学习到的密度比应用于WCP中，并证明通过适当膨胀覆盖率目标可以校正覆盖率不足，且膨胀水平可从数据中估计。

Result: 首次为共形推断中的权重截断提供了理论保证，实现了数据集条件下的覆盖率，且样本复杂度不会随真实密度比的高阶矩而爆炸，克服了以往工作的关键局限。在真实世界基准测试和合成数据上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提CLISF方法有效解决了WCP在协变量偏移下的覆盖率不足问题，为共形预测在实际应用中提供了更可靠的理论保证和实践方法。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides powerful, distribution-free prediction sets, but its guarantees rely on the exchangeability of training and test data, which is often violated in practice due to covariate shifts. While weighted conformal prediction (WCP) is designed to handle such shifts, it can suffer from significant undercoverage when the density ratio between the distributions is unbounded and/or must be learned. This is because of both overfitting in learning the density ratio, and high variance in estimating the nonconformity score threshold. To address this, we introduce clipped least-squares importance fitting (CLISF) as a reduced-variance method for density ratio estimation. Specifically, we show that density ratios learned using CLISF, when plugged into WCP, have bounded expected undercoverage. Furthermore, we show that the undercoverage can be corrected by running WCP with a slightly inflated coverage target; crucially, we are able to estimate the required level of inflation from the data. We provide the first theoretical guarantees for weight clipping in conformal inference, achieving dataset-conditional coverage with a sample complexity that does not blow up with the higher moments of the true density ratio -- a key limitation of prior work. We verify our results on real-world benchmarks and synthetic data.

</details>


### [197] [GETA-3DGS: Automatic Joint Structured Pruning and Quantization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02086)
*Baobing Zhang,Wanxin Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GETA-3DGS，首个针对3D高斯泼溅的端到端自动联合结构化剪枝与量化框架，在多个数据集上实现约5倍存储压缩，且无需人工设定阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS表示虽然能实现实时逼真的新视图合成，但单场景存储高达数百MB至数GB，超出移动端和沉浸式平台的预算；现有压缩方法将剪枝、量化、熵编码分阶段独立处理并依赖人工启发式规则，限制了跨场景泛化能力，且无法让用户指定目标码率或质量预算。

Method: 在GETA联合剪枝-量化方法基础上构建：1）3DGS感知的量化感知依赖图(QADG)，将每个高斯基元视为含五个属性子节点和度感知球谐子节点的组；2）渲染感知显著性，融合透射加权贡献、屏幕空间梯度与像素覆盖形成高斯级重要性分数；3）在投影部分显著性引导(PPSG)下降保证下，与结构稀疏性协同优化的异构逐属性混合精度方案。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples和Deep Blending数据集上，相比原始3DGS实现约5倍存储压缩，且无需场景特定阈值；位宽策略是率失真主控因素，均匀6位上限在视图依赖场景上相比异构分配最高损失6.74 dB，并与信息论逆水填充分析一致；方法与现有熵编码(HAC++、CompGS)正交可叠加。

Conclusion: GETA-3DGS首次实现了3DGS的端到端自动联合剪枝与量化，无需手工启发式规则，显著降低存储开销并保持渲染质量，可与下游熵编码方法互补，为移动端与沉浸式应用提供了实用的高效压缩方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is a state-of-the-art representation for real-time photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet a single high-fidelity scene typically occupies hundreds of megabytes to several gigabytes, exceeding the budgets of mobile, immersive, and volumetric video platforms. Existing 3DGS compression methods (e.g., HAC++, FlexGaussian, LP-3DGS) treat pruning, quantization, and entropy coding as separate stages and rely on hand-tuned heuristics (opacity thresholds, fixed bit-widths, SH truncation), limiting cross-scene generalization and preventing users from specifying a target rate or quality budget. We propose GETA-3DGS, to our knowledge the first end-to-end automatic joint structured pruning and quantization framework for 3DGS. Building on GETA for joint pruning-quantization of deep networks, we contribute: (i) a 3DGS-aware quantization-aware dependency graph (QADG) treating each Gaussian primitive as a group with five attribute sub-nodes and degree-aware SH sub-nodes; (ii) a render-aware saliency fusing transmittance-weighted contribution, screen-space gradient, and pixel coverage into a Gaussian-level importance score; and (iii) a heterogeneous per-attribute mixed-precision scheme co-optimized with structural sparsity under a projected partial saliency-guided (PPSG) descent guarantee. On Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks and Temples, and Deep Blending, GETA-3DGS operates directly on raw Gaussian primitives rather than a post-hoc anchor representation, delivering ~5x storage reduction over Vanilla 3DGS with no per-scene thresholds. Bit-width policy is the dominant rate-distortion lever: a uniform 6-bit cap costs up to -6.74 dB on view-dependent scenes versus our heterogeneous allocation, matching an information-theoretic reverse-water-filling analysis we develop. GETA-3DGS is complementary to existing codecs: entropy coding (HAC++, CompGS) is downstream, so the two can be composed.

</details>


### [198] [Bridging the Gap Between Average and Discounted TD Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02103)
*Haoxing Tian,Zaiwei Chen,Ioannis Ch. Paschalidis,Alex Olshevsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于平均奖励设定的时序差分学习新算法，通过从两条马尔可夫轨迹中采样，实现了收敛性保证并显著降低了样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均奖励设定下，贝尔曼算子不具备任何范数下的收缩性，导致传统的随机更新分析方法失效，现有理论方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出一种新算法，利用从两条马尔可夫轨迹中的采样进行策略评估，解决贝尔曼算子非收缩性带来的理论难题。

Result: 算法保证收敛到投影贝尔曼方程的唯一解，收敛分析统一适用于线性函数逼近和表格设置，收敛界限不显式依赖维度；样本复杂度从文献中的四次方降低到二次方，与折扣设定下的效率相匹配。

Conclusion: 该算法在平均奖励设定下实现了与折扣设定相当的理论保证和效率，填补了现有文献的理论空白。

Abstract: The analysis of Temporal Difference (TD) learning in the average-reward setting faces notable theoretical difficulties because the Bellman operator is not contractive with respect to any norm. This complicates standard analyses of stochastic updates that are effective in discounted settings. Although a considerable body of literature addresses these challenges, existing theoretical approaches come with limitations. We introduce a novel algorithm designed explicitly for policy evaluation in the average-reward setting, utilizing sampling from two Markovian trajectories. Our proposed method overcomes previous limitations by guaranteeing convergence to the unique solution of a properly defined projected Bellman equation. Notably, and in contrast to earlier work, our convergence analysis is uniformly applicable to both linear function approximation and tabular settings and does not involve explicit dimension-dependent terms in its convergence bounds. These results align with what is known to hold in the discounted setting. Furthermore, our algorithm achieves improved dependence on the problem's condition number, reducing the sample complexity from quartic, as in prior literature, to quadratic scaling, and thus matching the efficiency seen in the discounted setting.

</details>


### [199] [Sharpness-Aware Pretraining Mitigates Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02105)
*Ishaan Watts,Catherine Li,Sachin Goyal,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引导预训练优化走向更平坦的极小值（如使用SAM、大学习率、缩短退火期），能显著提升下游任务表现并减少遗忘，在OLMo-2-1B模型上遗忘减少31%-40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练优化器仅追求最强的基础模型，忽视了模型的几何特性（如损失地形的平坦度），这决定了基础模型在后续参数更新（如后训练、量化）后保留多少能力。作者假设优化至更平坦的极小值可提升模型对后续变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 研究了三种使优化偏向平坦极小值的预训练干预方法：Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM)、大学习率、缩短学习率退火周期。在20M到150M参数规模的模型上评估，并在OLMo-2-1B大规模模型上进行验证。

Result: 在五个常用数据集上，这些干预方法一致地提升了下游任务性能，遗忘减少高达80%。在OLMo-2-1B模型上，中期的短期SAM阶段使MetaMath后训练后的遗忘减少31%，4-bit量化后的遗忘减少40%。

Conclusion: 预训练时应考虑优化轨迹的几何特性，而不仅仅是最终损失值。平坦极小值能提升模型在后训练和量化后的性能保持能力，这一原则在不同规模上均成立。

Abstract: Pretraining optimizers are tuned to produce the strongest possible base model, on the assumption that a stronger starting point yields a stronger model after subsequent changes like post-training and quantization. This overlooks the geometry of the base model which controls how much of the base model's capabilities survive subsequent parameter updates. We study three pretraining optimization approaches that bias optimization toward flatter minima: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), large learning rates, and shortened learning rate annealing periods. Across model sizes ranging from 20M to 150M parameters, we find that these interventions consistently improve downstream performance after post-training on five common datasets with up to 80% less forgetting. These principles hold at scale: a short SAM mid-training phase applied to an existing OLMo-2-1B checkpoint reduces forgetting by 31% after MetaMath post-training and by 40% after 4-bit quantization.

</details>


### [200] [Geometric and Spectral Alignment for Deep Neural Network I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02108)
*Ziran Liu,Wei Wang,Jinhao Wang,Pengcheng Wang,Xinyi Sui,Cihan Ruan,Nam Ling,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了深度残差架构中Frobenius归一化层因子的奇异谱几何估计，提出了基于Cartan坐标的刚性定理，证明精确幂律谱构成轨迹归一化Cartan轨道，并给出了幂律拟合误差的定量界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度残差网络可建模为近单位雅可比矩阵的乘积，需要理解其层因子的奇异谱性质。现有理论缺乏对归一化层因子谱行为的几何刻画和定量估计，特别是在幂律谱分布和Cartan坐标框架下的刚性分析。

Method: 将满秩因子从GL(d)映射到正锥（A→A^T A），再映射到有序特征值数据；在Frobenius归一化下建立精确幂律谱的Cartan轨道结构；证明松弛感知的边际不等式作为主刚性定理；分离标量顶径向与全Cartan谱控制；发展近似幂律和度量图表版本、反向下界、Fisher-KL/Bures作用估计以及近单位展开。

Result: 精确幂律谱构成轨迹归一化Cartan轨道，该轨道是秩上的Gibbs族、Fisher信息线和具有线元d/4倍Fisher信息的Bures-Wasserstein曲线；在精确图表零松弛情况下，深度L预算给出指数漂移为(log M)/L阶；松弛和残差增量会增大该界；建立了有效秩作为谱能量分位数的理论，给出了有限宽度幂律尾界和稳健的秩窗转移估计。

Conclusion: 本文完成了静态权重指数谱分析的刚性理论基础构建，为深度残差网络的结构理解提供了几何和定量化工具；实证静态权重指数谱可作为诊断工具，完整验证还需要相同算子链的界面预算、松弛和残差分析。

Abstract: Deep residual architectures are modeled as products of near-identity Jacobians. This paper proves deterministic quotient-geometric estimates for singular spectra of Frobenius-normalized layer factors, emphasizing a normalized top-radial Cartan coordinate and fitted power-law chart.
  Full-rank factors are mapped from $\mathrm{GL}(d)$ to the positive cone by $A\mapsto A^\top A$, then to ordered eigenvalue data. Under Frobenius normalization, exact power-law spectra form a trace-normalized Cartan orbit. This orbit is a Gibbs family on ranks, a Fisher information line, and a Bures--Wasserstein curve with line element $d/4$ times Fisher information.
  The main rigidity theorem is a slack-aware margin inequality: interface radial amplitude, non-backtracking slack, and signed residual variation control displacement of the fitted Cartan coordinate. In the exact-chart zero-slack case, a depth-$L$ budget gives exponent drift of order $(\log M)/L$; generally, slack and residual increments augment the bound.
  We separate scalar top-radial from full-Cartan spectral control, which also needs Bures/Hellinger residual variation. We prove approximate-power-law and metric-chart versions, converse lower bounds, Fisher--KL/Bures action estimates, and near-identity expansions for normalized residual chains.
  Near-identity results verify transport budgets; chart quality remains measurable. Effective rank is a spectral-energy quantile, giving finite-width power-law tail bounds and robust rank-window transition estimates. Empirical static-weight exponent profiles serve as diagnostics; full verification also requires interface budgets, slacks, and residuals for the same operator chain.

</details>


### [201] [Detecting Adversarial Data via Provable Adversarial Noise Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02109)
*Furkan Mumcu,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了深度神经网络中对抗噪声逐层放大的现象，并据此提出了一种新的训练方法和轻量级检测机制，能有效识别对抗样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究观察到对抗噪声在网络各层间的非均匀增长现象并用于检测对抗样本，但缺乏严格的数学理论支撑。本文旨在为该现象提供形式化的理论证明，并基于理论设计更优的防御方法。

Method: 首先提出对抗噪声放大定理，形式化证明在特定充分条件下对抗噪声会被逐层放大；其次提出包含频谱损失函数和特定架构设计的训练方法，以增强该放大信号；最后设计一种纯推理时的轻量级检测机制，利用增强后的信号识别对抗数据。

Result: 所提出的检测方法在面对当前最先进的攻击以及专门构建的自适应攻击时均表现出有效性，验证了增强后的噪声放大信号可以作为对抗防御中稳健可靠的依据。

Conclusion: 本文填补了对抗噪声放大现象的理论空白，并证明了基于该理论的训练和检测方法能有效提升深度神经网络的对抗鲁棒性，为对抗防御提供了新的理论和实践支撑。

Abstract: The nonuniform and growing impact of adversarial noise across the layers of deep neural networks has been used in the literature, without a formal mathematical justification, to detect adversarial inputs and improve robustness. In this work, we study this phenomenon in detail and present a formal adversarial noise amplification theorem. We specify a set of sufficient conditions under which the adversarial noise amplification is mathematically guaranteed. Based on theoretical observations, we propose a novel training methodology with a custom spectral loss function and a specific architectural design to enhance the amplification signal for detecting adversarial data. Finally, we introduce a new, lightweight detection mechanism that leverages the enhanced amplification signal and operates entirely at inference time. To validate our approach, we demonstrate the detector's efficacy against both state-of-the-art attacks and a purpose-built adaptive attack, confirming that enhanced amplification can serve as a robust and reliable signal for adversarial defense.

</details>


### [202] [Adversarial Update-Based Federated Unlearning for Poisoned Model Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02110)
*Wenwei Zhao,Xiaowen Li,Yao Liu,Zhuo Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAUN框架，通过保留少量恶意更新并使用对抗优化生成反向更新，实现高效消除联邦学习中的恶意影响，效果媲美重新训练但开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习容易受到投毒攻击，现有的检测方法虽能移除恶意客户端，但模型已受影响。从头重新训练成本过高，而现有的反学习方法在有效性和效率上均不理想，因此亟需一种轻量级且高效的恶意影响消除方案。

Method: 提出联邦对抗反学习框架（FAUN）。该方法仅保留恶意客户端更新的短时间窗口，利用代理数据集进行对抗优化，推导出能够消除恶意方向的更新向量。通过少量反学习轮次应用这些更新，随后进行良性微调，从而快速移除恶意影响并恢复模型性能。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，FAUN能达到与从头重新训练相当的恢复效果，同时所需轮次极少。该方法将攻击成功率降至接近零，成功消除了被反学习客户端的贡献，验证了其有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: FAUN为联邦学习中的恶意客户端移除问题提供了一种轻量级且高效的解决方案，在保证恢复性能的同时显著降低了计算开销，成功消除了恶意客户端的负面影响。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to poisoning attacks, where malicious clients upload manipulated updates to degrade the performance of the global model. Although detection methods can identify and remove malicious clients, the model remains affected. Retraining from scratch is effective but costly, and existing unlearning methods remain unsatisfactory in both effectiveness and efficiency. We propose Federated Adversarial Unlearning (FAUN), a lightweight framework that retains only a short window of malicious clients' updates and employs adversarial optimization on a proxy dataset to derive updates that eliminate malicious directions. Applying these updates for a few unlearning rounds, followed by benign fine-tuning, enables fast removal of malicious effects and stable recovery. Experiments on three canonical datasets show that FAUN achieves recovery comparable to retraining while requiring far fewer rounds and reduces attack success rates to near zero, confirming FAUN successfully eliminates the contributions of unlearned clients.

</details>


### [203] [Geometric and Spectral Alignment for Deep Neural Network II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02111)
*Ziran Liu,Wei Wang,Jinhao Wang,Pengcheng Wang,Xinyi Sui,Cihan Ruan,Nam Ling,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了残差雅可比链的几何与谱对齐（GSA）理论框架，提出了静态认证方法来验证神经网络层间传输的主奇异子空间近似，并通过实验在CNN、语言模型和视觉/扩散骨干网络中验证了认证矩阵的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究神经网络中残差雅可比链的层间传输特性，需要理解主奇异子空间如何在相邻层之间传输。现有方法缺乏对截断传输误差的严格界限和物理通道坐标下的确定性认证，因此需要建立几何与谱对齐的理论框架来提供可验证的边界保证。

Method: 基于Cartan坐标刚性和拟合有效秩窗口，分析相邻层间主奇异子空间的传输特性；构建物理对齐矩阵的正交分解（核心+重叠+噪声）；推导全接口传输与主导窗口截断之间的误差界限；通过活动列间隙、成对重叠边界和噪声界限组合生成静态认证半径；针对不变通道映射的精细标签设计行能量和轮廓相关性边界测试。

Result: 获得了确定性的边界验证结果：对全接口传输与其主导窗口截断之间的误差进行了界限约束；添加拟合尾部误差以使经验谱能够针对Gibbs-Cartan尾部模型进行认证；区分了源模态入射与完全物理输入输出通道入射；在静态认证半径下，全传输与截断传输诱导相同的活动支撑、成对入射图、SRS集合、枢纽列和核心/重叠/噪声掩码。

Conclusion: 本文提出的物理GSA认证框架为神经网络层间传输特性提供了可验证的确定性保证，静态认证协议能够确保截断传输与全传输在关键结构性质上的一致性，为理解深度网络的几何与谱性质提供了新的分析工具和实验验证方法。

Abstract: This paper develops the angular and static-channel component of Geometric and Spectral Alignment for residual Jacobian chains. Starting from Cartan-coordinate rigidity and fitted effective-rank windows, we study how dominant singular subspaces are transported across adjacent layers and how the resulting finite matrices can be displayed in physical channel coordinates.
  The main results are deterministic, margin-verified results. We bound the error between full interface transport and its dominant-window truncation, add fitted-tail errors so that empirical spectra can be certified against the Gibbs--Cartan tail model, and distinguish source-mode incidence from fully physical input-output channel incidence. Given row groups and active supports, the Physical Alignment Matrix decomposes orthogonally as core plus overlap plus noise. Active-column gaps, pairwise overlap margins, and noise bounds combine into a static certificate radius under which the full transport and the truncated transport induce the same active supports, pairwise incidence graph, SRS sets, hub columns, and core/overlap/noise masks. The finer SC/SA/ST labels of the Invariant Channel Mapping require additional row-energy and profile-correlation margins, stated as explicit perturbation tests.
  The empirical section reports the matrices and block-energy heatmaps that measure these certificate quantities across CNNs, language models, and vision/diffusion backbones. The figures are interpreted as finite-dimensional measurements; complete membership in the Physical GSA certificate domain requires checking the numerical margin protocol stated in Section 10.

</details>


### [204] [Statistical Consistency and Generalization of Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02116)
*Yuanfan Li,Xiyuan Wei,Tianbao Yang,Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了对比表征学习（CRL）的统一统计学习理论，证明了对比损失在最优排序上的统计一致性，导出了能够解释负样本数量优势的泛化界，并通过实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比表征学习理论分析存在三大局限：（i）CRL的统计一致性尚不明确；（ii）现有泛化界随负样本数量增加而恶化，这与大规模负样本集的实证优势相矛盾；（iii）CRL的检索性能缺乏理论关注。这些不足促使作者建立一个统一的CRL统计学习理论框架。

Method: 提出统一统计学习理论框架：（1）针对下游任务，使用AUC型总体准则评估检索质量，证明对比损失相对于最优排序的统计一致性，并建立标定不等式以定量关联过量对比风险与检索次优性；（2）针对上游训练，分别研究监督和自监督对比目标，通过理论分析推导泛化界。

Result: 获得两大理论成果：（1）监督对比目标的泛化界为$O(1/m + 1/\sqrt{n})$；（2）自监督对比目标的泛化界为$O(1/\sqrt{m} + 1/\sqrt{n})$，其中$m$为负样本数，$n$为锚点数。这些界不仅解释了大规模负样本集的实证优势，还揭示了$m$与$n$之间的显式权衡关系。在大规模视觉-语言模型上的广泛实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了对比表征学习的统一统计学习理论，解决了统计一致性、泛化界与负样本数量关系的理论难题，为CRL的实证成功提供了坚实的理论基础，并为负样本规模与锚点数量之间的权衡提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Contrastive representation learning (CRL) underpins many modern foundation models. Despite recent theoretical progress, existing analyses suffer from several key limitations: (i) the statistical consistency of CRL remains poorly understood; (ii) available generalization bounds deteriorate as the number of negative samples increases, contradicting the empirical benefits of large negative sets; and (iii) the retrieval performance of CRL has received limited theoretical attention.
  In this paper, we develop a unified statistical learning theory for CRL. For downstream tasks, we evaluate retrieval quality using an AUC-type population criterion and show that the contrastive loss is \emph{statistically consistent} with optimal ranking. We further establish a \emph{calibration-style inequality} that quantitatively relates excess contrastive risk to excess retrieval suboptimality. For upstream training, we study both supervised and self-supervised contrastive objectives and derive generalization bounds of order $O(1/m + 1/\sqrt{n})$ and $O(1/\sqrt{m} + 1/\sqrt{n})$, respectively, where $m$ denotes the number of negative samples and $n$ the number of anchor points. These bounds not only explain the empirical advantages of large negative sets but also reveal an explicit trade-off between $m$ and $n$. Extensive experiments on large-scale vision--language models corroborate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [205] [STABLEVAL: Disagreement-Aware and Stable Evaluation of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02122)
*Akash Bonagiri,Gerard Janno Anderias,Saee Patil,Angelina Lai,Devang Borkar,Gezheng Kang,Ishant Gandhi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STABLEVAL框架，通过建模标注者分歧来生成更稳定、可靠的AI系统排名，解决了多数投票法在人工评估中存在的排名不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估是评估现代AI系统的主要标准，但标注者之间存在分歧、偏差和变异性。标准的多数投票聚合方法忽略了标注者的可靠性和项目层面的歧义性，导致系统排名在不同标注者子集下呈现脆弱性和不稳定性。

Method: 提出STABLEVAL框架，这是一种感知分歧的评估方法。该框架建模潜在的项目正确性和标注者特定的混淆模式，从而产生后验期望的项目信用和校准后的智能体分数。与Dawid-Skene等标签去噪方法不同，STABLEVAL专门针对稳定且感知不确定性的系统评估设计，而非硬标签恢复，并将排名稳定性形式化为一类评估目标。

Result: 在受控合成实验和多个真实世界人工标注基准测试中，多数投票法在标注者异质性和对抗性噪声下表现出评分误差增加和排名不稳定性加剧；而STABLEVAL产生了更稳定且具有统计基础的系统排名。

Conclusion: 建模分歧对于鲁棒且可复现的AI评估至关重要，该方法为解决人工评估中的不确定性问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Human evaluation remains the primary standard for assessing modern AI systems, yet annotator disagreement, bias, and variability make system rankings fragile under standard majority vote aggregation. Majority vote discards annotator reliability and item-level ambiguity, often yielding unstable comparisons across annotator subsets. We introduce STABLEVAL, a disagreement-aware evaluation framework that models latent item correctness and annotator-specific confusion patterns to produce posterior expected item credit and calibrated agent-level scores. Unlike label-denoising approaches such as Dawid-Skene, STABLEVAL is explicitly designed for stable and uncertainty-aware system evaluation rather than hard label recovery. We formalize ranking stability as a first-class evaluation objective and analyze how aggregation methods preserve or distort underlying annotator behavior. Across controlled synthetic experiments and multiple real-world human-annotated benchmarks, majority vote exhibits increasing score error and ranking instability under annotator heterogeneity and adversarial noise, while STABLEVAL yields more stable and statistically grounded system rankings. These results demonstrate that modeling disagreement is essential for robust and reproducible AI evaluation.

</details>


### [206] [Boundary Mass and the Soft-to-Hard Limit in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02124)
*Reza Rastegar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究了Softmax路由MoE模型在温度趋近于零时的奇异性问题，证明了硬路由极限由路由界面附近的薄几何层控制，并建立了软路由到硬路由的定量风险界、Γ-收敛性以及景观传递原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax路由的混合专家模型在温度趋近于零时逼近硬路由，但该极限在路由得分相近（路由平局）处存在奇异性。理解这一奇异性对于建立软路由到硬路由的理论桥梁、分析优化景观以及理解模型行为至关重要。

Method: 核心方法是分析“边界质量”（边界概率）——即前两个路由得分仅以微小差距分离的概率。在路由和输入分布的光滑性与横截性假设下，证明了coarea/管道估计，表明该边界质量与板宽度呈线性关系，主导常数由路由界面上的曲面积分给出。基于此几何核心，推导了软到硬的风险界、Γ-收敛性，并在教师-学生设置中分析了景观传递原理。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 边界质量的线性估计公式；(2) 从软路由到硬路由的定量风险界；(3) 在紧性和一致边际控制下，软目标泛函Γ-收敛到硬路由目标泛函；(4) 在教师-学生设置下，证明了条件性景观传递原理：当硬路由问题具有良好可识别性和曲率时，小温度软路由继承近似的教师恢复和严格鞍点行为；(5) 两专家高斯案例展示了与教师分隔符对齐的局部对称性破缺机制。

Conclusion: 零温度极限由路由界面周围的薄几何层控制，而非整个输入空间。这一几何洞见为理解软硬路由的关系提供了统一框架，并在特定条件下保证了优化景观的有利性质从硬路由传递到软路由。

Abstract: Softmax-routed mixture-of-experts models approach hard routing as the temperature tends to zero, but this limit is singular near routing ties. This paper studies that singularity at the population level for squared-loss MoE regression. The central object is the \emph{boundary mass}, namely the probability that the top two router scores are separated by only a small margin. Under smoothness and transversality assumptions on the router and input law, we prove coarea/tube estimates showing that this mass is linear in the slab width, with leading constant given by a surface integral over the routing interface in the binary case. These estimates yield quantitative soft-to-hard risk bounds and, under compactness and uniform margin control, $Γ$-convergence of the soft objectives to the hard-routing objective. The main conclusion is that the zero-temperature limit is controlled by a thin geometric layer around routing interfaces, not by the full input space. We then use this geometric core in two more model-dependent directions. In a teacher--student setting, we prove a conditional landscape-transfer principle showing that, when the profiled hard-routing problem has favorable identifiability and curvature and the relevant derivatives transfer at boundary-layer scale, small-temperature soft routing inherits approximate teacher recovery and strict-saddle behavior away from teacher-equivalent partitions. We also give a reduced two-expert Gaussian calculation that illustrates a local symmetry-breaking mechanism aligned with the teacher separator.

</details>


### [207] [LUMINA: A Grid Foundation Model for Benchmarking AC Optimal Power Flow Surrogate Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02133)
*Hongwei Jin,Keunju Song,Zeeshan Memon,Yijiang Li,Stefano Fenu,Hongseok Kim,Liang Zhao,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LUMINA-Bench，一个用于ACOPF代理模型学习的综合基准测试套件，解决了现有模型在网络拓扑泛化方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交流最优潮流（ACOPF）在电网运行中计算成本高昂，现有的学习型代理模型在不同网络拓扑间的泛化能力不足，无法有效应用于训练中未见过的电网和日常运行假设研究。

Method: 提出了LUMINA-Bench基准测试套件，涵盖多拓扑预训练、迁移和适应；在单拓扑和多拓扑学习设置下评估同构和异构架构；使用统一指标评估预测精度和物理约束违规；比较了MSE、增广拉格朗日和基于违规的拉格朗日损失等约束感知训练目标。

Result: 开发了开源的LUMINA套件，包含数据处理、训练和评估框架，能够表征不同设置下的精度-鲁棒性权衡，支持可行性感知OPF代理模型的可重复性研究。

Conclusion: LUMINA-Bench为ACOPF代理模型学习提供了全面的评估基准，通过开源套件促进了该领域的可重复性和未来研究发展。

Abstract: AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) is foundational yet computationally expensive in power grid operations, driving learning-based surrogates for large-scale grid analysis. These surrogates, however, often fail to generalize across network topologies, a critical gap for deployment on grids not seen during training and for routine operational what-if studies. We introduce LUMINA-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark suite for ACOPF surrogate learning covering multi-topology pretraining, transfer, and adaptation. The benchmark evaluates homogeneous and heterogeneous architectures under single- and multi-topology learning settings using unified metrics that capture both predictive accuracy and physics-informed constraint violations. We additionally compare constraint-aware training objectives, including MSE, augmented Lagrangian, and violation-based Lagrangian losses, to characterize accuracy-robustness trade-offs across settings. Data processing, training, and evaluation frameworks are open-sourced as the LUMINA suite to support reproducibility and accelerate future research on feasibility-aware OPF surrogates.

</details>


### [208] [On the Optimal Sample Complexity of Offline Multi-Armed Bandits with KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02141)
*Kaixuan Ji,Qiwei Di,Heyang Zhao,Qingyue Zhao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对KL正则化离线多臂老虎机（MAB）学习问题提供了几乎完整的样本复杂度刻画，证明KL-PCB算法在大正则化下达到$\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$的样本复杂度，在小正则化下为$\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$，并给出了匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化在离线决策中被广泛使用并具有多种优势，这激发了近期关于KL正则化性能度量下离线学习样本复杂度的研究。然而，KL正则化离线学习的精确样本复杂度仍未被完全刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在多臂老虎机（MAB）设定下，对KL-PCB算法进行精确分析，区分大正则化（$η= \tilde{O}(ε^{-1})$）和小正则化（$η= \tildeΩ(ε^{-1})$）两种情况，分别推导样本复杂度上界；同时提供更精确的样本复杂度下界证明。

Result: 证明了KL-PCB算法在大正则化下样本复杂度为$\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$，在小正则化下为$\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$；提供的下界在整个正则化强度范围内与上界匹配，实现了上下界的紧密贴合。

Conclusion: 本文结果为KL正则化离线多臂老虎机学习提供了几乎完整的样本复杂度刻画，上下界的匹配表明所得结果在理论上已达到最优或近乎最优，为离线强化学习理论提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization is widely used in offline decision-making and offers several benefits, motivating recent work on the sample complexity of offline learning with respect to KL-regularized performance metrics. Nevertheless, the exact sample complexity of KL-regularized offline learning remains largely from fully characterized. In this paper, we study this question in the setting of multi-armed bandits (MABs). We provide a sharp analysis of KL-PCB (Zhao et al., 2026), showing that it achieves a sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$ under large regularization $η= \tilde{O}(ε^{-1})$, and a sample complexity of $\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$ under small regularization $η= \tildeΩ(ε^{-1})$, where $η$ is the regularization parameter, $S$ is the number of contexts, $A$ is the number of arms, $C^{π^*}$ policy coverage coefficient at the optimal policy $π^*$, $ε$ is the desired sub-optimality, and $\tilde{O}$ and $\tildeΩ$ hide all poly-logarithmic factors. We further provide a pair of sharper sample complexity lower bounds, which matches the upper bounds over the entire range of regularization strengths. Overall, our results provide a nearly complete characterization of offline multi-armed bandits with KL regularization.

</details>


### [209] [Personalized Federated Learning for Gradient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02143)
*Dongwon Kim,Gyuejeong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出pFLAlign框架，通过在本地训练和聚合阶段对齐梯度方向，解决了个性化联邦学习中因数据受限和异构导致的梯度高方差及聚合失真问题，实现了最先进的个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化联邦学习方法难以可靠地保留个性化信息。其核心挑战在于：客户端数据有限且异构，导致本地训练产生高方差梯度；而全局聚合过程会进一步扭曲客户端特定的优化方向，损害个性化效果。

Method: 提出pFLAlign框架，包含两个互补机制：1）自适应本地梯度方向以减少客户端优化的方差；2）通过重新对齐全局模型与每个客户端的个性化方向，缓解聚合引起的失真。该方法基于PAC-Bayesian分析推导得出，从理论上揭示了个性化梯度对齐如何保留客户端特定信息。

Result: 实验和消融研究表明，pFLAlign持续提升了个性化性能和训练稳定性，取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

Conclusion: pFLAlign通过在本地训练和聚合两个关键阶段引入梯度对齐机制，有效解决了个性化信息丢失问题，为个性化联邦学习提供了一个理论上严谨且实践中高效的方法。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (pFL) aims to adapt models to client specific data distributions, yet it often fails to reliably preserve personalized information. Local training is hindered by high variance gradients induced by limited and heterogeneous client data, while aggregation further distorts client specific optimization directions. To address these challenges, we propose pFLAlign, a gradient alignment framework to maintain client specific information during both local training and aggregation. pFLAlign consists of two complementary mechanisms: one adapts local gradient directions to reduce variance during client side optimization, and the other mitigates aggregation induced distortion by realigning the global model with each client's personalized direction. Theoretically, we derive pFLAlign from a PAC Bayesian analysis, which reveals how personalized gradient alignment preserves client specific information. Our experiments and ablation studies show that pFLAlign consistently improves personalization performance and training stability, achieving state of the art results.

</details>


### [210] [Projection-Free Transformers via Gaussian Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02144)
*Debarshi Kundu,Archisman Ghosh,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了高斯核注意力(GKA)，用基于高斯RBF核的相似度扩散算子替代Transformer中传统的学习投影注意力机制，仅需学习带宽参数，在大幅减少参数量和计算量的情况下实现了稳定训练和竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer自注意力机制依赖学习的线性投影(Q、K、V)，作者探究这些学习投影是否必要，是否可以用更简单的基于相似度的扩散算子替代，从而简化注意力机制并提升可解释性。

Method: 提出高斯核注意力(GKA)作为点积注意力的替代方案：直接对每头标记特征应用高斯径向基函数(RBF)核计算标记亲和度，每个头仅学习带宽参数σ_h，保留单个输出投影W_O以维持标准Transformer接口。GKA可解释为标记上的归一化核回归，将现代Transformer架构与经典非局部滤波和核平滑方法关联。在自回归语言建模中通过掩码和重归一化高斯核实现因果掩码和滑动窗口约束。

Result: 在视觉和语言建模设置中评估GKA。在nanogpt框架下的自回归语言建模中，深度为20层的GKA模型仅使用标准注意力基线0.42×的参数量和0.49×的总训练FLOPs，训练稳定，训练-验证差距接近零，在标准基准测试上展现出竞争性行为，但在该计算规模下BPB略高。

Conclusion: GKA提供了一种最小化、可解释的注意力机制，具有明确的局部性尺度，为Transformer设计提供了准确性-效率权衡的新维度，证明了学习投影并非注意力机制的必要组件。

Abstract: Self-attention in Transformers is typically implemented as $\mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt{d})V$, where $Q=XW_Q$, $K=XW_K$, and $V=XW_V$ are learned linear projections of the input $X$. We ask whether these learned projections are necessary, or whether they can be replaced by a simpler similarity-based diffusion operator. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Kernel Attention} (GKA), a drop-in replacement for dot-product attention that computes token affinities directly using a Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel applied to per-head token features. Each head learns only a bandwidth parameter $σ_h$, while a single output projection $W_O$ preserves compatibility with the standard Transformer interface. GKA can be interpreted as normalized kernel regression over tokens, linking modern Transformer architectures to classical non-local filtering and kernel smoothing methods. We evaluate GKA in both vision and language modeling settings. For autoregressive language modeling within the \texttt{nanochat} framework, we implement causal masking and sliding-window constraints by masking and renormalizing the Gaussian kernel. At depth 20, a GKA model with $0.42\times$ the parameters and $0.49\times$ the total training FLOPs of a standard attention baseline trains stably, exhibits a near-zero train-validation gap, and demonstrates competitive behavior on standard benchmarks, albeit with higher bits-per-byte (BPB) at this compute scale. Overall, GKA provides a minimal, interpretable attention mechanism with an explicit locality scale, offering a dimension in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off for Transformer design.

</details>


### [211] [Combining Trained Models in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02159)
*Ujjwal Patil,Javad Ghofrani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过PRISMA系统性综述分析了深度强化学习中预训练知识重用的实证研究，发现正向结果主要集中在源任务与目标任务具有相似结构或包含显式门控/对齐机制的场景，集成与联邦方法证据有限，且公平计算对比稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽然取得显著成果，但仍面临高采样成本和弱迁移能力的问题。现有文献通过迁移学习、蒸馏、集成或联邦训练等方式重用预训练知识，但相关研究分散且难以比较，缺乏统一的评估标准。

Method: 采用PRISMA指南进行系统性文献综述，从IEEE Xplore、ACM数字图书馆和引用追踪中检索589条记录，经过筛选和资格评估最终纳入15项实证研究，从源-目标相似性、重用模型多样性、与从头训练基线对比公平性三个维度进行定性分析。

Result: 研究发现三个主要模式：(1)正向结果集中在源任务与目标任务共享大量结构或方法包含显式门控/对齐机制的场景；(2)集成和联邦聚合方法证据有限且多局限于狭窄场景；(3)计算匹配的公平对比稀缺，削弱了相对于更强单智能体基线的效率声明。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了更聚焦且内部一致的综述范围、研究层面的实证证据综合，以及初步的独立性谱系框架，该框架应作为未来基准测试的假设而非已验证的度量标准。研究强调需要更严格的计算匹配对比和标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has delivered strong results in domains such as Atari and Go, but it still suffers from high sample cost and weak transfer beyond the training setting. A common response is to reuse information from previously trained models through transfer, distillation, ensemble methods, or federated training instead of learning each target task from random initialization. The literature on these mechanisms is fragmented, and published comparisons are hard to interpret because tasks, baselines, and compute budgets differ.
  This paper presents a PRISMA-guided systematic review of empirical studies on pretrained knowledge reuse in DRL. Starting from 589 records retrieved from IEEE Xplore, the ACM Digital Library, and citation tracing, we screened 570 unique records and assessed 89 full texts. After applying the final eligibility criteria, 15 empirical studies remained in the main synthesis. We analyzed them qualitatively across three factors: source-target similarity, diversity among reused models, and the fairness of comparisons against from-scratch baselines.
  Three patterns recur across the surviving corpus. First, positive results are concentrated in settings where source and target tasks share substantial structure or where the method includes an explicit gating or alignment mechanism. Second, evidence for ensembles and federated aggregation is promising but sparse and mostly limited to narrow settings. Third, compute-matched comparisons are rare, which weakens claims about efficiency gains over stronger single-agent baselines. The paper contributes a narrower and internally consistent review scope, a study-level synthesis of empirical evidence, and a provisional independence spectrum that should be treated as a hypothesis for future benchmarking rather than a validated metric.

</details>


### [212] [Experience Constrained Hierarchical Federated Reinforcement Learning for Large-scale UAV Teams in Hazardous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02165)
*Qinwei Huang,Rui Zuo,Simon Khan,Qinru Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在经验受限的联邦强化学习场景下，增加学习者参与度并不一定提升学习性能；核心影响因素是经验重用策略和梯度过渡经验的主导地位，而非联邦聚合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习假设更高的学习者参与度能通过利用丰富的独立本地数据提升训练性能。然而，在危险环境中执行任务的无人机团队联邦强化学习（FRL）场景下，经验生成受到安全考量、能源限制和任务时长的严重制约，导致传统假设可能失效，需要重新审视参与度与性能的关系。

Method: 提出经验约束分层联邦强化学习（EC-HFRL）框架。该框架将集群作为联邦学习代理，集群内的多个学习者作为并行学习资源，共同复用共享经验池。研究分析了小批量大小对有效回放暴露的影响，以及集群内参与度对经验复用水平的调节作用。

Result: 研究表明在经验受限条件下增加参与度并不必然改善学习性能。学习性能与经验重用策略及集群内关键梯度过渡经验的主导地位密切相关。小批量大小主要决定有效回放暴露，而更高的集群内参与度增加复用水平。性能模式主要与学习信号结构相关，而非联邦聚合效应。

Conclusion: 在经验受限的联邦强化学习中，学习者参与度仅起到有限且次要的作用；学习性能主要由经验重用策略和学习信号结构决定，这挑战了传统联邦学习中参与度越高越好的普遍认知。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning assumes that greater learner participation improves training performance, by leveraging abundant, independently generated local data. However, in federated reinforcement learning (FRL) for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) teams in hazardous environments where experience generation is severely constrained by safety considerations, energy limitations, and mission duration, this assumption may break. This work introduces Experience-Constrained Hierarchical Federated Reinforcement Learning (EC-HFRL), a framework in which clusters act as federated learning agents, while multiple intra-cluster learners represent parallel learning resources that reuse a shared experience pool. We show that increasing participation does not necessarily improve learning performance. Instead, learning performance is strongly associated with experience reuse strategy and the dominance of key analytically identified gradient transition experiences within a cluster. In particular, minibatch size primarily determines effective replay exposure, while higher intra-cluster participation increases reuse level. Empirical results demonstrate that the performance regimes are strongly associated with the structure of the learning signal, rather than federated aggregation effects, clarifying the limited and secondary role of learner participation in experience-constrained FRL.

</details>


### [213] [Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients for Reliable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02167)
*Soyeon Kim,Seongwoo Lim,Kyowoon Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MA-GIG方法，通过在变分自编码器的潜空间中构建归因路径，解决了传统IG方法在积分路径经过噪声梯度区域时解释不可靠的问题，显著提升了特征归因的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中的特征归因对于模型诊断和信任至关重要，而积分梯度(IG)因其公理化性质被广泛使用。然而，当积分路径经过噪声梯度区域时，IG会产生不可靠的解释。虽然引导积分梯度(GIG)通过自适应更新低梯度幅度特征来降低敏感性，但输入空间引导仍会产生偏离数据流形的中间输入，这是一个关键限制。

Method: 提出流形对齐引导积分梯度(MA-GIG)方法，在预训练变分自编码器的潜空间中构建归因路径。通过解码中间潜状态，MA-GIG将路径偏向于学习的生成流形，减少暴露于不合理的输入空间区域。该方法聚合接近输入的路径特征上的梯度来生成解释。

Result: 通过定性和定量评估，MA-GIG通过聚合接近输入的路径特征上的梯度，能够产生忠实的解释。该方法有效减少了离流形噪声，在多个数据集和分类器上优于先前的基于路径的归因方法。

Conclusion: MA-GIG成功解决了传统积分梯度方法中积分路径偏离数据流形的问题，通过在潜空间构建归因路径，显著提升了特征归因的可靠性和准确性，为深度神经网络的可解释性研究提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Feature attribution is central to diagnosing and trusting deep neural networks, and Integrated Gradients (IG) is widely used due to its axiomatic properties. However, IG can yield unreliable explanations when the integration path between a baseline and the input passes through regions with noisy gradients. While Guided Integrated Gradients reduces this sensitivity by adaptively updating low-gradient-magnitude features, input-space guidance still produces intermediate inputs that deviate from the data manifold. To address this limitation, we propose \emph{Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients} (MA-GIG), which constructs attribution paths in the latent space of a pre-trained variational autoencoder. By decoding intermediate latent states, MA-GIG biases the path toward the learned generative manifold and reduces exposure to implausible input-space regions. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations, we demonstrate that MA-GIG produces faithful explanations by aggregating gradients on path features proximal to the input. Consequently, our method reduces off-manifold noise and outperforms prior path-based attribution methods across multiple datasets and classifiers. Our code is available at https://github.com/leekwoon/ma-gig/.

</details>


### [214] [Manifold-Constrained Adversarial Training for Long-Tailed Robustness via Geometric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02183)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流形约束对抗训练框架（MCAT），通过约束对抗样本在类条件流形上并促进类别间几何分离，有效提升了长尾分布数据上的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练在平衡数据集上表现有效，但在长尾分布下鲁棒性显著下降，尾部类别存在高鲁棒误差和不稳定决策边界的问题。

Method: 提出流形约束对抗训练（MCAT）统一框架：（1）通过惩罚特征空间中偏离类条件流形的偏差来强制对抗样本的语义有效性；（2）通过ETF启发的正则化促进类别间平衡的几何分离。

Result: 提供了几何分离与对抗鲁棒边界下界相关的理论结果，证明流形约束对抗风险上界界定高密度语义区域的鲁棒风险；在标准长尾基准测试上实验证明在整体、平衡和尾部类别对抗鲁棒性方面均有持续改进。

Conclusion: MCAT框架成功解决了长尾分布下对抗训练鲁棒性退化问题，通过流形约束和几何分离机制实现了更优的鲁棒性能，为不平衡数据下的对抗训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Adversarial training is effective on balanced datasets, but its robustness degrades under longtailed class distributions, where tail classes suffer high robust error and unstable decision boundaries. We propose Manifold-Constrained Adversarial Training (MCAT), a unified framework that enforces the semantic validity of adversarial examples by penalizing deviations from class-conditional manifolds in feature space, while promoting balanced geometric separation across classes via an ETF-inspired regularization. We provide theoretical results that link geometric separation to lower bounds on adversarially robust margins, and show that manifold-constrained adversarial risk upperbounds robust risk on high-density semantic regions. Extensive experiments on standard longtailed benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in overall, balanced, and tail-class adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [215] [KANs need curvature: penalties for compositional smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02190)
*James Bagrow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KANs）中激活函数存在病理高曲率振荡、导致可解释性降低的问题，提出了一种与基无关的曲率惩罚项，在保持模型准确率的同时显著提升了激活函数的平滑度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KANs通过可学习的单变量激活函数组合，在准确性和可解释性方面具有优势。然而，拟合良好的KANs激活函数往往表现出病理性的高曲率振荡，导致难以解释，且现有的标准正则化惩罚项无法有效防止这一现象。科学机器学习正日益受限于准确性与可解释性之间的权衡。

Method: 研究人员推导了一种与基无关（basis-agnostic）的曲率惩罚项，并考虑了函数组合如何影响曲率。在此基础上，证明了相对于曲率惩罚项的模型整体曲率上限，并以此提出了更丰富的惩罚形式。

Result: 施加该惩罚项后的模型在保持准确性的同时，实现了显著更平滑的激活函数。理论分析证明了模型曲率相对于惩罚项的上界关系。

Conclusion: 本研究在不牺牲准确性的前提下改善了KANs的可解释性，打破了科学机器学习中准确性与可解释性之间的瓶颈，进一步巩固了KANs作为预测和洞察实用工具的地位。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs) offer a potent combination of accuracy and interpretability, thanks to their compositions of learnable univariate activation functions. However, the activations of well-fitting KANs tend to exhibit pathologically high-curvature oscillations, making them difficult to interpret, and standard regularization penalties do not prevent this. Here we derive a basis-agnostic curvature penalty and show that penalized models can maintain accuracy while achieving substantially smoother activations. Accounting for how function composition shapes curvature, we prove an upper bound on the full model's curvature relative to the curvature penalty, and use this to motivate richer forms of penalties. Scientific machine learning is increasingly bottlenecked by the trade-off between accuracy and interpretability. Results such as ours that improve interpretability without sacrificing accuracy will further strengthen KANs as a practical tool for both prediction and insight.

</details>


### [216] [DurableUn: Quantization-Induced Recovery Attacks in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02196)
*Abdullah Ahmad Khan,Ferdous Sohel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INT4量化可导致已被“遗忘”的LLM内容恢复（量化恢复攻击），现有方法难以同时兼顾遗忘效果、模型效用与抗量化鲁棒性。本文提出DURABLEUN-SAF方法，实现了在BF16/INT8/INT4下的稳定遗忘认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估假设遗忘与部署阶段精度一致，忽视了生产环境LLM常以低比特（如INT4）部署。研究发现INT4量化可系统性恢复已被遗忘的内容，即使模型在BF16下通过合规审计，这对隐私合规构成威胁。

Method: 首次系统研究NF4+LoRA机制下适配器空间INT4量化的遗忘鲁棒性，在LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct上评估七种方法；识别FA-RA-Q-INT4三难困境；提出DURABLEUN-SAF（锐度感知遗忘），使用直通估计器梯度通过INT4舍入，实现量化感知目标优化。

Result: INT8量化表现良性，INT4诱导恢复率高达22倍，且随数据集难度增加而恶化；密集帕累托扫描显示一旦达到鲁棒性即发生急剧相变；DURABLEUN-SAF是唯一实现稳定经验(BF16, INT8, INT4)耐用性认证的方法，Q-INT4=0.047±0.002，认证率3/3。

Conclusion: 存在遗忘效果、模型效用与量化鲁棒性之间的三难困境，通过量化感知优化可有效缓解；建议将Q-INT4作为标准评估指标与FA、RA并列使用。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specified training data to satisfy privacy regulations such as GDPR. However, existing evaluations assume identical precision at unlearning and deployment, overlooking that production LLMs are deployed at low-bit precision. We show that INT4 quantization systematically restores forgotten content even when models pass compliance audits at bfloat16 (BF16), we term this the quantization recovery attack (QRA). We conduct the first systematic study of unlearning robustness under adapter-space INT4 quantization in the NF4+LoRA regime, evaluating seven methods on LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct across TOFU, MUSE-News, and WikiBio-WPU. INT8 is benign; INT4 induces recovery of up to 22x, worsening with dataset difficulty. We identify the FA-RA-Q-INT4 trilemma: no method simultaneously achieves strong forgetting, high utility, and quantization robustness. A dense Pareto sweep reveals a sharp phase transition once robustness is achieved, retaining accuracy collapses regardless of further tuning. To address this, we propose DURABLEUN-SAF (Sharpness-Aware Forgetting), a quantization-aware objective using Straight-Through Estimator gradients through INT4 rounding. DURABLEUN-SAF is the only method to achieve a stable empirical (0.047, {BF16, INT8, INT4})- durability certificate: Q-INT4= 0.043 +- 0.002, cert rate= 3/3, versus SalUn's cert rate= 1/3 at its own published hyperparameters.
