We call for Q-INT4 to be adopted as a standard evaluation metric alongside FA and RA.

</details>


### [217] [Demographic-Aware Transfer Learning for Sleep Stage Classification in Clinical Polysomnography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02245)
*S M Asif Hossain,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对人口统计学特征进行分层微调的睡眠分期模型，相比单一通用模型，在35/37种配置下显著提升了分类准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动睡眠分期方法通常采用单一的、忽略人口统计差异的模型。然而，睡眠模式在性别、年龄和阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停（OSA）严重程度方面存在显著差异，表明“一刀切”的方法对于多样化的临床人群可能不是最优解。

Method: 提出了一种基于人口统计分层和迁移学习的两阶段训练策略。首先在整个人群数据上预训练一个卷积循环模型，然后根据美国睡眠医学会（AASM）临床标准，针对由性别、年龄和呼吸暂停低通气指数（AHI）严重程度定义的人口亚组进行独立微调。

Result: 在包含100名临床受试者的DREAMT数据集上评估了37种微调配置。结果显示，37个微调模型中有35个优于基线模型，Cohen's kappa系数提升了0.9%至12.9%。

Conclusion: 针对特定患者人口统计特征量身定制的分层微调，比单一通用模型能产生更准确的睡眠分期结果，为个性化睡眠评估提供了一种实用且具有临床基础的范式。

Abstract: Automated sleep stage classification typically employs a single population-agnostic model, disregarding established demographic variations in sleep architecture. Sleep patterns, however, differ substantially across gender, age, and obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) severity, indicating that a onesize-fits all approach may be suboptimal for diverse clinical populations. In this paper, we propose a two stage training strategy based on demographic stratification and transfer learning framework. We first pretrains a convolutional recurrent model on the full population and then fine tunes it independently for demographic subgroups defined by gender, age, and Apnea-Hypopnea Index (AHI) severity according to the AASM clinical standard. Using the DREAMT dataset comprising 100 clinical subjects and 7 PSG channels, we evaluate 37 fine-tuned configurations across single-axis and two-way demographic combinations. Results demonstrate that 35 of the 37 fine-tuned models outperform the baseline, with Cohen's kappa improvements ranging from 0.9 to 12.9%. These findings indicate that stratified fine tuning tailored to specific patient demographics yields substantially more accurate sleep staging than a single generalized model, offering a practical and clinically grounded paradigm for personalized sleep assessment.

</details>


### [218] [Break the Block: Dynamic-size Reasoning Blocks for Diffusion Large Language Models via Monotonic Entropy Descent with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02263)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了b1框架，通过强化学习学习动态大小的推理块，解决了扩散大语言模型中固定块大小限制推理连贯性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)采用固定大小的块进行半自回归生成，存在两个关键问题：1)不同推理任务需要不同的最优解码块大小，"一刀切"的假设无效；2)即使在同一推理任务中，僵化的块划分会破坏逻辑流并降低推理连贯性。通过经验观察发现，错误推理的块间熵呈现波动不稳定趋势，而正确生成的任务遵循一致的下降趋势。

Method: 提出了b1框架，这是一个用于dLLMs的后训练框架，通过单调熵下降目标结合强化学习来学习动态大小的推理块。该框架利用块间熵的变化趋势作为优化目标，能够无缝集成到现有dLLM后训练算法中，作为即插即用模块使用。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试上的大量实验表明，b1相比现有的固定大小块基线方法有一致的改进，验证了动态块大小学习的有效性。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: b1成功解决了扩散大语言模型中固定块大小的局限性，通过学习动态推理块显著增强了推理连贯性，为dLLMs的推理能力提升提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Recent diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have demonstrated both effectiveness and efficiency in reasoning via a block-based semi-autoregressive generation paradigm. Despite their progress, the fixed-size block generations remain a critical bottleneck for effective and coherent reasoning. 1. From a global perspective, different reasoning tasks would correspond to different optimal decoding block sizes, which makes a ``one-size-fits-all'' assumption ineffective. 2. Even within a single reasoning task, the rigid block partitioning would break the logical flow and reduce reasoning coherence. Through empirical observations, we reveal that for block-wise entropy, incorrect reasoning exhibits a fluctuating and unsteady trend between blocks, whereas the correctly generated tasks follow a consistent descending trend. Therefore, this paper proposes b1, a novel post-training framework for dLLMs that learns dynamic-size reasoning blocks via a Monotonic Entropy Descent objective with reinforcement learning to enhance reasoning coherence.b1 integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module with existing dLLM's post-training algorithms. Extensive experiments across various reasoning benchmarks showcase b1's consistent improvement over existing fixed-size block baselines. Our code has been released at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/Block-R1.

</details>


### [219] [HELIX: Hybrid Encoding with Learnable Identity and Cross-dimensional Synthesis for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02278)
*Fengming Zhang,Wenjie Du,Huan Zhang,Ke Yu,Shen Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HELIX模型，通过为每个特征分配可学习的特征身份嵌入，解决了现有注意力方法缺乏持久特征表示的问题，在5个数据集上超越16个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于注意力的时间序列插补方法在每一层都重新发现特征关系，缺乏持久锚点来维持一致的特征表示；而基于图的方法依赖预定义拓扑且假设同质空间关系，无法有效处理混合空间位置和语义变量的数据集。

Method: 提出HELIX模型，为每个特征分配可学习的特征身份，这是一种持久的嵌入表示，能够在整个网络中捕获内在语义属性。该方法从时间协变中端到端学习任意特征依赖关系，并结合混合时间-特征注意力机制，自然处理空间位置与语义变量混合的数据集。

Result: 在5个公开数据集的21个实验设置上，HELIX超越所有16个基线模型达到最优性能。机制分析表明，HELIX能够使学习到的特征身份和依赖关系逐层与潜在的物理和语义结构对齐，更有效地将跨特征结构转化为插补精度。

Conclusion: HELIX通过引入持久的特征身份嵌入，成功解决了跨特征相关性利用中的表示一致性问题，证明了学习任意特征依赖关系的有效性，为时间序列插补提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Time series imputation benefits from leveraging cross-feature correlations, yet existing attention-based methods re-discover feature relationships at each layer, lacking persistent anchors to maintain consistent representations. To address this, we propose HELIX, which assigns each feature a learnable feature identity, a persistent embedding that captures intrinsic semantic properties throughout the network. Unlike graph-based methods that rely on predefined topology and assume homogeneous spatial relationships, HELIX learns arbitrary feature dependencies end-to-end from temporal co-variation, naturally handling datasets where features mix spatial locations with semantic variables. Integrated with hybrid temporal-feature attention, HELIX achieves the state-of-the-art performance, surpassing all 16 baselines on 5 public datasets across 21 experimental settings in our evaluation. Furthermore, our mechanistic analysis reveals that HELIX aligns learned feature identities and dependencies with latent physical and semantic structure progressively across layers, demonstrating that it more effectively translates cross-feature structure into imputation accuracy.

</details>


### [220] [Variational Matrix-Learning Fourier Networks for Parametric Multiphysics Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02280)
*Xinyu Li,Jianhua Zhang,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分矩阵学习傅里叶网络（VMLFN），用于高效的参数化多物理场代理建模，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于芯粒的系统-技术协同优化（STCO）中，多物理场仿真至关重要。然而，在参数化设计探索过程中，对偏微分方程控制问题进行重复的有限元求解计算成本极高，亟需一种高效的代理建模方法。

Method: 提出变分矩阵学习傅里叶网络（VMLFN）。该网络构建了对数空间正弦神经表示，包含随机采样频谱频率、频率依赖衰减调节和嵌入狄利克雷边界条件。固定隐层参数后，通过将控制偏微分方程重构为变分弱形式并强制能量泛函的平稳性条件，将物理约束训练转化为线性矩阵求解问题，仅需一阶导数，避免了高阶自动微分和罚系数调整。同时引入启发式频率扫描算法选择适应问题的最大频率。

Result: 在热传导、固体力学和亥姆霍兹波传播问题上进行了验证。五个基准案例的结果表明，VMLFN能够提供准确的全场预测，相比传统物理信息神经网络和重复有限元仿真实现了显著的加速。

Conclusion: VMLFN方法成功实现了高效且精确的参数化多物理场代理建模，有效解决了传统方法计算成本高昂的问题，为芯片设计中多物理场仿真提供了一种有力的新工具。

Abstract: Multiphysics simulation is critical for system-technology co-optimization (STCO) in chiplet-based design, but repeated finite-element solutions of PDE-governed problems are computationally expensive in parametric design exploration. This paper proposes a variational matrix-learning Fourier network (VMLFN) for efficient parametric multiphysics surrogate modeling. VMLFN constructs a log-space sine neural representation with randomly sampled spectral frequencies, frequency-dependent decay regulation, and embedded Dirichlet boundary conditions. With fixed hidden-layer parameters, the output-layer weights are determined by reformulating the governing PDEs into variational weak forms and enforcing the stationarity condition of the resulting energy functional. This converts physics-informed training into a linear matrix-solving problem, requiring only first-order derivatives and avoiding both high-order automatic differentiation and penalty-coefficient tuning. A heuristic frequency-scanning algorithm is further introduced to select a problem-adaptive maximum frequency that covers the dominant spectral range of the target problem. The proposed method is validated on heat conduction, solid mechanics, and Helmholtz wave propagation problems. Results from five benchmark cases demonstrate that VMLFN delivers accurate full-field predictions with substantial speedup over conventional physics-informed neural networks and repeated finite-element simulations.

</details>


### [221] [Graph Federated Unlearning for Privacy Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02297)
*Ruotong Ma,Wentao Yu,Qizhou Wang,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图联邦学习（GFL）中用户退出时的隐私泄露问题，提出了一种基于机器遗忘的解决方案，通过正交梯度更新和虚拟客户端机制，在彻底移除用户信息的同时最小化对模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图联邦学习虽然通过分散式训练保护了用户隐私，但在用户退出系统后，其信息仍可能通过模型参数传播到中央服务器及恶意客户端，造成隐私泄露。现有的机器遗忘方法会显著降低模型性能，而在GFL中由于局部消息传递和全局模型协作，这一问题更为严重。因此需要在确保用户信息彻底移除的同时，最小化对整体性能的影响。

Method: 提出两个关键调整：1）设计正交梯度更新策略，使遗忘更新的方向与学习其他数据的梯度正交，从而最小化对整体性能的影响；2）引入由中央服务器维护的虚拟客户端，在不恢复已移除实体信息的前提下，保留图拓扑结构和全局嵌入表示。此外，还提出了一种新颖的成员推理框架来评估隐私保护的可靠性。

Result: 在代表性的用户退出场景下进行了全面实验，实验结果表明该方法在隐私保护方面效果显著，同时性能超越了七个最新的基线方法。提出的成员推理框架验证了隐私保护的可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将机器遗忘技术应用于图联邦学习的用户退出场景，有效解决了隐私泄露与模型性能退化之间的权衡问题，为GFL中的隐私保护提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph federated learning (GFL) facilitates decentralized training on distributed graph data while keeping sensitive user information local, aligning with policies such as GDPR and CCPA that grant users the right to freely join or withdraw from learning systems. However, even decentralized, user information can persist after quitting, potentially propagating to central servers and then redistributing to malicious clients. This privacy leakage during user withdrawal, despite its importance, has received seldom attention in GFL. To fill the gap, we explore the potential of machine unlearning (MU) to thoroughly remove user information. However, classical MU methods are known to degrade overall performance, a problem that is exacerbated in GFL due to local message passing and global model collaboration. To this end, we make two adjustments to mitigate this challenge for GFL. First, we ensure unlearning updates that minimally affect overall performance, steering them in directions orthogonal to the gradients from learning other data. Second, we introduce virtual clients, maintained by the central server, to preserve graph topology and global embeddings without recovering information of removed entities. We conduct comprehensive experiments under a representative user-withdrawal scenario and propose a novel membership inference framework to rigorously evaluate and validate the reliability of our privacy preservation. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, which also surpasses the performance of seven state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [222] [A Meta Reinforcement Learning Approach to Goals-Based Wealth Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02300)
*Sanjiv R. Das,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Daniel Ostrov,Deep Srivastav,Hungjen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预训练的元强化学习方法（MetaRL），用于解决目标导向的财富管理问题，在推理阶段仅需数百分之一秒即可生成接近最优的投资组合与目标达成策略，且对市场机制变化具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态规划方法在解决目标导向的财富管理（GBWM）问题时，需要针对每个新问题单独进行训练和优化，计算成本高且在面对大规模状态空间时计算不可行。为了消除每次新问题的单独训练并提升求解效率，作者受零样本元学习和基础模型预训练概念的启发，开发了该MetaRL方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种元强化学习方法（MetaRL），该方法在数千个目标导向的财富管理（GBWM）问题上进行预训练。每个GBWM问题涉及多年场景，投资者需每年优化选择投资组合，并决定是否满足当年出现的各类财务目标，以最大化满足目标所获得的期望总效用。通过预训练，模型消除了对新问题的单独训练和优化过程。

Result: MetaRL模型在推理模式下，可在数百分之一秒内为新GBWM问题生成接近最优的动态投资组合和目标达成策略。其产生的期望效用平均达到动态规划方法确定的最优期望效用的97.8%。结果对资本市场机制变化表现出显著鲁棒性，即使训练仅使用单一市场机制。此外，该方法能够解决状态空间更大、动态规划计算不可行的问题。

Conclusion: 基于零样本元学习和基础模型预训练的MetaRL方法，能够高效、快速地为目标导向的财富管理问题提供近最优解决方案，在大幅降低计算时间的同时保持了极高的决策质量，并扩展了可求解问题的规模边界，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Applying concepts related to zero-shot meta-learning and pre-training of foundation models, we develop a meta reinforcement learning approach (denoted MetaRL) that is pre-trained on thousands of goals-based wealth management (GBWM) problems. Each GBWM problem involves a multiple year scenario over which the investor looks to optimally choose an investment portfolio each year and choose to fulfill all, some, or none of the different financial goals that arise each year. These choices seek to maximize the expected total investor utility obtained from the fulfilled financial goals. By eliminating separate training and optimization for each new investor problem, the MetaRL model in inference mode produces near-optimal dynamic investment portfolio and goal-fulfilling strategies for a new GBWM problem within a few hundredths of a second. This delivers expected utilities that are, on average, 97.8% of the optimal expected utilities (determined via Dynamic Programming). These results are remarkably robust to capital market regime changes, even when training uses only one capital market regime. Further, the MetaRL approach can enable solving problems with larger state spaces where Dynamic Programming becomes computationally infeasible.

</details>


### [223] [Differentiable Kernel Ridge Regression for Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02313)
*Jean-Marc Mercier,Gabriele Santin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了稀疏核（SKs），一种可微分、局部化且延迟训练的核岭回归变体，可作为模块化组件无缝集成到深度学习流程中，在减少训练的同时保持竞争力或提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络主导了现代机器学习，而替代的函数逼近器在大规模应用中相对未被充分探索。作者旨在重新审视核方法，将其作为标准深度学习流程中的“即插即用”组件，打破核方法与深度学习被视为分离范式的现状。

Method: 引入稀疏核，这是一种核岭回归（KRR）的变体。它具有可微分、局部化特性，并将训练推迟到推理时间（lazy），通过求解小型局部系统来降低计算成本。作者将其作为模块化层集成到PyTorch中，展示了三个可固定或学习的参数集：特征表示、目标值和评估点，从而支持免训练迁移、非线性探测及混合模型构建。

Result: 在卷积网络、视觉Transformer和强化学习等多种场景下，SK模块表现出双重优势：在某些设置中，仅需极少训练即可匹配训练好的神经读取层性能；在其他设置中，作为附加组件增强了现有模型并提升了性能。

Conclusion: 一旦解决了可扩展性和可微分性问题，核方法可以作为一种补充而非对立的范式，无缝集成到深度学习中，从而拓宽了模型设计的空间。

Abstract: Deep neural networks dominate modern machine learning, while alternative function approximators remain comparatively underexplored at scale. In this work, we revisit kernel methods as drop-in components for standard deep learning pipelines. We introduce \emph{Sparse Kernels} (SKs), a differentiable, localized, and lazy variant of kernel ridge regression (KRR) that defers training to inference time and reduces to the solution of small local systems. We integrate SKs into PyTorch as modular layers that preserve end-to-end trainability, and we show that they expose three distinct sets of parameters -- feature representations, target values, and evaluation points -- each of which can be fixed or learned. This decomposition broadens the design space available to practitioners, enabling, in particular, training-free transfer, nonlinear probing, and hybrid kernel-neural models. Across convolutional networks, vision transformers, and reinforcement learning, SK-based modules serve two complementary roles: in some settings, they match the performance of trained neural readouts with substantially less training; in others, they augment existing models and improve their performance when used as additional components. Our results suggest that kernel methods, once made scalable and differentiable, can be readily integrated with deep learning rather than treated as a separate paradigm.

</details>


### [224] [When Attention Collapses: Residual Evidence Modeling for Compositional Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02323)
*Niklas Houba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了注意力机制在加性叠加场景下的“槽位塌缩”失效模式，并提出“证据耗尽”方法，通过引入残差证据追踪有效解决了该问题，在音频分离和引力波源推断等任务中显著提升了多成分推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于注意力的组合推断模型在成分近似可分的场景（如以物体为中心的视觉）表现良好，但在加性叠加场景下存在结构性失效：多个槽位会收敛到同一主导成分，而较弱成分被忽略。作者追溯其根源发现，注意力机制对已解释证据是无记忆的，导致梯度被最强成分主导，无法实现非冗余分配。

Method: 提出残差证据建模方法，具体实现为“证据耗尽”（evidence depletion）。该方法通过结合乘性耗尽与注意力偏置，对顺序注意力引入残差状态，使后续槽位能够感知已被解释的证据，从而避免重复分配。

Result: 在合成基准和真实音频混合数据集（FUSS）上，证据耗尽将槽位塌缩减少了一个数量级；在欧空局/NASA LISA任务的引力波源推断中，标准注意力完全失效，而证据耗尽成功防止塌缩并实现了多源后验估计。控制消融实验表明，并行注意力、单独的顺序处理以及基于损失的正则化均无法解决该问题。

Conclusion: 在加性叠加条件下，残差证据追踪是防止槽位塌缩、实现组合推断的关键要素。证据耗尽作为一种最小化修改，能够有效解决注意力机制的根本局限性，拓展了组合推断模型的应用范围。

Abstract: Compositional inference - the decomposition of observations into an unknown number of latent components - is central to perception and scientific data analysis. Attention-based models perform well when components are approximately separable, as in object-centric vision. Under additive superposition, however - where multiple components contribute to every observation - we identify a structural failure mode we term slot collapse: multiple slots converge to the same dominant component while weaker ones remain unrepresented. We trace this to a general limitation: attention is memoryless with respect to explained evidence. All slots repeatedly operate on the same input without accounting for what has already been explained, so gradients are dominated by the strongest component, inducing shared fixed points across slots. As a result, attention fails to enforce non-redundant allocation under additive superposition. We address this by introducing residual evidence modeling, instantiated via evidence depletion - a minimal modification combining multiplicative depletion with an attention bias. Controlled ablations show that parallel attention, sequential processing alone, and loss-based regularization fail to resolve collapse; evidence depletion, which adds residual state to sequential attention, consistently succeeds. Across synthetic benchmarks and real-world audio mixtures (FUSS), evidence depletion reduces slot collapse by up to an order of magnitude, generalizing beyond synthetic settings. On gravitational-wave source inference for the ESA/NASA LISA mission, under identical architectures, data, and losses, standard attention fails while evidence depletion prevents collapse and enables multi-source posterior estimation. These results show that under additive superposition, residual evidence tracking is the operative ingredient for preventing collapse and enabling compositional inference.

</details>


### [225] [A Near-optimal SQ Lower Bound for Smoothed Agnostic Learning of Boolean Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02350)
*Tim Sinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平滑不可知模型下均匀分布上学习半空间问题的复杂度，给出了$ L^1 $多项式回归算法的上界以及几乎匹配的统计查询下界，并与近期高斯边界下的工作形成了补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑不可知学习模型旨在研究抗噪声学习的计算复杂度，已有工作在高斯边缘分布下取得了进展，但在离散超立方体$\{\pm 1\}^n$上均匀分布的情况尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究坐标独立翻转噪声下的半空间学习复杂度。

Method: 分析了$ L^1 $多项式回归算法在平滑不可知模型下的表现，并利用统计查询（SQ）框架证明了复杂度下界。具体包括：（1）构造多项式回归算法并分析其复杂度；（2）设计适当的统计查询下界证明技术，处理离散均匀分布和坐标翻转噪声的特殊性。

Result: $ L^1 $多项式回归算法达到复杂度$\tilde{O}(n^{O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/σ)})$；证明统计查询复杂度下界为$n^{Ω(\log(1+σ/\varepsilon^2)/σ)}$。这两个界在指数级别上几乎匹配，表明多项式回归方法在此设定下接近最优。

Conclusion: 本文在离散均匀分布设定下建立了平滑不可知半空间学习的几乎紧确复杂度界，补充了先前连续高斯设定下的结果，为理解噪声模型下学习半空间的计算复杂性提供了完整图景。

Abstract: We study the complexity of smoothed agnostic learning of halfspaces on $\{\pm 1\}^n$ under the uniform distribution in the model of \citet{KM25} where each input coordinate is independently flipped with probability $σ\in (0, {1}/{2})$. We show that $L^1$ polynomial regression achieves complexity $\tilde{O}(n^{O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/σ)})$, and prove a nearly matching Statistical Query complexity lower bound of $n^{Ω(\log(1+σ/\varepsilon ^2)/σ)}$. This complements the recent work of \citet{DK26}, which established analogous bounds in the continuous setting under Gaussian marginals.

</details>


### [226] [ZNO: Stable Rational Neural Operators in the Z-Domain for Discrete-Time Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02356)
*Xianli Zhu,Jia Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Z域神经算子（ZNO），一种因果神经算子，其层为直接在z平面参数化的稳定低秩MIMO有理滤波器，专为离散时间系统辨识问题设计。在近单位圆/长记忆动态系统中表现优异，但并非所有任务的通用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法主要针对连续时间问题，而大量系统辨识问题本质上是离散时间的。现有方法在处理离散时间动态系统时存在局限性，特别是对于具有稳定有理离散时间滤波器特性的系统，需要一种专门针对离散时间系统设计的神经算子方法。

Method: ZNO的核心是因果神经算子，其层采用稳定低秩多输入多输出（MIMO）有理滤波器，直接在z平面进行参数化。该方法将稳定性表示为单位圆盘极点约束，使学习到的离散时间极点可直接读取。模型整合了低秩通道混合、平滑稳定极点重参数化、因果递归，以及可选的短有限脉冲响应（FIR）分支，形成单一的z域有理递归层。

Result: 在受控离散系统辨识实验中，ZNO在目标动态为近单位圆的轻度阻尼极点的稳定有理系统时优势最明显。五档难度扫描显示，ZNO在从短期（约10步）到长期（约100-200步）的记忆机制中均达到最低平均误差。在五个公开非线性系统辨识基准测试中，ZNO与神经算子和状态空间基线方法具有竞争力，在动态特性与稳定有理离散时间滤波器一致的基准上达到最低平均误差，但在某些系统上经典或状态空间基线仍更优。

Conclusion: ZNO是稳定有理离散时间动态系统的强有力模型，特别适用于近单位圆和长记忆机制，但不是专用系统辨识方法的通用替代品。在匹配参数预算下ZNO并非均匀占优，但通过验证选择的配置，同一架构可在受控任务中实现最低平均误差。

Abstract: We introduce the Z-Domain Neural Operator (ZNO), a causal neural operator whose layers are stable low-rank multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) rational filters parameterized directly in the $z$-plane. ZNO addresses a limitation of existing operator learning methods, many of which are primarily tailored for continuous-time problems, while a large class of system-identification problems is intrinsically discrete-time. The $z$-domain form expresses stability as a unit-disk pole constraint and makes learned discrete-time poles directly readable. The model combines low-rank channel mixing, smooth stable pole reparameterization, causal recurrence, and an optional short finite impulse response (FIR) branch in a single $z$-domain rational recurrent layer. Across controlled discrete system-identification experiments, ZNO's advantage is most evident when the target dynamics are stable rational systems with lightly damped poles near the unit circle. Under matched parameter budgets, ZNO is not uniformly dominant; however, with validation-selected configurations, the same architecture can achieve the lowest mean error across the controlled tasks. A five-bin difficulty sweep over near-unit-circle / long-memory dynamics shows that ZNO has the lowest mean error across memory regimes, from short (approximately 10 steps) to long (approximately 100-200 steps). On five public nonlinear system-identification benchmarks, ZNO is competitive with neural operator and state-space baselines, achieving the lowest mean error on benchmarks whose dynamics align with stable rational discrete-time filters, while classical or state-space baselines remain preferable on some systems. These results position ZNO as a strong model for stable rational discrete-time dynamics, especially in near-unit-circle and long-memory regimes, but not as a universal replacement for specialized system-identification methods.

</details>


### [227] [Predicting Post Virality with Temporal Cross-Attention over Trend Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02358)
*Sarvagya Somvanshi,Mohan Xu,Rakhi Chadalavada,Nathan Canera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ViralityNET模型，通过融合Reddit平台内部特征与维基百科页面浏览量激增的外部时序信号来预测社交媒体病毒式传播，证明了引入外部注意力信号相比纯文本基线能带来显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预测社交媒体病毒式传播的模型主要依赖静态文本和结构特征，忽略了趋势信号的高度动态特性。作者旨在研究现实世界的注意力信号能否超越仅依赖帖子文本内容来改善社交媒体病毒式传播的预测效果。

Method: 提出ViralityNET架构，该模型整合四个帖子级别的数据流（标题嵌入、正文嵌入、结构元数据、学习到的子版块嵌入），并使用交叉注意力机制查询一个每日滑动窗口趋势矩阵（编码前7天维基百科中浏览量激增的前512个术语）。将病毒式传播定义为二元分类任务，根据帖子是否超过各子版块参与度的第90百分位和最小绝对分数阈值来标记。

Result: 引入外部注意力信号带来了持续的增益，相比纯文本基线在AUC-PR上提升了+0.015，总体AUC-ROC达到0.836。实证结果表明外部注意力信号能够带来可测量的改进。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在预测在线病毒式传播时，引入外部注意力信号相比纯文本基线能够产生可测量的改进，突出了现实世界动态在塑造在线病毒式传播中的重要性。

Abstract: Current models for predicting social media virality rely heavily on static textual and structural features, effectively ignoring the highly dynamic nature of trend signals. We study whether real-world attention signals can improve the prediction of social-media virality beyond what post text alone reveals. We introduce \model{}, an architecture that predicts Reddit post virality by fusing internal platform representations with exogenous temporal signals derived from Wikipedia pageview spikes. We frame virality as a binary classification task that accounts for differences in subreddit scale, labeling posts as viral if they exceed the 90th percentile of per-subreddit engagement and a minimum absolute score threshold.
  We introduce ViralityNET combines four post-level streams: title embeddings, body embeddings, structural metadata, and learned subreddit embeddings with a cross-attention block that queries a daily sliding-window trends matrix encoding the top-512 Wikipedia spike terms from the preceding seven days. Empirical results suggest that incorporating external attention signals yields consistent gains, outperforming text-only baselines by +0.015 AUC-PR and achieving an overall AUC-ROC of 0.836. Overall, we provide evidence that incorporating external attention signals yields measurable improvements over text-only baselines, highlighting the importance of real-world dynamics in shaping online virality.

</details>


### [228] [Binary Rewards and Reinforcement Learning: Fundamental Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02375)
*Marc Dymetman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从结构视角揭示了RLVR中二值奖励导致的多样性崩塌现象：在模型误设定下，降低β参数会使策略集中于少数有效输出，而非理想的过滤模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练虽然提升了单样本准确率，但常导致多样本覆盖率下降甚至低于基础模型（多样性崩塌），作者旨在从理论结构角度解释这一现象的根本原因。

Method: 分析二值奖励对策略梯度方法的退化影响，建立KL控制下倾斜分布与过滤模型的理论联系，推导超参数β与目标有效率μ的显式关系，并通过玩具自回归实验验证。

Result: 在模型误设定下，降低β参数驱动优化器向高度集中分布坍缩，而非收敛至过滤模型；直接以过滤模型为目标的替代散度方法可通过奖励覆盖性而非集中性来避免该失效模式。

Conclusion: 二值奖励的固有退化性及KL控制的非对称性导致了RLVR的多样性崩塌，采用直接面向过滤模型的替代散度方法是解决该问题的理论途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard approach for improving reasoning in language models, yet models trained with RLVR often suffer from diversity collapse: while single-sample accuracy improves, multi-sample coverage degrades, sometimes falling below the base model. We provide a structural account of this phenomenon grounded in the properties of binary rewards. Binary rewards create a fundamental degeneracy for policy gradient methods: the set of distributions maximizing expected reward is infinite, with no distinguished element. KL-control resolves this degeneracy by selecting, in the limit $β\to 0$, the filtered model $p_*:=a(\cdot\mid\mathcal{Y}_1)$ -- the base model conditioned on validity -- which is the unique fully valid distribution closest to the base model in KL divergence. This selection operates through a nontrivial asymmetry: the tilted distribution $p_{[β]}\propto a(y)\,e^{v(y)/β}$ converges to $p_*$ in forward KL as $β\to 0$, yet $p_*$ cannot serve as a direct optimization target because $\mathrm{KL}(q\,\|\,p_*)$ is infinite for any full-support policy $q$. We develop explicit formulas relating the hyperparameter $β$ to the more interpretable target validity rate $μ$. Under model misspecification -- the typical practical regime -- the pressure to decrease $β$ drives the optimizer toward highly concentrated distributions over a small number of valid outputs, collapsing toward ever fewer as $β$ decreases, rather than toward the filtered model. We illustrate this mechanism on a toy autoregressive experiment and discuss how alternative divergences that target $p_*$ directly -- as pursued empirically by \citet{kruszewski_whatever_2026} -- avoid this failure mode by rewarding coverage of $p_*$'s support rather than concentration on high-validity outputs.

</details>


### [229] [Statistically-Lossless Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02404)
*Michael Helcig,Eldar Kurtic,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统计无损量化的概念，在低于4比特实现任务级无损压缩，在5-6比特实现分布级无损压缩，并获得1.7-3.6倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM量化方法存在明显权衡：GPTQ和AWQ等有损压缩方法损失模型精度，而无损方法虽保持保真度但无法加速推理。本文旨在探索统计无损压缩的中间地带，在保持模型性能的同时实现高效压缩和加速。

Method: 提出三种互补的无损量化概念：(1)任务无损：零样本基准准确率在自然采样方差内保持；(2)分布无损：量化模型的下一词元分布与原模型实际不可区分，提出期望接受率(EAR)作为可解释的保真度指标；(3)证明γ²方差定律：对称量化相比非对称量化噪声方差放大γ²倍。基于此，提出SLQ方法——逐层非均匀量化方法，采用非对称量化和宽比特搜索。

Result: SLQ方法实现了：任务无损压缩可达每参数3.3比特（低于4比特）；分布无损压缩平均需5-6比特/参数；相比FP16实现1.7-3.6倍推理加速（配合优化内核）。在激进比特宽度下仍能保持任务级性能保真度。

Conclusion: 统计无损量化填补了有损压缩和传统无损方法之间的空白。非对称量化对于分布级无损保真度是必要的，但对任务级保持则非必需。SLQ方法证明了在显著压缩率和加速效果下，仍可保持LLM的统计特性。

Abstract: Model quantization has become essential for efficient large language model deployment, yet existing approaches involve clear trade-offs: methods such as GPTQ and AWQ achieve practical compression but are lossy, while lossless techniques preserve fidelity but typically do not accelerate inference. This paper explores the middle ground of statistically-lossless compression through three complementary notions of losslessness for quantized LLMs. First, task-lossless compression preserves zero-shot benchmark accuracy within natural sampling variance and remains achievable at aggressive bitwidths. Second, we formalize the stricter notion of distribution-lossless compression, requiring the quantized model's next-token distribution to be practically indistinguishable from the original, and propose the Expected Acceptance Rate (EAR), the maximum token-agreement probability under optimal coupling, as a directly interpretable fidelity metric (for example, EAR >= 0.99 indicates 99% agreement). Third, we prove a gamma-squared variance law showing that symmetric quantization inflates noise variance by gamma squared relative to asymmetric quantization, making asymmetry necessary for distribution-lossless fidelity but not for task-level preservation. Using SLQ, a layer-wise non-uniform method with asymmetric quantization and wide bitwidth search, we achieve task-lossless compression at well below 4 bits per parameter (as low as 3.3 bits depending on the model), distribution-lossless compression at 5 to 6 bits per parameter on average, and inference speedups of 1.7 to 3.6x relative to FP16 with optimized kernels. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/SLQ.

</details>


### [230] [Closed-Loop CO2 Storage Control With History-Based Reinforcement Learning and Latent Model-Based Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02405)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias,Vassilis Gaganis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于深度强化学习的CO2地质封存闭环管理框架。历史条件策略仅使用井筒实测数据即可恢复几乎全部的特权状态性能；潜在模型重调方法在异常工况下优于直接无模型重调，且在相同模拟器预算约束下表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质CO2封存的闭环管理需要能够适应储层不确定性的控制策略，且只能依赖实际操作中可获得的观测数据。现有方法在有限的可观测信息和模拟器预算约束下，难以高效地进行在线历史拟合与重优化。

Method: 将CO2注入与盐水生产控制建模为部分可观测序列决策问题，采用高保真油藏模拟训练深度强化学习控制器。对比了特权状态、仅井筒信息、历史条件、掩码课程及非对称师生无模型策略；提出了潜在模型自适应流程，在注入井失效、泄漏动态、奖励偏移及分隔储层连通性等场景下复用名义潜在动态并重调控制器。

Result: 历史条件策略仅利用可部署的井筒级信息即可恢复几乎全部特权状态性能。在异常工况下，潜在模型重调在相同场景特定真实模拟器预算下优于直接无模型重调。

Conclusion: 所提框架为CO2封存闭环控制提供了模拟器预算感知的替代方案，避免了重复的在线历史拟合与重优化，在实际部署约束下实现了高效控制。

Abstract: Closed-loop management of geological CO2 storage requires control policies that adapt to uncertain reservoir behavior while relying on observations that are realistically available during operation. This work formulates CO2 injection and brine-production control as a partially observable sequential decision problem and studies deployable deep reinforcement-learning controllers trained with high-fidelity reservoir simulation. We first compare privileged-state, well-only, history-conditioned, masking-curriculum, and asymmetric teacher-student model-free policies in order to quantify the value of temporal well-response information and training-time privileged simulator states. We then evaluate a latent model-based adaptation pipeline that reuses nominal latent dynamics and retunes controllers under known injector failure, leakage-induced dynamics and reward shift, and compartmentalized reservoir connectivity. The results show that history-conditioned policies recover nearly all of the privileged-state performance while using only deployable well-level information, and that latent model-based retuning outperforms direct model-free retuning under the same scenario-specific real-simulator budget in the abnormal operating cases. The proposed framework therefore provides a simulator-budget-aware alternative to repeated online history matching and re-optimization for closed-loop CO2 storage control.

</details>


### [231] [Inducing Permutation Invariant Priors in Bayesian Optimization for Carbon Capture and Storage Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02409)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias,Vassilis Gaganis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有排列对称性的优化问题，提出了一种新的高斯过程核函数（GP-Perm），用于碳捕获与封存项目中的井位优化，有效解决了传统核函数无法处理无序集合输入的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贝叶斯优化方法使用的高斯过程代理模型及其核函数主要适用于向量输入，难以有效处理具有排列对称性的输入（如无序的井位集合）。在碳捕获与封存（CCS）项目的井位优化问题中，注入井和生产井以组控制方式运行，产生了群组内的排列对称性，标准核函数无法利用这一特性，导致优化效率低下。

Method: 提出了两种排列不变的方法：1）一种新型高斯过程核函数（GP-Perm），通过比较由集合诱导的经验表示之间的稳定散度来编码排列不变性，并可与传统核函数结合处理额外的向量输入；2）作为学习不变基线，采用Deep Sets架构的深度核学习模型（DKL-DS），学习排列不变嵌入。

Result: 在8个用例上评估了所提方法，包括7个合成基准测试和1个真实的CCS案例研究（Johansen地层）。实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够有效利用排列对称性，提升了贝叶斯优化在具有此类对称性问题上的性能。

Conclusion: GP-Perm核函数成功地将排列不变性编码到高斯过程中，为具有排列对称性的昂贵黑盒函数优化问题提供了一个有效的解决方案，在碳捕获与封存项目的井位优化中展现了良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is an iterative method, tailored to optimizing expensive black box objective functions. Surrogate models like Gaussian Processes, which are the gold standard in Bayesian Optimization, can be inefficient for inputs with permutation symmetries, as the most common kernels employed are better suited for vector inputs rather than unordered sets of items. Motivated by this issue, we turn to permutation invariant Bayesian Optimization for well placement in Carbon Capture and Storage projects. The high fidelity black box simulator is instructed to operate wells under group control, giving rise to permutation symmetries within injector and producer groups that cannot be exploited with standard GP kernels. In this work, our main contribution is a novel Gaussian Process kernel (GP-Perm) that encodes permutation invariance by comparing sets through a stable divergence between their induced empirical representations, and can be combined with standard kernels for additional vector-valued inputs. As a learned invariant baseline, we also consider a Deep Kernel Learning model (DKL-DS) using the Deep Sets architecture to learn a permutation-invariant embedding. We evaluate the proposed methodology across 8 use cases, comprising seven synthetic benchmarks and one realistic CCS case study (Johansen formation)

</details>


### [232] [Generalized Distributional Alignment Games for Unbiased Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02435)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了分布式对齐博弈框架中由Jensen不等式导致的结构性估计偏差问题，提出了无偏估计器、最优多项式估计器以及方差最优增强多项式估计器，实现了零在线计算开销下的最优偏差和加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式对齐博弈框架为答案级微调(ALFT)提供了有效的变分视角，但标准算法依赖小批量对数奖励估计，因Jensen不等式引入系统性偏差，导致训练不稳定。

Method: (1)将博弈推广至任意Bregman散度，对多项式奖励构造基于U统计量的无偏估计器；(2)对KL散度博弈推导全局鲁棒极小极大多项式估计器，达到 Θ(1/K^2) 统计误差极限；(3)综合提出方差最优增强多项式优化程序(AQP)估计器，系统降低方差。

Result: 所提方法实现了最优偏差控制，并证明了统计误差极限为 Θ(1/K^2)；AQP估计器在零在线计算开销下实现了最优偏差与加速博弈收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地消除了结构性估计偏差，提供了理论最优性与收敛加速保证，使得训练更加高效稳定，无需额外计算成本。

Abstract: The Distributional Alignment Game framework provides a powerful variational perspective on Answer-Level Fine-Tuning (ALFT). However, standard algorithms for these games rely on estimating logarithmic rewards from small batches, introducing a systematic bias due to Jensen's inequality that can destabilize training. In this paper, we systematically resolve this structural estimation bias. First, we generalize the alignment game to arbitrary Bregman divergences, showing that for a family of geometries inducing polynomial rewards, we can construct provably exact and unbiased estimators using U-statistics. Second, for the canonical KL divergence game where an exact solution is impossible, we derive a globally robust minimax polynomial estimator that is provably optimal, achieving the fundamental statistical error limit of $Θ(1/K^2)$, which we establish via the Ditzian-Totik theorem. Finally, we synthesize these two approaches to propose a novel Variance-Optimal Augmented Polynomial Optimization Program (AQP) Estimator, proving that by systematically reducing variance, our method achieves not only optimal bias but also provably accelerated game convergence, leading to more efficient and stable training with zero online computational overhead.

</details>


### [233] [Reference-Sampled Boltzmann Projection for KL-Regularized RLVR: Target-Matched Weighted SFT, Finite One-Shot Gaps, and Policy Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02469)
*Yao Shu,Chenxing Wei,Hongbin Lin,Shuang Qiu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BOLT方法，证明了参考策略采样的加权SFT目标可以等价于KL正则化RLVR优化器，揭示了温度-覆盖-方差前沿的理论关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习（RLVR）虽能提供可验证奖励，但在优化路径上仍需进行rollout生成、验证器评分和参考策略评估，存在计算瓶颈。静态加权监督微调（SFT）看似能解决此问题，但加权似然并非仅由奖励决定，其采样器和权重共同影响所拟合的策略。因此需要找到能与RLVR优化器等价的加权SFT目标。

Method: 1. 识别出参考采样加权SFT目标，其诱导策略等于固定参考KL正则化RLVR优化器（即Boltzmann目标策略）
2. 证明在参考采样子类中，匹配目标策略所需的权重唯一确定为提示归一化的Boltzmann权重exp(r(x,y)/β)/Z(x)
3. 提出BOLT（Boltzmann目标SFT）作为该投影的经验估计器
4. 建立有限样本单次分析框架，分解各类误差来源

Result: 1. 理论上证明了参考采样加权SFT与KL正则化RLVR优化器的等价性
2. 揭示了温度-覆盖-方差前沿关系，解释了额外SFT轮次无法修复参考策略覆盖不足的原因
3. 展示了当需要自适应采样时，刷新的Boltzmann投影退化为KL策略镜像下降
4. Qwen实验验证了目标匹配权重、单次饱和、刷新采样器增益和优化时间节省

Conclusion: BOLT方法为加权SFT提供了理论基础，证明了通过正确的权重设计可以避免在线RL的计算开销。研究揭示了覆盖不足的根本限制，并为自适应采样场景提供了与镜像下降的联系，为高效策略优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) turns checkable outcomes into a scalable training signal, but it keeps rollout generation, verifier scoring, and reference-policy evaluations on the optimization path. Static weighted supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on precomputed rollouts seems to remove this bottleneck, yet a weighted likelihood is not specified by rewards alone: its sampler and weights induce the policy being fit. This paper identifies the reference-sampled weighted-SFT objective whose induced policy equals the fixed-reference KL-regularized RLVR optimizer. The optimizer is the standard Boltzmann target policy, obtained by exponentially tilting the reference policy by verifier reward. Matching a weighted-SFT induced policy to this target forces density-ratio weights; in the reference-sampled subclass, this reduces uniquely, up to prompt scaling, to the prompt-normalized Boltzmann weight $\exp(r(x,y)/β)/Z(x)$. BOLT, a Boltzmann-Targeted SFT procedure, is the empirical estimator of this projection. The finite one-shot analysis separates the exact stored-support price $β\log(1/π^*(S_N\mid x))$ from partition estimation, effective-sample-size variance, generalization, optimization, and approximation errors. This decomposition explains why extra SFT epochs cannot repair missing reference-policy coverage and exposes the temperature--coverage--variance frontier. When coverage needs adaptive sampling, refreshed Boltzmann projections become KL policy mirror descent; finite inner solves enter as additive drift from the exact mirror step. Single-run Qwen experiments provide projection evidence for the target-matched weight, one-shot saturation, refreshed-sampler gains, and optimization-time savings, within the stated single-run scope.

</details>


### [234] [Efficient Preference Poisoning Attack on Offline RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02495)
*Chenye Yang,Weiyu Xu,Lifeng Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线RLHF（如DPO）中的标签翻转攻击，提出了BAL-A和BMP-A两种攻击方法，通过结构化二进制稀疏逼近问题建模，实现了在有限翻转预算下的最优攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RLHF管道（如DPO）依赖预先收集的偏好数据集进行训练，使其容易受到偏好中毒攻击的威胁。针对这一安全隐患，本文研究针对对数线性DPO的标签翻转攻击，旨在揭示其脆弱性并开发有效的攻击方法。

Method: 作者首先证明翻转单个偏好标签会在DPO梯度中产生与参数无关的偏移，从而将目标投毒问题转化为结构化二进制稀疏逼近问题。基于此，开发了两种攻击方法：（1）Binary-Aware Lattice Attack (BAL-A)：将二进制翻转选择问题嵌入二进制感知格，应用LLL约简和Babai最近平面算法；（2）Binary Matching Pursuit Attack (BMP-A)：将二进制匹配追踪算法适应于非归一化梯度字典。

Result: BAL-A提供了强制二进制系数并恢复最小翻转目标的充分条件；BMP-A给出了基于相干性的恢复保证和K翻转预算下的鲁棒性（不可能性）证书。实验在合成字典和Stanford Human Preferences数据集上验证了理论，并揭示了字典几何结构如何支配攻击成功。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了对数线性DPO对标签翻转攻击的脆弱性，通过将攻击问题转化为稀疏逼近问题，提出了两种理论上可证明的攻击方法。研究结果强调了字典几何结构在攻击成功中的关键作用，为理解离线RLHF的安全风险提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pipelines such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) train on a pre-collected preference dataset, which makes them vulnerable to preference poisoning attack. We study label flip attacks against log-linear DPO. We first illustrate that flipping one preference label induces a parameter-independent shift in the DPO gradient. Using this key property, we can then convert the targeted poisoning problem into a structured binary sparse approximation problem. To solve this problem, we develop two attack methods: Binary-Aware Lattice Attack (BAL-A) and Binary Matching Pursuit Attack (BMP-A). BAL-A embeds the binary flip selection problem into a binary-aware lattice and applies Lenstra-Lenstra-Lovász reduction and Babai's nearest plane algorithm; we provide sufficient conditions that enforce binary coefficients and recover the minimum-flip objective. BMP-A adapts binary matching pursuit to our non-normalized gradient dictionary and yields coherence-based recovery guarantees and robustness (impossibility) certificates for $K$-flip budgets. Experiments on synthetic dictionaries and the Stanford Human Preferences dataset validate the theory and highlight how dictionary geometry governs attack success.

</details>


### [235] [Pretraining on Sleep Data Improves non-Sleep Biosignal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02500)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Phillip Hempel,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Rahul Thapa,Bryan He,Nicolai Spicher,Andreas Brink-Kjaer,Lars Kai Hansen,Emmanuel Mignot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了睡眠生物信号可作为有效的预训练分布，将学习到的表征迁移至非睡眠的EEG和ECG领域，在8个下游任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型在多导睡眠图任务上表现强劲，但尚不清楚睡眠生物信号是否可作为有效预训练分布，将学习到的表征迁移至相邻的非睡眠领域（如EEG和ECG）。

Method: 采用睡眠专用多模态对比预训练（留一目标），在异构数据集上将模型迁移至非睡眠EEG和ECG两种生物信号模态进行评估。

Result: 在跨多个EEG和ECG数据集的8个下游任务中，睡眠预训练相比从头训练持续提升性能；多项任务上达到甚至超越现有专用最先进模型及基础模型的表现。

Conclusion: 睡眠生物信号预训练具有强大的跨域迁移能力，可作为EEG和ECG下游任务的有效基础，为生物信号基础模型的构建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sleep foundation models have recently demonstrated strong performance on in-domain polysomnography tasks, including sleep staging, apnea detection, and disease risk prediction. In this work, we investigate whether sleep biosignals can serve as an effective pretraining distribution for learning representations that transfer beyond sleep to adjacent domains. Following sleep foundation models, we perform sleep-only multimodal contrastive pretraining (with a leave-one-out objective) and evaluate transfer to non-sleep EEG and ECG, two well-benchmarked biosignal modalities with heterogeneous datasets and clinically meaningful downstream tasks. Across eight downstream tasks spanning multiple EEG and ECG datasets, sleep pretraining consistently improves performance relative to training from scratch. Moreover, on several tasks, we achieve performance competitive with or surpassing prior specialized state-of-the-art and foundation models.

</details>


### [236] [A Novel Preprocessing-Driven Approach to Remaining Useful Life (RUL) Prediction Using Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02507)
*Florent Imbert,Tosin Adewumi,Hui Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的数据预处理流程，通过提升数据质量和时间表征能力，在航空发动机剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测任务上显著优于现有的多种先进深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的航空发动机RUL预测方法主要聚焦于模型架构设计，而对输入特征采取统一处理，忽略了数据预处理环节的影响。作者旨在弥补这一不足，证明预处理对于提升神经网络预测性能至关重要。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的预处理流程，利用完整的时间序列并在每个时间步生成RUL估计值，使模型能够捕捉细粒度的退化动态并提供贯穿发动机运行寿命的连续预测。研究在NASA C-MAPSS数据集上进行了验证。

Result: 与CNN、RNN、LSTM、DCNN、TCN、BiGRU-TSAM、AGCNN和ATCN等一系列最先进的神经模型相比，该方法在航空发动机RUL预测中始终实现了更高的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数据预处理在最大化神经网络预测模型效能方面发挥着关键作用，改进预处理流程能够显著提升RUL预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of Remaining Useful Life (RUL) in aero-engines is vital for predictive maintenance, improved operational reliability, and reduced lifecycle costs. While deep learning approaches have demonstrated strong potential in this area, most existing methods focus primarily on model architecture design and treat input features uniformly, often neglecting the influence of data preprocessing. In this work, we propose a novel preprocessing pipeline that enhances RUL prediction by improving data quality and temporal representation before model training. Our approach leverages complete temporal sequences and generates RUL estimates at each timestep, enabling the model to capture fine-grained degradation dynamics and deliver continuous prognostic insights throughout the engine's operational life. To validate the effectiveness of the proposed pipeline, we conduct experiments on the NASA C-MAPSS dataset. Comparative evaluations against a suite of state-of-the-art neural models including CNN, RNN, LSTM, DCNN, TCN, BiGRU-TSAM, AGCNN, and ATCN, demonstrate that our approach consistently achieves superior accuracy and robustness in aero-engine RUL prediction. These results highlight the critical role of preprocessing in maximizing the effectiveness of neural prognostic models.

</details>


### [237] [MPCS: Neuroplastic Continual Learning via Multi-Component Plasticity and Topology-Aware EWC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02509)
*Joern Hentsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MPCS持续学习架构，集成11种机制，在31任务基准测试中达到帕累托前沿。关键发现：傅里叶编码最关键（移除后性能下降30.7pp），全局EWC反而降低性能（移除后获得最高性能系统，计算成本降低4.7倍），帕累托前沿可作为模型压缩的有效指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统面临可塑性（获取新知识）与稳定性（保留旧知识）之间的根本矛盾。现有单一机制难以平衡这一张力，需要设计一个集成多种互补机制的架构来同时优化任务性能、表示多样性和梯度冲突率。

Method: 提出MPCS（Multi-Plasticity Continual System）架构，集成11种互补机制：任务驱动神经发生、傅里叶编码输入、EWC正则化、元回放、混合巩固、混合门控、突触修剪/再生、Hebbian更新、任务相似性路由、自适应增长控制和连续神经元重要性跟踪。在MEP-BENCH基准上评估，该基准涵盖31个任务（回归、分类、逻辑、混合域），使用三维帕累托准则（任务性能Perf、表示多样性RD、梯度冲突率GCR）。进行了15个消融配置实验（3个种子×4个轨道×2000轮）。

Result: MPCS达到94.2的归一化效率分数，在14个通过门控测试的系统中9个位于帕累托前沿。关键发现：(i)傅里叶编码是最关键组件（移除后性能下降30.7pp，14%任务未通过MEP门控）；(ii)全局EWC降低性能（NES=-4.2），拓扑局部EWC可缓解但不消除此惩罚；完全移除EWC得到MPCS_EFFICIENT，性能最高，在高任务相似性区域（s_bar≈0.95）呈现单调关系：全局EWC<拓扑EWC<无EWC；(iii)帕累托状态评估具有预测性：移除两个帕累托支配组件（EWC+Hebbian）得到MPCS_EFFICIENT，性能提升0.6pp，计算成本降低4.7倍（127 vs 602分钟），验证帕累托前沿可作为可操作的模型压缩指南。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了集成多种互补机制的有效性，更重要的是揭示了帕累托前沿分析可以作为模型压缩和架构优化的实用指导。某些传统认为有益的组件（如全局EWC）在某些场景下可能反而有害，而帕累托支配分析能够识别出可以安全移除的组件，实现性能提升与计算成本降低的双赢。这为持续学习系统的设计提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Continual learning systems face a fundamental tension between plasticity -- acquiring new knowledge  --  and stability  --  retaining prior knowledge. We introduce MPCS (Multi-Plasticity Continual System), a neuroplastic architecture that integrates eleven complementary mechanisms: task-driven neurogenesis, Fourier-encoded inputs, EWC regularization, meta-replay, mixed consolidation, hybrid gating, synapse pruning/regeneration, Hebbian updates, task similarity routing, adaptive growth control, and continuous neuron importance tracking. We evaluate MPCS on MEP-BENCH, a multi-track benchmark spanning 31 tasks across regression, classification, logic, and mixed domains, using a three-dimensional Pareto criterion over task performance (Perf), representation diversity (RD), and gradient conflict rate (GCR). Across 15 ablation configurations (3 seeds x 4 tracks x 2000 epochs), MPCS achieves a Normalized Efficiency Score of 94.2, placing it on the Pareto frontier among 9 of 14 gate-passing systems. Key findings: (i) Fourier encoding is the single most critical component (removal drops Perf by 30.7 pp and fails the MEP gate on 14% of tasks); (ii) global EWC degrades performance (NES = -4.2); topology-local EWC reduces this penalty (NES 90.5->91.8) but does not eliminate it; removing EWC entirely yields MPCS_EFFICIENT, the highest-Perf system -- establishing a monotone relationship in the high task-similarity regime (s_bar ~= 0.95): global EWC < topology EWC < no EWC; (iii) the Pareto status assessment is predictive: removing the two Pareto-dominated components (EWC + Hebbian) jointly yields MPCS_EFFICIENT, which improves Perf by 0.6 pp at 4.7x lower compute cost (127 vs. 602 min), validating the Pareto frontier as an actionable model-compression guide.

</details>


### [238] [Evaluating Tabular Representation Learning for Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02519)
*Muhammad Usman Butt,Andreas Hotho,Daniel Schlör*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了表格表示学习方法在NetFlow网络入侵检测中的性能，发现有监督方法显著优于无监督方法，TabICL在CIDDS数据集上表现最佳，且表示学习可跨网络环境迁移但受数据分布影响较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络入侵检测系统（NIDS）依赖人工特征工程，需要领域专业知识，这与现代机器学习和神经网络模型应直接从数据中学习有意义表示的原则相悖。因此，研究者希望探索表格表示学习技术是否能通过自动学习NetFlow数据的鲁棒特征表示来提升入侵检测性能。

Method: 在基准NetFlow数据集上系统评估最先进的表示学习方法，对比传统自编码器和端到端Transformer基线模型。使用有监督分类器和无监督异常检测器评估学习到的表示，并对每种组合进行全面的超参数探索。此外，还进行了跨数据集迁移实验以验证泛化能力。

Result: 实验揭示了强烈的“数据集-模型依赖性”，没有单一方法在所有场景中始终占优。在有监督分类任务中，TabICL在CIDDS数据集上取得最佳性能，而自编码器紧随其后，并与端到端Transformer模型在平均排名上并列最佳。有监督方法显著优于无监督异常检测方法，后者无单一最优组合。跨数据集迁移实验表明，选择合适的方法和分类器可实现跨网络环境的泛化，但性能受源-目标数据集组合影响显著，对网络环境间的分布差异敏感。

Conclusion: 表格表示学习技术在网络入侵检测中具有应用潜力，能够自动学习有效特征并支持跨环境迁移，但模型选择需针对具体数据集和应用场景（有监督/无监督）进行调整，不存在通用的最优解决方案。

Abstract: Classic Network Intrusion Detection Systems (NIDS) often rely on manual feature engineering to extract meaningful patterns from network traffic data. However, this approach requires domain expertise and runs counter to the widely adopted principle of modern machine learning and neural networks: that models themselves should learn meaningful representations directly from data. We investigate whether tabular representation learning techniques can improve intrusion detection performance by automatically learning robust feature representations for NetFlow data. This paper presents a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art representation learning methods on benchmark NetFlow datasets, comparing against traditional autoencoders and end-to-end transformer baselines. We evaluate learned representations using both supervised classifiers and unsupervised anomaly detectors, with comprehensive hyperparameter exploration for each combination. Our results reveal strong dataset-model dependency, with no single approach consistently dominating across all scenarios. For supervised classification, TabICL achieves the best performance on CIDDS, while autoencoders follow closely and tie with end-to-end transformer models for the best average rank across datasets. Supervised approaches substantially outperform unsupervised anomaly detection methods, where no single combination consistently dominates as optimal choices depend on the dataset. Cross-dataset transfer experiments demonstrate that learned representations can generalize across network environments with appropriate method and classifier selection. However, transfer performance varies substantially depending on the source-target dataset combination, indicating sensitivity to distributional differences between network environments.

</details>


### [239] [Physics-Informed Neural Learning for State Reconstruction and Parameter Identification in Coupled Greenhouse Climate Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02524)
*Sani Biswas,Khursheed J. Ansari,Md. Nasim Akhtar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种耦合物理信息神经网络(PINN)方法，用于温室环境中室内温度和湿度动力学的联合重建及关键模型参数的同时识别，在稀疏和噪声观测条件下实现了比纯数据驱动方法更高的重建精度和参数恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)已成为将数据驱动学习与物理知识相结合的有前景的框架。针对温室环境中室内温度和湿度动态重建问题，在稀疏和噪声观测条件下，需要一种能够同时进行状态重建和参数识别的方法。

Method: 开发了一种耦合物理信息神经学习框架，将降阶物理驱动模型融入学习过程中，在神经网络训练中整合控制动力学约束，实现状态重建与参数识别的联合求解。

Result: 在模拟昼夜强迫条件的受控合成基准测试中，相比纯数据驱动的神经网络基线，耦合PINN在温度和湿度误差方面取得了更好的重建精度，同时保持了高判定系数，在湿度通道的改进尤为显著。此外，该方法成功恢复了控制系统动力学的主导物理参数。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了物理信息学习在温室气候建模以及更广泛的数据稀缺环境系统中的应用潜力，证明了该方法能够在数据插值之外学习可解释的表示。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have recently emerged as a promising framework for integrating data-driven learning with physical knowledge. In this work, we propose a coupled PINN approach for the joint reconstruction of indoor temperature and humidity dynamics in greenhouse environments, together with simultaneous identification of key model parameters. The method incorporates a reduced-order physically motivated model into the learning process, enabling consistent estimation under sparse and noisy observations.
  The artificial intelligence contribution lies in the development of a coupled physics-informed neural learning framework that integrates governing dynamical constraints into neural network training, while the engineering application focuses on greenhouse climate state reconstruction and parameter identification.
  The proposed framework is evaluated on a controlled synthetic benchmark that mimics diurnal forcing conditions. Compared with a purely data-driven neural network baseline, the coupled PINN achieves improved reconstruction accuracy, reducing temperature and humidity errors while maintaining high coefficients of determination. The improvement is particularly pronounced in the humidity channel, where latent moisture dynamics are more difficult to infer from limited measurements.
  In addition to accurate state reconstruction, the method successfully recovers the dominant physical parameters governing the system dynamics, demonstrating its ability to learn interpretable representations beyond data interpolation. These results highlight the potential of physics-informed learning for greenhouse climate modeling and, more broadly, for data-scarce environmental systems.

</details>


### [240] [Recurrent Deep Reinforcement Learning for Chemotherapy Control under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02552)
*Firas Mohamed Elamine Kiram,Imane Youkana,Rachida Saouli,Gian Antonio Susto,Laid Kahloul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Chemotherapy dose optimization can be formulated as a dynamic treatment regime, requiring sequential decisions under uncertainty that must balance tumor suppression against toxicity. However, most reinforcement learning approaches assume full observability of the patient state, a condition rarely met in clinical practice. We investigate whether memory-augmented policies can improve chemotherapy control under partial observability. To this end, we employ a recurrent TD3-based approach with separate LSTM actor-critic networks and evaluate it on the AhnChemoEnv benchmark from DTR-Bench, considering both off-policy and on-policy recurrent architectures against feed-forward TD3 and Soft Actor-Critic. Pharmacokinetic and pharmacodynamic variability are held fixed to isolate hidden-state uncertainty and observation noise and to avoid confounding effects from inter-patient variability. Across ten random seeds, recurrence yields modest benefit under full observability but substantially stronger and more stable performance under partial observability, with more consistent tumor suppression and improved normal-cell preservation. These findings indicate that memory-based policies are particularly beneficial when clinically relevant state information is incomplete or noisy.

</details>


### [241] [StreamIndex: Memory-Bounded Compressed Sparse Attention via Streaming Top-k](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02568)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamIndex提出了一种分块分区-归并的top-k驱动器，避免实例化完整的中间分数张量，将DeepSeek-V3.2/V4的CSA索引器在单GPU上的可处理序列长度从65,536扩展至1,048,576（32倍），峰值HBM仅需6.21GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek-V3.2和V4引入了压缩稀疏注意力（CSA），但现有实现需要在top-k选择前实例化一个[B, S, H_I, T]的FP32分数张量。当序列长度S=65,536时，该中间张量达256GB，超出任何单GPU高带宽内存（HBM）容量，导致内存溢出。

Method: 提出StreamIndex，一种基于Triton的CSA流水线实现。其核心组件是一个分块分区-归并top-k驱动器，按块处理而不实例化完整中间张量。该驱动器与TileLang的流水线注意力内核可组合使用。

Result: 在NVIDIA H200上测试，现有实现路径在S=65,536时OOM；StreamIndex在相同索引器配置下可处理至S=1,048,576，峰值HBM仅6.21GB。与实例化路径的地面真值相比，小序列上比特精确匹配；在chunk大小、key-tile大小、top-k三个设计空间扫描中，平均召回率≈1.0000，最小召回率≥0.9980。在S=262,144时，实例化索引器+TileLang注意力OOM，而分块索引器+相同注意力运行耗时1.97秒，峰值HBM 18.56GB。

Conclusion: StreamIndex成功解决了CSA索引器步骤的内存瓶颈问题，在保持高召回率（≥0.9980）的同时实现了32倍的序列长度扩展，且可与现有注意力内核组合。该贡献针对索引器步骤本身，不涉及更快的注意力内核或真实检查点的端到端行为。

Abstract: DeepSeek-V3.2 and V4 introduce Compressed Sparse Attention (CSA): a lightning indexer (a learned scoring projection over compressed keys) scores them, the top-k are selected per query, and a sparse attention kernel reads only those. Public CSA implementations materialize a [B, S, H_I, T] FP32 score tensor before the top-k reduction. With H_I=64 indexer heads and the V4-Flash compression ratio m=4, that intermediate is 256 GB at sequence length S=65,536, exceeding any single-GPU high-bandwidth-memory (HBM) budget. We present StreamIndex, a Triton implementation of the CSA pipeline whose central component is a chunked partition-merge top-k driver that never materializes the full intermediate. On synthetic-but-realistic V4-shaped inputs at the indexer-step (layer) level on a single NVIDIA H200, the materialize path runs out of memory (OOMs) at S=65,536 with V4-Flash dimensions; StreamIndex runs the same indexer to S=1,048,576 with 6.21 GB peak HBM, a 32x regime extension. Set-overlap recall against the materialize ground truth is bit-exact at small S where both fit; across three 5-point design-space sweeps (chunk size, key-tile size, top-k), mean recall rounds to 1.0000 with min recall at least 0.9980 in every cell. The chunked driver composes with TileLang's pipelined attention kernel: at S=262,144 with V4-Flash dimensions, the materialize indexer paired with TileLang attention OOMs while the chunked indexer paired with the same attention runs in 1.97 s at 18.56 GB peak. Our contribution targets the indexer step; we make no claim of a faster attention kernel or of real-checkpoint end-to-end behavior. Code: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/StreamIndex.

</details>


### [242] [Gradient Boosted Risk Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02593)
*Costa Georgantas,Jonas Richiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于梯度提升的算法，用于构建紧凑且具有竞争力的风险评分模型，相比传统回归方法能显著减少规则数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险评分模型因其可解释性和可操作性在医疗、保险等领域应用广泛，但现有主流方法多基于线性回归，难以捕捉非线性关系。本研究旨在构建一种既能建模非线性效应、又能保持模型紧凑性的风险评分生成方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）的算法，并提供了带有Python和R接口的C++实现，用于构建紧凑且预测性强的风险评分模型。

Result: 在涵盖回归、分类和时间至事件任务的12个表格数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在预测性能上具有竞争力，且生成的评分规则更为紧凑。与AutoScore相比，分类任务规则平均减少60%，时间至事件任务规则平均减少16%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效生成更紧凑、更具预测力的风险评分，为需要高可解释性的应用场景提供了一种优于传统回归方法的新选择。

Abstract: Risk scores are an interpretable and actionable class of machine learning models with applications in medicine, insurance, and risk management. Unlike most computational methods, risk scores are designed to be computed by a human by attributing points to a data sample based on a limited set of criteria. The most common approaches for generating risk scores use linear regressions to estimate the effect of selected variables. We propose a simple and effective approach towards building compact and predictive risk scores. We provide an algorithm based on gradient boosting that is capable of modeling nonlinear effects, along with a C++ implementation with Python and R bindings. Through extensive empirical evaluation on twelve tabular datasets spanning regression, classification, and time-to-event tasks, we show that our method achieves competitive predictive performance while producing substantially more compact scores than regression-based alternatives, with 60% fewer rules for classification tasks and 16% fewer rules for time-to-event tasks on average, compared to AutoScore.

</details>


### [243] [HARMES: A Multi-Modal Dataset for Wearable Human Activity Recognition with Motion, Environmental Sensing and Sound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02596)
*Robin Burchard,Pascal-André Brückner,Marius Bock,Juergen Gall,Kristof Van Laerhoven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了HARMES数据集，首个结合手腕IMU、环境传感器和音频的多模态穿戴式HAR数据集，规模达80小时，涵盖20名参与者的15类日常生活活动，实验证明多模态融合能有效提升复杂活动的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一传感模态在识别相似或复杂的日常生活活动时往往产生模糊信号，存在固有局限性。为解决这一问题，研究动机在于利用多模态传感器的互补优势，提升穿戴式人体活动识别的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了HARMES多模态穿戴数据集，采集了三种手腕模态数据：惯性测量单元（IMU）运动传感、大气环境传感器（湿度、温度、气压）和音频。数据来自20名参与者在自家进行家务活动的记录，总计超过80小时数据，每人约3小时标注数据，涵盖15类ADL。进行了跨被试泛化基准测试和消融研究。

Result: 创建了目前最大的手腕惯性-声学HAR数据集，规模接近此前最大数据集的6倍。消融研究表明，不同模态的贡献取决于活动类型，对于仅凭运动数据难以区分的活动，多模态融合能提供显著的互补价值。数据集和示例代码已在Zenodo和GitHub公开。

Conclusion: HARMES填补了多模态穿戴式HAR数据集的空白，证明了环境传感器和音频与运动传感器结合的有效性，为日常生活活动识别研究提供了重要资源。多模态方法在处理运动信号模糊的活动时展现出明显优势，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: With each sensing modality exhibiting inherent strengths and limitations, multi-modal approaches for wearable Human Activity Recognition (HAR) are becoming increasingly relevant -- particularly for recognizing Activities of Daily Living (ADLs), where individual modalities often produce ambiguous signals for similar or complex activities. This work introduces HARMES, a multi-modal wearable dataset combining three wrist-recorded modalities: motion sensing via an Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU), atmospheric environmental sensors (humidity, temperature, and pressure), and audio. Collected from 20 participants performing household activities in their own homes, HARMES totals over 80 hours of recorded data, with approximately three hours of labeled activity data per participant across 15 ADL classes. To the best of our knowledge, HARMES is the first dataset to combine this particular sensor trio, and it is nearly six times larger than the previously largest wrist-inertial-acoustic HAR dataset. In an extensive benchmark, we evaluate cross-subject generalization and conduct an ablation study revealing that modality contributions are activity-dependent and can provide complementary value, particularly for activities that are ambiguous from motion data alone. HARMES is freely available at Zenodo, alongside example code for loading the dataset and training models on GitHub.

</details>


### [244] [Isotropic Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02597)
*Michael F. Staddon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了各向同性傅里叶神经算子（Isotropic Fourier Neural Operator），通过改进傅里叶层的线性变换以保持空间对称性，在提升模型性能的同时大幅减少了参数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶神经算子（FNO）在求解偏微分方程方面表现出色，但其傅里叶层中的线性变换依赖于波数参数，未考虑物理系统通常具备的各向同性（即结果与坐标系选择无关）这一对称性，导致模型引入了不必要的复杂性。

Method: 作者对傅里叶层中的线性变换进行了改进，强制其遵循空间对称性约束，从而提出了各向同性傅里叶神经算子。该模型确保了线性变换与坐标系的选取无关，符合物理系统的各向同性特征。

Result: 实验表明，该方法不仅提升了模型性能，还显著降低了参数量。在二维情况下参数减少至原来的1/16，在三维情况下参数减少至原来的1/96。

Conclusion: 通过在傅里叶神经算子中引入空间对称性约束，各向同性傅里叶神经算子实现了更高效、更简洁且性能更优的模型设计，为求解具有各向同性特征的物理问题提供了一种改进方案。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators are deep learning models that learn mappings between function spaces and can be used to learn and solve partial differential equations (PDEs), in some cases significantly faster than traditional PDE solvers. Within the model are Fourier layers, which apply linear transformations directly to the Fourier modes, with parameters depending on the wave numbers. However, most physical systems are isotropic, with the results being independent of the coordinate system chosen, but the linear transformations do not necessarily respect these symmetries. We propose a modification to the linear transformations that ensures spatial symmetries are respected, called the Isotropic Fourier Neural Operator, which both improves model performance and reduces the number of parameters by up to a factor of 16 in 2D and 96 in 3D.

</details>


### [245] [Gradient-Discrepancy Acquisition for Pool-Based Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02609)
*Mohamadsadegh Khosravani,Sandra Zilles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的基于梯度的主动学习数据获取准则，并从理论和实证两个层面验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习的性能在很大程度上取决于获取准则的选择，即学习算法如何选择潜在有信息量的数据点进行标签查询。现有的准则（如不确定性度量）可能存在不足，因此需要开发更有效、有理论依据的新型获取准则。

Method: 基于Luo等人（2022）提出的泛化界，推导出一种新颖的基于梯度的获取准则。该准则可以替代不确定性采样中的不确定性度量，也可以结合到考虑样本分布多样性的基于多样性的方法中。

Result: 论文提供了所提获取准则的理论证明，并通过实证评估展示了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于梯度的获取准则具有坚实的理论基础，在主动学习任务中表现出良好的性能，为主动学习中的数据选择提供了一个有效的新方案。

Abstract: The effectiveness of active learning hinges on the choice of the acquisition criterion by which a learning algorithm selects potentially informative data points whose label is subsequently queried. This paper proposes a novel gradient-based acquisition criterion, derived from a generalization bound introduced by Luo et al. (2022). This criterion can be applied in lieu of uncertainty measures in uncertainty sampling, or incorporated into diversity-based methods that consider the spread of sampled points in addition to the uncertainty of their labels. We provide a theoretical justification of the proposed acquisition criterion, and demonstrate its effectiveness in an empirical evaluation.

</details>


### [246] [Selective Prediction from Agreement: A Lipschitz-Consistent Version Space Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02611)
*Mohamadsadegh Khosravani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在固定池（直推）设定下研究选择性分类问题，通过Lipschitz一致性约束定义版本空间，实现了基于一致性的预测与弃权机制，并提出了一种预算约束下的贪心查询算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定池设定中，未标注数据池预先给定且仅能查询部分点的标签。研究动机在于解决如何在有限的标签查询预算下，对不确定样本进行弃权以保证预测可靠性的问题。

Method: 将选择性预测建模为一致性问题：基于已查询标签和嵌入空间中的Lipschitz边界约束，定义分类头的版本空间；推导上下Lipschitz边界以认证每个池样本的有效标签集；当且仅当版本空间中所有一致分类头达成一致时进行预测，否则弃权。同时，提出单调次模几何代理目标，采用贪心算法进行预算约束下的样本选择。

Result: 获得了定义每个池点认证有效标签集的Lipschitz边界上下界；证明了版本空间中所有一致分类头在认证标签集上的预测一致性；针对预算查询问题，证明贪心算法保持了标准近似因子。

Conclusion: 通过一致性视角和Lipschitz边界约束，为固定池选择性分类提供了理论认证框架，确保了仅在标签被强制确定时才进行预测，从而有效平衡了预测覆盖率和可靠性。

Abstract: We consider selective classification with abstention in the fixed-pool (or transductive) setting, where the unlabeled pool is given beforehand and only a subset of points can be queried for labels. Our main insight is to view selective prediction through agreement: given queried labels and Lipschitz margin constraints in an embedding space, the version space of Lipschitz-consistent classification heads is well defined. We obtain upper and lower Lipschitz margin bounds that define, for each pool point, a set of certified valid labels containing the prediction of every head in the version space. The model therefore predicts only when the label is forced (i.e., all consistent heads agree), and abstains otherwise. We also propose a monotone submodular geometric proxy for budgeted querying, and show that a greedy algorithm retains the standard approximation factor.

</details>


### [247] [Gradient-Gated DPO: Stabilizing Preference Optimization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02626)
*Inoussa Mouiche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gate-DPO方法，通过门控机制调节负样本梯度，解决了DPO训练中的概率挤压效应，实现了更稳定高效的大语言模型对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好优化（DPO）在训练过程中存在概率挤压效应，负梯度会抑制被拒绝响应，导致概率质量集中在高置信度预测上，甚至引发系统性的概率崩塌，影响模型对齐的稳定性。

Method: 提出梯度门控偏好优化（Gate-DPO），根据模型的概率几何特性对被拒绝样本的梯度进行调节。当更新目标为极低概率响应时，门控机制会衰减有害梯度，同时保持标准优化行为。该方法不改变底层偏好目标函数，且与现有方法（如扩展SFT、IPO、Cal-DPO）互补。

Result: 在多种架构和偏好数据集上的实验表明，Gate-DPO能持续减少挤压效应并提高被选响应的似然。质量动态分析显示优化行为更健康，被拒绝响应的抑制减少。值得注意的是，较小的门控模型比较未门控的大模型表现出更强的被选响应改进，表明控制梯度动态比单纯扩大规模更关键。

Conclusion: Gate-DPO通过控制梯度动态有效解决了DPO的优化病理问题，实现了更稳定高效的模型对齐。研究表明，相比于单纯增加模型规模，合理控制梯度动态对于实现稳定高效的对齐更为关键。

Abstract: Preference optimization has become a central paradigm for aligning large language models with human feedback. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) simplifies reinforcement learning from human feedback by directly optimizing pairwise preferences, removing the need for reward modeling and policy optimization. However, recent work shows that DPO exhibits a squeezing effect, where negative gradients applied to rejected responses concentrate probability mass on high-confidence predictions while suppressing alternative responses. This phenomenon arises even in simple softmax models and can lead to systematic probability collapse during training. We introduce Gradient-Gated Preference Optimization (Gate-DPO), a method that stabilizes training by modulating rejected gradients according to the model's probability geometry. When updates target extremely low-probability responses, the gate attenuates harmful gradients while preserving standard optimization behavior. Gate-DPO addresses this optimization pathology without modifying the underlying preference objective and is complementary to existing methods such as extended SFT, IPO, and Cal-DPO. Experiments across multiple architectures and preference datasets show that Gate-DPO consistently reduces squeezing and improves chosen-response likelihood. Mass-dynamics analysis further reveals healthier optimization behavior, with improved preferred responses and reduced suppression of the overall distribution. Notably, smaller gated models can exhibit stronger chosen-response improvements than larger ungated models, suggesting that controlling gradient dynamics, rather than scale alone, is key to stable and efficient alignment.

</details>


### [248] [CNNs for Vis-NIR Chemometrics: From Contradiction to Conditional Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02636)
*Dário Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述指出近红外光谱深度学习研究中出现的相互矛盾结论源于未受控的调节变量，提出了一个条件化设计框架，将模型架构和预处理选择与光谱物理、数据集特性和部署场景联系起来，以实现更可重现、物理感知且面向部署的模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱化学计量学深度学习研究中频繁出现相互矛盾的结论（如大核与小核、浅层与深层架构、原始光谱与预处理、单域训练与迁移学习等），同一架构在不同研究中表现优劣不一，导致实际应用者陷入困境，亟需理解这些矛盾的根源并提供系统性的解决方案。

Method: 通过系统回顾已发表的化学计量学和光谱学研究，将相互矛盾的结论归因于三个关键因素：(i)水主导基质中Vis-NIR测量的间接性质；(ii)有效感受野与信息性光谱结构宽度之间的不匹配；(iii)作为隐藏超参数的验证设计（包括划分策略、超参数调优预算、部署类似偏移的暴露程度），并在此基础上构建条件化设计框架。

Result: 识别出导致NIR深度学习研究结论矛盾的三个根本原因，建立了将架构和预处理选择与光谱物理特性、数据集特征和预期部署场景相联系的条件化设计框架，证明了这些矛盾并非方法本身不可调和，而是未控制调节变量的结构性预期结果。

Conclusion: 近红外光谱深度学习研究应从模板驱动的架构选择转向可重现、物理感知且与部署对齐的模型比较范式，通过控制关键调节变量和采用条件化设计框架，可以实现更加一致和可靠的研究结论。

Abstract: Near-infrared (NIR; a.k.a.\ NIRS) deep-learning studies in chemometrics increasingly report mutually inconsistent conclusions regarding convolutional neural network (CNN) design, including small versus large kernels, shallow versus deep architectures, raw spectra versus preprocessing, and single-domain training versus transfer learning. As a result, the same architecture can appear superior in one study and inferior in another, creating a practical impasse for chemometric practitioners. In this review, we argue that these contradictions are not evidence of irreconcilable methods but a structurally expected consequence of uncontrolled moderating variables. Specifically, we trace recurring disagreements to (i) the indirect nature of Vis--NIR measurement in water-dominated matrices, (ii) mismatch between effective receptive field (ERF) and the width of informative spectral structure, and (iii) validation design (including split strategy, hyperparameter tuning budget, and exposure to deployment-like shifts) acting as a hidden hyperparameter that can dominate model ranking. Building on evidence from published chemometrics and spectroscopy studies, we propose a conditional design framework that links architecture and preprocessing choices to spectral physics, dataset regime, and intended deployment scenario. Overall, the proposed perspective moves DL Chemometrics from template-driven architecture selection toward reproducible, physics-aware, and deployment-aligned model comparison.

</details>


### [249] [CARD: Coarse-to-fine Autoregressive Modeling with Radix-based Decomposition for Transferable Free Energy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02657)
*Ziyang Yu,Yi He,Wenbing Huang,Wen Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CARD框架，通过基于基数的分解方法实现3D坐标到混合离散-连续序列的双射转换，在未见过的系统上达到经典方法精度，同时推理速度提升约40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自由能差估计方法存在关键局限：经典计算方法因依赖大量分子动力学模拟而计算成本过高；深度学习方法受限于生成模型表达能力不足或输入维度与特定系统绑定，导致泛化能力可忽略不计。

Method: 提出CARD生成框架，采用新型基于基数的分解方法，将3D坐标双射转换为混合离散-连续序列，实现从粗到精的自回归建模，增强了表达能力。该模型对应于零自由能分布，作为任意系统绝对自由能计算的提议分布，无需依赖炼金术路径。

Result: 在多种任务的实验中，CARD在具有不同拓扑结构的未见系统上匹配了经典计算方法的精度，同时实现了约40倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: CARD成功克服了现有方法的局限性，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了计算效率，为化学和药物发现中的自由能计算提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating free energy differences quantifies thermodynamic preferences in molecular interactions, which is central to chemistry and drug discovery. Despite fruitful progress, existing methods still face key limitations: classical computational approaches remain prohibitively expensive due to their reliance on extensive molecular dynamics simulations, while deep learning-based methods are constrained by either less-expressive generative models or input dimensions tied to a specific system, resulting in negligible generalization. To address these challenges, we propose CARD, a generative framework that employs a novel radix-based decomposition to bijectively convert 3D coordinates into mixed discrete-continuous sequences, enabling coarse-to-fine autoregressive modeling with enhanced expressiveness. Notably, the model corresponds to a distribution with zero free energy, serving as a proposal for absolute free energy computation of arbitrary systems without relying on alchemical pathways. Experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate that CARD matches the accuracy of classical computational methods on unseen systems with diverse topologies, while achieving an approximately 40-fold speedup in inference.

</details>


### [250] [Spectral Model eXplainer: a chemically-grounded explainability framework for spectral-based machine learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02684)
*Jose Vinicius Ribeiro,Rafael Figueira Goncalves,Fabio Luiz Melquiades,Sylvio Barbon Junior*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMX框架，一种针对光谱数据的可解释AI方法，通过专家定义的光谱区域而非孤立变量来解释分类模型，并在多个真实和合成数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释AI方法（如SHAP、PFI、VIP）主要针对表格数据设计，将其应用于光谱数据时会将相关性分配给孤立变量而非化学意义明确的光谱区域。光谱数据具有物理连续性和高共线性特征，现有方法的变量级输出需要事后聚合才能恢复区域级信息，缺乏针对光谱特性的解释框架。

Method: 提出Spectral Model eXplainer (SMX)框架：1) 通过PCA总结每个光谱区域；2) 定义基于分位数的逻辑谓词；3) 在随机子样本中使用扰动估计谓词相关性；4) 在有向加权图中聚合袋级排序，并通过Local Reaching Centrality进行汇总。关键创新是阈值光谱重建技术，将谓词边界反向投影到原始光谱域的自然测量单位中，实现与测量光谱的直接视觉比较。

Result: 在八个真实光谱数据集（六个X射线荧光XRF数据集和两个伽马射线能谱数据集）和一个已知基准的合成数据集上进行了评估验证，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SMX框架成功填补了光谱数据可解释性的空白，通过区域级解释提供了化学意义明确的模型洞察，阈值光谱重建技术使得解释结果可以直接与原始光谱进行视觉对比，为化学计量学和光谱学领域的模型解释提供了专门化解决方案。

Abstract: Spectral-based machine learning models have been increasingly deployed in chemometrics and spectroscopy, where predictive accuracy is as important as explainability. Current employed eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) methods are largely adapted from tabular or generic multivariate domains, assigning relevance to isolated spectral variables rather than to the chemically meaningful spectral zones. Widely adopted tools such as SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), Permutation Feature Importance (PFI), and Variable Importance in Projection scores (VIP) were not designed for the physical continuity and high collinearity of spectral data, and their variable-level outputs require post-hoc aggregation to recover zone-level information. This study introduces the Spectral Model eXplainer (SMX), a post-hoc, global, model-agnostic XAI framework that explains spectral classifiers through expert-informed spectral zones. SMX summarizes each zone via PCA, defines quantile-based logical predicates, estimates predicate relevance with perturbation in stochastic subsamples, and aggregates bag-wise rankings in a directed weighted graph summarized by Local Reaching Centrality. A key component is threshold spectrum reconstruction, which back-projects predicate boundaries to the original spectral domain in natural measurement units, enabling direct visual comparison with measured spectra. The method was evaluated on eight real spectral datasets (six based on X-ray Fluorescence--XRF and two based on Gamma-ray Spectrometry) and one synthetic benchmark with known gr

</details>


### [251] [MSMixer: Learned Multi-Scale Temporal Mixing with Complementary Linear Shortcut for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02689)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MSMixer，一种轻量级多尺度MLP架构，通过三分支结构、可学习门控和DLinear短连接实现多尺度建模，在ETT基准上以112K参数量达到轻量级模型最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测需同时捕捉快速振荡、中等周期和缓慢宏观趋势，但现有轻量级MLP模型通常在单一时间分辨率上操作，限制了其显式建模多尺度模式的能力。

Method: MSMixer采用三项互补设计：(i)三个并行尺度分支（下采样因子{1x, 4x, 16x}）配独立MLP块；(ii)可学习softmax门控动态加权各分支输出；(iii)DLinear互补短连接提供全窗口趋势和季节性上下文。模型仅含112K参数，复杂度为O(T)。

Result: 在四个ETT基准的标准时序划分下，MSMixer在轻量级模型中达到最低平均MSE（0.357），优于DLinear（0.386, -7.4%）和NLinear（0.365, -2.1%），16个配置中赢得12个。与五个Transformer基线相比，16个配置中9个取得最佳或次佳MSE，参数量仅为PatchTST的1/5。消融实验证实了多尺度分支和DLinear短连接的互补贡献。

Conclusion: MSMixer通过显式多尺度建模和动态门控机制，在保持轻量级（112K参数）和线性复杂度的同时，有效提升了长期时间序列预测性能，为轻量级模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting requires models that simultaneously capture rapid oscillations, medium-range periodicities, and slowly evolving macro-trends from a fixed look-back window. Existing lightweight MLP-based models typically operate on a single temporal resolution, limiting their ability to explicitly model patterns at multiple scales. We propose MSMixer, a channel-independent multi-scale MLP architecture that addresses this limitation through three complementary innovations: (i) three parallel scale branches at down-sample factors {1x, 4x, 16x} with independent MLP blocks, (ii) a learnable softmax gate that dynamically weighs branch outputs, and (iii) a DLinear complementary shortcut that provides full-window trend and seasonality context. MSMixer contains only 112K parameters at H=96 and runs at O(T) complexity. Evaluated on four ETT benchmarks with standard chronological splits and three random seeds, MSMixer achieves the lowest average MSE (0.357) among lightweight models, outperforming DLinear (0.386, -7.4%) and NLinear (0.365, -2.1%), winning 12 of 16 configurations. Against five Transformer-based baselines from the literature, MSMixer achieves best or second-best MSE in 9 of 16 configurations while using 5x fewer parameters than PatchTST. Ablation and sensitivity analyses confirm the complementary contributions of the multi-scale branches and the DLinear shortcut.

</details>


### [252] [Visual Latents Know More Than They Say: Unsilencing Latent Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02735)
*Xin Zhang,Qiqi Tao,Jiawei Du,Moyun Liu,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了现有潜在视觉推理方法中的“视觉潜在变量被抑制”问题，提出了一种无需更新模型参数的推理时优化方法（包含对比对齐和置信度递增奖励两个阶段），有效释放了视觉潜在变量的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续潜在空间推理方法存在一种未被发现的优化病态：尽管视觉潜在变量在训练中获得了语义信息，但其在最终答案预测中的贡献被系统性地抑制。这是因为自回归目标倾向于依赖直接的视觉输入捷径，导致潜在token退化为过渡状态而非有效的推理内容。

Method: 提出一种推理时的潜在推理优化方法，保持主干网络参数冻结。第一阶段：通过查询引导的对比潜在-视觉对齐预热视觉潜在变量，提升语义质量并防止潜在变量坍缩；第二阶段：利用置信度递增奖励进一步优化潜在推理，激励潜在跨度上的预测token分布逐渐集中，使预测路径经过潜在推理而非绕过它。

Result: 在八个基准测试和四种模型骨干网络上的实验表明，该方法无需任何参数更新即可有效释放被抑制的视觉潜在变量推理能力。

Conclusion: 解耦冲突目标并直接在推理时优化潜在推理是一种有效策略，能够解决视觉潜在变量被抑制的问题，显著提升多模态模型的潜在视觉推理性能。

Abstract: Continuous latent-space reasoning offers a compact alternative to textual chain-of-thought for multimodal models, enabling high-dimensional visual evidence to be integrated without explicit reasoning tokens. However, we identify a previously overlooked optimization pathology in existing latent visual reasoning methods: although visual latents become semantically enriched during training, their contribution to final answer prediction is systematically suppressed. Within the shared parameter space, the autoregressive objective favors shortcut reliance on direct visual input, driving latent tokens toward transition-like states rather than informative reasoning content. We term this phenomenon Silenced Visual Latents. To address it, we disentangle the two conflicting objectives by directly optimizing the latent reasoning at inference time, keeping backbone parameters frozen. In Stage I, visual latents are warmed up via query-guided contrastive latent--visual alignment, improving semantic quality while preventing latent collapse. In Stage II, the latent reasoning is further optimized via a confidence-progression reward, which incentivizes predicted token distributions along the latent span to become progressively more concentrated, routing predictions through the latent reasoning rather than bypassing it. Experiments across eight benchmarks and four model backbones show that inference-time latent optimization, without any parameter updates, effectively unleashes the suppressed reasoning capacity of visual latents.

</details>


### [253] [Bolek: A Multimodal Language Model for Molecular Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02745)
