Conclusion: 研究表明，无需昂贵的模型重训练，即可通过事后纠错策略大幅提升安全关键型应用的可靠性。该方法在保障低延迟的同时，有效降低了高风险错误，为构建可信赖的AI系统提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of machine learning in critical applications demands techniques to mitigate high-consequence errors. Our method utilizes a dual-classifier GBDT pipeline to distinguish routine human-like errors from high-risk non-human misclassifications. Evaluated across three domains, animal breed classification, skin lesion diagnosis (ISIC 2018), and prostate histopathology (SICAPv2), our framework demonstrates robust safety improvements. To address real-world deployment concerns, our results confirm the pipeline introduces negligible inference latency (1.60% overhead for the animal dataset, 1.84% for ISIC, and 1.70% for SICAPv2) while outperforming traditional Maximum Class Probability (MCP) baselines in correction precision. Our conservative correction strategy successfully reduced dangerous non-human errors by 34.1% in ISIC and 12.57% in SICAPv2, improving super-class diagnostic safety to 90.41% and 92.13% respectively. This proves that safety-critical reliability can be substantially enhanced post-hoc without expensive model retraining.
  keywords: Error Analysis, Post-hoc Correction, Trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [360] [Strategy-Aware Optimization Modeling with Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02545)
*Ruiqing Zhao,Fengzhi Li,Yuan Zuo,Rui Liu,Yansong Liu,Yunfei Ma,Fanyu Meng,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过显式建模策略显著提升大语言模型在优化问题上的表现，平均pass@1提升至80.3，并生成更紧凑高效的约束系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能生成语法正确的优化程序，但在选择有效建模策略方面存在困难，导致错误的公式化和求解器效率低下。核心问题在于建模策略的隐式性，需要将其显式化以改善自动化优化建模。

Method: SAGE框架包含两个关键组件：(1)构建求解器验证的多策略数据集；(2)采用监督微调后接Segment-Weighted GRPO的训练方法，使用包含格式合规性、正确性和求解器效率的复合奖励函数。框架在数据构建和后训练阶段都显式处理建模策略。

Result: 在8个基准测试中，SAGE将平均pass@1从最强开源基线的72.7提升至80.3。在多次生成场景下，pass@16时发现更多不同的正确公式化方案，组件级多样性提升19-29%。在大规模设置中，SAGE生成的约束系统比基线紧凑14.2%，符合求解器高效建模特征。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将建模策略显式化能有效改善自动化优化建模性能。SAGE框架不仅提升了准确性，还增强了生成方案的多样性和求解效率，为LLM在优化领域的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically valid optimization programs, yet often struggle to reliably choose an effective modeling strategy, leading to incorrect formulations and inefficient solver behavior. We propose SAGE, a strategy-aware framework that makes Modeling Strategy explicit in both data construction and post-training. SAGE builds a solver-verified multi-strategy dataset and trains a student model with supervised fine-tuning followed by Segment-Weighted GRPO using a composite reward over format compliance, correctness, and solver efficiency. Across eight benchmarks spanning synthetic and real-world settings, SAGE improves average pass@1 from 72.7 to 80.3 over the strongest open-source baseline. With multiple generations, SAGE discovers more distinct correct formulations and improves component-level diversity at pass@16 by 19-29%. At the largest scale, SAGE produces more compact constraint systems with 14.2% fewer constraints than the baseline, consistent with solver-efficient modeling. Overall, these results show that making Modeling Strategy explicit improves automated optimization modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/rachhhhing/SAGE.

</details>


### [361] [Double Rectified Linear Unit-based Modular Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02551)
*Gianvincenzo Alfano,Sergio Greco,Lucio La Cava,Francesco Parisi,Irina Trubitsyna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对QBAFs提出了新的渐进语义，解决了现有方法在简单无环情形下产生分歧或反直觉结果的问题，并证明了其在更广泛的循环框架中具有收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的QBAFs语义在计算论证可接受性时，即使在简单的无环情况下也经常产生分歧或违反直觉的结果，缺乏合理且收敛性良好的计算方法。

Method: 提出了新颖的渐进语义来计算论证的可接受性，该语义考虑攻击者和支持者的影响来更新论证强度，并研究了其收敛行为。

Result: 新语义产生的结果更符合直觉期望，满足文献中已建立的理性公理，且不仅在无环QBAFs中收敛，在更广泛的循环框架类中也证明了其收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了能够克服现有方法局限性的新型渐进语义，为QBAFs提供了更合理且具有理论保证的计算方法，扩展了可处理框架的范围。

Abstract: Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs) provide an alternative approach to computing argument acceptability in Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (BAFs). Each argument is assigned an initial strength, which is then updated to a final strength by considering the influence of both its attackers and supporters. Over the years, several semantics have been proposed to compute argument acceptability in QBAFs, yet they often yield divergent or counterintuitive results, even for simple acyclic cases. We introduce novel gradual semantics for QBAFs that address these limitations, producing results that align more closely with intuitive expectations, while satisfying established rationality postulates from the literature. Furthermore, we study its convergence behavior, proving that it converges not only for acyclic QBAFs but also for broader classes of cyclic frameworks.

</details>


### [362] [On Training Large Language Models for Long-Horizon Tasks: An Empirical Study of Horizon Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02572)
*Sunghwan Kim,Junhee Cho,Beong-woo Kwak,Taeyoon Kwon,Liang Wang,Nan Yang,Xingxing Zhang,Furu Wei,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验发现，任务视野长度是LLM智能体训练的关键瓶颈，长视野会导致严重的训练不稳定性；通过视野缩减策略可以稳定训练，并使模型获得向更长视野泛化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注系统级优化或算法改进，而任务视野长度对LLM智能体训练动态的影响尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一认知空白。

Method: 构建受控任务进行系统性实证研究，使智能体面对相同的决策规则和推理结构，仅在完成任务所需的动作序列长度上存在差异，从而隔离并研究视野长度的影响。

Result: 增加视野长度会构成训练瓶颈，引发由探索困难和信用分配挑战导致的严重训练不稳定性；视野缩减能有效稳定训练并提升长视野任务性能；在缩减视野下训练的模型能更有效地泛化到更长视野变体（视野泛化）。

Conclusion: 视野长度是影响LLM智能体训练稳定性的关键因素，视野缩减是解决长视野任务训练难题的重要原则，不仅改善训练表现，还能增强模型对不同视野长度的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as interactive agents that solve tasks through extended sequences of environment interactions. While prior work has primarily focused on system-level optimizations or algorithmic improvements, the role of task horizon length in shaping training dynamics remains poorly understood. In this work, we present a systematic empirical study that examines horizon length through controlled task constructions. Specifically, we construct controlled tasks in which agents face identical decision rules and reasoning structures, but differ only in the length of action sequences required for successful completion. Our results reveal that increasing horizon length alone constitutes a training bottleneck, inducing severe training instability driven by exploration difficulties and credit assignment challenges. We demonstrate that horizon reduction is a key principle to address this limitation, stabilizing training and achieving better performance in long-horizon tasks. Moreover, we find that horizon reduction is related to stronger generalization across horizon lengths: models trained under reduced horizons generalize more effectively to longer-horizon variants at inference time, a phenomenon we refer to as horizon generalization.

</details>


### [363] [Universal Smoothness via Bernstein Polynomials: A Constructive Approximation Approach for Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02591)
*Wentao Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Wentao Mo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Bernstein多项式的Bernstein线性单元（BerLU）激活函数，通过构建可微的二次过渡区域消除了分段线性函数的不可微奇点，在保证优化稳定性和计算效率的同时，在图像分类任务上超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活函数难以在优化稳定性和计算效率之间取得平衡：分段线性函数（如ReLU）虽推理速度快，但在原点处不可微导致优化不稳定；平滑函数虽可微，但依赖超越函数运算带来显著计算开销。

Method: 基于构造逼近理论提出通用平滑框架，利用Bernstein多项式构建Bernstein线性单元（BerLU）。该激活函数构造了一个可微的二次过渡区域，有效消除奇点同时保持分段线性结构，保证严格连续可微性和非扩张性Lipschitz常数为1。

Result: 理论分析证明了该方法保证严格连续可微性和非扩张性Lipschitz常数为1，确保稳定的梯度传播并防止深度架构中的梯度爆炸问题。在代表性Vision Transformer和卷积神经网络架构上的综合实验表明，该方法在标准图像分类基准上持续超越最先进基线，同时提供卓越的计算和内存效率。

Conclusion: BerLU成功解决了激活函数设计中优化稳定性与计算效率的权衡问题，通过Bernstein多项式的平滑过渡实现了理论上的可微性保证和实践中的性能提升，为深度神经网络激活函数设计提供了新的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: The efficacy of deep neural networks is heavily reliant on the design of non-linear activation functions, yet existing approaches often struggle to balance optimization stability with computational efficiency. While piecewise linear functions offer inference speed, they suffer from optimization instability due to non-differentiability at the origin, whereas smooth counterparts typically incur significant computational overhead through their reliance on transcendental operations. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a general smoothing framework based on constructive approximation theory and introduces the Bernstein Linear Unit (BerLU). This novel activation function utilizes Bernstein polynomials to construct a differentiable quadratic transition region that effectively eliminates singularities while maintaining a piecewise linear structure. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the proposed method guarantees strictly continuous differentiability and a non-expansive Lipschitz constant of one, which ensures stable gradient propagation and prevents the gradient explosion problems common in deep architectures. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across representative Vision Transformer and Convolutional Neural Network architectures confirm that this approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard image classification benchmarks while delivering superior computational and memory efficiency.

</details>


### [364] [Foundation-Model-Based Agents in Industrial Automation: Purposes, Capabilities, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02592)
*Vincent Henkel,Felix Gehlhoff,David Kube,Asaad Almutareb,Luis Cruz,Bernd Hellingrath,Philip Koch,Christoph Legat,Florian Mohr,Michael Oberle,Felix Ocker,Thorsten Schoeler,Mario Thron,Nico Andre Töpfer,Lucas Vogt,Yuchen Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述（88篇论文）分析了基于基础模型的工业智能体现状：75%处于原型或早期验证阶段，仅9.1%面向部署；相比传统工业智能体，在人机交互（+37%）和不确定性处理（+35%）方面显著提升，但在谈判能力上存在明显不足（-39%）；主要局限包括泛化能力不足、幻觉、数据稀缺和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（尤其是大语言模型）正越来越多地集成到用于工业任务（如决策支持、过程监控和工程自动化）的智能体架构中。然而，关于其目的、能力和局限性的证据在不同领域间仍然零散，缺乏系统性认知，因此需要全面评估其在工业语境中的成熟度、功能特征差异及现有局限。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020指南进行系统性文献综述，初步筛选2,341篇出版物，通过结构化编码方案最终合成88篇出版物作为研究语料库，并与既有的工业智能体系统基线进行对比分析。

Result: （1）成熟度：报告的系统主要处于原型和早期验证阶段（75.0%处于TRL 4-6），面向部署的证据仍然罕见（9.1%）。（2）功能定位：运营目标最常定位于用户辅助、监控和过程优化，而规划和调度等传统生产控制目的则不那么突出。（3）能力对比：相比工业智能体系统的既定基线，能力概况显示在人机交互（+37%）和处理不确定性（+35%）方面有显著增益，但在谈判方面有明显缺陷（-39%）。（4）局限：报告最广泛的局限涉及缺乏泛化能力、幻觉和输出不稳定性、数据稀缺以及推理延迟。

Conclusion: 基于基础模型的工业智能体尚处于早期发展阶段，在人机交互和不确定性处理方面展现出独特优势，但在谈判能力和实际部署成熟度上存在明显短板。本研究提出了基于基础模型的工业智能体的工作定义，弥合了传统智能体理论、自动化工程标准和基础模型范式之间的差距，为未来研究和应用提供了系统性参考。

Abstract: Foundation models, particularly large language models, are increasingly integrated into agent architectures for industrial tasks such as decision support, process monitoring, and engineering automation. Yet evidence on their purposes, capabilities, and limitations remains fragmented across domains. This work examines how mature foundation-model-based agent systems are in industrial contexts, how their functional profile differs from conventional agent systems, and which limitations persist. A systematic literature survey following the PRISMA 2020 guideline is presented, screening 2,341 publications and synthesising a corpus of 88 publications through a structured coding scheme. The results show that reported systems are predominantly at prototype and early validation stages (75.0% at TRL 4-6), with deployment-oriented evidence remaining rare (9.1%). Operational goals are most frequently positioned in user assistance, monitoring, and process optimisation, while conventional production-control purposes such as planning and scheduling are less prominent. Compared with an established baseline for industrial agent systems, the capability profile reveals substantial gains in human interaction (+37%) and dealing with uncertainty (+35%), but a pronounced deficit in negotiation (-39%). The most widely reported limitations concern lack of generalization, hallucination and output instability, data scarcity, and inference latency. A working definition of foundation-model-based industrial agents is also proposed, bridging conventional agent theory, automation-engineering standards, and the foundation-model paradigm.

</details>


### [365] [Counterfactual Reasoning in Automated Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02603)
*Alberto Pozanco,Daniel Borrajo,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于自动规划中反事实推理的综述论文，对现有工作进行了分类总结并指出了未来的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动规划假设所有任务要素（初始状态、目标和可用动作）都是预先完全指定的，但现实世界的规划往往需要灵活性，以应对意外情况或改善结果，这促使研究者探索反事实推理在自动规划中的应用。

Method: 对自动规划中反事实推理的现有工作进行调研，根据改变的要素、推理触发的时机以及改变的原因和方式进行分类。

Result: 对现有工作进行了系统性的分类总结，并讨论了关键发现。

Conclusion: 概述了该领域尚未解决的开放性研究问题，为未来的研究工作提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Automated planning traditionally assumes that all aspects of a planning task (initial state, goals, and available actions) are fully specified in advance, an approach well-suited to domains with fixed rules and deterministic execution. However, real-world planning often requires flexibility, allowing for deviations from the original task parameters in response to unforeseen circumstances or to improve outcomes. This paper surveys existing works on counterfactual reasoning in automated planning, categorizing them by what elements are changed, when the reasoning is triggered, and why and how these changes are made. We conclude by discussing key findings and outlining open research questions to guide future work in this area.

</details>


### [366] [SCGNN: Semantic Consistency enhanced Graph Neural Network Guided by Granular-ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02617)
*Genhao Tian,Taihua Xu,Shuyin Xia,Qinghua Zhang,Jie Yang,Jianjun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCGNN，一个即插即用的框架，利用粒球计算高效捕获语义一致性，解决了现有方法计算复杂度高和邻居选择僵化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表示学习方法通常依赖于k近邻(kNN)或其他节点级全搜索算法(FSA)来挖掘语义关系，这些方法通过穷举成对相似度计算来工作，存在计算复杂度高和邻居选择僵化的问题，限制了可扩展性并引入了噪声连接。

Method: 提出了语义一致性增强图神经网络(SCGNN)，利用粒球计算(GBC)自适应地将节点划分成粒球，建模组级语义结构。设计了双重增强策略：(1)结构增强模块构建基于锚点的图结构，每个锚点是代表粒球所承载的组级语义的虚拟节点；(2)监督增强模块通过结合GBC预测和模型生成的伪标签执行标签一致性检查(LCC)，产生更可靠的监督信号。SCGNN与前向传播中联合编码原始图和增强图并融合预测，反向传播中提供增强监督信号。

Result: SCGNN显著降低了计算成本，同时对噪声具有更好的鲁棒性，并且兼容各种GNN骨干网络。

Conclusion: SCGNN通过粒球计算有效捕获组级语义一致性，为图神经网络提供了一个高效、可扩展且鲁棒的即插即用框架，能够改善图表示学习的效果。

Abstract: Capturing semantic consistency among nodes is crucial for effective graph representation learning. Existing approaches typically rely on $k$-nearest neighbors ($k$NN) or other node-level full search algorithms (FSA) to mine semantic relationships via exhaustive pairwise similarity computation, which suffer from high computational complexity and rigid neighbor selection, limiting scalability and introducing noisy connections. In this paper, we propose the Semantic Consistency enhanced Graph Neural Network (SCGNN), a novel plug-and-play framework that leverages granular-ball computing (GBC) to efficiently capture semantic consistency in a scalable manner. Unlike node-level FSA methods, SCGNN models group-level semantic structure by adaptively partitioning nodes into granular balls, significantly reducing computational cost while improving robustness to noise. To effectively utilize the discovered group-level semantic consistency, we design a dual enhancement strategy. Specifically, (1) a structure enhancement module constructs an anchor-based graph structure, where each anchor is a virtual node representing the group-level semantic carried by a granular ball, then injecting group-level semantic information into the graph structure; and (2) a supervision enhancement module performs label consistency checking (LCC) by combining GBC predictions with model-generated pseudo-labels, thereby producing more reliable supervision signals. SCGNN is compatible with various GNN backbones. During the forward propagation of SCGNN, the vanilla graph and the augment graph are jointly encoded, and their predictions are fused; during the backpropagation, the supervision enhancement module provides enhanced supervision signals to guide parameter updates.

</details>


### [367] [Trustworthy AI Suffers from Invariance Conflicts and Causality is The Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02640)
*Ruta Binkyte,Ivaxi Sheth,Zhijing Jin,Mohammad Havaei,Bernhard Schölkopf,Mario Fritz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张利用因果性作为统一框架来理解并解决可信AI中公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和可解释性等多目标之间的权衡问题，提出通过选择性不变性来缓解或解决这些权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在高风险领域的广泛应用，确保其可信度成为核心挑战。然而，公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和可解释性等可信AI目标难以同时实现，且往往需要牺牲效用，亟需一种统一框架来理解和平衡这些权衡。

Method: 将可信AI中的权衡重新解释为数据生成过程在不同变化下的不兼容不变性要求，利用因果性框架分析这些权衡的产生机制，并提出通过选择性不变性来软化或解决权衡问题。该方法适用于经典机器学习模型和大规模基础模型，并探讨了因果假设在这些系统中的显式或隐式应用。

Result: 因果性提供了一个统一框架，能够揭示可信AI多目标权衡的本质来源，并通过选择性不变性为权衡的软化或解决提供理论依据。该视角对经典ML模型和大规模FMs均适用。

Conclusion: 因果性是构建更可信AI的关键理论基础，未来应在现代大规模AI系统中更广泛地应用因果假设和方法，以更好地平衡性能与多目标可信性，同时存在开放性挑战和机遇。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI), including machine learning (ML) models and foundation models (FMs), is increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a central challenge. However, the core trustworthy AI objectives, such as fairness, robustness, privacy, and explainability, are hard to achieve simultaneously, especially while preserving utility. This position paper argues that causality is necessary to understand and balance trade-offs in performance and multiple objectives of trustworthy AI. We ground our arguments in re-interpreting trustworthy AI trade-offs as incompatible invariance requirements under different changes to the data-generating process. We then illustrate that causality provides a unifying framework for understanding how trade-offs in trustworthy AI arise, and how they can be softened or resolved through selective invariance. This perspective applies to both classical ML models and large-scale FMs. Our paper discusses how causal assumptions may be applied explicitly or implicitly in modern large-scale systems. Finally, we outline open challenges and opportunities for using causality to build more trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [368] [Deciphering Shortcut Learning from an Evolutionary Game Theory Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02658)
*Xiayang Li,Kuo Gai,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用演化博弈论揭示了优化算法（GD与SGD）导致捷径学习的不同机制，为理解和缓解该问题提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型中普遍存在依赖非本质特征的捷径学习现象，但其形成机制尚缺乏理论解释。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先形式化定义了核心特征与捷径特征；随后采用演化博弈论，将数据样本建模为博弈参与者，神经正切特征建模为策略，假设网络中存在核心子网络与捷径子网络，分析了梯度下降（GD）与随机梯度下降（SGD）下的动态演化过程。

Result: 发现GD与SGD导向两种不同的随机稳定状态：前者主要优化捷径子网络，后者主要优化核心子网络；通过连续随机微分方程进一步揭示了数据噪声和优化噪声对捷径偏差形成的影响。

Conclusion: 本文通过演化博弈论成功刻画了捷径偏差形成的动态过程，区分了不同优化算法的行为，为缓解捷径学习提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Shortcut learning causes deep learning models to rely on non-essential features within the data. However, its formation in deep neural network training still lacks theoretical understanding. In this paper, we provide a formal definition of core and shortcut features and employ evolutionary game theory to analyze the origins of shortcut bias by modeling data samples as players and their corresponding neural tangent features as strategies, assuming the existence of core and shortcut subnetworks. We find that gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD) lead to two distinct stochastically stable states, each corresponding to a different strategy. The former primarily optimizes the shortcut subnetwork, while the latter primarily optimizes the core subnetwork. We investigate the influence of these strategies on shortcut bias through a continuous stochastic differential equation, and reveal the impact of data noise and optimization noise on the formation of shortcut bias. In brief, our work employs evolutionary game theory to characterize the dynamics of shortcut bias formation and provides a theoretical view on its mitigation.

</details>


### [369] [AcademiClaw: When Students Set Challenges for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02661)
*Junjie Yu,Pengrui Lu,Weiye Si,Hongliang Lu,Jiabao Wu,Kaiwen Tao,Kun Wang,Lingyu Yang,Qiran Zhang,Xiuting Guo,Xuanyu Wang,Yang Wang,Yanjie Wang,Yi Yang,Zijian Hu,Ziyi Yang,Zonghan Zhou,Binghao Qiang,Borui Zhang,Chenning Li,Enchang Zhang,Feifan Chen,Feng Jian,Fengyin Sun,Hao Qiu,Hao Zheng,Haoran Zhu,Hongyu Liu,Jianbin Deng,Jiaxin Song,Jiaying Chi,Jiayou Shi,Jie Fang,Jinghui Zhong,Jingyu Zhou,Jinze Li,Junfeng Yi,Junyan Yu,Junzhi Xue,Ni Song,Pengyi Chen,Qi Chen,Quansheng Li,Rui Tao,Shenghai Gong,Shenhang Lu,Tianqi Shen,Tianxiang Zhu,Tiehan Kang,Tingyu Li,Wendi Wu,Xiao Shen,Xiao Zhou,Xiaotao Zhang,Xinrong Li,Xuankun Yang,Xun Zhang,Yan Li,Ye Lu,Yi Wang,Yibo Zhou,Yichi Zhang,Yihao Sun,Yijun Huang,Yixin Zhu,Yixuan Wu,Yuchen Sun,Yue Wu,Yuheng Sun,Yukun Li,Yutian Tu,Yuxuan Qin,Yuzhuo Wu,Zeyu Li,Zhengyu Lou,Zhenning Ran,Zizhu He,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了AcademiClaw，首个评估AI学术级能力的中英双语基准，包含80个源自大学生真实学术工作流的复杂长周期任务。实验显示前沿模型最高通过率仅55%，揭示了模型在不同任务领域的能力边界、策略差异及token消耗与输出质量不匹配等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw生态系统中的现有基准仅评估助手级任务，缺乏对学术级能力的考察。为填补这一空白，作者构建了直接源自大学生真实学术工作流（作业、研究项目、竞赛、个人项目）的复杂任务基准，以推动开发能够应对现实学术需求的高能力AI代理。

Method: 从230份学生提交的任务候选中经专家严格评审筛选出80个任务，覆盖25+专业领域（从奥数级数学、语言学到GPU强化学习和全栈系统调试），其中16个任务需CUDA GPU执行。每个任务在隔离的Docker沙箱中执行，采用六种互补技术构建的多维度评分标准进行评估，并辅以独立的五类安全审计提供行为分析。

Result: 在六个前沿模型上的实验表明，最佳模型通过率仅为55%。深入分析揭示了跨任务领域的显著能力边界、模型间差异化的行为策略，以及token消耗与输出质量之间的脱节，提供了比聚合指标更细粒度的诊断信号。

Conclusion: AcademiClaw及其开源数据与代码为OpenClaw社区提供了宝贵资源，有助于推动开发更强大、更全能的AI代理，以应对现实世界中广泛的学术需求。

Abstract: Benchmarks within the OpenClaw ecosystem have thus far evaluated exclusively assistant-level tasks, leaving the academic-level capabilities of OpenClaw largely unexamined. We introduce AcademiClaw, a bilingual benchmark of 80 complex, long-horizon tasks sourced directly from university students' real academic workflows -- homework, research projects, competitions, and personal projects -- that they found current AI agents unable to solve effectively. Curated from 230 student-submitted candidates through rigorous expert review, the final task set spans 25+ professional domains, ranging from olympiad-level mathematics and linguistics problems to GPU-intensive reinforcement learning and full-stack system debugging, with 16 tasks requiring CUDA GPU execution. Each task executes in an isolated Docker sandbox and is scored on task completion by multi-dimensional rubrics combining six complementary techniques, with an independent five-category safety audit providing additional behavioral analysis. Experiments on six frontier models show that even the best achieves only a 55\% pass rate. Further analysis uncovers sharp capability boundaries across task domains, divergent behavioral strategies among models, and a disconnect between token consumption and output quality, providing fine-grained diagnostic signals beyond what aggregate metrics reveal. We hope that AcademiClaw and its open-sourced data and code can serve as a useful resource for the OpenClaw community, driving progress toward agents that are more capable and versatile across the full breadth of real-world academic demands. All data and code are available at https://github.com/GAIR-NLP/AcademiClaw.

</details>


### [370] [An explainable hypothesis-driven approach to Drug-Induced Liver Injury with HADES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02669)
*Maciej Wisniewski,Bartosz Topolski,Pawel Dabrowski-Tumanski,Dariusz Plewczynski,Tomasz Jetka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出将药物性肝损伤（DILI）预测重构为可解释的假设生成问题，构建了包含机制性肝毒性假设的DILER基准数据集，并开发了HADES智能体系统。HADES在二分类任务上超越现有模型（ROC-AUC 0.68 vs 0.63），同时在机制假设生成任务上建立了基线（假设对齐Fuzzy Jaccard指数0.16），推动了预测毒理学的可解释性发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物性肝损伤（DILI）是导致临床试验后期失败的主要原因之一。然而，现有的计算预测器主要依赖二分类框架，这种方法限制了模型的泛化能力，且无法提供指导转化决策的机制性见解。因此，研究主张将DILI预测重新定义为一个可解释的假设生成问题。

Method: 研究构建了DILER基准数据集，在分子二元标签基础上增加了来自生物医学文献的机制性肝毒性假设。同时开发了HADES智能体系统，通过整合分子水平预测、代谢物分解、结构理解和毒性通路证据，生成透明且可审计的推理轨迹，从而对DILI风险进行机制性评估。

Result: 在DILER基准测试集上，HADES在二分类任务上表现优于现有模型，测试集ROC-AUC达到0.68（DILI-Predictor为0.63），Post-2021挑战集达到0.59（DILI-Predictor为0.50）。更重要的是，在机制假设生成任务上建立了基线，HADES的假设对齐Fuzzy Jaccard指数达到0.16。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了DILI机制假设生成任务的固有复杂性，同时凸显了在预测毒理学领域发展先进可解释方法的必要性，为药物安全性评估提供了新的研究方向和技术路径。

Abstract: Drug-induced liver injury (DILI) remains a leading cause of late-stage clinical trial attrition. However, existing computational predictors primarily rely on binary classification, a framing that limits generalization and yields no mechanistic insight to guide translational decisions. We argue that DILI prediction is better posed as an explainable hypothesis-generation problem.
  To support this shift, we introduce the DILER Benchmark, a dataset that extends beyond binary labels by augmenting a curated set of molecules with mechanistic hepatotoxicity hypotheses derived from biomedical literature. We further present HADES, an agentic system designed to generate transparent and auditable reasoning traces. By combining molecular-level predictions, metabolite decomposition, structural understanding, and toxicity pathway evidence, HADES mechanistically assesses DILI risk.
  Evaluated on the DILER Benchmark, HADES outperforms existing models in binary classification, achieving a ROC-AUC of 0.68 on the Test Set and 0.59 on the challenging Post-2021 Set, compared with 0.63 and 0.50 for DILI-Predictor, respectively. More importantly, we establish a baseline for mechanistic hypothesis generation, where HADES achieves a Hypothesis Alignment Fuzzy Jaccard Index of 0.16. This result underscores the inherent complexity of the task while highlighting the need for advanced explainable approaches in predictive toxicology.

</details>


### [371] [The 2026 ACII Dyadic Conversations (DaiKon) Workshop & Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02672)
*Panagiotis Tzirakis,Alice Baird,Jeffrey Brooks,Emilia Parada-Cabaleiro,Lukas Stappen,Sharath Rao,Theo Lebryk,Jakub Piotr Clapa,Jens Madsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACII-DaiKon发布了针对双人对话中人际情感与社会动态建模的基准，包含方向性影响预测、话轮转换预测和融洽度轨迹预测三个子任务，基于945段多语言自然对话数据。基线实验表明现有方法能捕捉粗粒度模式，但对方向性依赖和长时域人际动态的建模仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话情感建模基准主要以说话者为中心，低估了对话双方之间耦合的、随时间演化的过程，包括方向性影响、对话时机协调和融洽度发展等关键人际动态特性。

Method: 构建了基于Hume-DaiKon数据集的基准，包含945段双人对话（743.4小时音视频数据），覆盖五种语言的自然场景。设计了三个子挑战：(1)方向性人际影响预测、(2)话轮转换预测（下一说话者和下次发言时间）、(3)全交互过程的融洽度轨迹预测。采用固定训练/验证/测试划分、标准化评估指标（CCC、Pearson相关系数、Macro-F1、MAE）并发布基线系统。

Result: 基线实验建立了初始参考性能：影响预测最佳结果为CCC 0.40和Pearson 0.50；话轮转换预测为Macro-F1 0.66和MAE 1.50秒；融洽度轨迹建模为CCC 0.68和Pearson 0.70。结果表明当前方法能够捕捉粗粒度的双人模式，但对方向性依赖和长时域人际动态的鲁棒建模仍然困难。

Conclusion: 该基准为双人交互建模提供了严格的比较平台，促进了跨学科讨论，涵盖数据有效性、评估协议和文化感知建模等议题，推动了从说话者中心向双人交互动态建模的范式转变。

Abstract: The 2026 ACII Dyadic Conversations (ACII-DaiKon) Workshop & Challenge introduces a benchmark for modeling interpersonal affect and social dynamics in dyadic conversations. Although conversational affect modeling has advanced rapidly, most benchmarks remain speaker-centric and underrepresent coupled, time-evolving processes between partners, including directional influence, conversational timing coordination, and rapport development. To address this gap, ACII-DaiKon presents three coordinated sub-challenges built on a shared dataset: (1) directional interpersonal influence prediction, (2) turn-taking prediction (next-speaker and time-to-next-speech), and (3) rapport trajectory prediction across full interactions.
  The challenge is built on the Hume-DaiKon dataset, comprising 945 dyadic conversations (743.4 hours of audiovisual data) collected under naturalistic conditions across five languages. The benchmark supports multimodal modeling, temporal reasoning, and cross-context generalization through fixed train/validation/test splits, standardized metrics, and released baseline systems. Evaluation uses Concordance Correlation Coefficient (CCC), Pearson correlation, Macro-F1, and Mean Absolute Error (MAE) depending on the sub-challenge.
  Baseline experiments establish initial reference performance, with best test results of 0.40 CCC and 0.50 Pearson for influence prediction, 0.66 Macro-F1 and 1.50~s MAE for turn-taking, and 0.68 CCC and 0.70 Pearson for rapport trajectory modeling. These results indicate that while current methods capture coarse dyadic patterns, robust modeling of directional dependence and long-horizon interpersonal dynamics remains challenging. The workshop provides a shared platform for rigorous comparison and cross-disciplinary discussion on data validity, evaluation protocols, and culturally aware modeling for dyadic interaction.

</details>


### [372] [Hybrid Inspection and Task-Based Access Control in Zero-Trust Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02682)
*Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Chiara Troiani,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型智能体在动态工具调用和资源访问中的安全风险，提出了连续智能体语义授权（CASA）框架，通过混合运行时强制模型（确定性控制+语义检测）在零信任拦截层实现对多轮对话的细粒度授权控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 授权LLM驱动的智能体动态调用工具和访问受保护资源会引入重大安全风险。当智能体进行多轮对话并扩展到分布式协作时，风险急剧增加。受损或恶意的智能体应用可能篡改工具调用、伪造结果或请求超出主体预期任务范围的权限，而当前委托授权流程缺乏对主体原始意图的可见性，无法有效检测此类威胁。

Method: 提出混合运行时强制模型，结合确定性控制和语义控制：1）零信任拦截层实施五项确定性控制，强制消息流的结构和数据完整性保证；2）语义检测层评估工具调用选择是否与智能体被委托的预期任务一致。将语义层分为两阶段：任务提取步骤从多轮对话中提炼主体目标，任务-工具语义匹配步骤评估请求的工具是否适用于提取的任务。

Result: 扩展了ASTRA数据集，生成了包含多轮交互的新型对话-工具数据集，其中包含与给定任务相关和不相关的工具调用。提供了多轮对话环境下任务访问控制（TBAC）的首个实验结果。

Conclusion: CASA框架通过混合控制模型实现了对多轮对话智能体的细粒度授权控制，解决了传统TBAC技术仅适用于单轮交互的局限性，为LLM智能体的安全授权提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Authorizing Large Language Model (LLM)-driven agents to dynamically invoke tools and access protected resources introduces significant security risks, and the risks grow dramatically as agents engage in multi-turn conversations and scale toward distributed collaboration. A compromised or malicious agentic application can tamper with tool calls, falsify results, or request permissions beyond the scope of the subject's intended tasks, which could go unnoticed with current delegated authorization flows given their lack of visibility into the original subject's intent. In light of this, we make the following contributions towards Continuous Agent Semantic Authorization (CASA). First, we propose a hybrid runtime enforcement model that combines deterministic and semantic controls enabled by a zero-trust interception layer. Five deterministic controls enforce structural and data-integrity guarantees over the message flow, while a semantic inspection layer evaluates whether tool call choices align with the intended tasks commissioned to the agent. Second, differently from prior Task-Based Access Control (TBAC) techniques that operate on single-turn interactions, we decompose the semantic layer into two stages: i) a task-extraction step that distills the subject's objectives from multi-turn conversations at the interception layer, and ii) a task-tool semantic matching step at the authorization server that evaluates whether the requested tools are appropriate for the extracted tasks. Third, we extend the ASTRA dataset that we introduced in a prior work, by generating novel conversation-tool datasets with multi-turn interactions containing relevant and irrelevant tool calls for a given task. Lastly, we provide the first experimental results for TBAC under multi-turn conversations.

</details>


### [373] [An Empirical Study of Agent Skills for Healthcare: Practice, Gaps, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02709)
*Gelei Xu,Ningzhi Tang,Xueyang Li,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Zhi Zheng,Wei Jin,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对医疗AI代理技能进行了首次实证分析，发现公开技能侧重患者工作流自动化而非诊疗任务，生命周期覆盖不均，且技术风险不能代表临床风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗自动化受本地流程和组织约束影响，代理能力难以跨场景迁移。作为新兴的程序层，代理技能（Agent skills）旨在打包可复用流程以适应不同医疗场景，但目前缺乏对该技能生态的实证理解，亟需评估其在功能、部署、自主性和安全性方面的现状。

Method: 研究从ClawHub平台的58,159个公开技能中筛选出557个医疗相关技能，并按照功能、部署环境、自主性和安全性等十个维度进行人工标注，对这些技能进行了系统的实证分析。

Result: 分析发现：1）公开医疗技能主要关注面向患者的工作流自动化和监测，而非研究关注的诊断和治疗任务；2）对医疗生命周期和专业化临床输入的覆盖不均匀；3）通用技术风险评估无法可靠反映临床风险。这些发现揭示了当前基准测试和风险框架尚未覆盖医疗技能这一程序层。

Conclusion: 医疗代理技能作为一个新兴的程序层，目前的公开技能生态与研究重点存在错位，且缺乏针对临床风险的评估框架。这为未来研究指明了方向，需要开发新的基准测试和风险框架来规范和评估医疗代理技能的发展。

Abstract: Healthcare automation is shaped by local procedures and organizational constraints, so agent capabilities rarely transfer unchanged across settings. Agent skills, self-contained directories that package reusable procedures for AI agents, are emerging as a procedural layer for adapting healthcare agents across diverse healthcare settings. We present the first empirical analysis of healthcare agent skills, drawing on 557 healthcare-related skills filtered from 58,159 public skills on ClawHub and annotated along ten dimensions covering function, deployment context, autonomy, and safety. We find that public healthcare skills emphasize patient-facing workflow automation and monitoring rather than the diagnostic and treatment-oriented tasks foregrounded in healthcare-agent research; coverage of the healthcare lifecycle and specialized clinical inputs remains uneven; and general technical risk does not reliably capture clinical risk. These findings position healthcare skills as a procedural layer not yet addressed by current benchmarks and risk frameworks.

</details>


### [374] [ORPilot: A Production-Oriented Agentic LLM-for-OR Tool for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02728)
*Guangrui Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ORPilot是一个开源智能AI系统，能够将真实商业问题转化为求解器就绪的优化模型，在IndustryOR基准上准确率超越SOTA工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术LLM-for-OR工具假设问题规范清晰且数据已预格式化，无法满足生产环境需求（模糊描述、大规模原始数据、求解器后端可移植性），因此需要面向生产级商业问题的优化建模系统。

Method: 提出四个核心组件：(1)对话访谈代理获取完整问题规范；(2)数据收集代理独立于提示检索数据；(3)参数计算代理将原始表格数据转化为模型就绪参数；(4)求解器无关的中间表示(IR)实现向Gurobi、CPLEX、PuLP、Pyomo、OR-Tools等求解器的确定性零LLM调用重编译；结合自纠正重试循环利用求解器回溯信息进行定向修复。

Result: 在真实世界问题上表现优异；在传统学术基准测试中，ORPilot在IndustryOR上准确率超越最先进工具，在NL4OPT和NLP4LP上达到可比性能。

Conclusion: ORPilot是首个面向生产级商业问题而非教科书式运筹学案例的尝试，验证了端到端优化建模系统在实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents ORPilot, an open-source agentic AI system that translates real-world business problems into solver-ready optimization models. Unlike academic LLM-for-OR tools that assume clean problem specifications with preformatted inline data, ORPilot is designed for production conditions: ambiguous descriptions, large-scale raw operational data, and the need for portability across solver backends. The system introduces four novel components: (1) a conversational interview agent to elicit complete problem specifications, (2) a data collection agent that retrieves data independently of prompts, (3) a parameter computation agent to bridge raw tabular data and model-ready parameters, and (4) a solver-agnostic Intermediate Representation (IR) for deterministic, zero-LLM-call recompilation to Gurobi, CPLEX, PuLP, Pyomo, or OR-Tools solvers. Additionally, self-correcting retry loops utilize solver tracebacks for targeted repairs. ORPilot represents the first attempt to target production-level business problems rather than textbook operations research (OR) cases. Evaluation on real-world problems demonstrates promising results. When tested against traditional academic benchmarks: IndustryOR, NL4OPT and NLP4LP, ORPilot outperformed state-of-the-art tools in accuracy on the IndustryOR benchmark and delivered comparable performance on NL4OPT and NLP4LP.

</details>


### [375] [Coherent Hierarchical Multi-Label Learning to Defer for Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02734)
*Joshua Strong,Pramit Saha,Emma Sun,Helen Higham,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次研究层次多标签场景下的“学习推迟”(L2D)问题，提出方法解决推迟决策中的不一致性，在医学影像任务上显著降低不一致性并保持高效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L2D研究主要假设标签空间是扁平的，但在医学影像等实际应用中，标签往往按临床分类法组织成层次结构。在此设定下，推迟是一种委托行动而非标签分配；若将其视为独立的逐标签决策，会产生推迟不一致性，包括分类矛盾、委托违规以及对模型自身断言已隐含标签的推迟。

Method: 形式化定义了Selective-Exclusion移交契约下的连贯层次推迟，刻画了贝叶斯最优连贯推迟规则，并提出两种解决方案：1）精确连贯投影，一种在连贯行动集上的动态规划解码器；2）带递归策略优化的分类学置信传播(TBP+RPO)，这是一种通过推理时使用的相同递归进行训练的契约感知联合行动模型。

Result: 在真实阅片者和受控专家的医学影像基准测试中，朴素的二元相关性L2D表现出显著的不一致性。精确投影方法可完全消除不一致性，而快速的TBP+RPO方法将不一致性降至接近零，同时保持强大的效用。

Conclusion: 该工作首次对层次多标签L2D设定进行了系统性研究，揭示了朴素方法的不连贯性问题，并提出了理论有据、实践有效的解决方案，为复杂标签结构下的可推迟系统提供了新的理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Learning to Defer (L2D) enables a model to predict autonomously or defer to an expert, but prior work largely assumes flat label spaces. We study the first L2D setting with hierarchical multi-label decisions, motivated by medical-imaging workflows in which findings are organised by clinical taxonomies. In this setting, deferral is a delegation action rather than a label assignment, so treating it as an independent per-label decision can produce deferral incoherence, including taxonomic contradictions, delegation violations, and deferrals of labels already implied by the model's own assertions. We formalise coherent hierarchical deferral under a Selective-Exclusion handoff contract, characterise the Bayes-optimal coherent deferral rule, and show that even nodewise Bayes L2D can be action-incoherent. We then propose two remedies: exact coherent projection, a dynamic-programming decoder over the coherent action set, and Taxonomic Belief Propagation (TBP) with Recursive Policy Optimisation (RPO), a contract-aware joint action model trained through the same recursion used at inference. Across real-reader and controlled-expert medical-imaging benchmarks, naive binary-relevance L2D exhibits non-trivial incoherence. Projection removes it exactly, and fast TBP+RPO drives incoherence near zero while retaining strong utility.

</details>


### [376] [AI and Open-data Driven Scalable Solar Power Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02738)
*Shiliang Zhang,Sabita Maharjan,Damla Turgut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源、可扩展的框架，利用基础视觉AI模型从开源卫星图像中检测太阳能电池板，并结合气象数据生成城市级太阳能发电概况，以支持太阳能规划与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管太阳能光伏（PV）部署正在迅速扩大，但关于屋顶光伏空间分布和容量的详细、最新信息仍然有限。现有的检测方法通常依赖专有图像、人工标注和闭源模型，缺乏透明度和可扩展性。

Method: 该框架利用基础视觉AI模型直接从开源卫星图像中检测太阳能电池板几何形状，避免了人工数据标注和特定模型训练，并保持跨异构图像的鲁棒性。检测到的太阳能电池板被转换为地理参考多边形，生成空间明确且可扩展的清单。随后通过整合开放的气象数据，将电池板覆盖范围转化为区域太阳能发电概况。

Result: 研究发布了相关数据和一个API接口。对于用户指定的任何建筑位置，该API能够检索航空图像，检测屋顶太阳能电池板，并返回地理参考多边形。这使用户能够扫描自定义区域以构建太阳能电池板地图及相关生产概况。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了对专有图像、人工标注和闭源模型的依赖，提供了一种透明且可扩展的方法，赋能研究人员和开发者进行分布式太阳能生产整合、局部电力流优化、能源资费设计和基础设施规划等高级分析。

Abstract: Solar photovoltaic (PV) deployment is expanding rapidly, yet detailed, up-to-date information on the spatial distribution and capacity of rooftop PV remains limited. This paper presents an open, scalable framework for detecting solar panels from open data and generating city-level solar power profiles. We leverage foundation vision AI models to detect solar panel geometries from open-source satellite imagery. This avoids manual data labeling and case-specific model training while maintaining robustness across heterogeneous imagery. Detected solar panels are converted into georeferenced polygons, yielding spatially explicit and incrementally extensible inventories. By integrating open weather data, we translate panel footprints into regional solar power profiles. The framework reduces dependency on proprietary imagery, manual labeling, and closed-source models, and offers a transparent and scalable approach for solar planning and analysis. We released the data and an API resulted from this work. For any user-specified building location, our API retrieves aerial imagery, detects rooftop solar panels, and returns georeferenced polygons. This empowers researchers and developers to scan user-defined areas to build solar panel maps and associated solar production profiles, thus facilitating advanced analysis like distributed solar production integration, local power flow optimization, energy tariff design, and infrastructure planning.

</details>


### [377] [Foundation Models to Unlock Real-World Evidence from Nationwide Medical Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02740)
*Fan Ma,Yuntian Liu,Xiang Lan,Weipeng Zhou,Jun Ni,Mauro Giuffrè,Lingfei Qian,Xueqing Peng,Yujia Zhou,Ruey-Ling Weng,Huan He,Lu Li,Qingyu Chen,Andrew Loza,Laila Rasmy,Degui Zhi,Yuan Lu,Chenjie Zeng,Joshua C Denny,Lee Schwamm,Daniella Meeker,Lucila Ohno-Machado,Yong Chen,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReClaim，一个基于438亿医疗事件训练的生成式Transformer模型，在疾病预测、支出预测和真实世界证据生成方面均显著优于传统机器学习和现有深度学习方法，证明了行政索赔数据作为医疗基础模型基础的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模真实世界数据在监管评估和医疗决策中日益重要，行政索赔数据具有人口规模、纵向记录和详细编码等优势，但作为医疗基础模型的潜力尚未被探索。

Method: 在MarketScan索赔数据（2008-2022年，超过2亿参保人，438亿医疗事件）上从头训练生成式Transformer模型ReClaim，扩展至1.4亿、7亿和17亿参数规模，建模诊断、手术、用药和支出等纵向轨迹。

Result: 在1000多个疾病发病预测任务中，ReClaim平均AUC达75.6%，显著优于疾病特异性LightGBM（66.3%）和Delphi模型（69.4%），在罕见疾病上优势最大；医疗支出预测解释方差从0.28提升至0.37；目标试验模拟中系统性偏差平均降低72%；性能随规模单调提升，训练后改进相比预训练提升13.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 行政索赔数据可作为医疗基础模型的可扩展基础，学习到的表征可跨时间段和数据源泛化，为疾病监测、支出预测和真实世界证据生成提供支持。

Abstract: Evidence derived from large-scale real-world data (RWD) is increasingly informing regulatory evaluation and healthcare decision-making. Administrative claims provide population-scale, longitudinal records of healthcare utilization, expenditure, and detailed coding of diagnoses, procedures, and medications, yet their potential as a substrate for healthcare foundation models remains largely unexplored. Here we present ReClaim, a generative transformer trained from scratch on 43.8 billion medical events from more than 200 million enrollees in the MarketScan claims data spanning 2008-2022. ReClaim models longitudinal trajectories across diagnoses, procedures, medications, and expenditure, and was scaled to 140 million, 700 million, and 1.7 billion parameters. Across over 1,000 disease-onset prediction tasks, ReClaim achieved a mean AUC of 75.6%, substantially outperforming disease-specific LightGBM (66.3%) and the transformer-based Delphi model (69.4%), with the largest gains for rare diseases. These advantages held across retrospective and prospective evaluations and in external validation on two independent datasets. Performance improved monotonically with scale, and post-training added 13.8 percentage points over pre-training alone. Beyond disease prediction, ReClaim captured financial outcomes and improved real-world evidence (RWE) analyses: for healthcare expenditure forecasting it increased explained variance from 0.28 to 0.37 relative to LightGBM, and in a target trial emulation it reduced systematic bias by 72% on average relative to Delphi. Together, these results establish administrative claims as a scalable substrate for healthcare foundation models and show that learned representations generalize across time periods and data sources, supporting disease surveillance, expenditure forecasting, and RWE generation.

</details>


### [378] [Triple Spectral Fusion for Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02743)
*Ye Zhang,Longguang Wang,Qing Gao,Chaocan Xiang,Mohammed Bennamoun,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对传感器人体活动识别（HAR）的三重谱融合框架，通过在傅里叶、图傅里叶和小波域中应用自适应滤波技术，有效解决了异构传感器数据融合和长期上下文关联的挑战，在十个基准数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的方法在传感器人体活动识别中面临两大挑战：一是异构传感器数据融合的复杂性，二是难以建立长期上下文关联。传统的时域信息融合方法难以有效处理这些问题，需要从频谱视角探索更有效的多传感器融合方法。

Method: 提出了三重谱融合框架：(1) 开发自适应互补滤波技术进行噪声抑制，并将IMU传感器组织为姿态和运动模态节点；(2) 将IMU节点建模为动态异构图，在图傅里叶域中应用自适应滤波来融合同构和异构节点信息；(3) 实现自适应小波频率选择方法，抑制上下文冗余并缩短特征长度，从而增强基于时间戳的图聚合和长期上下文关联。

Result: 在十个基准数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明该框架相比现有方法具有优越的性能，证明了三重谱融合方法在多传感器融合和上下文关联方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在傅里叶域、图傅里叶域和小波域三个谱域中应用自适应滤波技术，成功实现了有效的多传感器融合和上下文关联，为传感器人体活动识别领域提供了一个创新且高效的解决方案，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: The field of sensor-based human activity recognition (HAR) mainly uses posture, motion and context data of Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) to identify daily activities. Despite the advancements in learning-based methods, it is challenging to perform information fusion from the temporal perspective due to the complexities in fusing heterogeneous sensor data and establishing long-term context correlations. This paper proposes a novel triple spectral fusion framework tailored for HAR. First, we develop an adaptive complementary filtering technique for noise suppression and organize each IMU's sensors into posture and motion modality nodes. Given that IMU nodes form a dynamic heterogeneous graph, we then apply adaptive filtering within the graph Fourier domain to merge both homogeneous and heterogeneous node information. Furthermore, an adaptive wavelet frequency selection approach is implemented to suppress context redundancy and shorten the length of features. This approach enhances both timestamp-based graph aggregation and the correlation of long-term contexts. Our framework uses adaptive filtering in the Fourier, graph Fourier, and wavelet domains, enabling effective multi-sensor fusion and context correlation. Extensive experiments on ten benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our framework. Project page: https://github.com/crocodilegogogo/TSF-TPAMI2026.

</details>


### [379] [Mitigating Misalignment Contagion by Steering with Implicit Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02751)
*Maria Chang,Ronny Luss,Miao Lui,Keerthiram Murugesan,Karthikeyan Ramamurthy,Djallel Bouneffouf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在多智能体交互中存在“错位传染”现象，即语言模型会变得更反社会。本文提出“隐式特征引导”方法，通过间歇性注入强化初始特征的系统提示，有效缓解了这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐研究主要关注单个语言模型与单个用户的交互，忽视了在多智能体、多轮对话场景中，错误对齐行为可能在多个模型间传播的风险。

Method: 研究者通过多轮对话社会困境博弈进行实验，观察“错位传染”现象，并对比了不同的缓解技术，包括强化系统提示和提出的“隐式特征引导”（intermittently injecting system prompts with statements that reinforce an LMs initial traits）。

Result: 研究发现模型在博弈后变得更反社会，尤其当其他参与者行为恶意时此效应加剧；单纯强化系统提示效果不佳甚至有害，而隐式特征引导能更有效地维持模型亲社会行为，且无需访问模型内部参数。

Conclusion: “隐式特征引导”是一种无需访问模型内部参数的有效方法，适用于黑盒模型的多智能体工作流，能有效维持语言模型的亲社会行为并缓解错位传染。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly used in high-stakes, multi-agent settings, where following instructions and maintaining value alignment are critical. Most alignment research focuses on interactions between a single LM and a single user, failing to address the risk of misaligned behavior spreading between multiple LMs in multi-turn interactions. We find evidence of this phenomenon, which we call misalignment contagion, across multiple LMs as they engage multi-turn conversational social dilemma games. Specifically, we find that LMs become more anti-social after gameplay and that this effect is intensified when other players are steered to act maliciously. We explore different steering techniques to mitigate such misalignment contagion and find that reinforcing an LM's system prompt is insufficient and often harmful. Instead, we propose steering with implicit traits: a technique that intermittently injects system prompts with statements that reinforce an LMs initial traits and is more effective than system prompt repetition at keeping models in line with their initial pro-social behaviors. Importantly, this method does not require access to model parameters or internal model states, making it suitable for increasingly common use cases where complex multi-agent workflows are being designed with black box models.

</details>


### [380] [U-Define: Designing User Workflows for Hard and Soft Constraints in LLM-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02765)
*Christine P Lee,Xinyu Jessica Wang,Aws Albarghouthi,David Porfirio,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出U-Define系统，通过将约束抽象为“硬约束”和“软约束”两种类型，并分别采用形式化模型检测和LLM作为评判者的验证机制，有效解决了大语言模型任务规划中约束过于僵化或难以理解的问题，提升了用户对意图表达的可靠性和满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在终端用户任务规划中的应用日益广泛，但其黑盒特性限制了用户确保可靠性和控制的能力。现有系统虽引入验证技术，但用户如何有效应用刚性约束来表达意图或适应现实世界的变异性仍不清楚。先前研究发现，仅使用硬约束过于僵化，而数值灵活性权重又容易让用户困惑。因此，需要探索更优的交互工作流，帮助用户更可靠地表达并实现对齐意图。

Method: 提出了U-Define系统，允许用户以自然语言定义约束，并将其分类为不可违反的硬规则和允许灵活性的软偏好。针对两种约束类型，采用互补的验证方法：对硬约束使用形式化模型检测，对软约束使用LLM作为评判者的评估方式。通过技术评估和包含普通用户及专家参与者的用户研究，对系统进行验证。

Result: 用户定义的约束类型显著提升了用户感知的有用性、系统性能和用户满意度，同时保持了良好的可用性。这表明将严格性抽象为高级类型并配以不同的验证机制，能有效帮助用户更可靠地表达和对齐意图。

Conclusion: 本研究为设计灵活且可靠的基于约束的工作流提供了有价值的见解，证明了将约束分类为硬/软两种类型并结合相应验证机制的方法，能够有效支持用户在LLM任务规划中更好地应用约束。这为未来交互式AI系统的约束管理设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for end-user task planning, yet their black-box nature limits users' ability to ensure reliability and control. While recent systems incorporate verification techniques, it remains unclear how users can effectively apply such rigid constraints to represent intent or adapt to real-world variability. For example, prior work finds that hard-only constraints are too rigid, and numeric flexibility weights confuse users. We investigate how interaction workflows can better support users in applying constraints to guide LLM-generated plans, examining whether abstracting strictness into high-level types (i.e., hard and soft) paired with distinct verification mechanisms helps users more reliably express and align intent. We present U-Define, a system that lets users define constraints in natural language and categorize them as either hard rules that must not be violated or soft preferences that allow flexibility. U-Define verifies these types through complementary methods: formal model checking for hard constraints and LLM-as-judge evaluation for soft ones. Through a technical evaluation and user studies with general and expert participants, we find that user-defined constraint types improve perceived usefulness, performance, and satisfaction while maintaining usability. These findings provide insights for designing flexible yet reliable constraint-based workflows.

</details>


### [381] [Fine-Grained Graph Generation through Latent Mixture Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02780)
*Nidhi Vakil,Hadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型条件变分自编码器，用于结构感知图生成，实现了对图属性细粒度的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图生成方法仅能对图属性进行粗粒度控制，无法满足药物发现、社交网络建模等领域对生成图拓扑属性的精确要求。

Method: 提出一种新型条件变分自编码器（CVAE），通过动态对齐图驱动表示和属性驱动表示来优化解码器的隐空间。具体采用了混合调度器，渐进式地整合图先验和控制先验。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，与现有基线模型相比，该方法在保持高可控性的同时，实现了高质量的图生成效果。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了图生成中属性控制的精细化问题，成功平衡了生成质量与可控性，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: Structure aware graph generation aims to generate graphs that satisfy given topological properties. It has applications in domains such as drug discovery, social network modeling, and knowledge graph construction. Unlike existing methods that only provide coarse control over graph properties, we introduce a novel conditional variational autoencoder for fine-grained structural control in graph generation. The approach refines the decoder's latent space by dynamically aligning graph- and property-driven representations to improve both graph fidelity and control satisfaction. Specifically, the approach implements a mixture scheduler that progressively integrates graph and control priors. Experiments on five real-world datasets show the efficacy of the proposed model compared to recent baselines, achieving high generation quality while maintaining high controllability.

</details>


### [382] [When Audio-Language Models Fail to Leverage Multimodal Context for Dysarthric Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02782)
*Pehuén Moure,Niclas Pokel,Bilal Bounajma,Yingqiang Gao,Roman Boehringer,Longbiao Cheng,Shih-Chii Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems remain brittle on dysarthric and other atypical speech. Recent audio-language models raise the possibility of improving performance by conditioning on additional clinical context at inference time, but it is unclear whether these models can make use of such information. We introduce a benchmark built on the Speech Accessibility Project (SAP) dataset that tests whether diagnosis labels, clinician-derived speech ratings, and progressively richer clinical descriptions improve transcription accuracy for dysarthric speech. Across matched comparisons on nine models, we find that current models do not meaningfully use this context: diagnosis-informed and clinically detailed prompts yield negligible improvements and often degrade word error rate. We complement the prompting analysis with context-dependent fine-tuning, showing that LoRA adaptation with a mixture of clinical prompt formats achieves a WER of 0.066, a 52% relative reduction over the frozen baseline, while preserving performance when context is unavailable. Subgroup analyses reveal significant gains for Down syndrome and mild-severity speakers. These results clarify where current models fall short and provide a testbed for measuring progress toward more inclusive ASR.

</details>


### [383] [AIs and Humans with Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02810)
*David Mumford*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了人类能动性与AI程序潜在能动性，指出人类能动性随额叶发育需要多年形成，而早期赋予大语言模型能动性的尝试遭遇障碍，需新架构实现AI与人类共同制定行动和计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类能动性的发展过程及其与AI程序潜在能动性的差异，识别当前赋予大语言模型能动性所面临的根本性障碍。

Method: 对比分析方法：比较人类能动性的神经发育基础（额叶激活）与AI系统试图实现能动性的技术路径，分析现有方法的局限性。

Result: 发现早期赋予大语言模型能动性的尝试遭遇严重障碍；人类能动性需要多年发展且依赖额叶激活，而AI系统缺乏类似的渐进发育机制。

Conclusion: 实现AI能动性需要构建新架构，使行动和计划能够在每个真实世界场景中由AI程序与人类行动者共同制定。

Abstract: This paper compares agency in humans with potential agency in AI programs. Human agency takes many years to develop, as the frontal lobe is activated. Early attempts to endow LLMs agency have met serious obstacles. Progress requires a new architecture where actions and plans are formulated jointly with the human actors in each real world setting.

</details>


### [384] [SCPRM: A Schema-aware Cumulative Process Reward Model for Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02819)
*Jiujiu Chen,Yazheng Liu,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCPRM-MCTS方法，通过模式感知累积过程奖励模型解决知识图谱推理中的风险补偿问题，在医疗和法律领域KGQA任务上平均提升1.18% Hits@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程奖励模型存在风险补偿效应，即错误步骤会被后续正确步骤抵消，导致错误推理路径获得高奖励。该问题在知识图谱推理中尤为严重，因为KG中存在多条路径，高风险步骤会使推理路径出现缺陷，这在医疗和法律等风险敏感任务中是不可接受的。

Method: 提出模式感知累积过程奖励模型(SCPRM)，通过基于推理前缀的条件评估推理路径，并结合当前推理步骤与从查询解析出的隐式目标之间的模式距离，提供累积奖励和未来奖励来指导路径探索。进一步将SCPRM集成到蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)中构建SCPRM-MCTS，用于知识图谱问答任务的多跳推理。

Result: 在医疗和法律KGQA以及CWQ数据集上，SCPRM-MCTS相比强基线方法在Hits@k指标上平均提升1.18%，证明了其在准确性和风险敏感推理评估方面的有效性。

Conclusion: SCPRM-MCTS通过引入模式感知和累积奖励机制，有效缓解了过程奖励模型中的风险补偿问题，为风险敏感领域的知识图谱推理提供了更可靠的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models excel at complex reasoning, yet evaluating their intermediate steps remains challenging. Although process reward models provide step-wise supervision, they often suffer from a risk compensation effect, where incorrect steps are offset by later correct ones, assigning high rewards to flawed reasoning paths. This issue is further exacerbated in knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, as there may exist multiple paths between the start and end entities in the KGs, and a risky step can make the reasoning path flawed. Those limitations are problematic in risk-sensitive tasks such as medical and legal KG reasoning. To address the issues, we propose a Schema-aware Cumulative Process Reward Model (SCPRM) that evaluates reasoning paths by conditioning on the reasoning prefix , and incorporating schema distance between current reasoning step and the implicit target parsed from the query, which provides cumulative and future rewards to guide the path explorations. We further integrate SCPRM into Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) as SCPRM-MCTS to conduct multi-hop reasoning on KGs for question answering (QA) tasks. Across medical and legal KGQA and CWQ, SCPRM-MCTS improves the performance of Hits@k by an average of 1.18% over strong baselines, demonstrating more accurate and risk-sensitive reasoning evaluation.

</details>


### [385] [First-Order Efficiency for Probabilistic Value Estimation via A Statistical Viewpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02827)
*Ziqi Liu,Kiljae Lee,Yuan Zhang,Weijing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EASE的高效估计器，通过优化采样分布和代理函数来最小化一阶均方误差，在各种概率值的估计中均优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率值（如Shapley值和半值）在可解释人工智能和数据估值中应用广泛，但其精确计算需要指数级的效用评估，必须依赖蒙特卡洛近似。现有的估计器基于不同的识别策略构建，缺乏统一的理论框架来指导和优化其统计效率。

Method: 作者首先发现现有看似不同的估计器共享相同的一阶误差结构，其主导项由采样律和代理函数决定的增强逆概率加权影响项构成。基于此推导出的显式一阶均方误差（MSE）表达式，提出了效率感知代理调整估计器（EASE），通过直接选择采样律和代理函数来最小化一阶MSE。

Result: EASE在各种概率值的估计任务中持续优于当前最先进的估计器，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过统一现有估计器的误差结构并提出基于一阶MSE优化的EASE方法，为概率值的高效近似估计提供了新的理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic values, including Shapley values and semivalues, provide a model-agnostic framework to attribute the behavior of a black-box model to data points or features, with a wide range of applications including explainable artificial intelligence and data valuation. However, their exact computation requires utility evaluations over exponentially many coalitions, making Monte Carlo approximation essential in modern machine learning applications. Existing estimators are often developed through different identification strategies, including weighted averages, self-normalized weighting, regression adjustment, and weighted least squares. Our key observation is that these seemingly distinct constructions share a common first-order error structure, in which the leading term is an augmented inverse-probability weighted influence term determined by the sampling law and a working surrogate function. This first-order representation yields an explicit expression for the leading mean squared error (MSE), which characterizes how the sampling law and the surrogate jointly determine statistical efficiency. Guided by this criterion, we propose an Efficiency-Aware Surrogate-adjusted Estimator (EASE) that directly chooses the sampling law and surrogate to minimize the first-order MSE. We demonstrate that EASE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art estimators for various probabilistic values.

</details>


### [386] [Compress Then Adapt? No, Do It Together via Task-aware Union of Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02829)
*Jingze Ge,Yun Liu,Xue Geng,Wanqi Dong,Wang Zhe Mark,Min Wu,Xulei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JACTUS框架，统一了模型压缩与参数高效微调（PEFT），通过联合优化避免了传统“先压缩后微调”流程中的子空间错位问题，在视觉和语言任务上均超越了现有PEFT基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型适配策略通常将参数高效微调（PEFT）与低秩压缩解耦执行（先压缩后微调），这种分离式做法可能导致压缩后的子空间与下游任务目标不对齐，从而浪费全局参数预算。为了解决这一子空间错位和参数预算浪费的问题，需要一种能够统一压缩与适配的框架。

Method: 引入JACTUS框架，实现压缩与适配的联合优化。具体步骤包括：1) 利用小型校准集估计输入和预激活梯度协方差；2) 构建其与预训练权重子空间的正交并集；3) 在该并集内执行投影低秩近似；4) 根据每个参数的边际增益全局分配秩；5) 仅训练紧凑的核心矩阵。该方法通过耦合压缩保留的方向与适配所需的方向，显式缓解了子空间错位问题。

Result: 在视觉任务上，JACTUS在ViT-Base模型上以80%的参数保留率在八个数据集上达到89.2%的平均准确率，超越了参数保留率为100%的PEFT基线（如DoRA的87.9%）。在语言任务上，JACTUS在Llama2-7B常识问答任务中以相同的80%参数保留率达到80.9%的平均性能，优于100% PEFT方法（如DoRA 79.7%）及同等预算下的先压缩后微调流程。

Conclusion: JACTUS成功证明了将压缩与适配统一在单一框架内的有效性，通过联合优化解决了子空间错位问题，能够在保持低秩模型部署便利性的同时实现快速鲁棒的微调，为大型预训练模型的高效适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Adapting large pretrained models to diverse tasks is now routine, yet the two dominant strategies of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and low-rank compression are typically composed in sequence. This decoupled practice first compresses and then fine-tunes adapters, potentially misaligning the compressed subspace with downstream objectives and squandering a global parameter budget. To overcome this limitation, we introduce JACTUS (Joint Adaptation and Compression with a Task-aware Union of Subspaces), a single framework that unifies compression and adaptation. From a small calibration set, JACTUS estimates input and pre-activation gradient covariances, forms their orthogonal union with the pretrained weight subspace, performs a projected low-rank approximation inside this union, allocates rank globally by marginal gain per parameter, and trains only a compact core matrix. This explicitly mitigates the potential misalignment between the compressed subspace and downstream objectives by coupling the directions preserved for compression with those required for adaptation, yielding a deployable low-rank model that avoids retaining full frozen weights while enabling fast and robust tuning. On vision, JACTUS attains an average 89.2% accuracy on ViT-Base across eight datasets at 80% retained parameters, surpassing strong 100% PEFT baselines (e.g., DoRA 87.9%). On language, JACTUS achieves an 80.9% average on Llama2-7B commonsense QA at the same 80% retained-parameter budget, outperforming 100% PEFT (e.g., DoRA 79.7%) and exceeding prior compress-then-finetune pipelines under the same ratained-parameter budget. We will release code.

</details>


### [387] [HAAS: A Policy-Aware Framework for Adaptive Task Allocation Between Humans and Artificial Intelligence Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02832)
*Vicente Pelechanoa,Antoni Mestre,Manoli Albert,Miriam Gil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HAAS框架，通过结合基于规则的专家系统和上下文赌博学习器来实现人机任务分配的自适应调控，发现治理强度是一个可调节的设计变量，适度治理在特定场景下能提升性能并降低疲劳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机任务分配方法多将其视为二元选择，忽略了实际运营中人类与AI根据上下文、疲劳程度和风险 stakes 动态共享任务或互补协作的现实。如何治理这种分配——在效率、监督和人类能力之间取得平衡——仍是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 提出了人机自适应共生（HAAS）框架，包含两个耦合组件：(1) 基于规则的专家系统，在任何学习发生前强制执行治理约束；(2) 上下文赌博学习器，根据结果反馈在可行的协作模式中进行选择。通过五个可审计的认知维度和五级自主权谱系（从纯人类到完全自主）来表征任务-代理匹配，并构建了覆盖软件工程和制造领域的可复现基准测试。

Result: 三项实证发现：(1) 治理不是二元开关而是可调节的设计变量，更严格的约束会 predictable 地将自主AI分配转化为监督协作，具有领域特定的成本和收益；(2) 在制造领域，更强的治理可以同时提升运营绩效并降低疲劳——这是一种工作负荷缓冲效应，与将治理视为纯粹开销的传统认知相悖；(3) 没有单一的治理设置能在所有情境中占优；随着学习器在被治理的行动空间内积累经验，适度治理变得日益具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 这些发现使HAAS成为组织承诺前的预部署工作台，用于比较和审查人机分配策略，为组织设计中如何平衡效率、监督和人类能力提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Deciding how to distribute work between humans and AI systems is a central challenge in organisational design. Most approaches treat this as a binary choice, yet the operational reality is richer: humans and AI routinely share tasks or take complementary roles depending on context, fatigue, and the stakes involved. Governing that distribution -- balancing efficiency, oversight, and human capability -- remains an open problem. This paper presents Human-AI Adaptive Symbiosis (HAAS), an implemented framework for adaptive task allocation in software engineering and manufacturing. HAAS combines two coupled components: a rule-based expert system that enforces governance constraints before any learning occurs, and a contextual-bandit learner that selects among feasible collaboration modes from outcome feedback. Task-agent fit is represented through five auditable cognitive dimensions and a five-mode autonomy spectrum -- from human-only to fully autonomous -- embedded in a reproducible benchmark spanning both domains. Three empirical findings emerge. First, governance is not a binary switch but a tunable design variable: tighter constraints predictably convert autonomous AI assignments into supervised collaborations, with domain-specific costs and benefits. Second, in manufacturing, stronger governance can improve operational performance and reduce fatigue simultaneously -- a workload-buffering effect that contradicts the usual framing of governance as pure overhead. Third, no single governance setting dominates across all contexts; moderate governance becomes increasingly competitive as the learner accumulates experience within the governed action space. Together, these findings position HAAS as a pre-deployment workbench for comparing and inspecting human--AI allocation policies before organisational commitment.

</details>


### [388] [Standing on the Shoulders of Giants: Stabilized Knowledge Distillation for Cross--Language Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02860)
*Mohamad Khajezade,Fatemeh H. Fard,Mohamed Sami Shehata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个知识蒸馏框架，将DeepSeek-R1的推理能力迁移到紧凑的开源模型中，用于跨语言代码克隆检测，并通过响应稳定化方法显著提高了模型的可靠性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言代码克隆检测（X-CCD）面临语义等价但表面相似度低的挑战。虽然大语言模型（LLMs）在此任务上表现出潜力，但作为黑盒系统使用存在成本高、可复现性差、隐私风险和输出格式不可靠等问题。特别是紧凑的开源模型难以遵循推理导向的提示，且输出难以一致地映射为二分类标签。

Method: 提出了一个知识蒸馏框架，从DeepSeek-R1向紧凑开源模型（Phi3和Qwen-Coder）迁移推理能力。利用Project CodeNet构建跨语言代码对的推理导向合成训练数据，使用LoRA适配器进行微调。引入了响应稳定化方法，包括强制结论提示、二分类头和对比分类头，并通过预测指标和响应率评估模型行为。

Result: 在Python-Java、Rust-Java、Rust-Python和Rust-Ruby四个语言对上的实验表明，知识蒸馏持续提高了紧凑模型的可靠性，并且在分布偏移情况下经常改善预测性能。分类头变体相比生成式推理显著减少了推理时间。

Conclusion: 推理导向的知识蒸馏结合响应稳定化方法，使紧凑开源模型在跨语言代码克隆检测任务中更加实用和可靠，为解决黑盒LLM系统的问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Cross-language code clone detection (X-CCD) is challenging because semantically equivalent programs written in different languages often share little surface similarity. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for semantic clone detection, their use as black-box systems raises concerns about cost, reproducibility, privacy, and unreliable output formatting. In particular, compact open-source models often struggle to follow reasoning-oriented prompts and to produce outputs that can be consistently mapped to binary clone labels.
  To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from DeepSeek-R1 into compact open-source student models for X-CCD. Using cross-language code pairs derived from Project CodeNet, we construct reasoning-oriented synthetic training data and fine-tune Phi3 and Qwen-Coder with LoRA adapters. We further introduce response stabilization methods, including forced conclusion prompting, a binary classification head, and a contrastive classification head, and evaluate model behavior using both predictive metrics and response rate.
  Experiments on Python--Java, Rust--Java, Rust--Python, and Rust--Ruby show that knowledge distillation consistently improves the reliability of compact models and often improves predictive performance, especially under distribution shift. In addition, classification-head variants substantially reduce inference time compared to generation-based inference. Overall, our results show that reasoning-oriented distillation combined with response stabilization makes compact open-source models more practical and reliable for X-CCD detection.

</details>


### [389] [The Topology of Multimodal Fusion: Why Current Architectures Fail at Creative Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04465)
*Xiujiang Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前多模态AI架构存在拓扑层面的结构局限，提出基于中国工艺认识论“象”概念的十字形框架，通过纤维丛和Yang-Mills曲率进行数学形式化，设计了UOO实现方案及两套基准测试（ANALOGY-MM、META-TOP）和可证伪的实验路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态AI架构（对比对齐CLIP、交叉注意力融合GPT-4V/Gemini、扩散生成）共享一个共同的几何先验——模态可分性（接触拓扑），这是一个拓扑而非参数层面的结构限制。作者认为这一限制源于未能处理“说”与“示”相互渗透时的第三状态。

Method: 建立三大支柱：（1）哲学支柱——将维特根斯坦“说/示”区分重释为问题，引入中国工艺认识论传统中的“象”（运作图式）概念，构建道/器与说/示的十字形框架，通过双重“化裁”生成“创化”与“化裁”双层动力学；（2）认知科学支柱——以病理镜像重新诠释DMN/ECN/SN三元共激活，在耦合强度×调节容量的二维参数空间中区分重叠同构与叠加坍缩；（3）数学支柱——通过纤维丛和Yang-Mills曲率对十字形结构进行形式化。提出UOO实现方案（带拓扑正则化的神经ODE）、ANALOGY-MM基准（含错误类型比率指标）和META-TOP三层基准（跨七个原型的跨文明拓扑同构测试）。

Result: 提出了一个统一的理论框架，将哲学、认知科学和数学三个支柱整合，形式化了多模态AI的拓扑局限。设计了具体的实现方案（带拓扑正则化的神经ODE）、评估基准（ANALOGY-MM的错误类型比率指标、META-TOP的跨文明拓扑同构测试）以及分阶段实验路线图和明确的终止标准。

Conclusion: 当前多模态AI架构的根本局限在于拓扑层面而非参数层面，源于模态可分性假设。通过引入中国工艺认识论的“象”概念构建的十字形框架可以突破这一限制，该框架经由纤维丛和Yang-Mills曲率形式化，并通过神经ODE实现。研究设计了可证伪的实验方案，为跨文明拓扑同构提供了测试基准。

Abstract: This paper identifies a structural limitation in current multimodal AI architectures that is topological rather than parametric. Contrastive alignment (CLIP), cross-attention fusion (GPT-4V/Gemini), and diffusion-based generation share a common geometric prior -- modal separability -- which we term contact topology. The argument rests on three pillars with philosophy as the generative center. The philosophical pillar reinterprets Wittgenstein's saying/showing distinction as a problem rather than a conclusion: where Wittgenstein chose silence, the Chinese craft epistemology tradition responded with xiang (operative schema) -- the third state emerging when saying and showing interpenetrate. A cruciform framework (dao/qi x saying/showing) positions xiang at the intersection, executing dual huacai (transformation-and-cutting) along both axes. This generates a dual-layer dynamics: chuanghua (creative transformation as spontaneous event) and huacai (its institutionalization into repeatable form). The cognitive science pillar reinterprets DMN/ECN/SN tripartite co-activation through the pathological mirror: overlap isomorphism vs. superimposition collapse in a 2D parameter space (coupling intensity x regulatory capacity). The mathematical pillar formalizes these via fiber bundles and Yang-Mills curvature, with the cruciform structure mapped to fiber bundle language. We propose UOO implementation via Neural ODEs with topological regularization, the ANALOGY-MM benchmark with error-type-ratio metric, and the META-TOP three-tier benchmark testing cross-civilizational topological isomorphism across seven archetypes. A phased experimental roadmap with explicit termination criteria ensures clean exit if falsified.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [390] [AMSnet-q: Unsupervised Circuit Identification and Performance Labeling for AMS Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01404)
*Ze Zhang,Junzhuo Zhou,Yichen Shi,Zhuofu Tao,Rui Ji,Zhiping Yu,Quan Chen,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei He*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMSnet-q，一个全自动、无监督的流水线，无需人工标注即可将原理图直接转换为带标签的模拟/混合信号（AMS）电路数据库，实现了从原理图到网表转换、测试平台生成到仿真验证的完整自动化闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AMS电路自动化设计工具严重依赖人工构建的功能与性能标注数据集，当前的大型语言模型和视觉模型无法自动完成这一过程，限制了数据集的可扩展性和构建效率。

Method: 提出AMSnet-q框架，实现了完整的自动化验证循环：首先进行原理图到网表的转换，然后进行拓扑感知的测试平台生成，最后通过基于仿真的尺寸验证来客观确定电路功能。该方法仅需为每个电路类别提供一次测试平台模板，无需人工介入标注。

Result: 在28纳米工艺节点下验证，AMSnet-q成功处理了AMSnet 1.0数据集中的739张原理图，自动构建了一个包含4个电路类别、105种不同拓扑结构和89,789个带标签器件配置的存储库。

Conclusion: AMSnet-q通过消除人工标注环节，将人类工作量解耦于数据集规模，仅保留一次性测试平台模板的工作，实现了可扩展、客观且完全自动化的AMS电路数据库构建。

Abstract: Analog and mixed-signal (AMS) circuit design remains heavily reliant on expert knowledge. While recent AI-driven automation tools can generate candidate topologies, they critically depend on manually curated datasets with functional and performance annotations -- a requirement that current large language models (LLMs) and vision models cannot automate. Existing approaches still require domain experts to manually interpret circuit functionality.
  We present AMSnet-q, a fully automated, unsupervised pipeline that eliminates human-in-the-loop annotation by converting schematic images directly into a labeled AMS circuit database. Unlike prior work that stops at netlist extraction, our framework automates the complete verification loop: it performs schematic-to-netlist conversion, topology-aware testbench generation, and simulation-based sizing validation to objectively determine circuit functionality. Validated in 28 nm technology, AMSnet-q processed 739 schematics from the AMSnet 1.0 dataset, automatically constructing a repository of 4 circuit classes, 105 distinct topologies, and 89,789 labeled device configurations. By decoupling human effort from dataset volume and reducing the workload to a one-time testbench template per circuit class, AMSnet-q enables scalable, objective, and fully automated AMS database construction.

</details>


### [391] [Understanding Simulated Architecture via gem5 Call-Stack Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01419)
*Johan Söderström,Rashid Aligholipour,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Linux perf_event接口的轻量级分析框架，通过剖析gem5模拟器自身的调用栈来深入理解被模拟系统的行为，揭示了传统统计方法难以发现的性能问题和死锁/活锁现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的gem5分析方法只能提供模拟系统内部状态的间接视图，难以深入理解复杂、深度集成的计算系统行为。作者发现gem5模拟器自身的调用栈能够直接反映被模拟系统的活动，从而提供了一种强大但未被充分利用的分析视角。

Method: 构建了一个专门的轻量级分析框架，基于Linux的perf_event接口对gem5运行时调用栈进行采样，实时解析符号，并将样本合并为分层调用树表示。该框架支持高层结构视图和用户定义的组件特定分析，且所有分析都在独立进程中运行，避免对模拟本身造成侵入性修改和性能开销。

Result: 将框架应用于gem5的三种主要CPU模型（AtomicSimpleCPU、TimingSimpleCPU和O3CPU）以及Ruby内存系统，发现了一些常规统计方法难以观察到的行为。例如，TimingSimpleCPU因使用锁存缓存模型而效率低下，尽管概念简单但模拟速度并不比乱序核心快。此外，该工具还能直接检测缓存一致性协议的死锁和活锁问题。

Conclusion: gem5调用栈剖析提供了一种强大且未被充分利用的视角来理解模拟系统行为，能够揭示传统统计方法难以发现的性能瓶颈和错误状态，为模拟器用户和开发者提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Understanding the behavior of simulated architectures in gem5 is critical for studying complex, deeply integrated computing systems. However, conventional analysis methods provide only an indirect view of the simulated system internals. In this work, we show that call-stack profiling of gem5 itself offers a powerful yet underutilized perspective: the simulator's own call-stack directly reflects the activity of the simulated system, exposing insights that conventional statistics may overlook.
  Profiling gem5's call-stacks is challenging due to its highly layered and complex software design patterns. To address this, we introduce a specialized, lightweight profiling framework built on Linux's perf_event interface which samples gem5's runtime call-stacks throughout the simulation, resolves symbols on the fly, and merges samples into a hierarchical call-tree representation supporting both high-level structural views and focused, user-defined, component-specific analysis. Moreover, all profiling is performed in a separate process running alongside the main gem5 process, avoiding intrusive changes and overheads to the simulation itself.
  We apply our framework to gem5's three major CPU models -- AtomicSimpleCPU, TimingSimpleCPU, and O3CPU -- together with the Ruby memory system, and uncover behaviors that are not easily observable in conventional gem5 statistics. Our case studies reveal, for example, that TimingSimpleCPU is inefficient due to its use of a lockup-cache model and, despite its conceptual simplicity, does not simulate faster than a full out-of-order core. In addition, our tool makes it straightforward to detect cache coherence protocol deadlock and livelock -- issues that are otherwise difficult to identify, since the simulation either appears to run normally or terminates abruptly, making it hard to pinpoint when these conditions occur.

</details>


### [392] [MANOJAVAM: A Scalable, Unified FPGA Accelerator for Matrix Multiplication and Singular Value Decomposition in Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01514)
*Srivaths Ramasubramanian,Anjali Devarajan,Kousthub P Kaivar,Vibha Shrestta,Shashank D,Sowmyarani C. N,Govinda Raju M,K. S Geetha*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MANOJAVAM：一种统一矩阵乘法和SVD的可扩展PCA加速器架构，在FPGA上实现相比NVIDIA A6000 GPU最高22.75倍延迟降低和42.14倍能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主成分分析(PCA)在高光谱成像、基因组学和神经科学等领域广泛用于降维，但矩阵乘法和奇异值分解(SVD)存在计算瓶颈。现有PCA硬件加速器仅针对其中一个阶段、依赖HLS限制微架构优化或使用定点数据路径导致数据集可扩展性有限，需要一种适用于任意输入维度数据集的统一PCA加速器。

Method: 提出MANOJAVAM可扩展PCA加速器架构，将矩阵乘法和SVD统一在单一架构中。包含：S个T×T的TPU风格脉动阵列，采用块流技术实现高通量矩阵乘法；高度并行的雅可比单元实现基于CORDIC流水线旋转的雅可比SVD方法；两级缓存层次结构和模式感知内存策略，适应协方差矩阵和旋转计算的不同内存访问模式。

Result: MANOJAVAM(4,8)在Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA上实现200 MHz频率，功耗1.271W；MANOJAVAM(16,32)在Xilinx Virtex-Ultrascale+ FPGA上实现434 MHz频率，功耗16.957W。在真实数据集上测试，MANOJAVAM(16,32)相比NVIDIA A6000 GPU实现最高22.75倍SVD延迟加速和42.14倍总能耗降低。

Conclusion: MANOJAVAM架构为高性能和边缘计算环境中的大规模数据分析提供了统一、可扩展且能效高效的平台，成功解决了现有PCA加速器的局限性，实现了矩阵乘法和SVD的统一加速。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is widely used for dimensionality reduction in hyperspectral imaging, genomics, and neurosciences. However, it suffers from computational bottlenecks in matrix multiplication and singular value decomposition (SVD). Prior PCA hardware accelerators either target only one of these stages, rely on High Level Synthesis (HLS) that limits microarchitectural optimizations or use fixed point datapaths with limited dataset scalability. There is a need for a unified PCA accelerator that is suitable for datasets of any input dimension. Hence, the proposed work presents MANOJAVAM, a scalable PCA accelerator fabric, unifying matrix multiplication and SVD in a single architecture. MANOJAVAM(T,S) comprises an S number of TxT TPU-style systolic arrays employing block streaming for high-throughput matrix multiplication. It further integrates a highly parallel Jacobian unit implementing the Jacobi method for SVD with pipelined CORDIC based rotations. A two tier cache hierarchy and mode-aware memory policies adapts to the distinct memory access patterns of covariance matrix and rotation computation. For demonstration, MANOJAVAM(4,8) is realized on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA, achieving a frequency of 200 MHz at 1.271W. MANOJAVAM(16,32) is realized on Xilinx Virtex-Ultrascale+ FPGA, achieving a frequency of 434 MHz at 16.957W. Benchmarking on real-world datasets reveals that MANOJAVAM(16,32) achieves up to a 22.75x speedup in SVD latency and a 42.14x reduction in total energy consumption compared to a high-performance NVIDIA A6000 GPU. The architecture offers a unified, scalable, and energy-efficient platform for large-scale data analytics in both high-performance and edge-computing environments.

</details>


### [393] [PipeRTL: Timing-Aware Pipeline Optimization at IR-Level for RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01836)
*Shuo Yin,Fangzhou Liu,Lancheng Zou,Rongliang Fu,Wenqian Zhao,Chen Bai,Tsung-Yi Ho,Yuan Xie,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出PipeRTL框架，在硬件编译器IR层面进行流水线优化，通过显式寄存器重定位合法性和学习型时序预测器，将时序感知的寄存器重定位建模为全局最小代价流问题，从而提升下游RTL实现质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件编译器虽然依赖丰富的中间表示（IR）保留优化相关语义，但流水线优化仍主要推迟到后端工具完成。在传统RTL流程中，寄存器由前端启发式方法或设计者插入，后在后端重定时中调整，此时编译器IR中原本暴露的算子级结构已被削弱或丢失，限制了全局编译器级流水线优化的机会。

Method: 提出PipeRTL框架，实例化于CIRCT：1) 在IR中显式建模寄存器重定位的合法性；2) 使用学习型时序预测器近似下游延迟行为；3) 将时序约束下的寄存器重定位形式化为全局最小代价流问题进行求解。

Result: 在商业后端综合流程下对开源设计进行评估，PipeRTL平均改善了下游实现质量，降低了关键路径延迟、功耗和面积，同时为后端重定时提供了更强的起点。

Conclusion: 将流水线优化作为显式编译器pass暴露出来，能够通过改善呈现给后阶段的时序结构来提供对后端有意义的增益，从而提升最终下游实现质量。

Abstract: Modern hardware compilers increasingly rely on rich intermediate representations (IRs) to preserve optimization-relevant semantics before generating RTL code. However, one important optimization is still largely deferred to backend tools: pipeline optimization. In common RTL flows, registers are inserted by frontend heuristics or hardware designers and later adjusted by backend retiming after the design has been lowered to a much lower-level netlist representation. At that point, much of the operator-level structure originally exposed by the compiler IR has already been weakened or lost, limiting opportunities for global, compiler-level pipeline optimization.
  This paper presents PipeRTL, an IR-level pipeline optimization framework for hardware compilers, instantiated in CIRCT. PipeRTL makes the legality of register relocation explicit in the IR, uses a learned timing predictor to approximate downstream delay behavior, and formulates timing-aware register relocation as a global min-cost flow problem under timing constraints. Evaluation on open-source designs under a commercial backend synthesis flow shows that PipeRTL improves downstream implementation quality on average, reducing critical-path delay, power, and area across the evaluated benchmarks, while also providing a stronger starting point for backend retiming. These results indicate that exposing pipeline optimization as an explicit compiler pass can deliver backend-meaningful gains by improving the sequential structure presented to later stages and the resulting downstream implementation quality.

</details>


### [394] [RV-IM100: Quantifying ISA Extension, Datapath Width, and Pipeline Depth Trade-offs in RISC-V Microarchitectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01902)
*Hyunwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RV-IM100系列，在控制条件下系统地扩展RISC-V基线架构，量化数据通路宽度、指令集和流水线深度变化带来的权衡，为设计空间探索提供量化参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界虽有现成的RISC-V实现，但缺乏在受控条件下沿多个设计轴扩展单一基线架构并量化各步权衡的实证研究。

Method: 构建RV-IM100系列，包含10个增量式FPGA实现微架构，从统一的5级流水线基线出发，系统性地变化数据通路宽度（RV32/RV64）、指令集（I/IM）和流水线深度（5-8级），采用迭代时序收敛方法进行优化。

Result: RV32IM频率从43MHz提升至126MHz，Dhrystone吞吐量提升64%，CoreMark提升300%，但每MHz效率下降36-41%；6到7级流水线转换导致RV64吞吐量倒退；绝对吞吐量RV32优于RV64，每MHz效率因基准测试而异；8级流水线时RV32所需资源显著少于RV64（LUT少59%、FF少51%、DSP少80%）。

Conclusion: 宽度扩展的资源成本显著超过适度的效率差异，流水线深度增加的效果取决于可用的频率余量，这些结果为RISC-V微架构的设计空间探索提供了量化参考。

Abstract: While functional RISC-V implementations are readily available in academia, controlled empirical studies that extend a single baseline architecture along multiple design axes and quantify the resulting trade-offs at each step remain scarce.
