This paper presents RV-IM100, a family of 10 incremental FPGA-implemented microarchitectures derived from a common 5-stage pipeline baseline, systematically varying datapath width from RV32 to RV64, instruction set from I to IM, and pipeline depth from 5 to 8~stages under controlled conditions. Using an iterative timing-closure methodology, RV32IM frequency improved from 43 to 126MHz, increasing Dhrystone throughput by 64% and CoreMark by 300%, while per-MHz efficiency decreased by 36--41%. The 6-to-7-stage transition caused throughput regression in RV64 despite higher frequency, revealing that the outcome depends on available frequency headroom. Cross-width comparison showed RV32 outperforming RV64 in absolute throughput, with per-MHz efficiency diverging by benchmark: RV64 led by 2.3% in DMIPS/MHz while RV32 led by 4.6% in CoreMark/MHz. At 8 stages, RV32 required 59% fewer LUTs, 51% fewer FFs, and 80% fewer DSPs, indicating that the resource cost of width extension substantially exceeds the modest efficiency differences. These results provide a quantitative reference for design-space exploration in RISC-V microarchitectures. All RTL sources and benchmark configurations are publicly available.

</details>


### [395] [ViM-Q: Scalable Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for Vision Mamba Model Inference on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01935)
*Shengzhe Lyu,Yuhan She,Patrick S. Y. Hung,Ray C. C. Cheung,Weitao Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ViM-Q，一个针对Vision Mamba模型在FPGA端侧部署的可扩展算法-硬件协同设计方案，通过混合量化策略与定制化加速器架构，在ZCU102上相比GPU基线实现了4.96倍加速和59.8倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Mamba (ViM) 模型凭借状态空间模型 (SSMs) 的线性复杂度在效率上优于Transformer，但在FPGA上高效部署面临三大挑战：(1) 线性层中动态激活离群值导致静态量化失效；(2) 低比特位宽下均匀量化无法有效捕捉权重分布；(3) GPU上的关联扫描 (associative scan) 内存访问模式与FPGA所需的流式数据流不匹配。

Method: 提出ViM-Q算法-硬件协同设计框架：(1) 硬件感知量化策略——结合动态逐令牌激活量化和逐通道平滑技术以缓解离群值，采用定制4比特逐块加性2的幂次方 (APoT) 权重量化；(2) 运行时可配置FPGA加速器——包含使用查找表 (LUT) 单元以移位-加法替代乘法的线性引擎，以及细粒度流水线化SSM引擎（在保持顺序递归的同时并行化状态维度）；(3) 支持运行时配置以适应ViM家族中不同维度和输入分辨率。

Result: 在AMD ZCU102 FPGA上实现，针对ViM-tiny低批量推理场景，相较于量化后的NVIDIA RTX 3090 GPU基线，ViM-Q平均实现4.96倍加速和59.8倍能效提升。

Conclusion: ViM-Q通过算法与硬件的协同优化，成功解决了ViM模型在FPGA部署中的量化与架构适配难题，为在资源受限的边缘设备上部署ViM模型提供了可行路径，证明了线性复杂度模型在端侧应用的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Vision Mamba (ViM) models offer a compelling efficiency advantage over Transformers by leveraging the linear complexity of State Space Models (SSMs), yet efficiently deploying them on FPGAs remains challenging. Linear layers struggle with dynamic activation outliers that render static quantization ineffective, while uniform quantization fails to capture the weight distribution at low bit-widths. Furthermore, while associative scan accelerates SSMs on GPUs, its memory access patterns are misaligned with the streaming dataflow required by FPGAs. To address these challenges, we present ViM-Q, a scalable algorithm-hardware co-design for end-to-end ViM inference on the edge. We introduce a hardware-aware quantization scheme combining dynamic per-token activation quantization and per-channel smoothing to mitigate outliers, alongside a custom 4-bit per-block Additive Power-of-Two (APoT) weight quantization. The models are deployed on a runtime-parameterizable FPGA accelerator featuring a linear engine employing a Lookup-Table (LUT) unit to replace multiplications with shift-add operations, and a fine-grained pipelined SSM engine that parallelizes the state dimension while preserving sequential recurrence. Crucially, the hardware supports runtime configuration, adapting to diverse dimensions and input resolutions across the ViM family. Implemented on an AMD ZCU102 FPGA, ViM-Q achieves an average 4.96x speedup and 59.8x energy efficiency gain over a quantized NVIDIA RTX 3090 GPU baseline for low-batch inference on ViM-tiny. This co-design shows a viable path for deploying ViM models on resource-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [396] [Monolithic 3D Integration for Null Convention Logic (NCL)-Based Asynchronous Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02047)
*Xiameng Zhang,Kushal Ponugoti,Ashiq Sakib,Madhava Vemuri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了将单片三维（M3D）技术与准延迟不敏感（QDI）异步NCL逻辑相结合的设计方法，成功解决了传统异步电路面积过大的问题，实现了面积、延迟和功耗的同步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准延迟不敏感（QDI）异步数字设计虽具有高速低功耗的潜力，但因缺乏成熟的CAD工具和较大的面积开销，其应用受到严重限制。单片三维（M3D）技术通过顺序集成制造高密度集成电路，可减少面积、缩短连线并提升性能，这为解决异步电路的面积瓶颈提供了新途径。

Method: 将M3D技术与QDI Null Convention Logic (NCL)相集成，提出了M3D-based NCL标准单元的设计方法论。具体采用阈值门（threshold gates）设计了一个M3D-NCL无符号阵列乘法器电路进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在保守的M3D连线长度缩减条件下，该设计实现了44%的面积缩减，同时延迟降低约31%，功耗降低约17%。

Conclusion: M3D技术与QDI NCL逻辑的结合能有效缓解传统平面（2D）异步电路的面积效率低下问题，在显著减少面积的同时还能提升性能和降低功耗，为异步电路设计提供了一条可行的发展路径。

Abstract: As the demand for high-speed and low-power electronics continues to grow, the quasi-delay-insensitive (QDI) asynchronous domain of digital design has emerged as a promising alternative to traditional clock-based designs. However, the adoption of the paradigm has been greatly limited due to the lack of mature computer-aided design (CAD) tools and a substantially larger area footprint, owing to various architectural constraints. Monolithic-3D (M3D) technology has recently paved the way for manufacturing highly dense integrated circuits (ICs) through sequential integration, resulting in a reduced area footprint, shorter wirelengths, and increased performance. In this study, we integrate M3D technology with QDI Null Convention Logic (NCL) and propose a design methodology for the implementation of M3D-based NCL standard cells, aimed at mitigating the area inefficiencies of traditional planar or 2D counterparts. Furthermore, we employed the threshold gates to design an M3D-NCL unsigned array multiplier circuit. Simulation results suggest that, for a conservative wirelength reduction resulting from M3D implementation, a substantial area reduction of 44% can be achieved while simultaneously reducing delay and power by approximately 31% and 17%, respectively.

</details>


### [397] [Cerberus: Cross-Layer ECC Co-Design for Robust and Efficient Memory Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02220)
*Junhwan Kim,Seunghyun Kim,Yesin Ryu,Saeid Gorgin,Jungrae Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Cerberus跨层ECC协同设计框架，通过一次编码多次解码（EODM）架构统一设备、链路和系统三层保护，在降低冗余开销的同时提升对集群故障和边缘故障的恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DRAM向更高密度和I/O速度发展，确保数据正确性变得愈发困难。业界采用三层ECC堆栈（片上ECC、链路ECC、系统ECC），但这些层级独立演化导致冗余重复、覆盖缺口和相互干扰问题。

Method: 提出Cerberus跨层ECC协同设计：1) 采用一次编码多次解码（EODM）架构，控制器执行单次编码，冗余信息被L-ECC用于立即写路径检测和重试，被O-ECC用于读取时的设备内修复，被S-ECC用于端到端恢复；2) 联合设计互补的奇偶校验和伴随式结构；3) 对解码器排序并分配纠错预算，防止误纠错放大并在严格冗余约束下实现选择性纠正。

Result: 评估表明，Cerberus提升了对集群故障和边缘故障的恢复能力，同时减少了冗余开销，验证了协调的跨层保护对下一代内存系统（如定制HBM）的重要性。

Conclusion: 协调的跨层保护对下一代内存系统至关重要，Cerberus证明了通过统一设计三层ECC可以在保持各层原生角色的同时，有效解决冗余重复、覆盖缺口和相互干扰问题。

Abstract: As DRAM scales to higher density and I/O speeds, ensuring data correctness becomes increasingly difficult. Industry has responded with a three-layer stack: on-die ECC (O-ECC), link ECC (L-ECC), and system ECC (S-ECC). However, these layers have evolved independently, often duplicating redundancy, leaving coverage gaps, and occasionally interfering.
  We propose Cerberus, a cross-layer ECC co-design that unifies protection across device, link, and system while preserving the native role of each layer. At its core is an Encode-Once, Decode-Many (EODM) architecture: the controller performs a single encoding whose redundancy is reused by L-ECC for immediate write-path detection and retry, by O-ECC for in-device repair on reads, and by S-ECC for strong end-to-end recovery. Cerberus jointly designs complementary parity and syndrome structures, orders decoders, and allocates the correction budget to prevent miscorrection amplification and enable selective correction under tight redundancy constraints. Our evaluations show improved resilience to clustered and peripheral faults while reducing redundant overhead, underscoring the importance of coordinated cross-layer protection for next-generation memory systems, such as custom HBMs.

</details>


### [398] [Performance and Energy Benefits of MRDIMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02371)
*Pau Díaz,Mariana Carmin,Pouya Esmaili-Dokht,Victor Xirau,Felippe Zacarias,Henrique Potter,Harald Servat,Miquel Moreto,Eduard Ayguadé,Petar Radojković*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对生产级服务器中的多路复用秩内存条（MRDIMM）进行了详细的性能、功耗和能效评估。结果显示，相比传统RDIMM，MRDIMM在带宽受限工作负载下性能提升27-41%，延迟降低数百纳秒，且在扩展带宽区域可实现高达30%的服务器能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多路复用秩内存条（MRDIMMs）作为一种新兴的内存技术，能够在不提高DRAM芯片频率的情况下提供更高的带宽。然而，对于这种新技术在实际生产服务器环境中的性能、功耗和能效表现缺乏详细的评估研究，因此需要对其进行系统性的分析。

Method: 在生产级高端服务器上，对搭载MRDIMM的主内存系统进行详细的性能、功耗和能效评估。通过与传统的寄存器内存条（RDIMMs）进行对比实验，分析带宽、延迟、功耗和能效等关键指标在不同工作负载下的表现。

Result: 从RDIMM升级到MRDIMM可将带宽扩展41%，为带宽受限工作负载带来27-41%的性能提升；延迟改善达到数百纳秒，惠及广泛的延迟敏感型工作负载；在相同带宽利用率下，两者功耗相近；在MRDIMM扩展带宽区域，性能提升远超功耗增加，为内存受限工作负载节省高达30%的服务器能耗。

Conclusion: MRDIMM是一种有效的内存升级方案，能够在不显著增加功耗的前提下，大幅提升服务器性能并降低能耗。该技术对带宽受限和延迟敏感型工作负载均具有显著优势，是提升数据中心能效的有价值技术路径。

Abstract: Multiplexed Rank DIMMs (MRDIMMs) have recently emerged as memory devices that enable higher bandwidth without increasing DRAM chip frequencies. This paper presents a detailed performance, power and energy evaluation of a production server with high-end MRDIMM main memory. The memory system upgrade from conventional registered DIMMs (RDIMMs) to MRDIMMs extends the bandwidth by 41% yielding 27-41% higher performance for bandwidth-bound workloads. Additionally, the latency improvement reaches hundreds of nanoseconds, benefiting a broad class of workloads sensitive to memory latency. At the same bandwidth utilization levels, RDIMMs and MRDIMMs exhibit similar power consumption. In the MRDIMM-extended bandwidth region, the performance improvements largely exceed the power increase, delivering up to 30% server energy savings for memory-bound workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [399] [VUDA: Breaking CUDA-Vulkan Isolation for Spatial Sharing of Compute and Graphics on the Same GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01352)
*Bin Xu,Pengfei Hu,Wenxin Zheng,Jinyu Gu,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出VUDA系统，打破CUDA计算与Vulkan渲染的执行隔离，实现GPU空间并行共享，在具身AI工作负载上相比时间共享基线提升高达85%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI的GPU仿真环境（物理仿真用CUDA，渲染用Vulkan）存在执行隔离问题：CUDA和Vulkan创建独立GPU上下文，绑定不同调度组，导致计算与图形被限制在互斥的时间片，无法实现空间复用。现有空间共享技术仅限CUDA生态，时间共享则资源利用率低。

Method: VUDA基于两点观察：(1) CUDA和Vulkan在驱动和硬件层收敛到共同的通道原语；(2) 二者虚拟地址空间天然不相交，可安全合并页表。VUDA提供轻量API标注可共调度的CUDA流，通过通道重定向进入Vulkan调度域，并采用页表嫁接统一地址空间，消除关键路径上的数据拷贝。

Result: 在代表性具身AI工作负载上，VUDA相比时间共享基线吞吐量提升高达85%，同时提高GPU利用率并降低端到端延迟。

Conclusion: VUDA成功打破执行隔离，首次实现CUDA计算与Vulkan图形的空间并行，为具身AI的仿真数据生成和RL训练提供了高效的GPU资源共享方案。

Abstract: GPU-based simulation environments for embodied AI interleave physics simulation (CUDA) and photorealistic rendering (Vulkan) on a single device. We observe that two foundational scenarios -- simulation data generation and RL training -- can be naturally adapted to execute their simulation and rendering phases concurrently, presenting a significant opportunity to improve GPU utilization through spatial multiplexing. However, a fundamental obstacle we term execution isolation prevents this: CUDA and Vulkan create separate GPU contexts whose channels are bound to different scheduling groups, confining compute and graphics to mutually exclusive time slices. Existing spatial-sharing techniques are limited to the CUDA ecosystem, while temporal-sharing approaches underutilize available resources.
  This paper presents VUDA, a system that breaks execution isolation to enable spatial parallelism between CUDA compute and Vulkan graphics workloads. VUDA is built on two key observations: although CUDA and Vulkan expose different programming abstractions, their execution paths converge to a common channel primitive at the driver and hardware level; meanwhile, their virtual-address spaces are inherently disjoint, making safe page-table merging feasible without remapping. VUDA exposes a thin API for developers to annotate co-schedulable CUDA streams, and realizes spatial sharing through channel redirection into Vulkan's scheduling domain and page-table grafting to unify address spaces, eliminating all data copying on the critical path. Experiments on representative embodied-AI workloads show that VUDA delivers up to 85% higher throughput than temporal-sharing baselines, while improving GPU utilization and reducing end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [400] [CityOS: Privacy Architecture for Urban Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02886)
*Giorgio Cavicchioli,Mark Chen,Navid Salami Pargoo,Shuren Xia,Xiaotian Zhou,Roxana Geambasu,Jason Nieh,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了CityOS，一种面向城市感知的操作系统，通过三层隐私约束API架构解决城市传感器数据访问中的隐私风险和政策执行问题，实现了在保障强隐私的同时支持实际城市应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前城市正在快速部署各类感知基础设施（摄像头、环境传感器、联网信息亭等），这些设施持续观测公共空间，但缺乏系统架构来管理应用程序如何访问、聚合和保留这些数据，导致隐私风险并阻碍了统一政策执行。现有的城市数据管理方式无法有效平衡数据利用与隐私保护。

Method: 提出CityOS操作系统，采用受隐私意识网络接口启发的三层API架构来调解应用程序对传感器数据的访问：(1)On-Scene层：支持实时感知，原始数据限制在本地上下文中；(2)Single-Locality Aggregation层：在固定位置启用差分隐私的纵向统计数据访问；(3)Cross-Locality Aggregation层：通过跨位置聚合支持全市范围分析，用户设备执行每用户隐私预算。CityOS作为边缘运行时运行，在临时容器中执行不可信应用程序，执行隐私策略并通过广播差分隐私损失提供透明度。

Result: 成功实现CityOS系统及跨所有层级的应用程序，包括行人安全警报、实时和预测停车可用性、交通仪表板和地铁轨迹测量等。实验结果表明，CityOS能够在强制执行强隐私保护的同时支持实际街景应用程序的运行，验证了三层架构的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: CityOS为城市感知提供了一种可行的系统架构解决方案，能够在支持多样化城市应用的同时有效保护用户隐私。通过分层API设计和边缘计算架构，实现了数据效用与隐私保护的平衡，为智慧城市数据治理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Cities are rapidly deploying sensing infrastructure -- cameras, environmental sensors, and connected kiosks -- that continuously observe public spaces, yet they lack a system architecture governing how applications access, aggregate, and retain this data, creating privacy risks and preventing consistent policy enforcement. We present CityOS, an operating system for urban sensing that mediates application access to sensor data through a three-tier API inspired by structured, privacy-conscious web interfaces. The tiers expand the spatial scope of data access while imposing progressively stronger privacy constraints: On-Scene supports real-time sensing with raw data confined to the local context; Single-Locality Aggregation enables differentially private longitudinal statistics at a fixed location; and Cross-Locality Aggregation supports citywide analytics via aggregation across locations, with user devices enforcing per-user privacy budgets. CityOS runs as an edge runtime that executes untrusted applications in ephemeral containers, enforcing these policies and providing transparency via broadcasts of differential privacy loss. We implement CityOS and applications across all tiers -- including pedestrian safety alerts, real-time and forecast parking availability, traffic dashboards, and subway trajectory measurement -- and show that it supports practical streetscape applications while enforcing strong privacy.

</details>
